Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.

Text:

<1>
śrīḥ
alaṅkāramaṇihāre tṛtīyabhāgaprārambhaḥ
atha sārālaṅkārasaraḥ
saivottarottarotkarṣe sāra ityucyate budhaiḥ ||
saiva śṛṅkhalā pūrvapūrvāpekṣayā uttarottaramutkarṣavarṇane sārālaṅkāra ityucyate | ayamevodārālaṅkāra iti sarvasvakṛtā vyavahriyate | tathāca tadīyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ  "uttarottaramutkarṣaṇamudāraḥ" iti | imaṃ cālaṅkāramekānekaviṣayatvena dvividhamāmananti | ekaviṣayatāyāmavasthābhedāśrayaṇamāvaśyakaṃ, utkarṣasya bhedaniyatatvāt | na hyavasthādikaṃ bhedaṃ vinā kiṃcidapi vastu svāpekṣayā svayamadhikaṃ bhavitumīṣṭe | tatrāpyuttarottaramutkarṣaḥ svarūpeṇa dharmeṇa vā bhavatītyasya cāturvidhyamiti vimarśanīkāraḥ ||
tatra ekaviṣaye svarūpeṇottarottarotkarṣo yathā
so'vyāttrivikramo'smānyasyoḍutatiḥ krameṇa vṛddhiṃ bhajataḥ | ādau vajravataṃso muktāhārastato'tha maṇikāñcyāsīt || 1407 ||
atra ekasyaiva bhagavatastattadavasthāviśiṣṭatayā svarūpeṇottarottaramutkarṣaḥ |
yathāvā
udayaṃstvatpadarucibhāgvigalitabālyo'tha kanakakaṭakaśrīḥ | prauḍhaścūḍāmaṇinā krīḍāṃ dyumaṇistanoti tava bhagavan || 1408 ||
yathāvā
bālastava padanakharucimathendurardhassameti phālatulām | saṃpūrṇo mukhalakṣmīṃ bhavati hi śuciruttarottarotkarṣam || 1409 ||
atra pūrvapūrvāvasthāviśiṣṭābhyāṃ sūryācandramobhyāṃ uttarottarāvasthāviśiṣṭayorevotkarṣa ukta ityekaviṣayatvam | ayamarthāntaranyāsaśiraska iti viśeṣaḥ ||
baliyajñabhuvaṃ vrajatassthalakamalamiva vyabhāttavopendra padam | utkṣiptamatha chatraṃ sutrāmṇo'tha ca mahadvitānaṃ jagatām || 1410 ||
atra bhagavaccaraṇasya pūrvāvasthāyāṃ sthalakamalavadalpaparimāṇasyaiva sataḥ chatrādivaduttarottaramahattvāvasthāviśiṣṭatayā utkarṣa ukta ityekaviṣayatvam ||
dharmeṇa yathā
dīpa ivādau dava iva tato'tha yugavigamaravirivojjvalitaḥ | grasati sma te pratāpo virādhamārīcadaśamukhānbhagavan || 1411 ||
atraikasyaiva bhagavatpratāpasya ujjvalitatvarūpadharmapuraskāreṇottarottarotkarṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
aṅkuritā pallavitā korakitā kusumitā ca mama bhaktiḥ | kramatastvayi madhusūdana mahatī bhūtvā prasauti phalamanagham || 1412 ||
atraikasyā eva bhakteḥ mahattvadharmapuraskāreṇottarottarotkarṣo nibaddhaḥ ||
yadyatraikasminnāśraye krameṇānekādheyasthitirūpaḥ paryāyālaṅkāro'tra pratīyate, tarhi pratīyatāṃ nāma | na hi tāvatā pūrvapūrvāpekṣayā uttarottarotkarṣarūpassāro galahastyate ||
anekaviṣayassvarūpeṇotkarṣo yathā
rajatācalamūrdhani yastanmūrdhani yā'sti sā'pi yatpadajā | so'pica yatpadalākṣālakṣitavakṣā jayatyasau lakṣmīḥ || 1413 ||
atrānekeṣāṃ svarūpeṇa pūrvapūrvāpekṣayā uttarottaramutkarṣaḥ ||
dharmeṇa yathā
prāṇiṣu manujāśśreṣṭhā manujeṣu brāhmaṇā budhāsteṣu | tvaccakralāñchitāsteṣvacyuta teṣu tvadaṅghridattabharāḥ || 1414 ||
yathāvā
ajñādgranthī śreyāṃstasmāttaddhāraṇe kṛtī tasmāt | jñānī tasmādvyavasitamatistato'pyacyuta svarūpajñaḥ || 1415 ||
taddhāraṇe kṛtī granthadhāraṇaṃ kṛtavānityarthaḥ | kṛtaśabdāt ‘iṣṭādibhyaśca' iti kartari iniḥ| ‘ktasyenviṣayasya karmaṇyupasaṅkhyānam' iti karmaṇastaddhāraṇaśabdātsaptamī | granthinaḥ granthādhyayanakṛtaḥ | anayorudāharaṇayoḥ manujaprabhṛtīnāṃ granthiprabhṛtīnāṃ ca śraiṣṭhyarūpadharmamukhenottarottarotkarṣaḥ | anayoścādya udāharaṇe
bhūtānāṃ prāṇinaśreṣṭhāḥ prāṇināṃ buddhijīvinaḥ |
buddhimatsu narāśśreṣṭhā nareṣu brāhmaṇāssmṛtāḥ ||
ityādimanuvacanacchāyā puraskṛtā | dvitīye tu
ajñebhyo granthinaśśreṣṭhā granthibhyo dhāriṇo varāḥ |
dhāribhyo jñāninaśreṣṭhā jñānibhyo vyavasāyinaḥ |
iti manuvacanakrama ukta iti dhyeyam | ayaṃ sarvo'pi ślāghyadharmotkarṣaḥ ||
aślāghyadharmotkarṣo yathā
malino mahiṣastasmādbalibhuktasmācca timiranikurumbam | mama hṛdayaṃ tasmādapi vimalayasi yadīdamīśvaro'si tvam || 1416 ||
ubhayarūpo yathā
avanitalaṃ vipulatamaṃ gaganaṃ tasmāttato'pi
jagadaṇḍam | tasmādgartāmbu tato'pyaghaṃ mama hare kathaṃ hareretat || 1417 ||
hareḥ hṛño liṅ madhyamaikavacanam | atra gartāmbuparyanteṣu vipulatamatvaṃ ślāghyo dharmaḥ | prakṛtārthe tvaghe aślāghyaḥ ||
atredamavadheyam  ekaviṣaye śṛṅkhalāyā acārutayā tadanuprāṇitassāro na cārutāṃ labhate, tasyāssvābhāvikabhedāpekṣitvenāvasthādikṛtabhede anullāsāt | ataevāsminviṣaye vardhamānakanāmālaṅkāro'nyairabhyupagataḥ | tallakṣaṇaṃ ca ‘rūpadharmābhyāmādhikye vardhamānakam' iti kṛtam | tasmātkāraṇamālādiryathā śṛṅkhalaikaviṣayā, na tathā sāraśśakyo vaktum, ekaviṣaye alaṅkārāntarābhyupagamaprasaṅgāt | ‘sāro guṇasvarūpābhyāṃ vaiśiṣṭye pūrvapūrvataḥ' iti tu lakṣaṇaṃ sārasyayuktam | guṇasvarūpābhyāṃ pūrvapūrvavaiśiṣṭye sāra iti tadarthaḥ | sa ca kvacicchṛṅkhalānuprāṇitaḥ, kvacitsvatantra ityanekaviṣayatvamekaviṣayatvaṃ ca sustham | evaṃ śṛṅkhalāviṣayāṇāmalaṅkārāṇāṃ vicchittivailakṣaṇyasyānubhavasiddhatvātpṛthagalaṅkāratve siddhe śṛṅkhalāyā virodhābhedasādharmyādivadanuprāṇayitṛtaivocitā na pṛthagalaṃkāratā | tathātve abhedādīnāmapi pṛthagalaṃkāratāpatteḥ | pūrṇāluptādau tu na vicchittivailakṣaṇyaṃ, apitūpamāvicchittireveti saṃpradāyaḥ | nanu keyaṃ vicchittiḥ? ucyate  alaṃkārāṇāṃ parasparavicchedasya vailakṣaṇyasya hetubhūtā janyatāsaṃsargeṇa kāvyaniṣṭhā kavipratibhā, tajjanyatvaprayuktā camatkāritā vā vicchittiriti gṛhyatām ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre sārasara ekapañcāśaḥ
<2>
atha yathāsaṃkhyālaṅkārasaraḥ
uddeśakramato'rthānāṃ saṃbandho yatra kathyate | prāñcastatra yathāsaṃkhyamapare kramamūcire ||
yathāsaṃkhyamiti padārthānativṛttirūpe yathārthe'vyayībhāvaḥ | saṃkhyāyā anativṛttiśca ādyasyādyenaiva dvitīyasya dvitīyenaivetyādikrameṇa saṃmbandho bhavatīti yogārtha evaṃ lakṣaṇama | anye enaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ kramālaṅkāra iti vyavāhārṣuḥ | sa ca śābdaḥ ārthaśceti dvividhaḥ | tatrāsamastānāmasamastaiḥ krameṇānvaye śābdaḥ, kramikasambandhasyātirohitatvāt | yatra tu samudāyadvayasya samāsenābhidhāne sati prathamamekasya samudāyasyāpareṇa samudāyena sāmānyatassamanvaye sati paścādarthānugamaparyālocanayā tatsamudāyināṃ krameṇa saṃbandhastatrārthaḥ ||
yathā
viṣṇo vinatajanānāṃ tṛṣṇāṃ hṛdayaṃ sukhaṃ ca duḥkhaṃ ca | pruṣṇāsi pluṣṇāsi ca puṣṇāsi vṛṣādrinātha muṣṇāsi || 1418 ||
he vṛṣādrinātha viṣṇo ! vinatajanānāṃ tṛṣṇāṃ pruṣṇāsi pūrayasi | hṛdayaṃ pluṣṇāsi sevase | tatra vasasītyarthaḥ | svasmin snehayasīti vā | ‘pruṣa pluṣa snehanasevanapūraṇeṣu' iti kraiyādikābhyāṃ dhātubhyāṃ laṭ | sukhaṃ puṣṇāsi duḥkhaṃ muṣṇāsi ca
'puṣa puṣṭau' ‘muṣa steye' imāvapi kraiyādikāveva | atra pruṣṇāsītyādikriyāṇāṃ krameṇa tṛṣṇādiṣvanvayaḥ | iha śābdaḥ, asamastānāmasamastaiḥkrameṇānvayāt ||
yathāvā
nayanarucā vadanarucā kamalaṃ vimalāṃśubimbamapi kamale | nūnaṃ jitaṃ bhavatyā jalaṃ niviśate mahābilaṃ cāpi || 1419 ||
mahābilaṃ mahatsuṣiraṃ antarikṣaṃca | ‘meghadvāraṃ mahābilam' ityamaraḥ | idamapi śābdaṃ utprekṣāgarbhaṃ, pūrvaṃ tu śuddhamiti vivekaḥ ||
yathāvā
yau śrīkālībhaktau surapo'trādyaḥ parassurāpo bhavitā | bhāsvattamo'tha mattassvabhāstadākāravipratīpo yadayam || 1420 ||
yau śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ kālī durgā tayoḥ bhaktau upāsakau, atra anayormadhye, ādyaḥ śrībhaktaḥ surānpātīti surapaḥ devādhīśaḥ tattulyo vā ata eva bhāsvattamaḥ atiśayena bhāsvān mahātejaśśālī ca bhavitā | paraḥ kālībhaktastu surāpaḥ surāṇāṃ devānāṃ napātītyapaḥ devārakṣaka ityarthaḥ | surāṃ pibatīti tathoktaḥ madyapa iti tattvam | kālikopāsakānāṃ vāmācārasya prasiddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ | krameṇa ‘pā rakṣaṇe, pā pāne' iti dhātvoḥ 'āto'nupasarge' iti kaḥ | ata eva mattaḥ madaśālī | avi
dyamānā bhāḥ yasya abhāḥ suṣṭhu abhāḥ svabhāḥ ativelatejohīna ityarthaḥ | na hi surāpasya brahmatejo bhavediti bhāvaḥ | mattaścāsau svabhāvaśceti viśeṣaṇobhayapadakarmadhārayaḥ | kuto'nayorvaiṣamyamityata aha  tadākāreti | yat yasmāt ayaṃ caramanirdiṣṭaḥ kālībhaktaḥ tadākāravipratīpaḥ tadākārasya prathamanirdiṣṭaśrībhaktākārasya tadācārasyeti yāvat vipratīpaḥ pratikūlaḥ sadācāravāmācārayorativiprakarṣāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe  ayaṃ surāpa iti śabdaḥ tadākāravipratīpaḥ tasya surapaśabdasya ākāreṇa āvarṇena madhyastheneti bhāvaḥ | vipratīpaḥ viruddharūpaḥ | madhye āvarṇasadbhāvātsurapaśabdaviruddharūpa iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ ayaṃ mattasvabhāvaśśabdaḥ tadākārasya bhāsvattamaśabdasvarūpasya vipratīpaḥ pratilomaḥ | bhāsvattama iti śabda eva prātilomyena dṛṣṭo mattasvabhā iti niṣpadyata ityartho'pi camatkārātiśayaṃ puṣṇāti | atrāpi śābda eva | pūrvasmādvailakṣaṇyaṃ tu sphuṭameva ||
yathāvā
amba tvadāśritānāmitareṣāmapi śarīriṇāṃ bhavati | vipulaiśvaryaṃ viralaiśvaryaṃ cātrāsti purata eva bhidā || 1421 ||
vipulaiśvaryaṃ bhūmīśvaratvaṃ viralaiśvaryaṃ alpavibhavaḥ | atra anayoḥ tvadāśritetarāśritayoḥ tadvibhavayorvā | purataḥ agrata
eva bhidā bhedaḥ asti | spaṣṭameva prakāśata ityarthaḥ | pakṣe atra vipulaiśvaryaviralaiśvaryaśabdayoḥ purata eva puvarṇaravarṇābhyāmeva bhidetyartho'pi camatkārī | atrāpi śābda eva ||
arthaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ yathā
jalaruhajalanidhivasatī hṛdayojjvalaratnahāravanamālau | hṛtsaudhe sadhryañcau prāñcau jāyāpatī viharatāṃ naḥ || 1422 ||
atra jalaruhajalanidhivasatī ityādisamudāyasya prathamaṃ jāyāpatirūpasamudāyānvaye sati paścādarthānurodhena jalaruhāderjāyāyāṃ jalanidhyādeḥ patyau ca samanvayadhīrityārtho yathāsaṃkhyālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
tāvakamativibhavābhyāṃ bṛhaspatidivaspatī samaṃ vijitau | bṛhadiva vailakṣaṇyaṃ prakāśayete mithastadapyetau || 1423 ||
bṛhaspatidivaspatī gurusutrāmāṇau | tāvakānāṃ bhāgavatānāṃ mativibhavābhyāṃ samaṃ tulyaṃ vijitau | tadapi vijitatvataulye'pi etau bṛhaspatidivaspatī mithaḥ anyonyaṃ vailakṣaṇyaṃ bhedaṃ bṛhadiva mahadiva prakāśayete vijitatvarūpeṇāvailakṣaṇye'pi tatmahadiva darśayete ityarthaḥ | yadvā ivaśabdo vākyālaṃkāre | guruśiṣyatvarūpaṃ mahadvailakṣaṇyaṃ darśayete iti hṛdayam | pakṣe bṛhaspatidivaspatiśabdau bṛha diva iti varṇābhyāmeva vailakṣaṇyaṃ prakāśayete | na tvanyavarṇata ityarthaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva sarvaṃ, śabdaparārthāntaravarṇanaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ |
yathāvā
daśavadanavibhīṣaṇayordeva tvatprātikūlyasakhyajuṣoḥ | bhavatassma śoṣaśeṣau bhagavaṃstatprakṛtiguṇabhidāvaśataḥ || 1424 ||
he deva bhagavan ! tvatprātikūlyasakhyajuṣoḥ daśavadanavibhīṣaṇayoḥ śoṣaśeṣau bhavataḥ sma abhūtām | rāvaṇasya śoṣaḥ vibhīṣaṇasya śeṣa iti bhāvaḥ | śuṣaśiṣorbhāve ghañ | tatra hetumāha  taditi | tayoḥ daśavadanavibhīṣaṇayoḥ prakṛtiguṇānāṃ svabhāvasiddhaguṇānāṃ ‘dambho darpo'bhimānaśca' iti, ‘abhayaṃ sattvasaṃśuddhirjñānayogavyasthitiḥ' iti ca gītānāṃ āsurasaṃpadguṇānāṃ daivasaṃpadguṇānāṃ ca bhidāvaśāt bhedavaśāt bhavataḥ smeti yojanā | bhavateḥ smayogena bhūte laṭ | āsurasaṃpadabhijātatayā bhagavatprātikūlyena daśānanasya nāśaḥ | daivasaṃpadabhijātatayā tatsakhyena vibhīṣaṇasyāyurādiśreyasvattayā pāriśeṣyaṃ cābhūtāmityarthaḥ | pakṣe prakṛtī pratyayavidhāvuddeśyabhūte dhāturūpe | tayoḥ guṇau tadbhidāvaśataḥ ghañnimittakatayā ‘pugantalaghūpadhasya' iti vihitaguṇayośca bhedavaśādityarthaḥ | śuṣ śiṣ iti prakṛtī bhinne tayorguṇau okāraikāralakṣaṇau ca bhinnau tādṛśabhedavaśāt śauṣa iti śeṣa iti śabdau niṣpannāviti bhāvaḥ | anyattulyaṃ pūrveṇa ||
atredaṃ vivecanīyaṃ  idaṃ yathāsaṃkhyamalaṃkārasaraṇimāḍhaukitumīṣṭe vā na veti | na hyasmin lokasiddhe kavipratibhānirmitatvasyālaṃkāratājīvātormātrayā'pyupalambho'sti | yenālaṃkāravyapadeśo manāgapi sāṃprataṃ syāt | ataḥ apakramatvarūpadoṣābhāva eva yathāsaṃkhyam | evaṃcodbhaṭamatānuyāyināmuktayaḥ kūṭakārṣāpaṇavadamanohāriṇya eva | etena yathāsaṃkhyameva kramālaṃkārasaṃjñayā vyavaharato vāmanasyāpi giro vyākhyātā iti navyāḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre yathāsaṃkhyasaro dvipañcāśaḥ
<3>
atha paryāyālaṃkārasaraḥ
adheyasya kramādekasyānekādhārasaṃśrayaḥ | varṇyate yatra tatroktā paryāyo'yamalaṃkṛtiḥ ||
yatraikamādheyamanekasminnādhāre kramāt svato bhavati kavinā vā nibadhyate tatra paryāyālaṃkāraḥ | atra 'antarbahiḥ puraḥ paścātsarvadikṣvapi saiva me' iti viśeṣālaṃkāravyāvṛttaye krameṇeti | tatra hyādheyasya yugapadanekādhārasaṃbandha iti nātiprasaṅgaḥ | ata eva kramāśrayaṇātparyāya ityanvarthā saṃjñā | asya ca prayojakābhidhānānabhidhānābhyāṃ dvaidhī ||
yathā
dugdhodanvati pūrvaṃ mugdhendau tadanu vidadhatī vāsam | adhunā tu sudhā nivasati madhunāśana tāvakīnacariteṣu || 1425 ||
atraikasyāssudhāyāḥ krameṇa dugdhodanvadādyanekādhārasaṃśrayaṇaṃ nibaddham | tatra dugdhodanvanmugdhendvossudhāyāssthitirvastusatyevoktā | varṇanīyabhagavaccariteṣu tu kavinā paraṃ nibaddhā | yatrādhārādheyatatsaṃbandhakrameṣu kvacidapi vā kavikalpanāpekṣā'sti tatraivāyamalaṃkāraḥ | yatra tu sarvāṃśe lokasiddhatvaṃ na tatra kaścidalaṃkāra iti tu tattvam ||
yathāvā
cintāmaṇissudhābdhessudhāśanādhipapade nidhāya
padam | vātāśanagiriśikhare jātāvāso ramānivāsātmā || 1426 ||
atra prayojakānabhidhānaṃ spaṣṭam |
tadabhidhāne yathā
pravarā mauktikamaṇayaḥ pravālamatiruciramamṛtasāraśca | vāridhinā svasutāyā vadane vātsalyato nyadhāyiṣata || 1427 ||
atra vāridhinā vātsalyata iti prayojakanirdeśaḥ | atrottamamuktāphalādīnāmarṇavalakṣmīvadanarūpānekādhāraniveśanam | arṇavasyādhāratā aārthī | pūrvodāharaṇeṣu tu sā śābdī | iyaṃ radanādīnāṃ muktāphalādikatvenādhyavasānādrūpākātiśayoktyanuprāṇitā ceti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
vidrumagatamāruṇyaṃ vidhinā tava daśanavasanayornyastam | tadanu padanalinayugme svaprauḍhiprathanakutukato'bdhisute || 1428 ||
atrāpi vidhinā svaprauḍhiprathanakutukata iti prayojakanirdeśaḥ | ayaṃ sarvo'pi saṃkocavikāsāspṛṣṭatayā śuddhaparyāyaḥ |
saṃkocaparyāyo yathā
jagadakhilaṃ vyāpya purā jaradibhamukhyeṣvatha kvacillagnā | agaṇitaguṇa tava karuṇā nigalitacaraṇā kilādya mayyeva || 1429 ||
idaṃ svasminneva bhagavato bhūyasī kṛpetyabhimanyamānasya kavervacanam | atra karuṇāyā uttarottaramādhārasaṅkocātsaṅkocaparyāyo'yam ||
yathāvā
parameṣṭhipadānmahato giriśajaṭājūṭakoṭare lagnā | aravindavāsini tato līnā mandasmite'mba tava gaṅgā || 1430 ||
atra gaṅgāyā uttarottaramādhārasaṅkocaḥ | atiśayoktyanuprāṇitatvamubhayatra tulyam | pūrvamudāharaṇamutprekṣāśiraskaṃ, idaṃ tu vakṣyamāṇamīlitaśiraskamiti bhidā ||
vikāsaparyāyo yathā
amba yadaiśvaryaṃ tvayi saṃbandhāttava tadambujākṣe'pi | saṃbaddhaṃ kiṃ bahunā tryambakamukhasurakadambake' pi tataḥ || 1431 ||
ambujākṣe bhagavati śrīnivāse | atraiśvaryasya pūrvapūrvādhārāparityāgenaivādhārāntarasaṃkramaṇam | vikāsaparyāyo'yam ||
yathāvā
vakṣasyevaikṣyata te kaustubhasauvastike kaṭhoratvam | etarhi pātakānmama cetasyapi daityaśātanodītam || 1432 ||
he daityaśātana daityāre ! idaṃ saṃsārabhāraniviṇṇasya kaverbhagavantaṃ pratyupālambhavacanam | atrāpi pūrvavadeva vikāsaparyāyaḥ | kaṭhoratvaśabdapratipādyakāṭhinyanairghṛṇyarūpārthadvayaśleṣanirvyūḍhatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ ||
atra rasagaṅgādharakṛt   "ekasaṃbandhanāśottaramaparasabandhe satyeva loke paryāyapadaprayogadarśanāt "śroṇībandhastyajati tanutāṃ sevate madhyabhāgaḥ' iti prakāśodāhṛte tathaiva dṛṣṭatvācca |
biṃmboṣṭha eva rāgaste tanvi pūrvamadṛśyata |
adhunā hṛdaye'pyeṣa mṛgaśābākṣi dṛśyate ||
ityatra paryāyakathanamayuktam" ityabhāṇīt | anya tu prakāśa eva 'bimboṣṭhe' iti padyasyodāharaṇena paryāyasya samarthanāt alaṅkāraśāstraparibhāṣitānāṃ śabdānāṃ lokavyavahāravisaṃvādasyākiṃcitkaratvācca taduktamacaturaśrameveti vadanti ||
kramādādhāra ekasminnādheyānekatā'pi saḥ |
ekasminnādhāre kramādanekādheyavarṇanaṃ ca paryāya ityarthaḥ | vakṣyamāṇasamuccayālaṅkāravāraṇāya kramāditi | tatra hi guṇakriyayoryaugapadyenānvayaḥ |vinimayābhāvātparivṛttivailakṣaṇyam | tasyāḥ vinimayo lakṣaṇatvenānupadameva vakṣyate ||
yathā
bhavajalanidhicintanato bhayacakitaḥ pūrvamatha sunirviṇṇaḥ | tvadguṇagaṇagaṇanādatha dhīro vindāmi mādhavānandam || 1433 ||
he mādhaveti saṃbuddhiḥ | atra prathamaṃ bhayacakitatvaṃ atha nirvedaḥ tato dhairyaṃ anantaramānanda ityekasminnevādhāre kramādanekādheyavarṇanam ||
yathāvā
dehātmabhramavān prāgatha svatantrātmadhīstato bhagavan | tvaditaraśeṣabhramavānadhunā manye tvadekaśeṣaṃ mām || 1434 ||
svatantrātmadhīḥ svatantrātmabhramavān | tvadanyeṣāṃ pitrādīnāṃ devatāntarāṇāṃ vā śeṣa iti bhramavān | atra dehātmabhramasvatantrātmabhramānyaśeṣatvabhramabhagavadekaśeṣatvānāmanekeṣāmādheyānāṃ kramādekasminnādhāre nibandhanam ||
yathāvā
ādau tamasā malinaṃ gurukṛpayā viśadamatha ca haritatvam| adhigamya tatra raktaṃ hṛdayaṃ mama citrametadabjākṣa || 1435 ||
atrādau mālinyaṃ ajñānaṃ nailyaṃ ca | atha vaiśadyaṃ jñānaprakāśaḥ dhāvalyaṃ ca | anantaraṃ haritatvaprāptiḥ bhagavadyāthātmyaniṣkarṣaḥ, haridvarṇatvaṃ ca | atha raktatā bhagavati prītiḥ āruṇyaṃ cetyekasminnādhāre krameṇānekādheyavarṇanam | citraṃ āścaryamidaṃ śabalavarṇaṃ cetyapyupaskāryam | pūrvodāharaṇaṃ śuddham | idaṃ tu śleṣasaṃkīrṇaṃ tamasetyādinā upāttaprayojakaṃ ceti viśeṣaḥ |
yathāvā 
hanta parasvastainyapraveśadhīro bhavannaro bhagavan | dṛṣṭo'vitasvayā cetparasvasainyapraveśadhīrassyāt ||
tvayā dṛṣṭaḥ kaṭākṣitaḥ | avitaḥ rakṣitaśca | pakṣe vita iti chedaḥ | vigatatakāra ityarthaḥ | dṛṣṭaśceti yojanā | parasvastai
nyapraveśadhīraśabdastakāravigame parasvasainyapraveśadhīra iti niṣpadyetetyarthaḥ | pūrvaṃ stena eva san bhavatkaṭākṣavīkṣitaśśūro bhavediti bhāvaḥ | 'śauryaṃ nāma asahāyasyāpi bhīme parabale svabala iva nirbhayapraveśasāmarthyam' iti gadyabhāṣye ācāryāḥ | tadetaduktaṃ parasvastainyetyādinā | parasvasya anyavittasya stainye caurye praveśaḥ tasmin dhīraḥ vidvān, pareṣāṃ sveṣāṃ ca sainye praveśaḥ tasmin dhīraḥ dhairyavānityarthaḥ | atraikasminnevādhāre coratvadhīratvayoḥ paryāyeṇa varṇanam ||
yathāvā
tvadviśliṣṭā sītā sutarāntaralatvametya dāśarathe | atha śubhaguṇavārāśisthirāntarāsīttvayā viśiṣṭaiva || 1437 ||
he dāśarathe ! śubhaguṇavārāśiḥ sītā tvadviśliṣṭā satī sutarāṃ taralatvaṃ cañcalacittatāṃ etya atha anantaraṃ tvayā viśiṣṭaiva tvatsaṃśleṣaṃ prāptaiva sthirāntarā sthiraṃ antaraṃ mano yasyāssā tathoktā āsīt | pakṣe viśliṣṭeti śabdavyaktiḥ sutarā atiśobhanā antaḥ madhye a tvaṃ avidyamānalakāratāṃ etya śubhāḥ guṇānāṃ vārāḥ samūhāḥ yasyāssā tathoktā | idaṃ sītāviśeṣaṇameva | antaḥ madhye śisthirā śiḥ śivarṇaḥ sthiro yasyāṃ sā tathāvidhā satī viśiṣṭaivāsīt | viśliṣṭāśabdaḥ lakārotsāraṇe śivarṇasthairye ca viśiṣṭeti bhavatītyartho'pi camatkārī | atraikasminneva sītārūpādhāre taralatvasthairyarūpādheyayoḥ krameṇa nibandhaḥ | udāharaṇadvaye'pi viśeṣasspaṣṭa eva ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre paryāyasarastripañcāśaḥ
<4>
atha parivṛttyalaṃkārasaraḥ
nimayaḥ parivṛttissyādarthānāṃ syātsamāsamaiḥ ||
nimayaḥ vinimayaḥ | 'paridānaṃ parīvarto naimeyanimayāvapi' ityamaraḥ | samaiḥ padārtheḥ samānāmarthānāṃ asamairasamānāṃ vā vinimayaḥ upādānaṃ parivṛttirnāmālaṅkāraḥ | tatra samaparivṛttirdvidhā uttamairuttamānāṃ nyūnairnyūnānāṃ ca | asamo'dhiko nyūnaśca bhavatītyadhikena nyūnesyopādāne nyūnenādhikasyopādāne cāsamaparivṛttirdvidhetyevaṃ caturdhā parivṛttiḥ ||
tatrādyā yathā
stutimamṛtamayīṃ bhujagakṣitidharacūḍāmaṇe tvayi samarpya | pratilabhate tvattaḥ punarativelaṃ mādṛgamṛtameva kṛtī || 1438 ||
atrāmṛtarūpastuteramṛtasya cottamatvena samayoḥ parivṛttiḥ | iyamamṛtaśabdagataśleṣamūlābhedādhyavasāyanirvyūḍhā ||
yathāvā
ādhāya kathamapi tvayi medhāvī hṛdayataralamātmānam | prativindate'nagha tvā śrutisudatīhṛdayataralamātmānam || 1439 ||
medhāvī vidvān hṛdayataralaṃ vakṣassthanāyakamaṇiṃ hṛdayena hṛdaye vā taralaṃ capalamānasāmiti tattvam | yadvā
ātmānamasya jagato nirlepamajarāmaram |
bibharti kaustubhamaṇisvarūpaṃ bhagavān hariḥ ||
ityastrabhūṣaṇādhyāyoktakrameṇa kaustubhasvarūpamiti | ātmānaṃ pratyagātmānaṃ svam | kathamapi aticañcalahṛdayatayā yatnagauravādityarthaḥ | tvayi ādhāya nyasya | śrutisudatyāḥ hṛdaye vakṣasi taralaṃ hāramadhyamaṇivaddedīpyamānaṃ śrutipratipādyamiti yāvat | ātmānaṃ paramātmānaṃ tvāṃ prativindate | 'taralo hāramadhyasthamāṇau nā capale triṣu' iti ratnamālā | atra hṛdayataralabhūtayorātmanoruttamasamayoḥ parivṛttiśśleṣānuprāṇitā ||
yathāvā
datvā lavamātramapāṃ puṣpamapi śrīpate bhavo bhavate | lavamātramapāṃ puṣpaṃ samupādatte'nyadasya ko lābhaḥ || 1440 ||
bhavaḥ śambhuḥ apāṃ lavamātraṃ kaṇamātraṃ ‘lavaleśakaṇāṇavaḥ' ityamaraḥ | puṣpamapi ekaṃ kusumamapi bhavate datvā ‘patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyam' ityuktarītyā samarpya anyat apāṃ lavamātraṃ puṣpaṃ ca labhate | tattīrthaṃ tannirmālyakusumaṃ ca vindate ityarthaḥ | asya samavastudānena samavastūpādadānasya ko lābhaḥ na ko'pi lābha iti bhāvaḥ| lavamātraṃ kalāmātraṃ apāṃ puṣpaṃ ‘candramā vā apāṃ puṣpam' iti śruteḥ | samupādatte iti vastusthitiḥ |
bhagavadarcanamahimnā'sya candraśekharatvamiti bhāvaḥ atrāpyuttamatvena samayoḥ parivṛttiḥ śleṣamūlābhedādhyavasāyalabdhaprasarā | yadvā nyūnatayā samayoḥ parivṛttiriti vakṣyamāṇaprabhedasyaivedamudāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati ||
dvitīyaparivṛttiryathā
datvā naśvarabhautikatanumīdṛśabhūtadehamādadatām | vārāṇasījanānāṃ nārāyaṇa ko nu vā bhavellābhaḥ ||
naśvarīṃ bhautikīṃ pañcabhūtātmikāṃ tanum | īdṛśaṃ naśvaraṃ pañcabhūtātmakaṃ ca śarīram | vastutastu 'piśāco guhyakassiddho bhūto'mī devayonayaḥ' iyuktabhūtayoniviśeṣaśarīramityarthaḥ | pāñcabhautikatvaṃ tasyāpyastyeveti prāgupadarśitārthasvīkāre'pi yadyapi vivakṣitasiddhirbhavatyeva | tathā'pi anantaroktārthaḥ śleṣeṇa camatkārātiśayaṃ puṣṇātītyavadheyam | prasiddhyati hi vāraṇāsīvāsināṃ śarīranyāsānantaraṃ bhūtadehaprāptyavaśyaṃbhāvaḥ | atra nyūnatvena samayorbhautikaśarīrayoḥ parivṛttiḥ | śleṣādyanuprāṇitatvaṃ ca pūrvavadeva | vārāṇasānivāsīnāṃ tvadabhivyaktikṣetrapuravāsināmivāvasāne divyāprākṛtadehalābho na bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ |
yathāvā
bhasmāntavarṣmadānāttādṛśatanumeva yadgiriśabhaktāḥ | ādadate tadbhagavaṃstuṣavitaraṇataḥ phalīkaraṇalābhaḥ || 1442 ||
bhasma antaḥ ante vā yasya tat bhasmāntaṃ varṣma gātraṃ tasya dānāt | 'athedaṃ bhasmāntagṃ śarīram' iti śruteḥ ‘kalebaraṃ viṭkṛmibhasmasaṃjñitam' iti purāṇavacanācca dāhe bhasmasaṃjñitatvaṃ kalebarasyeti bodhyam | bhasmasvarūpamiti vā | tādṛśatanumeva bhasmāntaśarīrameveti nyūnatayā samayoḥ parivṛttiḥ bhasmanā antaṃ ramyaṃ bhasma ante antike yasyeti veti tu tatvam |
‘antaḥ prānte'ntike nāśe svarūpe'pi manohare' iti viśvaḥ | sarvadhā bhasmoddhūlitamiti bhāvaḥ | bhagavadbhaktāstu divyāprākṛtaṃ 'śataṃ mālyahastāḥ' ityādyuktaprakāreṇa divyamālyānulepanābharaṇavasanādibrahmālaṃkārālaṃkṛtaṃ rūpameva dadhate | na tu giriśabhaktā iva bhasmoddhūlitāṃ paiśācikīṃ tanumiti bhāvaḥ |
yathāvā
acaladaraṃ pracuragadaṃ visṛjya vapurahimahībhṛti tato'nyat | tādṛgvapurādadato janasya maugdhyena ko na vismayate || 1443 ||
acalaḥ daraḥ bhayaṃ śaṅkhaśca yasya tat | pracuraḥ pracurā ca gadaḥ rogaḥ gadā kaumodakī ca yasya tattathoktam | atrāpi pūrvavadeva nyūnasamaparivṛttiḥ| śeṣādrau vapurvisṛjato janasya bhagavatsārūpyaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ |
yathāvā
ādadati sādhukṛtyāṃ brahmavidastatra dattasauhārdāḥ | vindanti pāpakṛtyāṃ nyastadoṣāstu tasya nanu manujāḥ || 1444 ||
sādhuścāsau kṛtyā kriyā 'kṛtyā kriyādevatayoḥ' ityamaraḥ | sukṛtamityarthaḥ| evaṃ pāpakṛtyāṃ duṣkṛtamityarthaḥ | tatretyuttarārddhe'pyanveti | tatra brahmāvadi | nyastadveṣāḥ | tasya brahmavidaḥ| atra pūrvārdhe uttamasamaparivṛttiḥ | uttarārdhe nyūnasamaparivṛttiḥ | iyaṃ śuddhā ‘tasya putrā dāyamupayanti suhṛdassādhukṛtyāṃ' dviṣantaḥ
pāpakṛtyām' iti śrutyarthānuvādinīti ca pūrvodāharaṇebhyo vailakṣaṇyam ||
dīkṣitāstv imaṃ samaparivṛttiṃ nādriyante, kiṃtvasamaparivṛttimeva | tathāca tadīyaṃ parivṛttilakṣaṇaṃ ‘parivṛttirvinimayo nyūnābhyādhakayormithaḥ' iti ||
tṛtīyaparivṛttiryathā
aṣṭāṅgayogabhūmnā nātha ṣaḍaṅgena vā prapadanena | tvatto'pavargadātuḥ kecitkrīṇanti hanta kaivalyam ||
atrāṣṭāṅgayogaṣaḍaṅgaprapadanābhyāmadhikābhyāṃ nyūnasya kaivalyasya parivṛttiḥ | kaivalyaṃ kevalātmānubhavaḥ ||
yathāvā
dārāgārāpatyaśrīrājyādiṃ samarpya vibhavaṃ te | veṃkaṭagiriparibṛḍha tava kaiṃkaryaṃ ke'pi hanta vindanti || 1446 ||
atrāpyadhikena rājyādivibhavena nyūnasya kaiṃkaryasya parivṛttiḥ | idaṃ ‘sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkham' iti lokadṛṣṭyā kaiṃkaryasya nyūnatāmabhisaṃdhāyodāhṛtam | vastutatsu dārāgārādivibhavāpekṣayā bhagavatkaiṃkaryasya jyāyastayā nyūnenādhikaparivṛttervakṣyamāṇāyā evedamudāharaṇaṃ saṃmanyeran brahmavedinaḥ ||
aṇutaramātmānaṃ tvayi phaṇigirināthārpayanta iha santaḥ | mahato'pi mahāntaṃ tvāṃ vihitopāyā vitanvate krītam || 1447 ||
‘eṣo'ṇurātmā | mahato mahīyān' iti śrutiratrānusaṃdheyā | vihitaḥ anuṣṭhitaḥ upāyaḥ bhaktiprapattyanyatarasādhanaṃ yaiste tathoktāḥ | krītaṃ vitanvate | ‘bhāvakrīto janārdanaḥ | bhaktikrīto janārdanaḥ' ityukteriti bhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
dhānāpūrṇaprasṛterdānāttvatto ramāpate'krīṇāt | dīnāvana mānātiganānāvidhasaṃpadaṃ kucelamuniḥ ||
dhānāpūrṇaprasṛterdānādityatra viśeṣyabhūtāyāḥ prasṛterdānāyogātprasṛtipūrṇadhānādānāditi viśeṣaṇabhūtadhānāsu dānasyānvayaḥ | svargī dhvasta ityādivat | vadanti hi ‘saviśeṣaṇe hi vidhiniṣedhau viśeṣye bādhe viśeṣaṇamupasaṃkrāmataḥ' iti nyāyavidaḥ | he dīnāvaneti saṃbuddhyantaṃ padaṃ ramāpate ityasya viśeṣaṇam ||
yathāvā
padasalilamayīṃ kāmapi datvā srotasvinīṃ payonidhaye | tejasvinīmupādāstasmātkanyāmaṇiṃ maṇiṃ ca vibho || 1449 ||
vibho bhagavan! payonidhaye srotasvinīṃ datvetyanena anapekṣitadānamuktam | srotasvinī nadī | atrodāharaṇatraye'pi nyūnenādhikaparivṛttisspaṣṭā | imāni aṣṭāṅgayogetyādīni pañcodāharaṇāni śuddhāni ||
yathavā
vasudhātanayāstanayossamarpayan pañcaśākhamatikutukāt | tābhyāṃ sahasraśākhaṃ pramodamanuvindate'ravindākṣaḥ || 1450 ||
vasudhāyāḥ dharaṇīdevyāḥ tanayā padmāvatī tasyāḥ | pañca śākhāḥ yasya taṃ kañcidvastuviśeṣaṃ, pāṇimiti tu tattvam | sahasraśākhaṃ anantaśakhānvitaṃ ativipulamati tattvam ||
yathāvā
yatkīṃcicchadamacyuta bhuvanalavaṃ cāpi tava pade datvā | adhipatvaṃ bhuvanānāmādadire bahuparicchadaṃ kṛtinaḥ || 1451 ||
yatkiṃcicchadaṃ tulasyādidalaṃ bhuvanalavaṃ jalakaṇaṃ ca tava pade datvā ‘patraṃ puṣpam' ityuktarītyā bahavaḥ paritaḥ chadāḥ dalāni yasmin, pakṣe bahavaḥ paricchadāḥ sacivādiparivārāḥ yasya tattathoktaṃ bhuvanānāṃ udakānāṃ lokānāṃ ca adhipatvaṃ varuṇatvamindratvaṃ dhātṛtvaṃ vā ādadire svīcakrire | kṛtina iti viśeṣyaṃ yatkiṃcinnyūnavastudānenātimātrābhyadhikavastūpādānacāturyābhiprāyagarbham | ata eveyaṃ śleṣaparikarāṅkurottejiteti pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ pūrvaṃ tu śleṣamātropaskṛtam ||
yathāvā
śrutiyuvatīsīmantapratiyatnamanarghamasitaruciratnam | ślakṣṇapravāladānācchaśivadanā kā'pi gopikā'krīṇāt || 1452 ||
ślakṣṇaḥ alpaḥ ‘ślakṣṇaṃ dabhraṃ kṛśaṃ tanu' ityamaraḥ | yaḥ pravālaḥ vidrumaḥ tasya, pakṣe ślakṣṇaḥ snigdhaḥ ‘masṛṇe ślakṣṇasomālacikkaṇāḥ' iti vaijayantī | yaḥ pravālaḥ tattvenādhyavasito'dharaḥ tasya pradānāt | spaṣṭamanyat ||
yathāvā
ākāśarājatanayā rākāśaśidāsyadāsyarucinicayā | jagadīśvaramakraiṣītpraguṇatamaṃ kanakakalaśakośābhyām || 1453 ||
praguṇatamaṃ atiśayena ṛjusvabhāvaṃ "ṛjāvajihmapraguṇau' ityamaraḥ | anena parakṛtātisandhānānabhijñatvaṃ vyañjitam | kanakakalaśakośābhyāmityanena hemakumbhanyastārthanivahābhyāmiti ca gamyate | atra śrutiyuvatītyudāharaṇe asitaratnatvena bhagavataḥ ślakṣṇapravālatvenādharasya cādhyavasānādrūpakātiśayoktiḥ | taduttambhitā nyūnādhikaparivṛttiḥ | ākāśarājetyudāharaṇe kanakakalaśakośābhyāmiti rūpakātiśayoktyā uktarītyā praguṇatamamiti sābhiprāyaviśeṣaṇarūpaparikarālaṃkāreṇa copaskṛtā seti viśeṣaḥ ||
eṣūdāharaṇeṣu dānādānavyavahāraḥ kavikalpita eva | na tu vāstavaḥ | yatra vāstavastatra nāṃlakāraḥ ||
yathāvā
adhivipaṇisaraṇi phaṇigiriramaṇamanaḥprīṇanāya sumaṇigaṇān | krīṇanti dyumaṇighṛṇīnkaticana kṛtinassutulyamūlyena || 1454 || |
dyumaṇeḥ ghṛṇayo raśmaya iva ghṛṇayo yeṣāṃ tān | atitejasvina ityarthaḥ | nātra dānādāne kavipratibhākalpite ||
śambaradharamaṇikadambaiḥ krīṇantyakhilāmba jāmbavaphalāni | kurūvindakanikurumbairbimbāni ca
tāvakīnagṛhabālāḥ || 1455 ||
bimbāni vimbīphalāni tāvakīnānāṃ tvadbhaktānāṃ gṛheṣu sthitāḥ bālāḥ mugdhalalanāḥ māṇavakā vā | yadyapyatra samṛddhimadvastuvarṇanātmakodāttālaṃkāradattahaste dānādāne | athāpi na te kavipratibhonmīlite | krayasya vāstavatvāditi nedṛśānyudāharaṇatāmarhanti ||
yadīdṛśadānādānayorapi kavipratibhonmīlitatvamabhyupagamyate tadā idamapyudāharaṇameva, na tu adhivipaṇīti padyavatpratyudāharaṇam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre parivṛttisaraścatuḥpañcāśaḥ
<5>
bimbāni vimbīphalāni tāvakīnānāṃ tvadbhaktānāṃ gṛheṣu sthitāḥ bālāḥ mugdhalalanāḥ māṇavakā vā | yadyapyatra samṛddhimadvastuvarṇanātmakodāttālaṃkāradattahaste dānādāne | athāpi na te kavipratibhonmīlite | krayasya vāstavatvāditi nedṛśānyudāharaṇatāmarhanti ||
yadīdṛśadānādānayorapi kavipratibhonmīlitatvamabhyupagamyate tadā idamapyudāharaṇameva, na tu adhivipaṇīti padyavatpratyudāharaṇam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre parivṛttisaraścatuḥpañcāśaḥ
atha parisaṃkhyālaṃkārasaraḥ
ekasyānekasaṃprāptau yadekatra niyantraṇam | parisaṃkhyeti tatprāhuralaṃkārādhvayāyinaḥ ||
ekānekaprastāvādihāsyoktiḥ | ekaṃ vastu yadā'nekatra yugapatsaṃbhāvyate tadā tasyāsaṃbhāvyatayā dvitīyaparihāreṇaikatra niyamanaṃ parisaṃkhyā | kasyacitparivarjanena kvacitsaṃkhyānaṃ varṇanīyatvena gaṇanaṃ parisaṃkhyā | sā caiṣā prathamaṃ dvidhā śuddhā praśnapūrvikā ceti | dvividhā caiṣā ārthī śābdīti punardvividhetyasyāścatvāraḥ prabhedāḥ | yadyapi pākṣikaprāptārthanivartako niyamaḥ| yugapatprāptaviṣayakānyavvāvṛttiphalakastu parisaṃkhyeti pūrvatantramaryādā | tathā'pyasmin darśane pākṣikapraptinityaprāptirūpo'vāntaraviśeṣo na vivakṣyate, prayojanābhāvāt | ataeva 'pañca pañca nakhā bhakṣyāḥ' ityatra parisaṃkhyāyā niyamapadenoktirmahābhāṣye'pi dṛśyate | krameṇodāharaṇāni
yadyasti tava didṛkṣā sākṣāllakṣmīvilāsinaṃ paśyeḥ | iṣṭā cetparicaryā paryāptaṃ taṃ tvamaṅga paricaryāḥ || 1456 ||
atra yadicecchabdābhyāṃ niveditasya rāgaprāptasya darśanādeḥ karmatāyāḥ bhagavati śrīnivāse viṣayāntare ca prāptatvena liṅarthaghaṭitavākyārthavaiyarthyaprasaṃgādviṣayāntaraṃ na paśyerityādirūpā viṣayāntare tattatkriyākarmatvavyāvṛttistātparyaviṣayatayā kalpyamānatvādārthī śuddhā ceyam ||
ko rogo bhavayogaḥ ko vaidyassa khalu yaḥ pumānādyaḥ | kiṃ bhaiṣajyaṃ prājyaṃ yattannāmānuvādasāmrājyam || 1457 ||
atra bhavayogādikameva rogādikaṃ nānyadityarthastātparyamaryādayā pratīyata ityārthīyaṃ praśnapūrvikā ca ||
kṣetraṃ phaṇivaragotraṃ sthāne jāne'nyadūṣarakṣetram | devassa vāsudevassutarāmitarassavigrahamṛdeva ||
atra ūṣarakṣetrasavigrahamṛtpadābhyāṃ divyakṣetratvadivyadevatvavyāvṛttiḥ pratīyata itīyaṃ śābdī śuddhā ||
yathāvā
svāntaṃ mama madhumathane'tyantaṃ vilagatu kadācidapi na dhane | ratirastu tasya caraṇe'pratimā na tu me vṛthā tapaścaraṇe || 1959 ||
tapaḥ kṛchracāndrāyaṇādiḥ | atrāpi śābdī vyāvṛttiḥ pūrvavadeva śuddhā ||
sāphalyaṃ kvanu vacasāṃ sārasanayanasya varṇane yaśasām | na tu vitathanṛpatipāśapratatistotre'timātracitre'pi || 1460 ||
atra śābdī vyāvṛttiḥ, praśnapūrvikā ca ||
idaṃ prācāṃ matam | anye tu vyāvṛtterārthatva eva parisaṃkhyālaṃkāraḥ | anyathā tu śuddhā parisaṃkhyaiva | yathā hetutvasyārthatva eva hetvalaṃkāraḥ | anyadhā hetumātram | ato bhedadvayamevāsyā ityāhuḥ ||
apare tu  vyāvṛtterārthatve'pi nālaṃkāratvaṃ, ‘pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyāḥ, same yajeta, rātsasya, ityādāvapi tadāpatteḥ | kiṃtu kavipratibhānirmitā yā tādṛśavyāvṛtterevālaṃkāratvam ||
sā yathā
āsargamaviratānāṃ duritānāṃ śāntaye durantānām | bhagavanmama śeṣatayā bhavato nirveśa eva nirveṣaḥ || 1461 ||
he bhagavan! āsargaṃ āsṛṣṭeḥ aviratānāṃ durantānāṃ duritānāṃ śāntaye mama śeṣatayā śeṣatvena bhavato nirveśa eva tvadekaśeṣasya mama śeṣiṇo bhavato'nusaṃdhānameva nirveṣaḥ prāyaścittam | na tu kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādiriti bhāvaḥ |
svojjīvanecchā yadi te svasattāyāṃ spṛhā yadi |
atmadāsyaṃ haressvāmyasvabhāvaṃ ca sadā smara ||
yathā'gniruddhataśikhaḥ kakṣaṃ dahati sānilaḥ |
tathā cittasthito viṣṇuryogināmakhilāśubham ||
ayaṃ hi kṛtanirveṣo janmakoṭyaṃhasāmapi |
yadvyājahāra vivaśo nāma svastyayanaṃ hareḥ ||
ityādipramāṇānyatrānusaṃdheyāni | śeṣatayetyatra śe ṣatayeti padadvayam | śe śakāre ṣatayā ṣakāratayā śakārasthāne ṣakāranyāsāditi yāvat | nirveśaḥ nirveśaśabdaḥ nirveṣaśabdo bhavatīti vicchittiviśeṣopyanusaṃdheyaḥ ||
yathāvā
śrīrajanividhau bhagavati nīrajanayane vidoṣamavati bhuvam | rajanimukha eva nitarāmajani janānāṃ pradoṣatākhyātiḥ || 1462 ||
śrīḥ lakṣmīreva rajaniḥ rātriḥ tasyāḥ vidhau candramasītyarthaḥ
yathāvā
avati hare tvayi bhuvanaṃ vipattiśabdo'padātisainye'bhūt | aśrāvi kṛchraśabdaḥ prāyaścittaprasaṅga eva janaiḥ || 1463 ||
ityādau | vipattiśabdaḥ vipadvārtā | vigatāḥ pattayo yasmiṃstat vipatti vipattitiśabdaḥ | kṛchraṃ kaṣṭaṃ parākādikṛchraṃ cetyarthaḥ | 'yasmin śāsati vasumatīpākaśāsane mahānaseṣu santāpaḥ' ityādiparakīyaprabandheṣu ca |
atra hi prathamāntārthaḥ kavipratibhayā ekīkṛta iti taddvārā tatpratiyogivyāvṛttirnirmitā | evañca ‘yadyasti tava didṛkṣā'
ityatra nānyo draṣṭavya ityarthasya pratīteḥ parisaṅkhyā'stu, na tu parisaṅkhyālaṃkāraḥ, vyāvṛttervāstavatvena kavipratibhāyā anapekṣaṇāt | vyāvṛttirvyavacchedaḥ | ‘ko rogo bhavayogaḥ' ityatra tu praśnapūrvakaṃ dṛḍhāroparūpakam | anyathā 'na viṣaṃ viṣamityāhurbahmasvaṃ viṣamucyate' ityatrāpi parisaṅkhyāpatteḥ | ‘sāphalyaṃ kva nu vacasāṃ' kṣetraṃ phaṇivaragotraṃ' ityatrāpi parisaṃkhyaiva śuddhā, prāguktāddhetoranārthatvāt' tasmāt ‘mahānaseṣu santāpaḥ' ityādikamevāsyā udāharaṇamiti vadanti |
vastutastu vyāvṛtteśśābdatve'pi kavipratibhonmīlitaśleṣādinā camatkārātiśayaścettatrāpi na nivāryate parisaṃkhyālaṃkṛtiḥ ||
yathā
kvacidapyanūnamamba svāmitvaṃ te tvameva tatsevyā | kiṃ tena śauriṇā nassa yadūnāṃ svāmitāṃ kvacidudūhe || 1464 ||
saḥ śauriḥ yat yasmāt kvacit samaye ūnāṃ nyūnāṃ svāmitāṃ īśvaratāṃ udūhe, kvacidavasare yadūnāmiti samastaṃ padaṃ, yaduvaṃśyānāṃ svāmitāmiti tu vastusthitiḥ | yaduśabdastadapatye lākṣaṇikaḥ| atra kiṃ tena śauriṇeti vyāvṛtteśśābdatve'pi tasyāḥ śleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyamūlakātiśayoktidattahetūpanyāsasamarthitatvena camatkārātiśayasya sahṛdayairdurapahnavatvādbhavatyeva parisaṃkhyālaṃkṛtiḥ | evañca īdṛśasthale vyāvṛtteśśābdatve'pi parisaṃkhyālaṃkṛteravaśyāśrayaṇīyatayā anyatrāpi tasyā ātmalābho nāpalapanīyaḥ | tathāca udāhariṣyamāṇeṣu padyeṣvapi parisaṃkhyālaṃkārassuprasara eva | ataeva kuvalayānandakārāḥ ‘snehakṣayaḥ pradīpeṣu na svānteṣu natabhruvām' ityudājahruḥ ||
yathāvā
vittaṃ vṛttamiti dve bhavadīyairvṛttameva rakṣyamiha | na tu vittamaiti yāti ca tadetayorvivṛta eva nanu bhedaḥ || 1465 ||
nanviti bhagavata āmantraṇe | tat vittaṃ eaiti āyāti | yāti gacchati ca | etayoḥ vittavṛttayoḥ bhedaḥ vivṛta eva |
vṛttameva sadā rakṣedvittamāyāti yāti ca |
akṣīṇo vittataḥ kṣīṇo vṛttatastu hato hataḥ ||
iti smaradbhirmunibhirvailakṣaṇyaṃ vyākhyātamevetyarthaḥ | tayoḥ vittavṛttaśabdayoḥ vivṛta eva vi vṛ iti varṇābhyāmeva, sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | bhedaḥ tāvadeva vailakṣaṇyaṃ anyavarṇataulyādityartho'pi camatkārī | atra na tu vittamiti vyāvṛtteśśābdatve'pi upapāditacamatkārātiśayapoṣitatayā bhavatyeva parisaṃkhyālaṅkāraḥ atrodāharaṇe smṛtivacanagṛhītavṛttavittaśabdayoḥ vaiparītyena grahaṇaṃ vivṛta eva bheda ityupapāditacamatkārānurodheneti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
śaraṇāgatamenaṃ māṃ yadi gamayasi nāyakaṃ svamatha madhumathanaḥ | nāyakamayamapi paramabhigamayedavyāstvameva tatkiṃ tena || 1466 ||
he śrīḥ! śaraṇāgataṃ enaṃ māṃ svaṃ nijaṃ nāyakaṃ vallabhaṃ bhagavantaṃ gamayasi prāpayasi yadi atha ayaṃ madhumathano'pi paraṃ svasmādanyaṃ nāyakaṃ nāthaṃ abhigamayet svayaṃ na rakṣet kiṃtu viṣṭibhāramivānyaṃ prāpayediti bhāvaḥ | saṃbhāvanāyāṃ liṅ | tat tasmāt
tvameva avyāḥ rakṣeḥ | tena evaṃ nāyakāntaraṃ prāpayatā bhagavatā kiṃ na kimapi sādhyamastīti bhāvaḥ | ‘gamyamānā'pi kriyā kārakavibhaktau prayojikā' iti tṛtīyā | paramārthatastu  śaraṇāgatamenaṃ māṃ tvadvallabhaṃ prāpayasi cet atha avilambenaiveti bhāvaḥ | madhumathanaḥ ayaṃ nāyakamiti yojanā | ayaṃ apagatayakāraṃ nāyakaṃ nākamityarthaḥ | paraṃ nākaṃ paramaṃ vyoma divyaṃ sthānamityarthaḥ | abhigamayet tvayi vāllabhyabhūmnā puruṣakārabhūtayā tvayā svasaṃnidhiṃ prāpitamavilambenaiva niśśreyasena yojayatīti bhāvaḥ | tat tasmāt tvameva īdṛśaviśeṣalābhanidānabhūteti bhāvaḥ | avyāḥ | kiṃ tenetyetat lakṣmyāṃ gauravātibhūmnā | na tu bhagavatyudāsīnatayeti rahasyam | atrāpi pūrvavadeva vyāvṛtteśśābdatvādikamanusaṃdhayam ||
yathāvā
niryātaya bhartre māmiti tvayā'bhyarthitopi dharaṇisute | laṅkāmeva daśāsyo niryātayati sma na tu janani bhavatīm || 1467 ||
bhartre bhagavate dāśarathaye | niryātaya samarpaya 'niryātanaṃ vairaśuddhau dāne nyāsārpaṇe'pica' ityamaraḥ | anena ‘māṃ cāsmaiprayato bhūtvā niryātayitumarhasi' iti bhagavatyāssītāyā vākyaṃ pratyabhijñāpitam | laṅkāmeva niryātayati sma dattavān na tu bhavatīm | vastutastu nissāritāni niśśeṣitāni vā yātūni rakṣāṃsi yasyāssā niryātuḥ tathoktāṃ karoti sma niryātuśabdāt ṇāviṣṭhavadbhāve ṭilopaḥ | anviṣyarākṣasāṃ kṛtavānityarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
sthālībilīyataṇḍularāśerevāsti niṣphalīkaraṇam | na tu sakalaphalada bhagavaṃstvadīyapadavinatakāṅkṣitārthānām || 1468 ||
sthālībilamarhanti sthālībilīyāḥ | pākayogyā ityarthaḥ | sthālībilāt' ityarhārthe chaḥ | ye taḍuṇlāḥ teṣāṃ rāśāveva niṣphalīkaraṇaṃ phalīkaraṇasyābhāvo niṣpīkaraṇam | nirmakṣikamitivadarthābhāve'vyayībhāvaḥ | phalīkaraṇaṃ nāma vrīhyādidhānyasya sūkṣmatuṣaḥ| anyatra vyarthīkaraṇaṃ netyarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
akhilāghahṛtinidāne mama hṛdi nārāyaṇe samindhāne | duritamaritāṃ bhajedyadi bhajatu dutaṃ syāttadeva na mano me || 1469 ||
duritaṃ mayi aritāṃ vidveṣaṃ bhajedyadi bhajatu māṃ kadarthayitumudyuñjīta cedudyuṅktāmiti bhāvaḥ | tadeva duritameva dutaṃ syāt paritaptaṃ syāt ‘ṭu du upatāpe' iti sauvādikāddhātoḥ ktaḥ | me manastu na dutaṃ syāt | bhagavato harestatraiva nivāsāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe duritaṃ duritamiti padaṃ aritāṃ avidyamānarivarṇatāṃ bhajeccet dutamityeva niṣpadyetetyarthaśca camatkurute |
yathāvā
saṃsārākūpāre'pāre ghore'tra viluṭhataśśaure |
mama nāsi pāradastvaṃ capalatayā pārado'hamevāsam || 1470 ||
tvaṃ
saṃsārasāgaraṃ ghoramanantakleśabhājanam |
tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ prāpya nistaranti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
ityuktamahāprabhāvo'pīti bhāvaḥ | apāre ghore saṃsārākūpāre luṭhataḥ mama pāradaḥ paratīraprāpakaḥ nāsi | audāsyādvā aśaktatvādvā na jāne ityupālambho dyotyate | kiṃtu capalatayā tāvakaparatīraprāpakatāpratīkṣaṇākṣamatayetyarthaḥ | ahameva pāradaḥ tava mama vā paratīraprāpakaḥ āsam | paramārthatastu  capalatayā cañcalasvabhāvatayā pāradaḥ rasaḥ ‘rasassūtaśca pāradaḥ' ityamaraḥ | āsaṃ tattulyo'bhūvam | iyantaṃ samayaṃ pratīkṣyamāṇo'pi bhavajalanidhipāramaprāpayitā tvaṃ kadā māṃ tārayervetyavatapte nakulasthitamāśrayāmīti bhāvaḥ |
yathāvā
durmatitā krodhanatā durmukhitā vā'pi bharmaśikharīndo | vatsaraviśeṣa eva tvatsaṃśritasamudaye tu na kadā'pi || 1471 ||
prādigaṇa eva bhagavan durgativārtā ca durupasargakathā | tvaccaraṇanalinaśaraṇe viprādigaṇe tunonmiṣati jātu || 1472 ||
durgativārtā dāridryanarakādivārtā ‘durgatinarake naissvye' iti medinī | durupasargāṇāṃ duṣṭotpātānāṃ ‘ajanyaṃ klībamutpāta upasargassamaṃ trayam' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe durityasyāvyayasya gativārtā gatisaṃjñeti yāvat | durityasya upasargasya kathā kathanaṃ
ca prādigaṇa eva ‘prādayaḥ | gatiśca' iti sūtrābhyāṃ prādigaṇapaṭhitānāṃ śabdānāmupasargagatisaṃjñayorubhayorvidhānāddurityasya tatra paṭhitatvāditi bhāvaḥ | viprādigaṇe prādigaṇaviruddhe ityapyartha upaskāryaḥ||
eṣūdāharaṇeṣu vyavacchedasya śābdatve'pi śleṣamūlakābhedādhyavasāyasaṃbhinnatayā cāmatkārātiśayādbhavatyeva parisaṃkhyālaṃkāraḥ | evaṃca parisaṃkhyālaṃkṛterārthītvaśabdītvābhyāṃ dvaidhī draṣṭavyā | asyāḥ praśnapūrvakatvatadabhāvābhyāṃ dvaividhyaṃ prāktanairudāhṛtamapi vicchittiviśeṣavirahātkuvalayānandakārairupekṣitam | asyāśca śleṣānuprāṇitatve atimātracamatkāritā yathā anupadamevodāhṛteṣu padyeṣu pāradaniṣphalīkaraṇādiśabdaśleṣeṇātyantacārutā pratīyata ityavadheyam |
asyāṃ ca parisaṃkhyālaṃkṛtau vyavacchedaniyamanayorna pauvoparyaniyamaḥ | yathā śuddhāpahnutau nihnavāropayoḥ | tathāca vyavacchedapūrvakatāyāmapi niyamanapūrvakatāyāmiva parisaṃkhyālaṃkṛtirakṣataiva |
na prakṛtivaiparītyādanarthado nandanandana bhavestvam | tvatto'nyastu tathā syādbrahmā rudro'thavā na saṃdehaḥ || 1473 ||
he nandanandana tvaṃ prakṛtivaiparītyāt ‘yatsatvaṃ sa harirdevaḥ, mahānprabhurvai puruṣassatvasyaiṣa pravartakaḥ' ityādyuktasattvasvabhāvaviparītatvāt anarthadaḥ rajastamaḥprācuryaprayuktaṃ puruṣārthaṃ dātā na bhaveḥ |
jāyamānaṃ hi puruṣaṃ yaṃ paśyenmadhusūdanaḥ |
sāttvikassa hi vijñeyassa vai mokṣārthacintakaḥ ||
ityuktarītyā satvonmeṣaṇapūrvakaṃ niśśreyasarūpaparamapuruṣārthapradātā tvaṃ kathaṃ heyapuruṣārthaṃ dadyā iti bhāvaḥ | tvatto'nyastu brahmā caturmukhaḥ rudro vā tathā syāt prakṛtivaiparītyādanarthadassyāt nātra sadehaḥ |
paśyatyenaṃ jāyamānaṃ brahmā rudro'thavā punaḥ|
rajasā tamasā cāsya mānasaṃ samabhiplutam ||
ityuktarītyā rajastamaḥpravartakatayā heyapuruṣārthapradāveva syātāṃ, na tu tvamiva paramapuruṣārthapradāviti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe nandanandaneeti śabdaḥ prakṛteḥ uktavidhaprakṛteḥ vaiparītyāt prātilomyena pāṭhāt anarthadaḥ yathoktārthāprakāśakaḥ na bhaveḥ | nandanandaneti prakṛtervailomyena pāṭhe'pi svarthāprahāṇāditi bhāvaḥ | tvadanyaḥ brahmā bahmetiśabdaḥ rudraḥ rudra iti śabdo vā tathā syāt prakṛtivailomye pūrvānupūrvivyatyayātkasyāpyarthasya prakāśako na bhavediti bhāvaḥ | atra vyavacchedapūrvakatā, pūrvodāharaṇeṣu niyamapūrvakateti vibhāvanīyam ||
eṣūdāharaṇeṣu svabhāvato vā śleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyena vā ubhayatra prasiddhasaṃbandhasya vastuna ekatra vidhānenānyatra niṣedhaḥ pratipāditaḥ| kecitu ekatra prasiddhasaṃbandhasyāpi vastuno'nyasmin dharmavidhānena tatprasiddhiśālini dharmyantare tatpratiṣedhaḥ pratipādyate || yathā
yastubhyamabhyasūyati tena vigītena durvinītena | bhūratibhāravatīyaṃ śailaissālaiśca nātha na viśālaiḥ ||
śailādīnāmevātimātrabhūbhāratā prasiddhā, na tu bhagavadabhyasūyakasya janasya | atraca padye tādṛśajane atimātrabhāratvavidhānaṃ
śailādau tanniṣedhaścetyuktaparisaṃkhyābhedo vyakta eva | ayaṃ ca tādṛśajanasya tādṛśabhāratvāsaṃbandhe'pi tatsaṃbandhakathanarūpātiśayoktiśabaliteti dhyeyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre parisaṃkhyālaṃkārasaraḥ pañcapañcāśaḥ
atha vikalpālaṃkārasaraḥ
vikalpaḥ pākṣikaprāptirvarṇyate cedviruddhayoḥ ||
ekasmin dharmiṇi svasvaprāpakapramāṇaprāptayoḥ ata eva tulyabalayorviruddhayorviruddhatvādeva yugapaprāpterasaṃbhavātpākṣikyeva prāptiḥ paryavasyati 'ayaṃ ca vakṣyamāṇasamuccayapratidvaṃdvī vyatireka ivopamāyāḥ' ityalaṃkārasarsvasvakṛdanuyāyī rasagaṅgādharakṛt ‘samuccaye hi dvayorapi yugapadavasthānaṃ, iha tvanyadheti tatpratidvaṃdvitvam' iti vimarśinīkāraḥ | dīkṣitāstu  vakṣyamāṇakārakadīpakamalaṃkārāntaraṃ varṇayantastasya prathamasamuccayapratidvaṃdvitāṃ tadanantarabhāvinassamādherdvitīyasamuccayapratidvaṃdvitāṃ cāhuḥ |
maraṇaṃ taraṇaṃ vā stānmahāmbudherityaśāyi matabhedāt | jaladhitaṭe bata bhavatā raghuvara bhavajaladhitārakeṇāpi || 1475 ||
'adya me maraṇaṃ vā'stu taraṇaṃ sāgarasya vā'
iti śrīrāmāyāṇaśloko'trānusaṃhitaḥ | matabhedāt vikalpāditi yāvat | lyablopa pañcamī | vikalpamabhisaṃdhāyetyarthaḥ | mara…
<6>
ṇataraṇaśabdayormakāratakāramātravaiṣamyādanyataramupādāyetyapyarthaścamatkṛtiviśeṣāyopaskāryaḥ ||
pratiyannakhilāṃhastatimocanavacanaṃ vrajāmi śaraṇaṃ tvām | prāptastasyāvasaraḥ pratāraṇaṃ vā'stu tāraṇaṃ vā tat || 1476 ||
akhilāṃhastatimocanavacanaṃ ‘sarvapāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi' iti tava vacaḥ pratiyan viśvasan tvāṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajāmīti caramaślokārthānuvādaḥ | tasya pratijñātasarvapāpavimocanasya avasaraḥ prāptaḥ | sarvapāpaśevadhirūpamādṛśaviṣayalābhāditi bhāvaḥ | tat tava vacanaṃ pratāraṇaṃ atisaṃdhāyakaṃ vā'stu tāraṇaṃ saṃsṛtinistārakaṃ vā astu ||
bhaktyā'thavā prapattyā kṛtāparādho'pi nātha vinde tvām | māmevaṃ kutayatanaṃ gṛhāṇa nigṛhāṇa vā kimayaśo me || 1477 ||
evaṃ kṛtayatanaṃ anuṣṭhitopāyaṃ māṃ gṛhāṇa rakṣatayā svīkuru | nigṛhaṇa vā nigṛhīṣva vā me mama kimayaśaḥ kā vā'kīrtiḥ, pratyuta prapannanigrahe tavaivākīrtiriti bhāvaḥ | atra maraṇataraṇayoḥ, pratāraṇatāraṇayoḥ grahaṇanigrahayośca aupamyapratītyabhāvātkevalaṃ vikalpaḥ | yathā  jīvanaṃ maraṇaṃ vā'stu naiva dharmaṃ tyajāmyaham ityādau | atra jīvanamaraṇayornaupamyapratītiriti rasagaṅgādharakārādayaḥ ||
udāharaṇaṃ
niśśaṅkaṃ śaraṇaṃgatameṣa dvedhā'pyariṃ na bādheta | rāghavamāhavamathavā rajanicareśa drutaṃ prapadyethāḥ ||
idaṃ rāvaṇaṃ pratyaṅgadavacanaṃ | eṣaḥ rāghavaḥ dvedhā'pi niśśaṅkaṃ śaraṇaṃgataṃ ariṃ na bādheta niśśaṅkaṃ ayaṃ māṃ bādhetati saṃśayarahitaṃ yathāsyāttathā śaraṇaṃ gataṃ ariṃ na bādheta, śaraṇāgataparitrāṇadharmadhurīṇamūrdhanyatvāt | pakṣe niśśaṃ nirgataśśakāro yasmāttaṃ tathoktaṃ śaraṇaṃ raṇamityarthaḥ | gataṃ yuddhārthamabhimukhāgataṃ kaṃ ariṃ na bādheta sarvamapyabhiyātinaṃ bādhata evetyarthaḥ | tasmāt rāghavaṃ drutaṃ prapadyethāḥ śaraṇaṃ vraja | prapūrvasya padyatestādṛśārthakatvāt | athavā āhavaṃ yuddhaṃ prapadyethāḥ prāpnuhi | atra prapadane saṃdhivigrahakartavyatābodhakapramāṇābhyāṃ karmatayā rāghavāhavayoryaugapadyāsaṃbhavātparyāyeṇa prāptiḥ | karmaṇoḥ kriyāphalena prapadanenaiva samānadharmeṇaupamyam ||
nanvatra yathā prapūrvakapadidhātvarthaphalabhūtaprapadanarūpadharmekyātkarmaṇoraupamyaṃ gamyate tathā ‘jīvanaṃ maraṇaṃ vā'stu' ityādau sattārūpadharmaikyājjīvanamaraṇayoḥ kartrorapi aupamyaṃ gantuṃ yujyata iti cet yujyate na tu gamyate | atha tatkuto hetoriti cet kavitātparyavirahāditi gṛhāṇa | na hyatra maraṇaṃ jīvanaṃ ca samānamiti kavestātparyaṃ, kiṃtu viṣaṃ bhuṅkṣva mā cāsya gehe bhuṅthā itivat dharmāddhetormaraṇamapi jyāyaḥ na tu dharmatyāga iti niṣiddhagatadveṣādhikye | tadarthaṃ ca maraṇasyopāttatvādavikṣitādhikaraṇatayā aupamyasyāniṣpattirevetyāhuḥ | evaṃ maraṇaṃ vā taraṇaṃ veti śloke gamyamānapratijñāhānarūpaniṣiddhamādāya tadgatadoṣādhikye pratiyanniti śloke tu śaraṇavrajanarūpavihitagatādarādhikye kave
stātparyaṃ, na tu maraṇe vā pratāraṇe vā | evaṃ bhaktyeti śloke'pi bodhyam ||
yathāvā
śaṅkātaṅkavidūrairlaṅkādvāraṃ nyarodhi harivīraiḥ | sītāṃ vitara sphītāṃ yudhamathavā kosalādhināthāya || 1479 ||
atra ‘sītāṃ vā'smai prayaccha tvaṃ suyuddhaṃ vā pradīyatam'
iti śrīrāmāyaṇaślokānusāriṇi rāvaṇaṃ prati sāraṇavākye rāvaṇaviṣaye saṃdhivigrahapramāṇaprāptayorata eva tulyabalayoryugapadasaṃbhavatossītāyuddhavitaraṇayoḥ pākṣikānyataraprāptyanupamardenānyatarapratikṣepalakṣaṇasya vikalpasya varṇanaṃ karmaṇossītāyuddhayoḥ kriyāphalena vitaraṇenaiva samānadharmeṇaupamyam ||
yathāvā
nāgeśagirimaṇe bhayayogena tvāṃ prapanna eṣa janaḥ | bhogaṃ vā yogaṃ vā dehi kiyān syāttavaiṣa bhayabhedaḥ || 1480 ||
nāgeśagirimaṇe śeṣādriśiromaṇe eṣa janaḥ bhayayogena bhītisaṃbandhena tvāṃ prapannaḥ | bhogaṃ sāṃsārikasukhaṃ vā yogaṃ bhoganivṛttikāraṇabhūtaṃ bhaktiyogaṃ vā tvadanubhavaṃ vā dehi niraṅkuśasvatantratayā yatheṣṭaṃ vitara | tava eṣa bhayabhedaḥ prapannabhītinirākaraṇaṃ kiyān syāt na duṣkara ityarthaḥ | bhogayogaśabdayorbhakārayakārābhyāṃ bhedaḥ kiyān akiṃcitkaraḥ varṇāntaraikarūpyāditi cābhi
prāyaḥ | atra bhogayogayoranyonyaviruddhayoryaugapadyāyogādvikalpaḥ | atrāpi karmaṇorbhogayogayoḥ kriyāphalena dānenaiva samānadharmeṇaupamyam ||
yathāvā
natarakṣaṇakṛtadīkṣastvamiti tvāṃ nātha śaraṇamabhināthe | ārtirvā mama śāmyatu kīrtirvā tava natāvanaikāntā || 1481 ||
tvaṃ rakṣasi cenmamārtiśśāmyati |
tāvadārtistathā vāñchā tāvanmohastathā'sukham |
yāvanna yāti śaraṇaṃ tvāmaśeṣāghanāśanam ||
ityukteḥ| nacet tava śaraṇāgataparitrāṇaparāyaṇatvanibandhanakīrtiśśāmyati |
vinaṣṭaḥ paśyatastasya rakṣiṇaśśaraṇāgataḥ |
ādāya sukṛtaṃ tasya sarvaṃ gacchedarakṣitaḥ |
asvargyaṃ cāyaśasyaṃ ca balavīryāvināśanam ||
ityukteḥ| atrodāhṛtatulyabalapramāṇābhyāmārtiśāntikīrtiśāntyoryugapatprāptayoritaretaraviruddhatayā yugapadbhāvāsaṃbhavādvikalpaḥ | ārtikīrtyośśamanarūpasamānadharmeṇaupamyaṃ jñeyam ||
kvacilluptaṃ samānadharmamādāyāpyaupamyasya gamyatve'yamalaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
kiṃcidvā bhūyo vā pañcāyudhamañca muñca jāḍyamaye | dhātrīmetāṃ bhokṣyasi vaidhātrīṃ prāpsyasi śriyaṃ vā'pi || 1482 ||
atra dhātrībhogavaidhātrasaṃpatprāptyoḥ ślāghyatvenaupamyaṃ vivakṣitam | dhātvarthayorayaṃ vikalpaḥ na tu dhātrīvaidhātrasaṃpadoḥ, tayoḥ kārakatvenaiva kriyānvayaṃ vinā vikalpāsaṃgateḥ | na ca dhātvarthayorekasya kartṛrūpasādhāraṇadharmasyoktatvātkathaṃ luptateti vācyaṃ, kartṛrūpasādhāraṇadharmamādāya aupamyasyātra sundarasyāniṣpatteḥ | anyathā 'hato vā prāpsyase svargaṃ jitvā vā bhokṣyase mahīm' ityatreva ‘hato vā narakaṃ gantā jitvā vā bhokṣyase mahīm' ityatrāpyaupamyapratyayāpatterityāhuḥ | evaṃcāsminnalaṃkāre vikalpyamānayossundaramaupamyamalaṃkāratābījaṃ, tadādāyaiva camatkārollāsāt | anyathā tu vikalpamātramiti rahasyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihare vikalpasaraṣṣaṭpañcāśaḥ
<7>
atra dhātrībhogavaidhātrasaṃpatprāptyoḥ ślāghyatvenaupamyaṃ vivakṣitam | dhātvarthayorayaṃ vikalpaḥ na tu dhātrīvaidhātrasaṃpadoḥ, tayoḥ kārakatvenaiva kriyānvayaṃ vinā vikalpāsaṃgateḥ | na ca dhātvarthayorekasya kartṛrūpasādhāraṇadharmasyoktatvātkathaṃ luptateti vācyaṃ, kartṛrūpasādhāraṇadharmamādāya aupamyasyātra sundarasyāniṣpatteḥ | anyathā 'hato vā prāpsyase svargaṃ jitvā vā bhokṣyase mahīm' ityatreva ‘hato vā narakaṃ gantā jitvā vā bhokṣyase mahīm' ityatrāpyaupamyapratyayāpatterityāhuḥ | evaṃcāsminnalaṃkāre vikalpyamānayossundaramaupamyamalaṃkāratābījaṃ, tadādāyaiva camatkārollāsāt | anyathā tu vikalpamātramiti rahasyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihare vikalpasaraṣṣaṭpañcāśaḥ
atha samuccayālaṃkārasaraḥ
yaugapadyātpadārthānāmanvayassyātsamuccayaḥ ||
yaugapadyāditi yathāsaṃkhyavyāvṛttyarthaṃ, na tvekakṣaṇapratipattyartham | tena śatapatrapatraśatabhedanyāyena kvacitkiṃcitkālabhede'pi na samuccayabhraṃśaḥ | sa tāvaddvividhaḥ dharmibhedadharmaikyābhyām | dharmaikye'pi dvaividhyaṃ kāraṇatvātiriktasaṃbandhenaikadharmyanvaye kāraṇatayā ekadharmyanvaye ceti | evaṃ trividhe'smin prāthamikayorbhedayoḥ guṇānāṃ kriyāṇāṃ guṇakriyāṇāṃ ca | tṛtīyaprabhede śobhanānāma śobhanānāṃ śobhanāśobhanānāṃ tathāvidhaissamanvayaḥ | ayaṃ tṛtīyaprabheda eva | ‘ahaṃprathamikābhājāmekakāryānvayo'pi saḥ' iti dvitīyasamuccayatvena lakṣito dīkṣitaiḥ | asyaiva tatkara iti nāmāhuḥ prāñcaḥ | taduktaṃ ‘tatsiddhihetorekasmin yatrānyastatkaraṃ bhavet' iti | vikalpapratikṣepeṇāsyā sthitiḥ | na cāsmintṛtīyaprabhede vakṣyamāṇasamādhyalaṃkāratvamāśaṅkanīyaṃ, yatra hyekena kārye nirvartyamāne'pyanyenākasmādāpatatā kāraṇena saukaryādirūpo'tiśayassaṃpādyate sa tasya viṣayaḥ | asmiṃstu tṛtīyasamuccaye yatraikakāryaṃ nirvartayituṃ yugapadaneke khale kapotā ivāhaṃprāthamikayā samāpatanti kāryasya ca na ko'pyatiśayaḥ so'sya viṣayaḥ ||
tatra bhinnadharmyanvayo yathā
śaradindukarairviśadaṃ gaganaṃ yamunāvanaṃ tu haridhāmnā | ānīlaṃ smararāgādātāmraṃ cāpi gopikānayanam || 1483 ||
snihyati nayanayugaṃ tvayi muhyati saṃmodaparavaśaṃ cetaḥ | sravati pramodabāṣpaṃ dravati ca tava sevanādbhayaṃ bhūman || 1484 ||
atrādye guṇānāṃ dvitīya kriyāṇāṃ ca yaugapadyena bhinnadharmyanvayaḥ ||
ekadharmyanvayo yathā
maṇivarabhāsā'ruṇitaṃ hārarucā smeramasitamātmarucā | śrīvihṛtikṛte racitaṃ sthalamiva citraṃ hareruro bhāti || 1485 ||
atra āruṇyādiguṇānāṃ yaugapadyenaikasmin dharmiṇyurorūpe ādheyatāsaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | evamuttaratrāpi yathāyathaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ ||
kalyāṇaguṇagaṇaughasmaraṇāttava nātha bhāgyavānmanujaḥ | vindati harṣaṃ tarṣaṃ nindati nandati ca tāvakotkarṣam || 1486 ||
tarṣaṃ viṣayatṛṣṇāṃ nindati | tāvakotkarṣaṃ nandati abhinandatītyarthaḥ ||
nanu vāsudeva yaṃ kṣaṇamacintyavaibhava na cintyate sa bhavān | bhrāmyati sujanaśśrāmyati tāmyati nitarāṃ tadā na śāmyati ca || 1487 ||
atra ‘yanmuhūrtaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vā'pi vāsudevo na cintyate' ityādipramāṇārtho'nukrāntaḥ ||
ruṣyati sadbhyo duṣyati hṛṣyati dussaṅgatastvadanabhijñaḥ | tuṣyati viṣayaiśśuṣyati puṣyati duritaṃ viśiṣya muravairin || 1488 ||
atrodāharaṇatraye'pi kriyāṇāṃ yugapadekadharmiṇyanvayaḥ | tatrāpyādyayośślāghye dharmiṇi tṛtīye tvaślāghye ||
guṇakriyāṇāṃ yaugapadyenānvayo yathā
calitabhru lalitamadhuraṃ lulitālakacāru vivalitāpāṅgam | jagadīśvarīmukhaṃ phaṇinaganāthamano dhinoti satatamapi || 1489 ||
atra lalitamadhuramiti guṇayoḥ calitabhru ityādikriyāṇāṃ ca sāmastyena yugapadekadharmiṇyanvayaḥ ||
evaṃ dharmaikyarūpe dvitīyaprabhede kāraṇatvātiriktasaṃbandhenaikadharmyanvayarūpaḥ prāthamiko bhedaḥ pradarśitaḥ | kāraṇatayā ekadharmyanvayarūpe prabhede śobhanānāṃ śobhanaissamuccayo yathā
jñānabalaiśvaryādyā gāmbhīryāsthairyadhairyamukhyāśca | kalyāṇaguṇāḥ prathayantyanitarasādhāraṇāstavaunnatyam || 1490 ||
atra jñānādiṣvekenāpyutkarṣasaṃbhave balādayo'pyutkarṣaprakāśanārthaṃ spardhaye vā patantaḥ śobhanāḥ ||
yathāvā
kamalābhaṃ nayanayugaṃ kamalādayitānilāśanādrimaṇe | rūpaṃ ca nīlajaladasarūpaṃ prathayati tavākhileśatvam || 1491 ||
atra ‘tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevamakṣiṇī | hīśca te lakṣmīśca patnyau | nīlatoyadamadhyasthā' ityādiśrutiprasiddhapuṇḍa
rīkākṣatvādiṣu triṣu ekenāpi jagadīśvaratvaprakhyāpanasaṃbhave saṃgharṣādiva trayo'pi tadarthamāpatantaśśobhanāssamuccitāḥ ||
yathāvā
premodayaḥ kutūhalamāmodavyatikaraśca romāñcaḥ | tvāmālokayato mama sīmāṃ cittasya lolayati bhūman || 1492 ||
atrāpi premodayādessamuccayaḥ pūrvavaddraṣṭavyaḥ ||
idaṃ sarvaṃ śobhanaiśśobhanānāṃ samuccaye udāharaṇam | aśobhanairaśobhanānāṃ samuccayo yathā
durvārāḥ khalu viṣayā durdāntānīndriyāṇi mama nātha | duṣkarma ca niravadhikaṃ marma nikṛntanti cintyamānāni || 1493 ||
atra durvāratvādinā'śobhanānāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ durdāntatvādibhiraśobhanairindriyādibhissamuccayaḥ | atra viṣayadurvāratvenaikenaiva cintyamānena marmanikṛntanasiddhau indriyadurdāntatvādikamapi tadarthamāpatitaṃ samuccīyate ||
śobhanāśobhanasamuccayo yathā
tava nirvarṇanahīnaṃ nayanaṃ varṇanavihīnamapi rasanam | ākarṇanahīnaṃ ca śravaṇaṃ pravaṇaṃ manaḥ kathaṃ tvayi me || 1494 ||
atra nayanādīni svābhāvyācchobhanānīti na nindanīyāni viśeṣaṇamāhātmyācca | śobhanāni nindāgocaratāṃ prapadyanta iti
śobhanāśobhanasamuccayaḥ | śobhanāśobhanānāmityatra karmadhāraya āśritaḥ | na dvaṃdvaḥ | sahacarabhinnatvadoṣāpatteḥ ||
evamaśobhanaśobhanānāmapyekakāryajananārthamāpatatāṃ samuccayassaṃbhavati ||
yathāvā
kāvyaṃ tvaccaritamayaṃ bhavyabhavannāmakomalaṃ gītam | tiryaktvamapi girau tava paryāptaṃ śreyase śriyaḥkānta || 1415 ||
atra 'kāvyālāpāṃśca varjayet | na ca rakto virāvayet' ityādipramāṇaiḥ kāvyagītādīni svabhāvato'śobhanānyapi viśeṣaṇabalācchobhanāni samuccitāni | tava girau tiryaktvaṃ tvadgiryadhiyaraṇakaṃ tiryaktvamityarthaḥ||
yathāvā
tvannidhyānajamaśru tvaddhyānajamahigirīśa yajjāḍyam | tvannāmakathanajanyaṃ sagadgadatvaṃ ca sadgatessaraṇiḥ || 1496 ||
atrāśruprabhṛtayo nisargato'śobhanā api bhedakamahimnā śobhanāḥ ||
kecittu dravyajātyorapi samuccayaṃ menire | sa ca vicchittiviśeṣavirahādasmābhirupekṣyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre samuccayasarassaptapañcāśaḥ
<8>
atha kārakadīpakasaraḥ
kramikāṇāṃ kriyāṇāṃ cedekakārakagāminām |
gumbhanaṃ kriyate tattu bhavetkārakadīpakam ||
ekakārakagatānāṃ kramikāṇāṃ kriyāṇāṃ gumbhanaṃ kārakadīpakamityalaṅkāraḥ | yadyapi nibadhyamānānāṃ kriyāṇāṃ prastutatvena sarvoddeśenaiva dharmiṇaḥ kārakasya pravṛttiḥ | ataḥ kiṃciduddeśena pravṛttasyānyatra prāsaṅgikatvalakṣaṇadīpakasādṛśyavirahānna dīpakavyapadeśo yuktaḥ | tathā'pi ekasyānekānvayamātreṇa dīpakasādṛśyamāśritya tathā vyapadeśaḥ | daṇḍinā tu kārakadīpakaṃ dīpaka evāntarbhāvya tulyayogitā'pi tadbhedatvenaiva pratipāditetyavocāma dīpakālaṃkāranirūpaṇāvasara eva | vastutastu mālādīpakavatpṛthagevāyamalaṃkāratāmarhati na dīpake'ntarbhāvaṃ, tajjīvātorupamāgarbhatayā ihābhāvāt | samuccayapratidvaṃdvitayā vicchittiviśeṣasadbhāvāccetyayaṃ pūrvairakṛtaviveko'pi pradarśito dīkṣitaivikalpālaṃkāra ivālaṃkārasarvasvakṛtā ||
udāharaṇaṃ
adhirohati vṛṣaśikhariṇamabhimajjati nātha tatra sarasi kṛtī | tvāmañcatyatha muñcati mudā'śru
tatraiva vāñchati nivastum || 1497 ||
yathāvā
paśyati harirupasarpati tiṣṭhatyālapati hasati parihasati | sallapati spṛśati samāliṅgati gopīmupaiti tadabhedam || 1498 ||
ālāpaḥ ābhāṣaṇaṃ, sallāpaḥ mithobhāṣaṇam | atrādye kṛtirūpakartṛkārakasya ekasyaiva adhirohaṇādikriyāsvanvayaḥ kramiko nibaddhaḥ | dvitīye harirūpakartṛkārakasya ekasyaiva darśanādikriyāsvanvayaḥ kramika iti prathamasamuccayapratidvaṃdvīdam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre kārakadīpakasaro'ṣṭāpañcāśaḥ
<9>
atha samādhyalaṃkārasaraḥ
kāraṇāntarasāṃnidhyavaśātkāryasya kasyacit | saukaryaṃ varṇyate yatra samādhistatra gīyate ||
ekakāraṇajanyasya kāryasyākasmikakāraṇāntarasamavadhānāhitaṃ yatsaukaryaṃ tasya samyagādhānātsamādhiralaṃkāraḥ | tacca kāryasyānāyāsena siddhyā sāṅgasiddhā ca | samuccayaviśeṣāpekṣayā'sya vailakṣaṇyaṃ tvabhihitameva prāk ||
yathāvā
yādṛcchikasukṛtavaśānmādṛkṣaṃ tvaṃ samuddidhīrṣurasi | tādṛśasādhusamāgama īdṛśasamaye haṭhādupanato me || 1499 ||
yathāvā
paśyati harirupasarpati tiṣṭhatyālapati hasati parihasati | sallapati spṛśati samāliṅgati gopīmupaiti tadabhedam || 1498 ||
ālāpaḥ ābhāṣaṇaṃ, sallāpaḥ mithobhāṣaṇam | atrādye kṛtirūpakartṛkārakasya ekasyaiva adhirohaṇādikriyāsvanvayaḥ kramiko nibaddhaḥ | dvitīye harirūpakartṛkārakasya ekasyaiva darśanādikriyāsvanvayaḥ kramika iti prathamasamuccayapratidvaṃdvīdam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre kārakadīpakasaro'ṣṭāpañcāśaḥ
<10>
atha samādhyalaṃkārasaraḥ
kāraṇāntarasāṃnidhyavaśātkāryasya kasyacit | saukaryaṃ varṇyate yatra samādhistatra gīyate ||
ekakāraṇajanyasya kāryasyākasmikakāraṇāntarasamavadhānāhitaṃ yatsaukaryaṃ tasya samyagādhānātsamādhiralaṃkāraḥ | tacca kāryasyānāyāsena siddhyā sāṅgasiddhā ca | samuccayaviśeṣāpekṣayā'sya vailakṣaṇyaṃ tvabhihitameva prāk ||
yathāvā
yādṛcchikasukṛtavaśānmādṛkṣaṃ tvaṃ samuddidhīrṣurasi | tādṛśasādhusamāgama īdṛśasamaye haṭhādupanato me || 1499 ||
atra yādṛcchikasukṛtādeva siddhyantyā bhagavatkartṛkoddidhīrṣāyāssādhusamāgamādanāyāsena siddhiḥ ||
yathāvā
kāṅkṣantyā vrajasudṛśaśśairerdātuṃ svayaṃgrahāśleṣam | ānanasaurabhalobhādāpatadalibṛndamakṛta sāhāyyam || 1500 ||
atrotkaṇṭhitāyā gāopalalanāyā ākasmikamilindavṛndāpatanarūpakāraṇāntarasamavadhānena śaurikarmakasvayaṃgrahāśleṣasyānāyāsena siddhiḥ ||
śaurinavacāṭubhaṅgīmaṅgīcakre yadā vrajalatāṅgī | ślathamapi tadopagūhanamakāryakasmātpikāravaissāndram || 1501 ||
atra pikaninādairupagūhanasya sāṅgatvasiddhiḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre samādhisara ekonaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
atha pratyanīkālaṃkārasaraḥ
balini pratipakṣe vā tatprakṣe vā tiraskṛtiḥ|
tatpratidvaṃdvisāhyaṃ vā pratyanīkamitīryate ||
atra yādṛcchikasukṛtādeva siddhyantyā bhagavatkartṛkoddidhīrṣāyāssādhusamāgamādanāyāsena siddhiḥ ||
yathāvā
kāṅkṣantyā vrajasudṛśaśśairerdātuṃ svayaṃgrahāśleṣam | ānanasaurabhalobhādāpatadalibṛndamakṛta sāhāyyam || 1500 ||
atrotkaṇṭhitāyā gāopalalanāyā ākasmikamilindavṛndāpatanarūpakāraṇāntarasamavadhānena śaurikarmakasvayaṃgrahāśleṣasyānāyāsena siddhiḥ ||
śaurinavacāṭubhaṅgīmaṅgīcakre yadā vrajalatāṅgī | ślathamapi tadopagūhanamakāryakasmātpikāravaissāndram || 1501 ||
atra pikaninādairupagūhanasya sāṅgatvasiddhiḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre samādhisara ekonaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
atha pratyanīkālaṃkārasaraḥ
balini pratipakṣe vā tatprakṣe vā tiraskṛtiḥ|
tatpratidvaṃdvisāhyaṃ vā pratyanīkamitīryate ||
yatra balini pratipakṣe durbalena sākṣāttiraskāraḥ tatra pratikartumanīśānena durbalena tadīyasya tiraskāraḥ svayamasamarthasya svavirodhipratidvandvini sāhāyakaṃ vā pratyanīkam | anīkasya sadṛśaṃ pratyanīkam | sādṛśyasya yathārthatvenaiva siddhe 'avyayaṃ vibhakti' ityādisūtre punassādṛśyagrahaṇādguṇībhūte'pi sāddaśye'vyayībhāvaḥ | loke pratipakṣasya tiraskārāyānīkaṃ prayujyate | tadasāmarthye tatsaṃbandhinaḥ kasyacittiraskāraḥ kriyate | tadabhāve ca prabalasvavirodhipratikartussāhyaṃ kriyate | sa cānīkasadṛśatayā prayujyamānatvātpratyanīkamityākhyāyate | atra ca pratipakṣagataṃ prābalyaṃ ātmagataṃ daurbalyaṃ ca gamyate | tatrādyā yathā
mahasā tava sāditabhāssahasā divasādhipasya kila bimbam | avilambaṃ kaustubhatāmavalambyākrāmati sma tava hṛdayam || 1502 ||
atra sākṣātpratipakṣatiraskāraḥ pratipāditaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvadrucikṛtaparibhavatastāpiñchaṃ bhraṃśitātmamukhavarṇam | piñchaṃ bhūtvā'marṣātpadaṃ nyadhāttava śiraspade śaura || 1503 ||
tāpiñchasya tamālasya vikāraḥ puṣpaṃ tāpiñchaṃ "dvihīnaṃ prasave sarvam' iti klībatā | tvadrucikṛtāt paribhavataḥ parābhavāt bhraṃśitaḥ bhraṃśaṃ prāpitaḥ ātmano mukhavarṇaḥ vadanatejaḥ yasya tathoktaṃ prāptamukhavaivarṇyamityarthaḥ | piñchaṃ barhaṃ bhūtvā tattvenāvirbhūyetyarthaḥ |
pakṣe tāpiñchamiti padaṃ bhraṃśo'syāstīti bhraṃśi | tā iti ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya saḥ tātmā mukhavarṇaḥ prathamākṣaraṃ yasya tattathābhūtaṃ sat piñchaṃ bhūtvā tāpiñchamitipadaṃ adyatākārotsāraṇena piñchamiti niṣpadyetyarthaḥ || sugamamanyat ||
yathāvā
urasāpāstaṃ śaureḥ kapāṭamatha kaṭatayā madonnaddham | ūrdhvaṃ kamañcayannanu taduraśchādayati kaṅkaṭātmā'ho || 1504 ||
kapāṭaṃ kavāṭaṃ ‘kavāṭamararaṃ tulye' ityatra kapāṭamiti pāṭhameva mukhyatayā'bhyupetya kaṃ śiraḥ pāṭayatīti vigrahaḥ pradarśitassudhāyām | pakṣe kapāṭamiti padaṃ ca | anayostādātmyam | śaureḥ urasā apāstaṃ nirastaṃ sat 'kavāṭavakṣāḥ pariṇaddhakaṃdharaḥ' iti kavāṭasyorassādṛśyoktestasya tadapāstatvoktiḥ | pakṣe pāstamiti bhedaḥ | pāḥ pākāraḥ astaḥ nirasto yasmiṃstattathoktaṃ sadityarthaḥ | kaṭatayā gajagaṇḍatayā "gaṇḍaḥ kaṭa' ityamaraḥ | madonnaddhaṃ madena garveṇa dānodakena ca unnaddhaṃ udvṛttaṃ sat | ataeva kaṃ śiraḥ ūrdhvaṃ añcayat unnamayadityarthaḥ | pakṣe ūrdhvaṃ agrabhāge iti yāvat |
kamañca yat iti cchedaḥ | kamañca kamityākārakaṃ māntaśabdaṃ ca yat prāpnuvatsat iṇaśśatari ‘iṇo yaṇ' iti yaṇ| kaṅkaṭa ityātmā svarūpaṃ yasya tat kaṅkaṭātma kavacarūpaṃ sat ‘uraśchadaḥ kaṅkaṭakaḥ' ityamaraḥ | taduraḥ bhagavadvakṣaḥ chādayati | pakṣe kaṅkaṭātmā kaṅkaṭa iti niṣpannasvarūpamityarthaḥ | kaṭaśabdasyādau kam ityasya nyāse anusvāre parasavarṇe ca kaṅkaṭatvena niṣpannamityarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
jetuṃ tmāmapaṭīyānmallastrībhūyametya mallī san | jayati śiro'dhivasaṃstava lalanāḥ kila nandatanaya tava jaitryaḥ || 1505 ||
he nandatanaya! idaṃ vivakṣitamallajayādyupaskārāya | mallaḥ cāṇūrādiḥ tvāṃ mallayuddhaviśāradaṃ tvaṃ jetuṃ apaṭīyān san | strībhūyaṃ strītvaṃ 'bhuvo bhāve' iti kyap | etya mallī san mallaśabdājjātilakṣaṇe ṅīṣi mallīti niṣpatteriti hṛdayam | pakṣe mallīkusumatāmāśrita ityarthaḥ | mallīśabdādvaikārike'ṇi ‘puṣpamūleṣu bahulam' iti tasya lup ‘lupi yuktavadvyaktivacane' iti yuktavadbhāvaḥ | tava śiraḥ adhivasan san jayati tvāṃ parābhavatītyarthaḥ | pakṣe śiromālyatayā utkarṣaṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ | tathāhi  lalanāḥ striyaḥ tava jaitryaḥ kila | tava gopavadhūvaśaṃvadatvāditi bhāvaḥ | yathoktamācāryaiḥ ‘tadapi paramaṃ tatvaṃ gopījanasya vaśaṃvadam' iti | kiletyupālambhe | ataḥ puṃstvena dussādhaṃ tvajjayaṃ strītvena sādhitavāniti bhāvaḥ | eṣu triṣūdāharaṇeṣvapi sākṣātpratipakṣatiraskāraḥ śabdārthatādātmyamūlakaśleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyānuprāṇita iti viśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ |
yathāvā
nijarucimapahṛtya payodharalīnollasati yā kṛtārtheva | tāṃ capalāṃ hāramiṣāṃ sapayodharamamba nahyase kaṇṭhe || 1506 ||
yā capalā vidyut nijaruciṃ tava śriyaṃ apahṛtya | capaletyanenānavasthitacittatayā asamīkṣyakāritā dyotitā | payodhare jalade līnā satī kṛtārtheva netaḥparaṃ mama bhayaṃ kiṃcidapi saṃbhavediti kṛtakṛtyeva | ullasati hṛṣyatītyarthaḥ | prakāśata iti tattvam | hāramiṣāṃ vajramaṇihāracchalāṃ tāṃ capalāṃ sapayodharaṃ svarucipratibhaṭataṭillatāśrayabhūtajaladharasahitaṃ yathāsyāttathā | pakṣe stanasahitaṃ yathāsyāttathā kaṇṭhe nahyase ‘kaṇṭhe badhdvā daśagrīvam' ityādivadgrīvāyāṃ badhnāsītyarthaḥ | pakṣe kandharāyāṃ pariṣkārahetoḥ pratimuñcasītyarthaḥ | atra sākṣātpratibhaṭabhūtāyāstaṭillatāyāssāśrayāyāstiraskāraḥ kaitavāpahnutiśleṣamūlābhedādhyavasāyābhyāmujjīvita iti pūrvodāharaṇebhyo vailakṣaṇyam ||
dvitīyaṃ yathā
tanurucijitastavācyuta mukhaṃ vijetuṃ vidhurbhavanneva | makāro bhūtvā madhukara ibhamagrasata tvadekaśaraṇamaho || 1507 ||
he acyuta! madhukaraḥ bhramaraḥ madhukaraśabdaśca | tayostādātmm | tava tanurucyā śarīraśriyā alpayaiva tviṣetyapi gamyate | jitassan tava mukhaṃ vijetuṃ vidhurbhavanneva candramā bhavanneva vigatadhuvarṇo bhavanneva ca vidhūbhavanasamasamayameveti bhāvaḥ | makaraḥ grāhaḥ bhūtvā pakṣe madhukaraśabdaḥ dhukāralope makaro bhūtvetyarthaḥ | tvadekaśaraṇaṃ ibhaṃ gajaṃ agrasata grastavān | atra bhagavattanurucivijitasya tatpratīkārānīśasya madhukarasya rūpāntaraparigraheṇa tadekaśaraṇe vāraṇe parākramaḥ prakaṭitaḥ | idamupadarśita
śabbārthatādātmyamūlakavicchittiviśeṣaśāli | evamagrimodāhāraṇayorapi draṣṭavyam ||
yathāvā
jetrīṃ tava gāṃ bhettuṃ prathamaṃ kopāśritaḥ kilopagato'mba | lopagato'nte kokila iha kolassan bhinatti gāṃ tadabhikhyām || 1508 ||
he amba kokilaḥ jetrīṃ svāvamantrīṃ tava gāṃ bhettuṃ vidārayituṃ prathamaṃ ādau kopaśritaḥ krodhānvitassan upagataḥ kila tvadvacanasamīpaṃ gato nanu | ante paryavasāne lopagataḥ svayameva chedanaṃ prāptassan kolassan varāho bhavan tadabhikhyāṃ gonāmadhāriṇīṃ gāṃ bhuvaṃ bhinatti bhūdāratvaprasiddhestasyeti bhāvaḥ || pakṣe kokilaḥ kokilaśabdaḥ prathamaṃ ko iti varṇena apāśritaḥ aśritaḥ kilopaṃ kivarṇabhraṃśaṃ gataḥ ‘dvitīyā śrita' iti samāsaḥ | ante avasāne lopagataḥ lavarṇena yuktaḥ | luptakivarṇamātraḥ kokilaśabdaḥ kola iti niṣpanna ityarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
ikṣurgavā tava jito drutamanitaradṛśyamāsamukha eva | avalambya tarakṣutvaṃ hinasti gāstadabhidhāḥ krudhā bhagavan || 1509 ||
he bhagavan ! ikṣuḥ tava gavā vācā jitaḥ tiraskṛtaḥ ata eva drutaṃ anitaradṛśyamānamukhaḥ parājitatvaprayuktāvamānenānyeṣāmadarśitamukha iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe avidyamānaḥ iḥ ityākārakavarṇaḥ
yasmin karmaṇi tadyathāsyāttathā ani | tarābhyāṃ takārarephābhyāṃ dṛśyamānaṃ mukhaṃ ādiḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | tarakṣutvaṃ mṛgādanatvaṃ 'tarakṣustu mṛgādanaḥ' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe ikṣuśabdaḥ ikārotsāraṇe tatraiva takārarephayornyasane tarakṣuriti niṣpanna ityarthaḥ | tadabhidhāḥ gośabdābhilapyavāgabhidhānāḥ gāḥ hinastītyarthaḥ |
yathāvā
phaṇiśikharividho bhavatā tirohitaṃ tatpratikriyānīśam | parvaṇi kadācana vidhuṃ tvannāmānaṃ tamastiraskurute || 1510 ||
tama āśritanāmajñānaṃ rāhuśca | idaṃ kartṛ, phaṇiśikhariṇi vidyamānā vidhurviṣṇuḥ | tasya saṃbuddhiḥ bhavatā tirohitaṃ nāśitaṃ sat ||
teṣāmevānukampārthamahamajñānajaṃ tamaḥ |
nāśayāmyātmabhāvastho jñānadīpena bhāsvatā ||
iti gānāt | spaṣṭamanyat | atra bhagavatā tirohitena tamasā tasmin pratividhātumakṣameṇa tannāmadharitayā tadīyatvena kalpitakhya vidhostiraskṛtiḥ ||
yathāvā
tava tanubhāsā'dharito navajaladastvannivāsamahiśailam | adharīkṛtyodgarjati na ghano nyakkāramātmanassahate || 1511 ||
atra bhagavattanurucā adharīkutasya navajaladasya tatpratikaraṇāpaṭutayā tadīyaśailādharīkaraṇam | udāharaṇadvaye'pi adharavidhuprabhṛtiśabdagataśleṣottambhitassākṣātpratipakṣatiraskārākṣamasya tadīyatiraskārastulya eva | antime tvarthāntaranyāsaśekharitatvaṃ viśeṣa iti dhyeyam |
kvacitpratipakṣasaṃbandhisaṃbandhitiraskāre'pīdaṃ dṛśyate | yathā
nūnamayaṃ svarbhānurlūnaśirāśśrīniketanena purā | tadbhāsamanuvibhāto vibhāvasormaṇḍalaṃ kvacidgrasate ||
anuvibhāta iti bhācchabdasya ṣaṣṭhyekavacanaṃ vibhāvasorityasya viśeṣaṇaṃ 'tameva bhāntamanubhāti sarvam' iti śruticchāyā'trānusaṃhitā | maṇḍalaṃ bimbaṃ, rāṣṭramityapi gamyate | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | atra bhagavallūnaśīrṣeṇa tatpratikārānīśvareṇa svarbhānunā tadīyabhāsamanuvibhāsamānasya bhānoryanmaṇḍalaṃ tasya tiraskṛtiriti pratipakṣasaṃbandhisaṃbandhitiraskṛtiḥ ||
yathāvā
avadhīrito mukharucā tava dhavalāṃśurmadamba nayanasakham | paribhūya nalinamalinastadāśritāṃstatra nigalayati rātrau || 1513 ||
tavetyetat mukharucā nayanasakhaṃ ityubhayatrāpi saṃbadhyate | atra
sākṣātpratipakṣabhūtajagajjananīvadanasaṃbandhi nayanaṃ, tatsakhatayā tatsaṃbandhi nalinaṃ, tatsaṃbandhino'linaḥ, tattiraskṛtirabhihiteti pūrvasmādvicchittiviśeṣaḥ ||
tṛtīyaṃ yathā
harirucibharaparibhūtaṃ kuvalayamakhilāmba tāvakākṣivapuḥ | tadrūpajitasyāgāccharatāṃ madanasya tatpraticikīrṣoḥ || 1514 ||
atra svavirodhihariparibhavānīśena kuvalayena lakṣmīnayanātmanā'vatīrya tadrūpavijitatayā tatpraticikīrṣormadanasya śaratayā sāhāyyaṃ vihitamiti tatpratidvandvi sāhyarūpaṃ tṛtīyaḥ pratyanīkaprakāraḥ ||
atra vicāryate  hetūtprekṣayaiva gatārthatvānnedamalaṃkārāntaraṃ bhavitumarhati | tatra ‘tvadrucikṛtaparibhavataḥ' ityudāharaṇe tāvaddhetvaṃśaśśābdaḥ | utprekṣāṃśamātramārthaṃ, anyeṣu tūdāharaṇeṣu dvayamapyārtham | asminnalaṃkāre hetutvaṃ niścīyamānaṃ hetūtprekṣāyāṃ tu saṃbhāvanamityasti viśeṣa iticet pratīyamānahetūtprekṣāyā anutprekṣātvāpatteḥ | vācakasya ivāderabhāvāddhetutvasya niścīyamānatāyāstatrāpi vaktuṃ śakyatvāt |
yasya kiṃcidapakartumakṣamaḥ kāyanigrahagṛhītavigrahaḥ|
kāntavaktrasadṛśākṛtiṃ kṛtī rāhurindumadhunā'pi bādhate |
ityalaṃkārasarvasvodāhṛte prācīnapadye'pi bhagavadvairānubandhādiva bhagavadvaktrasadṛśaminduṃ rāhurbādhata iti pratīterutprekṣaiva gamyamānā | 'mama rūpakīrtimaharadbhuvi yaḥ' iti kuvalayānandodāraṇe tu hetvaṃśa utprekṣāṃśeścatyubhayamapi śābdam | yadyapi pratipakṣagatabalavattvasvātmagatadurbalatvayoḥ pratīterhatūtprekṣāntarādasya vailakṣaṇyam | naitāvatā hetūtprekṣāyā bahirbhavitumidamīṣṭe, tadavinābhāvitvāt |
kiṃtu tadavāntaraviśeṣo bhavitum | na hi pṛthivyavāntarabhedādghaṭātpaṭo vilakṣaṇa iti pṛthivyā bahirbhavatītyapi vadanti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre pratyanīkasaraṣṣaṣṭitamaḥ
atha kāvyārthāpattisaraḥ
daṇḍāpūpikayā yatrārthāntarāpatanaṃ bhavet |
kāvyārthāpattireṣā syādalaṃkāravidāṃ matā ||
daṇḍāpūpaṃ vidyete yasyāṃ nītau sā daṇḍāpūpikā nītiḥ ahaṃ śaktos śakto'syāṃ kriyāyāmityahamahamiketivanmatvarthīyaṣṭhan | daṇḍāpūpāviva daṇḍāpūpikā ‘ive pratikṛtau' iti kanvā | atra hi mūṣakakartṛkeṇa daṇḍabhakṣaṇena tatsahabhāvyapūpabhakṣaṇamapyarthasiddham | evaṃvidho nyāyo daṇḍāpūpikāśabdenocyate | yathā daṇḍabhakṣaṇādapūpabhakṣaṇamarthāyātaṃ tadvatkasyacidarthasya niṣpattau sāmarthyātsamānanyāyalakṣaṇādarthāntaramāpatati sā'rthāpatiḥ | tāntrikābhimatārthāpattivyāvartanāya kāvyapadam | ayameva kaimutikanyāya ityapyucyate | ata eva ‘kaimutyenārthasaṃsiddhiḥ kāvyārthāpattiriṣyate' iti lakṣaṇaṃ praṇītaṃ kuvalayānande | nanvevamarthādathāntarapratīteranumānamevedamiti cenna | samānanyāyasya saṃbandharūpatvābhāt | daṇḍabhakṣaṇe hyapūpabhakṣaṇaṃ samānanyāyatvāducitamapi na niścitameva | daṇḍabhakṣaṇe'pi pṛthakpradeśāvasthānādinā kenāpi nimittena apūpānāmabhakṣaṇasyāpi bhāvāt | anumānaṃ punarniyatamevārthādarthāntarasyāpatanamityasyāstataḥ pṛthagbhāvaḥ ||
kiṃtu tadavāntaraviśeṣo bhavitum | na hi pṛthivyavāntarabhedādghaṭātpaṭo vilakṣaṇa iti pṛthivyā bahirbhavatītyapi vadanti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre pratyanīkasaraṣṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<11>
atha kāvyārthāpattisaraḥ
daṇḍāpūpikayā yatrārthāntarāpatanaṃ bhavet |
kāvyārthāpattireṣā syādalaṃkāravidāṃ matā ||
daṇḍāpūpaṃ vidyete yasyāṃ nītau sā daṇḍāpūpikā nītiḥ ahaṃ śaktos śakto'syāṃ kriyāyāmityahamahamiketivanmatvarthīyaṣṭhan | daṇḍāpūpāviva daṇḍāpūpikā ‘ive pratikṛtau' iti kanvā | atra hi mūṣakakartṛkeṇa daṇḍabhakṣaṇena tatsahabhāvyapūpabhakṣaṇamapyarthasiddham | evaṃvidho nyāyo daṇḍāpūpikāśabdenocyate | yathā daṇḍabhakṣaṇādapūpabhakṣaṇamarthāyātaṃ tadvatkasyacidarthasya niṣpattau sāmarthyātsamānanyāyalakṣaṇādarthāntaramāpatati sā'rthāpatiḥ | tāntrikābhimatārthāpattivyāvartanāya kāvyapadam | ayameva kaimutikanyāya ityapyucyate | ata eva ‘kaimutyenārthasaṃsiddhiḥ kāvyārthāpattiriṣyate' iti lakṣaṇaṃ praṇītaṃ kuvalayānande | nanvevamarthādathāntarapratīteranumānamevedamiti cenna | samānanyāyasya saṃbandharūpatvābhāt | daṇḍabhakṣaṇe hyapūpabhakṣaṇaṃ samānanyāyatvāducitamapi na niścitameva | daṇḍabhakṣaṇe'pi pṛthakpradeśāvasthānādinā kenāpi nimittena apūpānāmabhakṣaṇasyāpi bhāvāt | anumānaṃ punarniyatamevārthādarthāntarasyāpatanamityasyāstataḥ pṛthagbhāvaḥ ||
tathāhi
tava tejasi bhāti vibho na ravirna vidhurna bhāni na ca taṭitaḥ | bhānti kuto'gnervārtā bhāntaṃ tvāmanuvibhāti sarvamidam || 1515 ||
atra ‘na tatra sūryo bhāti na candratārakaṃ nemā vidyuto bhānti kuto'yamagniḥ | tameva bhāntamanubhāti sarvam' iti śrutipratipāditaprabhāve bhagavattejasi sahasrabhānuprabhṛtīnāmeva bhānadaurlabhye tato'pyatimātranyūnasya salilāsecanamātreṇāpi śāmyato'gnerbhānadaurlabhye kaimutikanyāyena pratipādyate ||
yathāvā
hāralatā yākācinnātha tavorasi padaṃ yadi nidadhyāt | tvaddayitā jaladhisutā kṛtāspadā'treti naitadāścaryam || 1516 ||
atra hāralatākartṛkabhagavadvakṣo'dhikaraṇakapadanyāsena aprakṛtena bhagavadvakṣo'dhikaraṇakaśrīpadanyāsanarūpasya prakṛtasya saukaryaṃ daṇḍāpūpikayā pratipādyate | iyaṃ ca hāralateti strīliṅgaśabdena nāyikātvapratītessamāsoktyā saṃkīrṇā | evamuttaratrāpi yathāsaṃbhavamūhyam ||
yathāvā
dinakaravitīrṇapūrṇadyutibharamabjaṃ jayedyadi padaṃ te | vadanaṃ tatheti sudhiyo vadituṃ ke nāma nātha saṃdihatām || 1517 ||
padaṃ vadanaṃ ca kartṛ | abjaṃ karma | kamalaṃ candramasaṃ cetyarthaḥ | dinakaretyādiviśeṣaṇamubhayostulyam | vadanaṃ tatheti dinakaravitīrṇapūrṇadyutibharamabjaṃ jayatīti vadituṃ ke nāma sudhiyassaṃdihatāmiti yojanā ||
yathāvā
arthijanārdanacakito bhajase'cyuta cettvameva giridurgam | kiṃ citraṃ śrayate śrīstvadurodurgaṃ nisargato bhīruḥ || 1518 ||
arthijanānāṃ ardanaṃ pīḍanaṃ yācanaṃ ca | tasmāt cakitaḥ nega cakita iti vā | bhīta ityarthaḥ |
yathāvā
devasya lokabandhoḥ prasādataḥ kamalamapi mukhe kavitām | samupetya yadi ca vimalaṃ bhavati kavikulaṃ tatheti kiṃ citram || 1519 ||
kamalaṃ lokabandhordevasya bhānoḥ ‘sa no bandhurjanitā' ityuktarītyā nirvyājalokabāndhavasya bhagavataśca | prasādāt prakāśāt anugrahācca | mukhe vadane agrabhāge ca | kavitāṃ kavanaṃ kasya kavarṇasya vitāṃ vivarṇatvaṃ samupetya vimalaṃ bhavatyeva śuci bhavatyeva ‘vipraśśuciḥ kaviḥ' iti śruteḥ | vimalamiti śabdarūpaṃ niṣpadyata iti tattvam ||
yathāvā
samatāyāṃ cedyatate sudhā bhavati nātha tavavācā | anyattāṃ tulayediti vārtā vārtaiva bhujagaśikharīndo || 1520 ||
he nātha! sudhā tava vācā samatāyāṃ tulyatāyāṃ yatatecet mudhā bhavati vyarthodyamā saṃpadyate | nissāratvānna tattulyā syāditi bhāvaḥ | sudhā sudheti śabdavyaktiḥ samatāyāṃ sasya sakārasya matāyāṃ mavarṇatāyāṃ yatatecet mudhā bhavati mudheti niṣpadyata iti camatkārātiśayaḥ | anyat vastvantaraṃ tāṃ tulayediti vārtā saṃbhāvanā'pi vārtaiva phalgureva ‘vārtaṃ phalgunyaroge triṣu' ityamaraḥ ||
yathāvā
paṭurucinā yena jitaḥ kalyābjavyūha eva tasyāsya | tava vadanasya kiyāniha bhavitā jaḍarājamaṇḍalasya jayaḥ || 1521 ||
atra vadanasyeti śeṣe ṣaṣṭhī | rājamaṇḍalasyetyatra tu karmaṇi | tena ‘ubhayaprāptau karmaṇi' iti niyamasya nāyaṃ viṣaya iti dhyeyam | kartṛkarmaṇoryugapatkṛtiprāptāveva tanniyamapravṛtteḥ | yadvā ‘śeṣe vibhāṣā' iti vārtike vibhāṣāṅgīkārāt śabdānāmanuśāsanamācāryeṇācāryasya vetyādāviva uktaniyamāpravṛtteḥ | kartṛkarmaṇorubhayorapi ṣaṣṭhī niṣpratyūhaiva | kalyaḥ nīrogaḥ dakṣo vā
sajjo vā ‘kalyamaharmukhe | kalyā kalyāṇavāci syātriṣu nīrogadakṣayoḥ' iti ratnamālā | ‘kalyau sajjanirāmayau, ityamaraḥ | tasya tādṛśasya kasyacidrājña iti gamyate | yaḥ abjavyūhaḥ padmākārassenāvinyāsaviśeṣaḥ tastho vīrajana iti yāvat | vastutastu kalyaṃ prabhātaṃ tasmin yaḥ abjapyūhaḥ padmasamūha ityarthaḥ | jaḍaḥ stabdhaḥ buddhihīno vā | ‘jaḍasstabdhe buddhihīne himagraste'pi vācyavat' iti ratnamālā | tathāvidho yo rājā nṛpatiḥ tasya yat maṇḍalaṃ rāṣṭraṃ tasya | paramārthatastu jaḍaḥ śiśiraḥ yaḥ rājā candraḥ tasya maṇḍalaṃ bimbaṃ tasyetyarthaḥ | anyattugamam | atra kalyābjavyūhanirjeturvadanasya jaḍarājamaṇḍalajayaḥ kaimutikanyanyasiddhaḥ | śleṣasamāsoktyuttambhiteyam ||
yathāvā
sarvajñaślāghye tava vadanena satā kalānidhau vijite | svata eva bhaṅgabhājo jaḍajātasya tu jaye'tra ko viśayaḥ || 1522 ||
satā viduṣā praśastena ca tava vadanena sarvajñaślāghye vidvanmaṇiślāghanīye dhūrjaṭiśirovidhṛte ca | kalānidhau sarvavidyānivāsabhūte viduṣi candramanasi ca | vijite sati | svata eva bhaṅgaṃ parābhavaṃ taraṅgaṃ ca bhajatīti tathoktasya jaḍajātasya stabdhajanmanaḥ | nisargata eva jaḍajanmanaḥ sarvatra parābhavajuṣaścoti bhāvaḥ | īdṛśasya jaye abhibhave ko viśayaḥ kassaṃdehaḥ na ko'pīti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi mahāvidvajjayino vadanasya nisargabhaṅgaśālijaḍajātavijayarūpārtho daṇḍāpūpikayā nibaddhaḥ ||
yathāvā
karikumbhavijayinastaba harimahile paramurojayugalasya | acalavijayaḥ kiyān syādyadasāvavireva viśruto jagati || 1523 ||
abireva śrutaḥ meṣa ityeva khyātaḥ | aviśabdo girimeṣobhayavācī | ‘avayaśailameṣārkāḥ' ityanuśāsanāt | karikumbhavijayino meṣajayaḥ kiyāniti bhāvaḥ | atra yadyapi na giriḥ karikumbhato nyūnaḥ| yena tajjayaḥ kaimutikanyāyena nyūnassyāt | tathā'pi aviśabdapratipādyameṣaśailarūpārthadvayasya śleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyenaikīkaraṇānnyūnatvamiti dhyeyam | evaṃ ‘paṭurūcinā yena jitaḥ kalyabjavyūha eva' iti prāgudāhṛte padye'pi draṣṭavyam ||
yathāvā
nāko'pi tvaddhāmno nāraka evāntato bhaveditaraḥ | madhye lopagato'cyuta naralāko naraka iti kimāścaryam || 1524 ||
he acyuta! nākaḥ svargo'pi antataḥ samyagvimarśe sati tava dhāmnaḥ divyasya sthānasya itaraḥ prasiddhanarakebhyo vilakṣaṇaḥ nārakaḥ naraka eva bhavet |
ramyāni kāmacārīṇi vimānāni sabhāstathā |
ākrīḍā vividhā rājan padminyaścāmalodakāḥ ||
ete vai nirayastāta sthānasya paramātmanaḥ |
iti bhāratokteriti bhāvaḥ | ‘syānnārakastu narakaḥ' ityamaraḥ | madhyelopagataḥ | madhyā svargapātālayormadhyavartinī yā ilā bhūriti upagataḥ avagataḥ jñāta ityarthaḥ | naralokaḥ martyalokaḥ narako bhavatīti kimāścaryam | mahāsukhatvena purāṇādipratītasya svarlokasyaiva narakaprāyatve duḥkhasahasranirantarakabalito martyaloko narakaprāyo bhavatīti kimu vaktavyamiti bhāvaḥ ||
pakṣe  nāko'pi nāka iti śabdopi antataḥ madhye itaraḥ prāptarephaḥ itaḥ raḥ yena sa iti vigrahaḥ| nāraka eva nārakaśabda eva bhavet | naralokaḥ naraloka iti śabdaḥ madhye antarāle lopagataḥ lo iti varṇaḥ apagato yasmātsa tathoktaḥ | āhitāgnyoderākṛtigaṇatvānniṣṭhāyāḥ paranipātaḥ | apagatalovarṇaḥ naraka iti niṣpadyata iti kimāścaryam ||
yathāvā
śaravarṣaistava śamito muro'gninibha iti kimadbhutaṃ śaure | sa puraskurutāmanyaṃ muramayamapi murmuro bhavañ śāmyet || 1525 ||
he śaure! tava śaravarṣaiḥ bāṇavarṣaiḥ salilavarṣaiśca | āgninibhaḥ muraḥ tannāmā daityaḥ śamita iti kimadbhutam | saḥ muraḥ anyaṃ muraṃ murāsuraṃ puruskurutāṃ tvadvijayaya svāpekṣayā prabalaṃ murāntaramevāgre kurutāṃ 'praiṣātisarga' ityādinā atisargārthe loṭ | atisargaḥ kāmacārānujñā | so'pi anyo muro'pi murmuraḥ tuṣāgniḥ bhavan śāmyet | 'murmurastu tuṣānalaḥ' iti viśvaḥ | pakṣe mura iti śabdaḥ anyaṃ svāpekṣayā bhinnaṃ muraṃ mur
ityākārakaṃ śabdaṃ puraskurutāṃ svamukhabhāge kurutām | ayamapi muraśabdo'pi murmuraḥ murmura iti niṣpadyamāna ityarthaḥ | atra prabalatarasurāntarasyaiva tuṣāgnitulyatayā śāntau murāsurasyāgninibhasya śāntiḥ kaimutikanyāyena siddhā ||
yathāvā
sakṛdīkṣase'mba yaṃ tvaṃ sa eva kalyāṇaguṇagaṇanidhiścet | tava nityanivāso'yaṃ tādṛgbhavatīti vismayaḥ ko'tra || 1526 ||
atra
sa ślāghyassa guṇī dhanyassa kulīnassa buddhimān |
sa śūrassa ca vikrānto yaṃ tvaṃ devi nirīkṣase ||
ityasyārtho'nusaṃhitaḥ ||
yathāvā
indīvarameva hare vindecchrutiśikharaśekharatvaṃ cet | tvaṃ tadbhajasītyetatkasya punarvismayāya kalpeta || 1527 ||
indīvarameva bhagavatsavarṇamevetyabhiprāyaḥ | śrutiśikharaśekharatvaṃ karṇāvataṃsatvaṃ vedāntavedyatvaṃ ca ||
vyavadhāyako'vyayaścedvadhāyakassyāt kṣamo hiraṇyasya | prahlādasya vipattau varahitatāṃ prapya dhāyakaśca bhavet || 1528 ||
avyayaḥ bhagavān | nāśarahito'piti bhāvaḥ 'avyayo'strī śabdabhede nā viṣṇau nirvyaye triṣu' iti medinī | prahlādasya vipattau āpadavasare hiraṇyasya hiraṇyakaśipoḥ vadhāya vadhaṃ kartuṃ vidhāyakaścet vyavadhānaṃ cāsaṃsaktatayā'vasthānaṃ audāsīnyabhākcediti yāvat | vyavapūrvakāddadhāteḥ kartari ṇvul | kaḥ kṣamaḥ | ko'nyastadvadhasamartha ityarthaḥ | kiṃca varahitatāṃ prāpya | prahlādasyeti ka iti cānuṣajyate | prahlādasya varahitatāṃ varadānenānukūlyaṃ prāpya | dhāyakaśca poṣako'pi ko bhavet ‘yatrāmṛtaḥ puruṣasso'vyayātmā | avyayaḥ puruṣassākṣī' ityavyayatayā prathitaḥ paramapuruṣa eva hiraṇyakaśipuvadhatannandanapoṣaṇayorudāsīnaścetkastatsaṃhāryo'nyaḥ prabhavediti bhāvaḥ ||
pakṣe byavadhāyakaḥ vyadhāyakaśabdaḥ avyayaḥ vyaṃ vya ityākārakavarṇasamudayaṃ yātīti vyayaḥ sa na bhavatītyavyayaḥ | vyaśabdopapadakādyāteḥ ‘ātonupasarge kaḥ' iti kapratyaye ‘āto lopa iṭi ca' ityālopaḥ | vyakārarahitaścedityarthaḥ | vadhāyakassyāt vadhāyaka iti niṣpadyeta | avapūrvakāddadhāterṇvuli 'vaṣṭi bhāgurirallopamavāpyorupasargayoḥ' iti avetyupasargāvayavākāralopaḥ | tataḥ varahitatāṃ vakāravidhuratāṃ prāpya dhāyakaśca dhāyaka iti śabdo'pi bhavet ||
yathāvā
gopāyāpāyānmāṃ pāpamapi dvipa itīmamapi bhagavan | payasijanayana kiyadidaṃ dvipāvanaṃ lokapāvanasya tava || 1529 ||
he bhagavan ! pāpamapi duṣkṛtinamapi atrāpirvirodhe | imamapi māmapīti bhāvaḥ | atrāpissamuccaye | dvipa iti | dvirada iti pāpaśabdasya pakāradvayaghaṭitatvācchabdārthayostādātmyāttadarthasyāpi dvipatvamiti hārdo'bhiprāyaḥ | apāyāt vipadaḥ | gopāya rakṣa | lokapāvanasya sakalalokapālānāṃ rakṣakasya dvipāvanaṃ gajarakṣaṇaṃ kiyat | dvau pātīti dvipaḥ tasya vastudvayapālayituḥ gopanaṃ kiyadityapyartho gamyate | dvipāvanamityatra śeṣaṣaṣṭhyā karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhyā vā samāsaḥ | lokapāvanasya sakalabhuvanapāvayituḥ dvipāvanaṃ dvayoḥ gajasya mama cetyubhayoḥ pavitrīkaraṇaṃ idaṃ kiyadityapyarthaḥ pratīyate | tadā pūrvārdhe gopanaṃ pavitrīkaraṇarūpamityanusaṃdheyam | imāni sarvāṇyudāharaṇāni prācāmanurodhena ||
rasagaṅgādhararītyā tvidaṃ lakṣaṇam
arthena kenacittulyanyāyādarthāntarasya cet |
apattiḥ kathyate saiṣā kāvyārthāpattirucyate ||
kenacidarthena tulyanyāyatvādarthāntarasyāpattiḥ kāvyārthāpattiḥ | nyāyaśca kāraṇam | sā ca prakṛtena prakṛtasya, aprakṛtenāprakatasya, prakṛtenāprakṛtasya, aprakṛtena prakṛtasyeti tāvaccaturbhedā | pratyekamarthāntarasya sāmyādhikyanūnattvairdvādaśadhā | tato bhāvābhāvatvābhyāṃ caturviṃśatibhedā ||
prakṛtena prakṛtasyāpādanaṃ yathā
sahajaśrītvādyadi te hṛdyatvaṃ kaustubhaḥ prapadyeya | sahajaśrīko na kutaḥ prapadyatāṃ hṛdyatāṃ maṇikirīṭaḥ || 1530 ||
hṛdyatvaṃ ca hṛdayasthitatvaṃ hṛdayapriyatvaṃ ca | atra āpādakātprakṛtādāpādyamānasya prakṛtasya sāmyam || śleṣottambhitamidam ||
nātha sumahācchavidyutpuṣkarapuṣkalavikāsi tava bhāsā | asitābhraṃ yadi vijitaṃ jitamasitābjamiti tatsamaṃ sthāne || 1531 ||
