he nātha! sumahācchā atimātranirmalā vidyut taṭit yasmiṃstat | anyatra sumahatī cchaviḥ dīptiḥ tayā vidyotata iti sumahācchavidyut | dyotateḥ kvip | puṣkare vyomni salile ca | puṣkalaṃ atiśobhanaṃ yathātathā vikāsi vijṛmbhamāṇaṃ utphullaṃ ca | asitābhraṃ nīlajaladaḥ tatsamaṃ | asitābjaṃ indīvaraṃ ca | sthāne yuktam | sugamamitarat | atra dvayorapi prakṛtatvaṃ sāmyaṃ ca ||
madrakṣaṇonmukhā yadi rakṣoktyā sā ramaiva tava dayitā | tarhi hare tadadhīnā kṣamā'pi bhavitā tatheti nāścaryam || 1532 ||
he hare ! rakṣoktyā rakṣa trāyasva ityuktimātreṇaiva sā jagadīśvaratvena divyamahiṣītvena ca prathitā tava dayitā ramaiva madrakṣaṇonmukhā yadi tarhi tadadhīnā ramāyā vaśaṃvadā kṣamā bhūdevyapi | yadvā tadadhīnā ukta evārthaḥ | kṣamā bhagavataḥ kṣāntirapi bhagavatkartṛkāśritāparādhakṣamāyāḥ lakṣmīpuruṣakāratvaniṣpādyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā madrakṣaṇonmukhā bhaviteti nāścaryam | ‘nityonnato nṛpatirava vaśīkṛtaścetkiṃ kurvate parijanā iti kaḥ prayatnaḥ' iti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe ramā rameti śabdavyakiḥ rakṣoktyā
rephasya kṣakāroktyā tadadhīnā kṣakāravaśyā satī kṣamā bhaviteti kṣameti niṣpatsyata iti nāścaryamityapyarthaścamātkāramatiśāyayati | atra tvaddayitaramārūpaprakṛtārthāpekṣayā prakṛtāyāḥ kṣamāyāḥ tadadhīnatvena nyūnatvam ||
yathāvā
karuṇāmayi kṛtamantuṃ tvameva māṃ yadi rame parityajasi | krodhoṣmalo'parādhiṣu nātho'pi sudūrameva saṃtyajati || 1533 ||
atrapi prastutenāpādyamānasya prastutasya nyūnatvam | tacca karuṇāmayi krodhoṣmala iti viśeṣaṇābhyāmavagamyate | ata eveyaṃ parikarottejitā | pūrvā tu śleṣasaṃkīrṇeti viśeṣaḥ |
puruṣottama pāpmānaṃ mama yadi hantuṃ tvameva nāśaknoḥ | kimidaṃ nu vismayapadaṃ kṣamā'pi tava naiva śakṣyati nihantum || 1534 ||
tava kṣamā kṣāntiḥ | atra pāpasya atimahattaratvena bhagavatā'pi durūcchedatvaṃ siddhavatkurtuḥ kasyaciduktiḥ paramapuruṣāpekṣayā kṣamāyāḥ strītvena nyūnatvam | ata eva kiṃ vismayapadamidamityuktam | atrāpādyāpādakayordvayorapi prakṛtatvam ||
aprakṛtena aprakṛtasyāpādanaṃ yathā
yadi kaścittava tulanāṃ hṛdi vāñchati janturacyuta tadānīm | bhūmerdhaurandharyaṃ saumeravameva vahati paramāṇuḥ || 1535 ||
atrāpyaprakṛtāt apādakāt aprakṛtasyāpādyamānasyādhikyam ||
prakṛtena aprakṛtasyāpādanaṃ yathā
sarvajña yadi tavāgre varvarti madīyametadapi kavanam | suravaiṇikavarasaṃsadi nirajīyata tarhi maśakagānena || 1536 ||
nirajīyata nirjitam | niḥ pūrvakājjayaterbhāve laṅ | atra varvatīti vidyamānatārūpātprakṛtārthāt nirajīyatetyāpādyamānasyāprakṛtārthasya sarvotkṛṣṭatayā'vasthānarūpasyādhikyam ||
jāgrati ramāpate tvayi jagadavanārthe yateta cedanyaḥ | sulabhaṃ vilasati tārkṣye śalabhasya tadā śakuntasāmrājyam || 1537 ||
atra yatata iti yatanarūpātprakṛtārthāt āpādyamānasya sāmrājyalābharūpasyāprakṛtasyādhikyam ||
bhūmībhikṣāvasare bhagavānevātikharvamūrtiścet | sarvo'pyanyaḥ kṛpaṇaḥ kharvībhavatīti kiṃ nu vaktavyam || 1538 ||
bhūmibhikṣāvasare ityetaduttarārdhe'pyanveti ||
arthinivahepsitārthāṃstvameva cennanu rameśa na dadīthāḥ | itaro bhikṣākaḥ kiṃ sakṛttiko mṛttikāmapi dadīta || 1539 ||
atrābhāvenābhāvāpādanam | prakṛtādaprakṛtasyāpādyamānasya nyūnatvaṃ ca |
yathāvā
kṣamayā'pyasamadhigamye ramayā harihṛdayasadanayā sāmye | itarāsāṃ mahiṣīṇāṃ sutarāṃ vārtā'pi tasya dūratarā || 1540 ||
atrāpyabhāvenābhāvāpādanam | prakṛtādaprakṛtasya nyūnatā ca ||
aprakṛtena prakṛtasyāpādanaṃ yathā
adhijaladhi nimajjantaṃ madhusūdana mandaraṃ tamudadhārṣīḥ | bhavajaladhau plavamānaṃ māmuddhartuṃ kiyānprayāsaste || 1541 ||
atrāprakṛtāttathāvidhamajjanmandaroddharaṇavṛttāntāt prakṛtasyāpatato bhavajaladhiplavamānoddharaṇasya nyūnatā | kiṃca āpatataḥ punararthāntarasyopādānānupādānābhyāṃ saṃbhavatyasyā vaicitryam| tatrāpatato'rthasyopādāne udāharaṇāni darśitāni ||
anupādāne yathā
varuṇālayadharaṇīgatamaṇayo'pi kvacana gaṇayituṃ śakyāḥ | phaṇigirimaṇestu suguṇā gaṇayitumamunaiva naiva śakyāssyuḥ || 1542 ||
amunaiva śrīnivāsenaiva | atra śrīnivāsādanyeṣāṃ kā kathetyāpatadarthāntaramanupāttam ||
yathāvā
tvanmahimaparijñāne sarvajño'pyajña eva deva bhavet | pūrṇaṃ taṃ varṇayituṃ caturāsyo'pyacaturāsya eva hare || 1543 ||
atrāpyanyeṣāṃ kā vārtetyāpatadarthāntaramanupāttam | virodhābhāsasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
jāgrati nākīśe tvayi bhavekādhīnago hi yo jantuḥ | sa bhavedbāliśa eva syādapi nagabālikādhīśaḥ || 1544 ||
he bhagavan! akaṃ duḥkhaṃ pāpaṃ ca eṣāmastītyakinaḥ | te na bhavantīti nākinaḥ teṣāṃ devānāmiti tu hṛdayam | adhīśe adhīśvare tvayi jāgratyapi | ‘ṣaṣṭhī cānādare' iti saptamī | tvāmanādṛtyetyarthaḥ | yo jantuḥ prāṇī akādhīnagaḥ akasya sāṃsārikaduḥkhasya adhīnān gacchatīti tathoktaḥ |
ābrahmastambaparyantā jagadantarvyavasthitāḥ |
prāṇinaḥ karmajanitasaṃsāravaśavartinaḥ ||
ityādyuktarītyā bhavaduḥkhabhājo brahmādayaḥ tān śrita ityarthaḥ | saḥ nagabālikādhīśassyādapi sarvajñaśśambhureva bhavannapīti bhāvaḥ | bāliśa eva vaidheya eva bhavet | niratiśayānandadāyinamānandamayaṃ bhagavantamupekṣya karmavaśagacaturānanādyāśrayakārī sarvajñopi mūrkha eva syāt | kimutānye iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe nagabālikādhīśaśabdaḥ
avidyamānāḥ ka dhī na gā iti catvāro varṇāḥ yasya saḥ akādhīnagaścet bāliśa iti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atra lupyamānānāṃ varṇānāṃ kramo na vivakṣitaḥ | atra kimutānye ityāpatadarthāntaramanupāttam ||
sāvaraṇānbrahmāṇḍān kabalayato yugapadamitataraśakteḥ | nākādayotra lokāsstokāḥ ke'mī tavācyuta pulākāḥ || 1545 ||
atra bhagavato yugapadanekabrahmāṇḍakabalanena tadantargatānāṃ nākādilokānāṃ kabalanasyārthikatvaṃ kaimutikanyāyena pratipādyate ||
yathāvā
akhilajagadduritamuṣo madduritamidaṃ janārdana kiyatte | pralayābdhiśoṣaṇapaṭoḥ pracaṇḍakiraṇasya ko nu veśantaḥ || 1546 ||
ko nu kaḥ padārtha ityarthaḥ | atra prativastūpamā mahāvākyārthaḥ | daladvayamupameyavākyārtha upamānavākyārthaśca | tatropameyavākyārthagatāyāmarthāpattau āpādyamānastannimittabhūtaścetyubhāvapyarthau prakṛtau | upamānavākyārthagatāyāṃ tvaprakṛtāvitīdameva pūrvodāharaṇebhyo vailakṣaṇyam | anayā diśā anyadapyūhyam ||
atra vicāryate  neyaṃ vākyavitsaṃmatāyāmarthāpattau niviśate, āpādakasyārthasyāpatitamarthaṃ vinā anupapatteratrābhāvāt | nāpi yadyarthātiśayoktau, tasyā viparītārtha eva dvayorviśrānteḥ, na ceha tathā, āpādakasya siddhatvādāpatataśca saṃbhāvyamānatvā
dyathāśruta eva dvayorviśrānteḥ | tasmādyena nyāyenaikorthassiddhastenaiva nyāyenāparopyarthasseddhumarhatītyevaṃrūpeyamarthāpattiḥ | asyāṃ cārthāntaraṃ loke avidyamānamapi kavinā svapratibhayā kalpayitvā yadyāpādyate tadā'laṃkāratvaṃ, yathā "suravaiṇikavarasaṃsadi' ityādau | anyathā tu kaimutikanyāyatāmātraṃ yathā 'sāvaraṇān brahmāṇḍān' ityādau | prācīnarītyā tadudāhṛtamasmābhiḥ | ata eva tatra kaimutikanyāyenetyuktam | yattu ‘kaimutyenārthasaṃsiddhiḥ' iti kuvalayānandakṛdbhirlakṣaṇamuktaṃ tannātīvahṛdayaṅgamaṃ, kaimutikanyāyasya nyūnārthaviṣayatvenādhikādyarthāpattāvavyāpteḥ | yathā
tvaccaraṇaśaraṇagatamapyabhibhavati śrīśa yadi ca bhavatāpaḥ | pīyūṣakiraṇamapyabhibhavitā śanakairnidāghabhavatāpaḥ || 1547 ||
atra śanakaiśśabdamahimnā bhagavatprapanneṣu bhavatāpābhibhavāpekṣayā pīyūṣakiraṇe grīṣmatāpakartṛkābhibhavo duṣkara eveti viditamapi nyāyasāmyādāpādyate | na tu kaimutikanyāyena ||
atra vaidyanāthaḥ  "kenacidarthena tulyanyāyatvādarthāntarasyāpattirarthāpattiḥ | nyāyaścātra kāraṇam | iti rasagaṅgādharoktalakṣaṇamayuktaṃ, kā vārtā sarasīruhāmiti kaimutyanyāyaviṣayārthāpattāvavyāpteḥ | kaimutikanyāyasya nyūnārthaviṣayatvena tulyanyāyatvābhāvādāpādanāpratīteśca | na cātra kaimutyanyāyatāmātraṃ na tvalaṃkāratvamiti yuktaṃ, alaṃkāratatvavidāmabhiyuktānāṃ prāktanānāṃ śūnyahṛdayatāyā apāmareṇa saṃbhāvayitumaśakyatvāt | lokavyavahāre'pi kaimutyanyāyasya camatkāritvānubhavena tenaiva nyāyena
tasyā alaṃkāratāsiddheśca | itthaṃ ca tvaduktārthāpattyudāharaṇe vakṣyamāṇassaṃbhāvanālaṃkāraḥ ‘yo'nyairyadyarthoktau ca kalpanam' iti yadyarthātiśayatvenoktaḥ yadyarthātiśayokau āpādyāpādakayorviparītārthaviśrāntatvam | iha tu āpādakasya siddhatvaṃ āpādyasya saṃbhāvyamānatvamiti vaicitryaṃ tu tadavāntarabhedatāyāssādhakam | na tu tadvahirbhūtatāyā iti na tatrāvyāptiśaṅkā'pītyalam" iti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre kāvyārthāpattisara ekaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<12>
atha kāvyaliṅgasaraḥ
yatsāmānyaviśeṣatvānālīḍhaṃ syātsamarthanam |
samarthanīyasyārthasya kāvyaliṅgaṃ taducyate ||
arthāntaranyāsavāraṇāyānālīḍhāntam ||
yathāvā
bho doṣā yaṣmābhirvastuṃ sthānāntarāṇi mṛgyantām | labhyo'tra nāvakāśo mayyaghakūlaṃkaṣo vasati kaścit || 1548 ||
atra doṣāṇāmavakāśālābharūpaḥ pūrvavākyārthaḥ | tasya ca sakaladoṣaniśśeṣaharaṇacaṇaśrīharisāṃnidhyapradarśanarūpottaravākyārthena samarthanaṃ kāvyaliṅgam ||
tasyā alaṃkāratāsiddheśca | itthaṃ ca tvaduktārthāpattyudāharaṇe vakṣyamāṇassaṃbhāvanālaṃkāraḥ ‘yo'nyairyadyarthoktau ca kalpanam' iti yadyarthātiśayatvenoktaḥ yadyarthātiśayokau āpādyāpādakayorviparītārthaviśrāntatvam | iha tu āpādakasya siddhatvaṃ āpādyasya saṃbhāvyamānatvamiti vaicitryaṃ tu tadavāntarabhedatāyāssādhakam | na tu tadvahirbhūtatāyā iti na tatrāvyāptiśaṅkā'pītyalam" iti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre kāvyārthāpattisara ekaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
atha kāvyaliṅgasaraḥ
yatsāmānyaviśeṣatvānālīḍhaṃ syātsamarthanam |
samarthanīyasyārthasya kāvyaliṅgaṃ taducyate ||
arthāntaranyāsavāraṇāyānālīḍhāntam ||
yathāvā
bho doṣā yaṣmābhirvastuṃ sthānāntarāṇi mṛgyantām | labhyo'tra nāvakāśo mayyaghakūlaṃkaṣo vasati kaścit || 1548 ||
atra doṣāṇāmavakāśālābharūpaḥ pūrvavākyārthaḥ | tasya ca
sakaladoṣaniśśeṣaharaṇacaṇaśrīharisāṃnidhyapradarśanarūpottaravākyārthena samarthanaṃ kāvyaliṅgam ||
yathāvā
tvāmāśritavatsala iti vadatāṃ nācyuta vivekaleśo'pi | yena kṛtā ripusuhṛdorviśvāśvagajāvanīprāptiḥ || 1549 ||
yena tvayā ripusuhṛdoḥ viśvāśvagajāvanīprāptiḥ kṛtā | ripupakṣe  viśvā vigataḥ śva ityākārakavarṇaḥ yasyāssā | aśvagajāvanīprāptiḥ śvavarṇāpāye agajāvanīprāptiriti niṣpadyate | śailapradeśagatabhūmyavāptiḥ teṣāṃ sarvasvabhraṃśanādvanāntaraprāpaṇaṃ kṛtamiti bhāvaḥ | suhṛtpakṣe viśvaṃ samastaṃ yat aśvagajaṃ tasya avanī pālayitrī prāptiḥ abhyudayaḥ aiśvaryamityarthaḥ | ‘udaye'dhigame prāptiḥ' ityamaraḥ | kṛtā | yadvā viśvāśvagajaṃ ca avanī ca tāsāṃ prāptiḥ lābha iti | atrottaravākyārthena pūrvavākyārthasamarthanaṃ śleṣottambhitatulyayogitāviśeṣapariṣkṛtamiti pūrvāpekṣayā viśeṣaḥ |
nanu viśvāśvagajāvanīśabdena śailapradeśagatabhūmyavāptirūpārthasya kathaṃ pratītiḥ ? vācakaśabdaprayogamantarā'rthapratīterayogāt | viśvāśvagajāvanīśabdasya tadarthavācakaśabdasvābhāvāditi cetsatyam | vigataśvavarṇasyāśvagajāvanīśabdasyetyarthāntarasya śleṣamāhimnā upasthitau tādṛśaśabdasyaivātra vivakṣitatvāt | tādṛśaśabdasya prakṛtopayogitvaṃ na svataḥ | 'kiṃtūktārthenābhedādhyavasāyasahakārāt | abhedādhyavasāyanimittaṃ ca yo'rthassa śabdaḥ iti tādātmyavyavahāraḥ | tādātmyaṃ ca mañjūṣādau vyaktamityutprekṣālaṃkāraprakaraṇe ‘vidyunmukhe vikāram' ityatra sphuṭaṃ nyarūpayāma | yadvā śleṣamahimnā agajāvanīśabdasyopasthitau tadarthasyāpyupasthitirityālaṃkārikasamayaḥ | ato nārthapratītyanupapattiriti ||
padavākyaratnākare tu ekaśabdopāttayorvācyārthayoriva śabdarūpavyaṅgyārthasyārtharūpavācyārthasya cābhedādhyavasāya ityuktam | ayaṃ hi tadgranthaḥ "ālaṃkārikāstu vyañjanayā vṛttyā upasthitānāṃ padānāmanvayabuddhau kvacittādātmyena kvacidvācyavācakabhāvena padārthānupraveśaḥ | tathāhi
"dayā titikṣāṃ satyaṃ ca yuktaṃ vyaktaṃ nanu tvayā |
apavargavyañjanāni kathaṃ syurmimabhūmipa ||
ityatra bhīmābhidhāne rājani prakṛte bhīmabhūmipapadasya vyañjanālabhyasya tādātmyamāropya tasminnapavargavyañjanānāmāsannasiddhisūcakānāṃ dayātitikṣādīnāṃ prastutānāṃ dhvanipratītapavargetarasvarānyavarṇābhedenāsaṃbhavaḥ kathaṃ syurityanena pratipādyate" ityādi | adhikaṃ tu tatraivānusaṃdheyam | evaṃca tādātmyaṃ śābdikānāmivālaṃkārikāṇāmapyabhimatamityatra na ko'pi saṃdehaḥ | anayā diśā 'mūkībhāvo viśiṣyate' ityādyudāharaṇeṣvapi nirvāho draṣṭavyaḥ ||
yathāvā
syājjātu nāpayāto bhavadādhāro hare payonidhirāṭ | aparājitatāṃ prāpto nirahitatāṃ cāpi yodhirāḍbhavati || 1550 ||
he hare ! bhavadādhāraḥ bhavān ādhāraḥ āśrayaḥ yasya sa tathābhūtaḥ, tattvaṃ tu bhavataḥ adhāraḥ adhikaraṇamiti | bhavatassamudraśāyitvāditi bhāvaḥ | payonidhirāṭ jaladhirājaḥ jātvapi apayātaḥ raṇe apayānaṃ prāptaḥ parābhūtaḥ na syāt | bhavadāśritasyāpayānaṃ na bhavediti bhāvaḥ | vastutastu na āpayāta iti
chedaḥ | apāṃ samūhaḥ āpaṃ āpaṃ yātaṃ gataṃ yasmātsa tathoktaḥ vigatasalilanivaha ityarthaḥ | na syāt sadā'pi salilapūrṇattvāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe na apayātaḥ pena pakāreṇa yātaḥ payātaḥ na payātaḥ apayātaḥ na syāt | payonidhirāṭchabdasya pakāraghaṭitatvādityarthaḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyam | yaḥ payonidhirāṭ aparājitatāṃ aparābhūtatāṃ, nirgatāḥ ahitāḥ yasya saḥ nirahitaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ nirahitatā | tāṃ nissapatnatāṃ ca prāptassan adhirāṭ rājādhirājo bhavati | sa kathamiva apayātaḥ syāditi yojanā | pakṣe payonidhirāṭchabdaḥ aparājitatāṃ pavarṇabhāsuratvābhāvaṃ nirahitatāṃ nivarṇarāhityaṃ ca prāptaḥ yodhirāṭ bhavati yodhirāḍiti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atrāpyuttarārdhavākyārthena pūrvārdhavākyārthasya samarthanam | vakṣyamāṇapadārthahetukasyāpīdamudāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati ||
yathāvā
indrādhikastvadīyastandrādhika eva bhavati parakīyaḥ | kathamanayoraikyaṃ syādabhidadhati tayoritastato bhedam || 1551 ||
indrādhikaḥ indrādapyatiśayitaśrīḥ | tandrādhikaḥ adhikatandraḥ alasa ityarthaḥ | tayoḥ tvadīyaparakīyayoḥ itastataḥ tatratatra bhedaṃ, pakṣe tayoḥ indradhikatandrādhikaśabdayoḥ itaḥ ikāreṇa tataḥ takāreṇa ca bhedaṃ vailakṣaṇyaṃ abhidadhati | janā iti śeṣaḥ | tayostāvāneva bhedo nānya iti bhāvaḥ | atra abhidadhatītyādivākyārthena uttareṇa pūrvavākyārthasamarthanam | bhagavadīyānyadī
yayossāmyaṃ gaditāraṃ pratyuktirūpatāmabhipretyeti dhyeyam | anyathā tu padārthena vākyārthasya samarthanam ||
yathāvā
saṅgasya satāṃ mahimā niravadhiko'bjākṣasevināṃ yena | dyusadagraṇīryayātirdyucyutimānapi sadagraṇīrāsīt || 1552 ||
abjākṣasevināṃ satāṃ saṅgasya mahimā niravadhikaḥ | yena satsaṅgamahimnā dyusadāṃ divaukasāṃ agraṇīḥ yayātiḥ dyucyutimānapi tridivātkenacinnimittena bhraṣṭo'pi sadagraṇīḥ santaḥ svadauhitrāḥ pratardanaśibiprabhṛtayaḥ agraṃ nayantīyagraṇyaḥ uttārakāḥ yasya sa tathokta evāsīt | tridivādbhreśyan yāyātissadbhissvadauhitraistārita ityaitihāsīkī kathā ||
yathā yāyātissanmadhye patitaḥ punarāptavān |
svaḥpadaṃ tattathā satsu patito na vimuhyati ||
ityādipramāṇānyatrānusaṃhitāniḥ | pakṣe dyusadagraṇīritiśabdaḥ dyu iti varṇasamudayacyutau sadagraṇīriti niṣpanna ityarthāntaramapyatra camatkāri | atrāpyuttaravākyārthena pūrvavākyārthasamarthanam ||
yathāvā
tvaccaraṇameva śaraṇaṃ tvatto'pi variṣṭhamiti vṛṇe śaraṇam | sugatiṃ dadāti yadidaṃ jagatāṃ sugatipradasya bhavato'pi || 1553 ||
he bhagavan! tvaccaraṇameva tvatto'pi variṣṭhaṃ śaraṇaṃ rakṣakaṃ iti matvā śaraṇaṃ vṛṇe upāyatayā vṛṇomi |
upāye gṛharakṣitrośśabdaśśaraṇamityayam |
vartate sāṃprataṃ caiṣa upāyārthaikavācakaḥ ||
ityahirvudhnyasaṃhitokteḥ | yadidaṃ tvacaraṇaṃ jagatāṃ sugatiṃ śobhanamupābaṃ gamyaṃ sthānaṃ uttamadaśāṃ vā pradadātīti tathoktaḥ | tathāvidhasya bhavato'pi sugatiṃ dadāti, ukta evārthaḥ | ramaṇīyaṃ gamanamiti tu vastusthitiḥ | ‘gatiḥ strī mārgadaśayorjñāne yātrābhyupāyayoḥ' iti medinī | atrāpyuttaravākyārthena pūrvavākyārthasya samarthanam ||
yathāvā
mā gāstāmasasamatāṃ mādhavasaktaṃ kadā'pi mānasa bhoḥ | sā na nivarteta tatassahasrakṛtvo'pyadhaśśiraḥpatane || 1554 ||
bho mānasa! tvaṃ mādhavavimukhaṃ sat kadā'pi tāmasasamatāṃ tāmasaprakṛtibhistulyatāṃ mā gāḥ | vaiparītye aniṣṭamāpadyetetyāha  setyādinā | tataḥ tāmasasamatāptyanantaraṃ sā tāmasasamatā sahasrakṛtvaḥ adhaśśiraḥpatane'pi na nivarteta iti lokoktiḥ | ato mā tāmasaṃ bhūriti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe sā tāmasasamateti śabdavyaktiḥ | arthagataṃ strītvaṃ śabde āropyata ityasakṛdavocāma | sahasrakṛtvaḥ adhaśśiraḥpatane'pi vaiparītyaprāptāvapīti yāvat | na nivarteta nivṛttiprayuktavailakṣaṇyaṃ nāpnuyāditi yāvat | pūrvānupūrvīviśiṣṭaiva bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | atrāpyuttarārdhavākyārthena pūrvā
rdhavākyārthasamarthanam | atra tāmasasamatāvāptipratiṣedhasya mādhavasaṅgarūpapadārthena samarthanaṃ vā ||
yathāvā
kuru dīrghākārāṃ śrīḥ kṛpāṃ vihitasaṃprasārāṇāṃ sadaye | na tava khyāyeta kṛpā sutarāmayi saṃprasāraṇākaraṇe || 1555 ||
he sadaye śrīḥ! dīrghaḥ ākāraḥ svarūpaṃ yasyāstāṃ tathoktāṃ kṛpāṃ karuṇāṃ vihitaṃ kṛtaṃ saṃprasārāṇaṃ sudūravvāpanaṃ yayā tāṃ tathoktāṃ kuru | mayyavicchinnāṃ kṛpāṃ prasārayetyarthaḥ | mayi tvatkṛpāvalambaikārthinīti bhāvaḥ | saṃprasaraṇākaraṇe tava sutarā śobhanatarā kṛpā na prakhyāyeta na pratheta | tvatkṛpaikaśaraṇe dīne mayi tvaṃ kṛpāṃ na prasārayasi cettava kṛpāyāḥ khyātireva nonmiṣediti bhāvaḥ ||
pakṣe dīrghaḥ akāraḥ avarṇo'nte yasyāstāṃ ākārāntāmityarthaḥ | kṛpāṃ kṛpeti śabdavyaktiṃ vihitaṃ vakṣyamāṇasūtreṇānuśiṣṭaṃ saṃprasāraṇaṃ repharūpayaṇsthānikasaṃprasāraṇasaṃjñakaṛvarṇaṃ yasyāstāṃ tathoktāṃ kuru | sutarāṃ ayi iti chedaḥ | ayi śrīriti yojanā | saṃprasāraṇākaraṇe ‘krapessaṃprasāraṇaṃ ca' iti bidādyaṅsaṃniyogaśiṣṭasaṃprasāraṇasya akaraṇe kṛpā kṛpeti śabdavyaktireva tava sutarāṃ na khyāyeta | na sidhyediti yāvat | atra pūrvārdhavākyārthasya
kṛpāsaṃprasāraṇāvaśyakatārūpasya tadakaraṇe tadakhyātyāpādanarūpo
ttarārdhavākyārthena samarthanam | atrāpi pūrvavadeva sadayatvarūpapadārtho vā hetuḥ ||
yathāvā
tvaccaraṇaśaraṇavaraṇādakutobhayatāmalapsmahīdānīm | kīnāśamanādaraṇātkīśamivekṣāmahe maheśa vayam || 1556 ||
he maheśa! bhagavan!
yadvedādau svaraḥ prokto vedānte ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
tasya prakṛtilīnasya yaḥ parassa mehaśvaraḥ ||
iti śruteḥ | ataeva ‘maheśa hātuṃ tava pādapaṅkajam' iti yamunācāryāścāhuḥ | alapsmamahi alabhāmahi | idānīṃ īdṛśalābhānantaramityarthaḥ | kīnāśaṃ kṛtāntaṃ kṣudramityapi gamyate | ‘kṛtānte puṃsi kīnāśaḥ kṣudrakarṣakayostriṣu' ityamaraḥ | anādaraṇāt nirlakṣyamityarthaḥ | kīśamiva kapimivekṣāmahe akiṃcitkaraṃ mamyāmahe
iti bhāvaḥ | 'markaṭo vānaraḥ kīśaḥ' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe kīnāśaśabdaṃ naḥ nākārasya ādaraṇaṃ nādaraṇaṃ tato nañsamāsaḥ anādaraṇaṃ tasmāt nākāratyāgāditi yāvat | kīśaṃ kīśaśabdaṃ īkṣāmahe | kīnāśaśabdaṃ nākārotsāraṇena kīśaśabdaṃ vidma ityarthaḥ | ivaśabdo vākyālaṅkāre | spaṣṭamanyat | atra bhagavaccaraṇaśaraṇavaraṇanibandhanākutobhayatvaprāptirūpapūrvavākyārthena idānīmityādeḥ kīnāśakīśatādarśanarūpottaravākyārthasya samarthanam | ayameva pūrvodāharaṇebhyo viśeṣaḥ | pūrvatra uttaravākyārthena pūrvavākyārthasamarthanaṃ hi kṛtam ||
yathāvā
agrīyaṃ śamavantaṃ śaraṇaṃ vindāmahe śaraṇya tvām | pura evāśamayuktaṃ punaśśaraṇyāntaraṃ bhajema kutaḥ || 1557 ||
śaraṇe sādhuḥ śaraṇyaḥ | tasya saṃbuddhiḥ he śaraṇya! bhagavan agrīyaṃ parārthyaṃ niratiśayamityarthaḥ | śaṃ sukhaṃ avantaṃ prāpnuva taṃ avateravāptyarthakatvenānuśiṣṭācchatā | ‘kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma' iti niratiśayavipulasukharūpatvaśravaṇāt ‘sukhaviśiṣṭābhidhānādeva ca' iti sūtritatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tvāṃ, yadvā agrīyaṃ ukto'rthaḥ śamavantaṃ śāntaṃ aśanāyādyūrmiṣaṭkapratibhaṭamityarthaḥ | ‘yattacchāntamajaram' iti śruteḥ | iha asmin janmani śaraṇaṃ upāyaṃ rakṣitāraṃ ca vindāmahe | punaḥ itaḥparamapīti yāvat | pura eva ādāveva aśaṃ sukhavihīnaṃ ata eva ayuktaṃ āśrayaṇānarhaṃ ca | yadvā aśamayuktaṃ aśāntamityarthaḥ | śaraṇyāntaraṃ anyaṃ śaraṇyaṃ
tu kuto bhajema | na bhajemaivetyarthaḥ | pakṣe he śaraṇya! rakṣaṇasādho agrīyaṃ ādyaṃ śaṃ śakāraṃ avantaṃ prāpnuvantaṃ tvāṃ bhavantaṃ śaraṇaṃ gṛhaṃ labdhvā pura eva ādāveva aśaṃ śakārarahitaṃ ayuktaṃ śakārasthāne akārayuktaṃ śaraṇyāntaraṃ araṇyāntaramityarthaḥ | antaraṃ madhyaṃ kuto bhajema | rakṣaṇasādhu gṛhaṃ labdhvā asukhamayuktaṃ araṇyāntaraṃ kuto bhajemetyarthaśca camatkārī | atra śaraṇyatvādiguṇaśālibhagavaccaraṇalābharūpapūrvavākyārthena atādṛśaśaraṇyāntarabhajanaudāsyarūpottaravākyārthasamarthanaṃ śleṣamūlakavyatirekagarbhamupapāditacamatkārāntarapoṣitaṃ cetyavadheyam ||
yathāvā
jagatāṃ janani bhavatyā kramādanālokitau yadi bhavetām | nākeśo vā satyaṃ lokeśo vā'pi keśabhūtau staḥ || 1558 ||
he jagatāṃ janani! nākeśaḥ indro vā lokeśaḥ brahmā vā | ‘hiraṇyagarbho lokeśaḥ' ityamaraḥ | kramāt yathāpūrvamanugrahāt anālokitau akaṭākṣitau bhavetāṃ yadi tadā keśabhūtau keśaprāyau staḥ ativelanissārau bhavata iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe nākeśaśabdo lokeśaśabdaśca kramāt anālokitau avidyamānau nā lo varṇau yayostau anālokau ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā' iti kap | anālokau kṛtau anālokitau bhavetāṃ yadi krameṇa nākeśaśabdo nāvarṇena lokeśaśabdo lavarṇena ca virahitau cedityarthaḥ | keśabhūtau keśaśabdatayā niṣpannāvityarthaḥ | atra vivakṣite lakṣmīkaṭākṣalakṣitatvābhāvapratiṣedhe uttaravākyārthasya hetutvam ||
yathāvā
yo bhāti vāsudevaḥ prathate yaścāpi vāmadeva iti | śrautassumato bhedo'styanayorabhidāṃ vadanna kiṃ mūrkhaḥ || 1559 ||
anayoḥ vāsudevavāmadevayoḥ bhedaḥ śrautaḥ sumataḥ ‘nārāyaṇādrudro jāyate' ityādiśrutiprasiddhatayā sarvasammata ityarthaḥ | anayorityetanmadhyamaṇinyāyenobhayato'nveti | anayoḥ evaṃ bhinnatvena śrutiprasiddhayoḥ abhidāṃ abhedaṃ vadan janaḥ mūrkho na kiṃ mūrkha
evetyarthaḥ | kiṃca vāsudevavāmadevaśabdayoḥ sumataḥ sukāramakārābhyāṃ sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | bhedaḥ śrautaḥ kaṇṭharaveṇoktaḥ | anayoḥ evaṃ vidhayoḥ śabdayoḥ abhedaṃ vadan mūrkha evetyarthāntaramapi camatkārātiśayamādhatte | atra yo bhātītyādipūrvavākyārthena anayorabhidāmityādyuttaravākyārthassamarthyate ||
yathāvā
vaśinaṃ nārāyaṇamiha vadanti santatamanantanigamāntāḥ | yadi naśivaṃ taṃ brūyādyaḥ kaścitsa pratīpadṛṅnūnam || 1560 ||
anantāḥ nigamāntāḥ ‘sarvasya vaśī | eko vaśī ' ityādayaḥ upaniṣadaḥ saṃtataṃ nārāyaṇaṃ ‘eka ha vai nārāyaṇa asīt | atha puruṣo ha vai nārāyaṇo'kāmayata prajāssṛjeya' ityādiśrutibhissarvajagatkāraṇatvena joghuṣyamāṇamiti bhāvaḥ | vaśinaṃ vaśaḥ prabhutvaṃ āyattatvaṃ vā | so'syāstīti vaśī | taṃ tathoktaṃ vadanti ‘vaśo janispṛhāyatneṣvāyattatvaprabhutvayoḥ' iti viśvaḥ | ‘jagadvaśe vartatedam' ityādikamapīha smartavyam | taṃ evaṃ vaśinaṃ sarveśvaraṃ yaḥkaścit naśivaṃ naśabdena samāsaḥ | akalyāṇaguṇaṃ brūyādyadi sa eva vaktā pratīpadṛk nūnaṃ viruddhārthadraṣṭaiva | sarveśvaratvaupayikakalyāṇaguṇagaṇaparipūrṇamapyaśivaṃ brūyāccet tasya śrutipratikūlārthadṛktve kassaṃdeha iti bhāvaḥ | yadvā sarvaṃ jagadyadāyattamiti bravītiśrutiḥ tasya sarvaniyantuḥ ‘nārāyaṇādrudro jāyate' iti śivasyāpi kāraṇatayā tathaiva śrutyā pratipāditasya ‘śivaśca nārāyaṇaḥ' iti kaṇṭharaveṇoktaṃ kāryakāraṇatvanibandhanaṃ niyāmyani
yāmakabhāvanibandhanaṃ śarīrātmabhāvanibandhanaṃ vā sāmānādhikaraṇyamaviditvā taṃ naśivaṃ brūyādyadi tasya pratīpadarśitve ko viśaya iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe vaśinamityānupūrvīmatpadaṃ naśivamiti vadeccet saḥ pratilomadṛṣṭireva | uktānupūrvyāḥ prātilomyena tathā niṣpatteriti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi vaśinamityādipūrvavākyārthena sa pratīpadṛgityuttaravākyārthasya samarthanas | pūrvasminnudāharaṇe bhagavato rudrasya ca niṣkṛṣṭarūpanibandhano bheda uktaḥ | iha tu śarīravācakaśabdānāṃ śarīriparyantatvanibandhanaṃ kāryakaraṇabhāvādinibandhanaṃ vā sāmānādhikaraṇyamavalambyābheda iti bodhyam ||
yathāvā
padme'tyaruṇarucā tava caraṇena spardhate taraṇirenam | bhāsvarataramapyāhurvisarjanīyāgragaṇyantam ||
he padme! aruṇaṃ bhānumatikrāntā atyaruṇā ruk bhāḥ yasya tena atimātrāruṇabhāsā vā tava caraṇena taraṇiḥ bhānuḥ spardhate | ata eva tamenaṃ taraṇiṃ bhāsvarataramapi atidyutiśālinamapi visarjanīyānāṃ bahiṣkāryāṇāṃ agragaṇyaṃ āhuḥ | santa iti śeṣaḥ | tvaccaraṇaspardhino'pyanyaḥ ko nāma bahiṣkārya iti bhāvaḥ | vastutastu enaṃ taraṇiriti śabdaṃ bhāsvarau tarau takārarephau yasmiṃstaṃ visarjanīyaḥ visargaḥ agragaḥ caramabhāgavidyamānaḥ yasya saḥ tādṛśo ṇiḥ ṇivarṇaḥ ante yasya taṃ visarjanīyāgragaṇyantamāhuḥ taraṇiśabdasya tathāvidhatvāditi bhāvaḥ | śabdārthayontādātmyam | atrāpi pūrvārdhavākyārthenottarārdhavākyārthasamarthanam | aprastutapraśaṃsāsaṃkīrṇam ||
yathāvā
ādau saśrīkamatha tvatpadavasuhārivipratīpamalam | kamalaṃ kalamatvamayacchritānyaliṅgaṃ vanāntare'vātsīt || 1562 ||
he padme! iti pūrvapadyādanuṣajyate | kamalaṃ ādau saśrīkaṃ saśobhaṃ savaibhavaṃ vā sat atha anantaraṃ tvatpadavasuhāri tvaccaraṇaśrīharaṃ tvadbhavanaruciharaṃ ca sat ata eva vipratīpaḥ apratidvaṃdvaḥ malaḥ pāpaṃ mālinyaṃ ca yasya tattathoktam | jagajjananīpadasaṃpadapahārādanyatkimatiśayitaṃ duritaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ | vigataḥ pratīpaḥ pratikūlaḥ yasya taditi vigrahaḥ| kalamatvaṃ coratvaṃ vrīhisasyatvaṃ ca agāt prāpnot | tataḥ anantaraṃ śritaṃ anyaliṅgaṃ cihnāntaraṃ yena tattathoktaṃ veṣāntarapraticchannapūrvarūpamiti yāvat | vane kvāpi vipine jale cāvātsīt vasati sma | pakṣe kamalamiti padaṃ ādau saśrīḥ kaḥ yasya tathoktaṃ prakāśamānakavarṇamityarthaḥ | atha vipratīpau malau makāralakārau yasya tattathoktaṃ vyatyāsitamakāralakāramityarthaḥ | ata eva kalamatvaṃ kalamaśabdatvaṃ agāt | śritaṃ anyaliṅgaṃ liṅgāntaraṃ yena tattathoktam | kamalaśabdatvāvasthāyāṃ sthitaṃ klībatvaṃ tyaktvā pulliṅgatāṃ prāptamityarthaḥ | 'kalamo lekhanīcoraśālikākṣurakeṣu ca' iti | kalamaḥ puṃsi medinyāṃ śālau pāṭaccare'pi ca' iti viśvamedinīkośābhyāṃ kalamaśabdasya pulliṅgatānuśāsanādīti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvodāharaṇavadeva sarvaṃ
draṣṭavyam |
yathāvā
kalayasi kamalavilocana kāñcana sutarāṃhatiṃ hitāhitayoḥ | sarvasamastvaṃ na kathaṃ darvīkaraśekharādrinātha bhaveḥ || 1563 ||
hitāhitayoḥ kāñcanasutarāṃhatiṃ kalayasi | tvaṃ sarvasamaḥ kathamiva na bhaveḥ | hitasya janasya sutarā śobhanatarā aṃhatiḥ viśrāṇanaṃ kāñcanasya suvarṇopalakṣitavibhavasya sutarāṃhatiḥ, tāṃ 'apasarjanamaṃhatiḥ' iti viśrāṇanaparyāyeṣvamaraḥ | hanti duritamanayā aṃhatiḥ ‘hanteraṃhaśca' ityauṇadike atipratyaye hanteraṃhādeśaḥ | ahitasya tu kāñcana anirvacanīyāṃ sutarāṃ atimātrahatiṃ hiṃsām | hanteḥ ktin | ‘aṃhatistyāgarogayoḥ' iti hemacandrakośaparyālocanāyāṃ tu yadyapi ahitapakṣe'pyaṃhatiśabdamevādāya aṃhatiṃ rogamiti vyākhyāne'pi vivakṣitasiddhirbhavatyeva | tathā'pi padabhaṅgakṛtavicchittiviśeṣasadbhāvātprāguktaevārthaśśreyān | yadvā puṃvākyeṣu nānārthakatvasya doṣābhāvādarthadvayamapyastu | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | atra pūrvārdhavākyārthena sarvasamatvamityādyuttaravākyārthasya samarthanam | idaṃ hita hitāvṛttitaulyalakṣaṇatulyayogitayā śleṣapratibhonmīlitayā dattahastamiti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
ekā kuvalayabandhurjagatāṃ bandhuḥ parā tu dṛgyasya | asmādapi ko bandhurnamatāṃ saṃbhāvyatāṃ dayāsindhuḥ || 1564 ||
atra pūrvārdhavākyārthena jagadbandhubhūtalocanadvitayavattvarūpeṇottarārdhagatasya bhagavataśśaraṇāgatabandhutānirṇayarūpavākyārthasya kaimutyena samarthanamiti vicchittiviśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvadapāṅgānvayabhājastadabhājaścāpi bhavati parabhedaḥ | ādyo viṣṭapabhṛtsyādantyaḥ phaṇiśailanātha viṣṭarabhṛt || 1565 ||
parabhedaḥ utkṛṣṭo bhedaḥ mahān bheda ityarthaḥ | pakārarephābhyāṃ bheda ityartho'pyupaskāryaḥ | ādyaḥ tvadapāṅgānvayabhāk viṣṭapabhṛt bhuvanabhartā lokapāla ityarthaḥ | antyaḥ tadabhāk viṣṭarabhṛt pareṣāṃ pīṭhādyāsanabhṛt kiṃkara iti yāvat | darbhamuṣṭidhārī itarayājanādikarteti yāvaditi vā | ‘viṣṭaro viṭapī darbhamuṣṭiḥ pīṭhādyamāsanam' ityamaraḥ | lokapālānyakiṃkarayormahān bheda iti bhāvaḥ| viṣṭapabhṛdviṣṭarabhṛcchabdayoḥ pakārarephābhyāmeva bhedaḥ | anyavarṇānupūrvyā ubhayatra tulyatvāditi parabheda ityuktam | atra pūrvārdhapratipāditaparabhedabhavanarūpavākyārthasyaikasyottarārdhavākyārthābhyadvābhyāṃ samarthanaṃ vyatirekagarbhamiti pūrvebhyo vailakṣaṇyam ||
yathāvā
dūre'pasara tamastvaṃ cakradharo mama cakāsti hṛdi nityam | yadi ceṣṭase'lpamapi vā tavāvaśiṣyeta nottamāṅgamapi || 1566 ||
he tama iti tamoguṇābhedenādhyavasitasya rāhossaṃbuddhiḥ | atra tamaso dūrāpasarpaṇaṃ anapasarpaṇena yatkiṃñcidapi ceṣṭase pūrvavahutta
māṅgāvaśeṣaṇamapi durlabhamityetau pūrvottaravākyārthau | cakradharo me cakāsti hṛdīti svahṛdayādhikaraṇakarāhuśiraśchetttṛsudarśanāyudhadharabhagavatprakāśarūpavākyārthena madhyamaṇikalpena samarthitāvityekena vākyārthena vākyārthedvayasamarthanaṃ pūrvebhyo vicchittiśāli ||
mālārūpamapīdaṃ dṛśyate | yathā
tvāṃ ya udāste mādhava madavṛttissa manujo'pi danujassyāt | nityamupāste yastvāṃ damavṛttissa danujo'pi manujassyāt || 1567 ||
he mādhava! madavṛttiḥ made garve vṛttiryasya sa tathoktaḥ | sa manujo'pi mānavo'pi mattatayā udāsitṛtvāddanuja eva syāt āsurasvabhāva eva bhavet | pakṣe manujaśabdaḥ makārasthāne dakāranyāse danuja iti niṣpadyetetyarthaḥ | yastu tvāṃ nityaṃ dame dāntatve vṛttiryasya sa tathoktaḥ | dakārasthāne makāranyāse ityarthaḥ | sa danujo'pi manujaḥ damaśīlatvānmanujatulya eva bhavet | pakṣe danujaśabdo manuja iti niṣpadyetetyarthaḥ | atra vivakṣitayorudāsanapratiṣedhopāsanāvaśyakatayoḥ pūrvavākyārthayoruttaravākyārthābhyāṃ samarthanamiti malārūpatvam | evamuttaratrāpi yathāyathaṃ draṣṭavyam ||
yathāvā
ālokitātsvayā ye bhavanti te lokapālabhṛta eva | te punaralokitā yadi janani bhaveyuḥ kapālabhṛta eva || 1568 ||
alokitāḥ adṛṣṭāḥ, avidyamānaḥ lovarṇo yeṣāṃ te alokāḥ 'śeṣādvibhāṣā' iti kap | alokāḥ kṛtāḥ alokitāḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva mālārūpatvam ||
yathāvā
śamamabhivardhaya satataṃ mama śaṅkāmatra yadi tanoṣi tvam | kāmo bhavitā vṛddhaḥ kṣemo me syāttataḥ kathaṃ śaure || 1569 ||
he śaure! satataṃ mama śamaṃ śāntiṃ abhivardhaya | atra śāntyabhivardhanaviṣaye tvaṃ śaṅkāṃ viśayaṃ tanoṣi yadi kāmaḥ viṣayābhilāṣaḥ vṛddhaḥ abhivṛddho bhavet | tataḥ anantaraṃ me kṣemaḥ saṃsārottārādikuśalaṃ ‘kuśalaṃ kṣemamastriyām' ityamaraḥ | kathaṃ syāt viṣayalolupatāyāṃ na kathaṃcidapi śreyassulabhamiti bhāvaḥ | atra śaṃkāṃ iti padadvayaṃ ‘vā padāntasya' iti śamo makārasthānikānusvārasya vaikalpikaḥ parasavarṇaḥ | śaṃ śamaśabde śakāraṃ kāṃ kākāraṃ tanoṣi cet śamaśabdaḥ kāmaśabdo bhavan vṛddhassyāt | ādyaco vṛddhasaṃjñakatvādvṛddhasaṃjñako bhavati | ‘vṛddhiryasyācāmādistadvṛddham' iti vṛddhasaṃjñāvidhānāt tataḥ śakārasthāne kākāranyāsānantaraṃ kṣemaḥ kathaṃ syāt kākāranyāse kāma ityeva śabdassyānna tu kṣema itītyarthāntaramapyanusaṃdheyam | atra śamābhivardhanaudāsyarūpavākyārthena kāmābhivṛddhibhavanarūpavākyārthasya tena ca kṣemabhavanasaṃdeharūpavākyārthasya ca pūrvapūrveṇottarottarottarasya samarthanamiti mālārūpatvam ||
yathāvā
mama cetanā viceyākārā'cyuta yātanāmayī bhavabhayataḥ | yācekṛtāvabhīteḥ kva yātanā tarhi cetanaiva syātsā || 1570 ||
he acyuta! mama cetanā buddhiḥ bhavabhayataḥ saṃsṛtibhīteḥ yātanāmayī pracuratīvravedanā satī | prācuryārthe mayaṭ | viceyākārā viceyaḥ gaveṣaṇīyaḥ ākāraḥ ākṛtiḥ yasyāssā tathoktā bhavati | saṃsārodvegajanitavedanāpracuratayā vinaṣṭaprāyā bhavatītyarthaḥ | ataḥ abhīteḥ abhayasya kṛtau karaṇe viṣaye yāce | abhayapradāne tvāṃ prārthaye ityarthaḥ | tataḥ kiṃ tava setsyatītyatrāha  kveti | tarhi tadā tvadabhayadānānantaramityarthaḥ | yātanā pūrvoktavedanā kva syāt na syādeva, kiṃtu sā cetanaiva pūrvoktā buddhireva syāt sattāṃ labhetetyarthaḥ | tvadabhayavitaraṇānantaraṃ mama buddhirastīti vyavahāryā bhavediti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe cetanā cetaneti śabdavyaktiḥ viceyākārā vibhraṃśitaḥ ceḥ ce ityākārakavarṇaḥ yena sa tathoktaḥ
viceḥ yākāraḥ yāvarṇaḥ yasyāssā tathoktā vibhraṃśitacekārātatsthānaprāpitayāvarṇā satī yātanāmayī 'tatprakṛtavacane mayaṭ' ityatra taditi yogavibhāgātsvārthiko mayaṭ | yātaneti rūpaṃ prāptā bhavatītyarthaḥ | abhīteḥ vidyamānavarṇabhraṃśanatatsthānavarṇāntaranyasanābhyāṃ śabdarūpaṃ viparītaṃ syāditi bhayarāhityāditi bhāvaḥ | yācekṛtāviti samastaṃ padam | yāvarṇasthāne cevarṇakaraṇe yātanā kva | uktarītyā yākārasthāne cekāranyāsena yātaneti śabdarūpaṃ kva syāt | tatsvarūpasyaiva gatatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
pratyuta sā pūrvoktā cetanaiva cetaneti śabdavyaktireva syāt | yātanātvaṃ prāptā cetaneti śabdavyaktiścetanetyeva sattāṃ prapadyeta | āgantukayākārasthāne punaścekāranyāsāditi bhāvaḥ | atra bhavabhayayātanāmūlakabuddhibhraṃśarūpavākyārthena bhagavadabhayayācanarūpavākyārthasya tena ca yātanānivṛttirūpavākyārthasya tena ca cetanāsvāsthyarūpavākyārthasya samarthanamiti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
bhavadava valgasi kiyadiva dūraṃ sphāraṃ hareḥ kṛpāpūram | mayi sṛtvaramatisatvaramabhipaśya tadā vinaśyati madaste || 1571 ||
atra kṛpāpūradarśanena vākyārthena madavināśavākyārthaḥ tena bhavadavavalganopasaṃhārarūpavākyārthassamarthitaḥ | atra madhyagatapūrvavākyārthahetukottaravākyārthena tatpūrvavākyārthasamarthanam | pūrvodāharaṇebhyo viśiṣṭamidaṃ mālārūpaṃ rūpakasaṃkīrṇaṃ ceti |
padārthahetukakāvyaliṅgaṃ yathā
anyonyajayaprepsāśuṅgāśṛṅgiprasaṅgiṇorubhayoḥ | tunnaḥ pannagagiriṇā kanakagirirmerurityūce || 1572 ||
kanakagiriḥ pannagagiriṇā tunnassan śṛṅgeṇa viddhassannityarthaḥ | karmaṇi niṣṭhānatvaṃ ca | ata eva me mama aruḥ vraṇamajanīti ūce uktavān | brūñaḥ kartari liṭ 'bruvo vaciḥ' iti vacyādeśaḥ | pakṣe meruḥ sumeruriti ūce ucyate sma janairiti śeṣaḥ | karmaṇi liṭ | atra merurityūce iti vākyārthastunna iti padā
rthena samarthitaḥ śleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyānuprāṇitavakṣyamāṇaniruktyuttaṃbhitaḥ ||
yathāvā
rājāsanahṛdayaṅgamavṛttirapi tvayi nirāsahṛdaya iha | jāyeta jagadadhīśvara jānaṅgamavṛttireva janturaho || 1573 ||
rājāsanena rājārhasiṃhāsanena rājāsane vā hṛdayaṅgamā hṛdyā vṛttiḥ sthitiḥ yasya sa tathābhūto'pi tvayi viṣaye nirāse audāsye tiraskāre vā hṛdayaṃ cittaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | he jagadadhīśvara! jantuḥ janaṅgamaḥ antyajaḥ tasyeyaṃ jānaṅgamī vṛttiḥ jīvikā yasya sa eva bhavitā | tvadudāsitā mahārājo'pi ‘yo viṣṇuṃ satataṃ dveṣṭi taṃ vidyādantyaretasam' ityuktarītyā caṇḍālavṛttireva bhavedityarthaḥ | janaṅgama ityatra ‘gamaśca' iti sūtreṇa saṃjñāyāṃ khacpratyayaḥ ‘arurdraṣadajantasya' iti mum | ‘caṇḍālaplavamātaṅgadivākīrtijanaṅgamāḥ' ityamaraḥ | janādgacchati dūramapasarpatīti janaṅgama iti vigrahaḥ | tasyeyaṃ jānaṅgamī aṇṇantalakṣaṇaṅīṣ tāddaśī vṛtiryasya | puṃvadbhāvaḥ | pakṣe rājāsanahṛdayaṅgamavṛttiśabdaḥ nigatāḥ rāsahṛdaya ityākārakā varṇāḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ata eva jānaṅgamavṛttirityākārakavarṇānāmeva pāriśeṣyājjānaṅgamavṛttiriti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atra rājāsanahṛdayaṅgamasyāpi jānaṅgamavṛttitāvāptirūpavākyārthaḥ tvayi nirāsahṛdaya iti padābhyāṃ samarthitaḥ | camatkāraviśeṣastu vyakta eva ||
yathāvā
tava sutarāmbarahitatāṃ svavarṇato'mbānuvindatī
kabarī | madhuripuhṛtpadmākaraviloḍane bata karī bhavati || 1574 ||
he amba! sutarā atimātraśobhanā tava kabarī svavarṇataḥ svakīyaśyāmalimnā | sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | ambarāya vyomne hitatāṃ mitratāṃ anuvindatī satī vyomatulanāṃ prāptavatī satī | madhuripuhṛdeva padmākaraḥ padmanāmāvāsabhūtastaṭākaḥ tasya viloḍane vikṣobhaṇe karī bhavati gajassaṃpadyate | tattulyo bhavati paṭūbhavatīti yāvat | pakṣe kabarī kabarīti śabdavyaktiḥ sutarāṃ atimātraṃ svavarṇataḥ svasminvidyamāneṣu varṇeṣu pūrvavatsārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | varahitatāṃ vavarṇavidhuratāṃ anuvindatī satī karī bhavati kabarīśabdo bavarṇarāhitye karīti nipadyata ityarthaḥ | atra kabaryāḥ karībhavanamaghaṭamānaṃ sutarāmbarahitatāmityādyanekapadārthaissamarthyate | idaṃ ca śleṣaparaṃparitarūpakābhyāṃ paripoṣitamiti draṣṭavyam ||
yathāvā
nanu dugdhasāgaraḥ prākkaurme rūpe'gamathanato bhagavan | samatāni dugdhasāro bhavatā kaustubharame samādadatā || 1575 ||
he bhagavan! agamathanataḥ agena mandareṇa mathanaṃ ‘sādhanaṃ kṛtā' iti samāsaḥ | tasmāt agamathanataḥ agamathanaṃ kṛtveti lyablope karmaṇi pañcamyāstasiḥ | kaustubhaḥ ramā lakṣmīścetyete samādadatā svīkurvatā ‘lakṣaṇahetvoḥ' iti sautranirdeśena
pūrvanipātaprakaraṇasyānityatājñāpanātkaustubhaśabdasya pūrvanipātaḥ |
bhavatā dugdhasāgaraḥ dugdhasāraḥ dugdhaḥ virecitaḥ sāraḥ sthirāṃśaḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ samatāni | sāratamaśrīkaustubhādānena dugdhasāgarasyākhilassāro'pi dugdha iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe dugdhasāgaraśabdaḥ rūpegamathanata ityatra gathanata iti chede gasya gakārasya mathanathaḥ vilopanāditi yāvat dugdhasāraḥ dugdhasāraśabdaḥ samatāni | gakārotsāraṇena tathā niṣpādita ityartho'pi camatkārī | atrāpi agamathanapūrvakakaustubharamāsamādānarūpairbahubhiḥ padārthaiḥ dugdhasāgarasyāprasiddhaṃ dugdhasāratvaṃ samarthyate ||
yathāvā
sūryakaraḥ kvacanāryo bhūtvā'nukasaṅgamamba jahadāsīt | aparityaktādyottamavarṇassūrībhavaṃstvadaṅghriruciḥ || 1576 ||
he amba ! sūryakaraḥ bhānukiraṇaḥ kvacana āryaḥ kulīnaḥ nyāyyaḥ sādhurvā bhūtvā ‘gauryāmāryā triṣu tveṣa kulīnanyāyyasādhuṣu' iti ratnamālā | anukānāṃ kāmukānāṃ anukāmayata ityarthe ‘anukābhika' ityādinā anoḥ kannipātyate ‘kamuke kāmitā'nukaḥ' ityamaraḥ | viṣayarasalālasānāmiti yāyat | saṅgaṃ saṃbandhaṃ jahat tyajan ‘nābhyastāt' iti nunmiṣedhaḥ | aparityaktaḥ ādyaḥ prathamabhavaḥ ata eva uttamaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ varṇo yasya sa tathoktaḥ
anupekṣitabrāhmaṇavarṇaḥ ābhyāṃ pratikūlavarjanānukūlāvarjane darśite | brāhmaṇavarṇocitācāraśīla iti yāvat | yadvā kvacanārya ityatra kvacana aryaiti chedaḥ| aryaḥ vaiśyo bhūtvā ‘aryassvāmivaiśyayoḥ iti nipātanātsādhuḥ 'syādaryassvāmivaiśyayoḥ' ityamaraḥ | anu
kasaṅgatiṃ jahat ukta evārthaḥ | aparityaktādyottamavarṇaḥ aparityaktau anusṛtāviti yāvat | tādṛśau ādyau svāpekṣayā pūrvau uttamau svasmādvariṣṭhau varṇau brahmakṣatravarṇau yena sa tathoktaḥ | brahmakṣatravarṇamānayiteti yāvat | ata eva sūrībhavan asūrissūrissaṃpadyamāno bhavan brahmavidbhavan tvadaṅghrau rucirabhiratiryasya sa tathoktaḥ āsīt | pakṣe sūryakaraśabdaḥ kvacana aryaḥ avidyamānaryavarṇaḥ bhūtvā anukasaṅgaṃ anu anantaravidyamānasya kasya kavarṇasya saṅgaṃ jahat ryaka iti varṇasamudayaṃ tyajannityarthaḥ | aparityaktau ādyaḥ prāthamikaḥ uttamaḥ antimaśca ādyottamau varṇau sūra ityākārakau varṇau yena sa tathoktaḥ ‘uttamau pravarāntimau' iti ratnamālā | ‘uttamaṃ ca naidāgham' ityādayaḥ prayogāśca | ata eva sūrībhavan asūrassūrassaṃpadyamāno bhavan sūryakaraśabdaḥ ryavarṇatyāge sūra iti niṣpanna ityarthaḥ | tvadaṅghreḥ ruciriva ruciryasya sa tathokta iti vastusthitiḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyena sūraśabdasya śrīcaraṇatulyatvamityavadheyam | atra sūryakarasya surībhavanamaprasiddhatayā samarthanasāpekṣamanekapūrvapadārthasamarthitam | śleṣamūlābhedātiśayoktyuttambhitatvādikaścamatkārastu vyakta eva ||
yathāvā
svarṇaṃ svasmāduttamavarṇaṃ śrīdehamadhamavarṇaṃ svaṃ ca | avayattamuttamarṇaṃ vidadhatsvasyādhamarṇamabhavatsarṇam || 1577 ||
svarṇaṃ kanakaṃ kartṛ | śriyo dehaṃ divyavigrahaṃ svasmāt uttamavarṇaṃ svaṃ ca ātmānaṃ ca adhamavarṇaṃ nikṛṣṭavarṇaṃ avayat jāna
tsat | avapūrvādiṇaśśatari ‘iṇo yaṇ' iti yaṇādeśaḥ | taṃ śrīdivyadehaṃ svasya ātmanaḥ uttamarṇaṃ varṇarūpaṛṇaprayoktāraṃ vidadhat tanvatsat ‘lakṣaṇahetvoḥ' iti śatā | uttamarṇakaraṇena hetunetyarthaḥ | adhamarṇaṃ varṇarūpaṛṇagrāhakaṃ ata eva sarṇaṃ ṛṇasahitamevābhūt | na tu tvatpratyarpaṇakṣamamiti bhāvaḥ | svarṇamiti nāmnā svata eva ṛṇavattvaṃ ca pratīyate ||
pakṣe  svarṇamiti padaṃ kartṛ uttamavarṇamiti padaṃ avayat avidyamānavakāraṃ kurvat uttamarṇamiti kurvat | adhamavarṇamiti padaṃ tathā kurvat adhamarṇaṃ ātmānaṃ svarṇamiti padaṃ tathā kurvat sarṇaṃ cābhūdityarthāntaraṃ camatkāramatiśāyayati | atra svarṇasyādhamarṇatvādikamalaukikatayā saṃmarthanāpekṣaṃ svasmāduttamavarṇamityādipadārthaissamarthyate | tacca lakṣmīdivyavigrahasvarṇayoruttamarṇatvādhamarṇatvāsaṃbandhe'pi tatsaṃbandhavarṇanarūpātiśayoktiprasādasamāsāditasāmrājyamiti dhyeyam | ‘uttamarṇādhamarṇau dvau prayoktṛgrāhakau kramāt' ityamaraḥ ||
yathāvā
tvadvacanasudhāharaṇātsvādyassamajani sudhākaro bhūtvā | svādyaḥ kathaṃnvitaradhā so'yaṃ syātkumudabāndhavo vāntaḥ || 1578 ||
he bhagavanniti prakaraṇāllabhyate | kumudabāndhavaḥ candramāḥ tvadvacanasudhāyāḥ haraṇāt apahārādeva sudhākaraḥ sudhāyāḥ ākaraḥ sthānaṃ bhūtvā | pakṣe sudhākaraḥ amṛtakiraṇa ityarthaḥ | svādyaḥ āsvādanīyaḥ sukāraḥ ādyavarṇo yasya sa tathokta iti tattvam |
śabdārthayostādātmyaṃ na vismartavyam | samajani ajāyata itaradhā prakārāntareṇa uktavaiparītyenetyarthaḥ | vāntaḥ vamanakarmībhūtaḥ ūdgīrṇa ityarthaḥ | so'yaṃ kumudabāndhavaḥ candramāḥ pakṣe kumudabāndhavaśabdaśca tasyānte vakāraśravaṇāt vāntatvaṃ bodhyam | kathaṃ svādyassyāt | vāntasya vastunaḥ kathamitarairāsvādyatvaṃ syāt antarā tvadvacanasudhāharaṇahetukasudhākaratvaprāptimiti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe kumudabāndhavaśabdasya vāntatvaṃ saṃbhavet na tu svādyatvaṃ, tasya kvādyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atra vāntasya kumudabāndhavasya svādyatvānyadhānupapattyā bhagavadvacanasudhāharaṇahetukasudhākaratvaṃ kalpyata iti vakṣyamāṇārthāpattirūpapramāṇālaṃkāro mahāvākyārthaḥ | tatrāvāntaravākyārthadvayaṃ candramasassvādyatvasamarthane bhagavadvacanasudhāharaṇahetukasudhākaratvaṃ svādyatvākṣepasamarthane vāntatvaṃ ceti padārthau hetū iti kāvyaliṅgadvayarūpamiti viśeṣaḥ | śleṣottambhitatvena camatkṛtiviśeṣaḥ pūrvavadeva | vastutastvidamarthāpattereva prādhānyāttadudāharaṇatārhameveti yuktam ||
yathāvā
tvadvarṇotkarṣepsaṃ sādhvastamathāpi tatra saṅgamitam | kāsārajalamakhileśvari kāsarajaṃ ko nu vā na jānīyāt || 1579 ||
he akhileśvari! kāsārajaṃ sarasijaṃ tvadvarṇādapi utkarṣe īpsā aptumicchā yasya tattathoktam | tava varṇotkarṣe īpsā'syeti tathoktaṃ vā | ata eva sādhu yathā tathā astaṃ tvayā nirastaṃ pakṣe kāsārajamiti padaṃ sādhvastaṃ dhvastasāvarṇamityarthaḥ | athāpi evamapi tatra tvadvarṇotkarṣepsāyāṃ saṅgaṃ abhiṣaṅgaṃ itaṃ prāptaṃ
iṇaḥ kartari ktaḥ | tatra dhvastasya sākārasya sthāne sādhu yathā tathā saṃ hrasvasakāraṃ gamitaṃ prāpitaṃ kāsarajaṃ mahiṣajātaṃ kāsarajaśabdaṃ ca konu vā na jānīyāt | adau durlabhavastuviśeṣaprepsayā pravṛttassamyakparāhataśca san mahiṣatulyamatirna cetkovā tadarthaṃ punaḥ pravarteteti bhāvaḥ | atra kāsārajasya kāsarajatvamalaukikatayā samarthanasāpekṣamanekaiḥ pūrvapadārthaissamarthyate | camatkārāntaraṃ tu sphuṭameva ||
yathāvā
saṃkhyāvatāṃ kulaṃ tvayyanuraktaṃ svāntare śamupayātam | niśśreyasasāmrājyaṃ sādhayituṃ kuśalameva bhavati hare || 1580 ||
saṃkhyāvatāṃ viduṣāṃ kulaṃ bṛndaṃ tvayi anuraktaṃ ata eva svāntare nijacitte śaṃ sukhaṃ ānandaṃ upayātaṃ sat niḥśreyasasāmrājyaṃ sādhayituṃ kuśalameva bhavati | pakṣe kulamiti padaṃ svāntare varṇadvayātmakasya svasya madhye śaṃ śakāraṃ upayātaṃ sat kuśalamityeva niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atra saṃkhyāvatkulasya niśśreyasasāmrājyasādhanakuśalatvaṃ tvayyanuraktamityādipadārthahetukam ||
yathāvā
prastaravarakaustubhamaṇisauvastikamambujākṣa tava hṛdayam | arigaṇaśarakiṇadanturamatyantaṃ mayi kaṭhoramityucitam || 1581 ||
atra śrīnivāsahṛdayakaṭhoratvaṃ prastaravaretyādyarigaṇetyādipadārthābhyāṃ samarthitam |
yathāvā mamaiva śrīhayavadanastutau
amṛtakare vasasi hare kṣīrarucistvaṃ nisargato madhuraḥ | tava saindhava iti vādo bhavitā kaṇṭhoparītyucitameva || 1582 ||
he hare! śrīhayavadana! amṛtakare sudhāvidhāyake pīyūṣakiraṇe iti tu tattvam | vasasi | anenāgantukaṃ mādhuryamuktam | nisargataḥ svabhāvata eva madhuraḥ madhurarasaviśiṣṭaḥ jagatāṃ priya iti tu tattvam | 'svādupriyau tu madhurau' ityamaraḥ | ata eva kṣīrasya dugdhasya ruciḥ svādimeva ruciḥ svādimā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | tava īdṛśasya saindhavaḥ lavaṇamiti vādaḥ uktiḥ kaṇṭhopari kaṇṭhādūrdhvaṃ pravṛttaḥ ahṛdaya eva | na tu hṛdayapūrvaka ityucitameva | atimātramadhurasya lavaṇamiti kathanaṃ kathaṃ manaḥpūrvakaṃ bhavediti bhāvaḥ | paramārthatastu  saindhavaḥ aśva iti vādaḥ kaṇṭhādupari tava hayāsyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ‘saindhavo lavaṇe na strī haye nā sindhuje triṣu' iti ratnamālā | atra amṛtakare vasasiti vākyārthena kṣīraruciḥ nisargato madhura iti padārthābhyāṃ ca saindhavatāvādasya kaṇṭhādupari pravṛttatvaucityarūpasya samarthanamiti viśeṣaḥ ||
kvacitpadārthavākyārthau parasparasāpekṣau hetubhāvaṃ bhajataḥ | yathā
tajjayatu kanakamakuṭaṃ yajjagadīśo bibharti śirasaiva | kanakagiriṇā'sya tulanā sakalasurairapyadhaḥ kṛtena katham || 1583 ||
atra kanakagirebhargavatkirīṭasādṛśyābhāve yajjagadīśo bibharti śirasaiveti vākyārthaḥ sakalasurairapyadhaḥkṛteneti padārthaścetyubhayaṃ saṃbhūya hetutāmaśnute | nanu sakalasurairapyadhaḥkṛteneti padārthaḥ kanakagiriniṣṭhatvena tanniṣṭhe bhagavatkirīṭasādṛśyābhāve hetubhāvaṃ bhajatāṃ, yajjagadīśo bibhartīti vākyārthastu tadavṛttitayā kathaṃ nāma tatra hetūbhavitumarhatīticet sādhyakirīṭasādṛśyābhāvānupraviṣṭakirīṭagatatayopanibadhyamāno vākyārthastathāvidhakirīṭaghaṭitasāmyābhāvaprāyojakatayā heturityubhayaṃ saṃbhūya hetutāṃ bhajata iti gṛhāṇa ||
yathāvā
nirvidye'hamavidye vidyeta mayā na te sukhalavo'pi | vijahīhi māmakiṃcanamakiṃcanajanāśrayaṃ śritaṃ kiñcit || 1584 ||
nirvidye nirviṇṇo'smi | akiṃcanajanānāṃ daridrāṇāṃ upāyāntaravidhurāṇāṃ ca āśrayaṃ kaṃcitpuruṣaṃ bhagavantamiti vastusthitiḥ atrāvidyāyāḥ svaparityāge pūrvārdhavākyārthaḥ, akiṃcanajanetyādipadārthaścetyubhayaṃ hetuḥ | vākyārthapadārthayossāpekṣatāvirahe'pi hetutvamiti pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ | samāsoktisaṃkīrṇaṃ cedamityaparo'pi viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
vitarati bahuprasādo vipulāṃ vinatāya saṃpadaṃ bhagavān | nanu kathamiva nityaśrīrasānvito'yaṃ
bahuprado na syāt || 1585 ||
bhagavān bahuprasādassan vinatāya vipulāṃ saṃpadaṃ vitarati | nityaśrīḥ ayaṃ rasānvitaḥ anugrahānvitaścet kathamiva bahuprado na syāt śrīsamṛddhimattve anugrahaśālitve ca vinatāya bahupradānaṃ nāścaryamiti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe bahuprasādaśabdaḥ nityaśrīḥ asānvita iti chedaḥ | asānvitaḥ sā ityākārakavarṇahīnaścet bahuprada ityeva śrūyata ityarthaḥ | atra vinatoddeśyakavipulasaṃpatpradānarūpavākyārtho bahuprasāda iti padārthena nanukathamivetyuttaravākyārthena ca samarthyata iti viśeṣaḥ | uttaravākyārthe'pi nityaśrīḥ rasānvita iti padārthābhyāṃ bahupradatvaṃ samarthyata ityaparo'pi viśeṣaḥ | camatkṛtiviśeṣastu sphuṭa eva ||
kvacitsamarthanīyārthasamathanārthe vākyārthe padārtho hetuḥ |
yathā
viṣayacalaḥ prāgadhunā tvadguṇavivaśastaveśa na smartā | ānandanimagno'gre'pyāgaskaraṇaṃ trirasya sahyamidam || 1586 ||
atra trirāgaskaraṇaṃ samarthanīyam , asphuṭahetukatvāt | tatsamarthanaṃ ca bhagavadvismaraṇarūpairvākyārthairnibaddham | tatra prativākyārthaṃ viṣayacalatvādayastrayo hetavaḥ padārtharūpāḥ | tavetyatra ‘adhīgartha' ityādinā ṣaṣṭhī | na smartetyatra ayamiti ahamiti vā sahyamityatra tvayeti cādhyāhāraḥ ||
yathāvā
prahlādimabadhnāstvaṃ mugdho'titarāmamūmucaḥ kaṅkam | svātaṃtryadaurlalityaṃ soḍhā ko'cyuta taveśvarasyāpi || 1587 ||
he acyuta! atitarāṃ mugdhaḥ mūḍhaḥ saumyaśca tvaṃ ‘mugdhassaumye nave mūḍhe' iti ratnamālā | prāhlādiṃ prahlādagotrāpatyaṃ baliṃ abadhnāḥ varuṇapāśairiti śeṣaḥ | prāhlādimityanena atimātrāntaraṅgatā dyotyate | kaṅkaṃ jaṭāyuṃ amūmucaḥ amocayaḥ muktabhavabandhamakaroḥ kaṅkamityanena tiryaktayā ativelodāsīnatā dyotyate | kiṃca kamapi kamapītyajñātaśīlamatibahiraṅgamityartho'pi vyajyate | atra bhagavataḥ svātantryadaurlalityaṃ samarthanasāpekṣaṃ, avyaktahetukatvāt | tatsamarthanaṃ ca antaraṅgabhūtabalibandhanodāsīnatamagṛdhramocanalakṣaṇavākyārthābhyāṃ kriyate | tatra prativākyārthaṃ mugdha iti padārtho hetuḥ ||
yathāvā
bhavyaguṇāyatto'vidamīśvaramātmānameva pūrvamahama | nyastabharo'pi tathaivetyacyuta mantudvayaṃ kṣamethā me || 1588 ||
pūrvaṃ bharanyasanātprāk ahaṃ bhavyāḥ bhave sādhavaḥ ‘tatra sādhuḥ iti yat | saṃsāraupayikā iti yāvat | ye guṇāḥ rajaḥprabhṛtayaḥ teṣu āyattaḥ adhīnaḥ | yadvā bhavī aguṇāyatta iti chedaḥ bhavī saṃsārī ahaṃ aguṇāyattaḥ aguṇāḥ apraśastaguṇāḥ mātsaryādayaḥ nañatrāprāśastye | yadvā bhagavatkaṭākṣalābhaupayikaśāntyādiguṇāḥ teṣāmanadhīnassannisyarthaḥ | ātmānaṃ māmeva īśvaraṃ tvāṃ
avidaṃ ahameva brahmeti svasvarūpādabhinnamavidam | yadvā 'īśvaro
'hamahaṃ bhogī' ityuktarītyā svatantrātmabhramavānasmītyarthaḥ | nyastabharo'pi tvayi samarpitātmātmīyarakṣābharo'pi tathaivāvidam | bhavyaguṇāyattaḥ ātmānameveśvaramajñāsiṣamityarthaḥ | iti evaṃprakāraṃ bharārpaṇapūrvottarakālakṛtaṃ me mama mantudvayaṃ aparādhayugmaṃ kṣamethāḥ | vāstavārthastu  bharanyasanottaraṃ bhavyāḥ kalyāṇāḥ ye guṇāḥ sauśīlyādayastāvakāḥ śāntyādayo māmakāśca teṣvāyattassan ātmānameva īśvaramavidaṃ paramātmānaṃ tvāmeva śeṣiṇamajñāsiṣam | yadvā ātmānaṃ pratyagātmaśarīrakaṃ īśvaramavidam | īśvaraśarīrabhūtamātmānamavidamiti bhāvaḥ | anena svatantrātmabhramo nivartyate | atra tāvadaparādhadvayaṃ samarthanīyamasphuṭahetukatvāt | tatsamarthanaṃ ca bharārpaṇapūrvāparakālayoraikarūpyeṇa śleṣamūlakena yat ātmana īśvaratvavedanaṃ tadrūpābhyāṃ vākyārthābhyāṃ kriyate | atra vākyadvaye'pi bhavyaguṇāyatta ityeṣa uktarītyā ekapadārtho'nekapadārtho vā hetuḥ śleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyānuprāṇitatvaṃ pūrvato viśeṣaḥ ||
kvacitsamarthanīyayoranyonyamaviruddhayorubhayoḥ kramādubhau hetubhāvaṃ bhajete |
yathā
yasya ramāṃ dayitatamāṃ tanayaṃ pratilomatadabhidhāvācyam | āhustasya tavācyuta vibhavaṃ rūpaṃ ca ko gaditumīṣṭām || 1589 ||
pratilomā vyatyastā tasyāḥ ramāyāḥ abhidhā ākhyā tasyāḥ vācyaṃ abhidheyaṃ māramityarthaḥ | atra bhagavadvibhavarūpayorniratiśa
yatve yasya ramāṃ dayitatamāṃ tanayaṃ pratilomatatpadārthaṃ cetyubhau hetutayā nibaddhau ||
kvacitparasparaviruddhayordvayoḥ kramādubhau hetubhāvamaśnuvāte |
yathāvā
śrutanakrākrāntadvipadīnaninādo hare bhavānatha ca | pravimṛṣṭatadaikāntyaḥ khedaṃ modaṃ ca yugapadagāt || 1590 ||
hare! iti saṃbodhanaṃ gajendramokṣārthagṛhītāvatāratvajñāpanāya | aikāntyaṃ bhagavatyekasminneva antaḥ śaraṇyatvena niścayo yasya sa ekāntaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ aikāntyaṃ, ekaścāsāvantaśca ekāntaḥ so'syāstītyekāntī tasya bhāva iti vā | tat pravimṛṣṭaṃ samyagvicāritaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ | atra bhagavataḥ yugapatkhedamodayoḥ parasparaviruddhayoḥ kramāt nakrākrāntagajendrārtaninādaśravaṇaṃ tadīyaikāntyavimarśanaṃ cetyubhau padārthau hetutayā nibaddhau ||
kvacitparasparaviruddhayorubhayossamarthanīyayoreka eva hetuḥ |
yathāvā
ākāṅkṣitāvalokaṃ lokādhīśaṃ cirādvilokitavān | nirvedaṃ kharvetaramavindamapyahamamandamānandam || 1591 ||
apekṣitadarśanaṃ sarvalokeśvaraṃ śrīnivāsaṃ cirāya paśyato mama iyantamapi samayaṃ vṛthaiva īdṛśadivyamaṅgalavigrahanirvarṇanaṃ nāghaṭateti
nirvedaḥ tallābhenānandaḥ | atra nirvedānandayordvayorviruddhayorbhagavadvilokanamekameva hetuḥ ||
atra dīkṣitāḥ  nanu sābhiprāyapadārthavākyārthavinyasanarūpātparikarādasya kāvyaliṅgasya ko bheda iticet śrūyatām | parikare padārthavākyārthabalātpratīyamānāvarthau vākyopaskārakatāṃ bhajataḥ | kāvyaliṅge tu padārthavākyārthāveva hetubhāvaṃ bhajata iti bhedaḥ | tathāhi ‘sudhāṃśukalitottaṃsa' ityādau viśeṣaṇabalātpratīyamānasya tāpanivārakāmṛtakiraṇasannidhānasya tāpaharaṇarūpavācyopaskārakatā, na tu padārthavākyārthayostatropaskārakatā | atastatra kāvyaliṅgasaṃbhavātparikara eva yuktaḥ | kāvyaliṅge tu padārthavākyārthayoreva hetutvaṃ na tu pratīyamānārthāpekṣā | ‘sukhālokocchedini | agre'pyanatimān' ityādipadārthavākyārthahetukakāvyaliṅgodāharaṇe ca sukhālokoccheditvottarakālānamanarūpābhyāṃ padārthavākyārthābhyāmeva pratīyamānārthanairapekṣyeṇa vācyārthasya samarthanādubhayorviviktaviṣayatayā bheda iti vadanti | evaṃ prācāmanurodhena kāvyaliṅgaṃ pradarśitam ||
rasagaṅgādharakārastu  "anumitikaraṇatvena sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvābhyāṃ cānāliṅgitaḥ prakṛtārthopapādakatvena vivakṣito'rthaḥ kāvyaliṅgam | upapādakatvaṃ ca tanniścayajanakajñānaviṣayatvam | anumānārthāntaranyāsayorvāraṇāyānāliṅgitāntam | upamādivāraṇāya upapādakatvenetyantam | pañcamyādiśabdapratipāditahetutākasya hetoreva vāraṇāyopapādakatvena vivakṣita iti | na tu śabdāttena rūpeṇa bodhita ityetadarthaphalakam | tena ‘bhayānakatvātparivarjanīyo dayāśrayatvādasi deva sevyaḥ, ityādau nāyamalaṃkāraḥ |
gamyamānahetutākasyaiva hetossundaratvenālaṃkārikaiḥ kāvyaliṅgatāyā abhyupagamāt | tacca subantatiṅantārthatvābhyāṃ tāvaddvividham | ādyamapi śabdāntarārthaviśeṣitaśarīraṃ śuddhaikasubantarūpaṃ ceti dvedhā | atrāpyādyaṃ sākṣātparaṃparayā vā vākyārthaviśeṣitaṃ subantārthamātraviśeṣitaṃ ceti dvibhedam | tiṅantārthabhūtamapi sākṣātparaṃparayā vā vākyārthaviśeṣitaṃ subantārthamātraviśeṣitaṃ cetidviprakāram | śuddhabhedastu tiṅantārthasya na saṃbhavati | kriyāyāḥ kārakaviśeṣitatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt" ityāha | tatprakāreṇodāharaṇāni
añjalimātracikīrṣostvaṃ jagadhikāṃ śriyaṃ dadadbhagavan | jānannālaṃ tadidaṃ vadānyamūrdhanya iti nutau trapase || 1592 ||
atra hyanitarasādhāraṇatvena varṇitāyāḥ bhagavataḥśrīnivāsasya vadānyamūrdhanyatāprayojyalajjayā āpātato'ghaṭamānāyā upapādanāya añjalicikīrṣumuktyaiśvaryadānasamānādhikaraṇaḥ paryāptyabhāvassubantamātrārthaviśeṣitātmā subantārtho hetutvenopanibaddhaḥ ||
nanu ‘lakṣaṇahetvoḥ' iti sūtravihitaśatrantena jānannityanena sākṣāddhetorabhidhānātkathaṃkāraṃ kāvyaliṅgam | tādṛśahetuvācakapadena hetorabhidhāne tadanavatārasya sarvālaṃkārikasaṃpratipannatvāditi cenmaivam | tadvihitaśśatā na hetuvācakaḥ yenaivaṃ śaṅkyeta | kiṃtu taddyotakaḥ | tathāca tatsūtraśekharagranthaḥ | ‘iha lakṣaṇādi dyotyaṃ
śatrādyarthastu kartrādireva' iti | evaṃ śānajantahetusthale'pi bodhyam | ataeva ‘yadā punaḥ padārthasya hetutvaṃ tadā yadi tṛtīyāntaṃ pañcamyanyaṃ vā padamupādīyate tadā hetumātraṃ nālaṃkāraḥ, yadā tu
viśeṣaṇadvārā kāraṇopanibandhaḥ tadā padārthavṛtti kāvyaṃliṅgam' | ityekāvalīkāreṇa tṛtīyāpañcamīvibhaktyantayorevedṛśasthale hetutvamasundaramiti kaṇṭhata eva varṇitam | ataeva rasagaṅgādharakārasya ‘imaṃ taṃ māmamba tvamatha karuṇākrāntahṛdaye punānāsarveṣāmaghamathanadarpaṃ dalayasi' iti kāvyaliṅgodāharaṇaṃ darśayataḥ pavitrīkaraṇasyānyāghadarpadalanasamarthakatoktissaṃgacchate | asya cāyamabhiprāyaḥ  hetośśābdatāyāmeva na kāvyaliṅgāvatāraḥ ‘lakṣaṇahetvoḥ' iti vihitaśatṛśānajantasthale hetuhetumadbhāvasya saṃsargatayā tṛtīyāpañcamyantasthala iva hetorna śābdatvamiti | yadyapi punānetyatra tācchīlyārthe śaktyarthe vā cānaśā nirvāhassukaraḥ tasyāhetvarthakatvāt | tathā'pi ‘namanmuktassaṃprīta' ityādau hetvarthavihitaśatrantasya namannityādipadasya muktatvaṃ prati hetutvavarṇanāt śaturhetutvamaśābdamityevāvaseyam | na hi cānaśa ivāsya śatustācchīlyādyarthakatvenānyadhāsiddhiśśakyā kartuṃ, tādṛśānuśāsanādarśanāt | evamudāhṛtasūtravihitaśānajantasthale'pi draṣṭavyamityanyatra vistaraḥ ||
yathāvā
upagūhitādadhīyannudadhisutākucayugātkaṭhoratvam | taddhārayan hṛdī hare dīneṣu bhaveḥ kathaṃ nu mṛduhṛdayaḥ || 1593 ||
atra durghaṭasya bhagavato mṛduhṛdayatābhāvasyopapādanāya udadhisutākucayugāpādānakakaṭhoratādhyayanasamānādhikaraṇastaddhṛdayadhāraṇarūpassubantamātrārthaviśoṣitassubantārtho hetutayopāttaḥ | adhīyan dhārayannityatra ‘iṅdhāryośśatravṛcchiṇi' ityakṛcchrārthe śatā ||
yathāvā
api nātha brahmāṇḍānyadhijaṭharaṃ dhārayannakhinnastvam | nyasto mayā'vanabharaḥ kiyānapi syāttato nu bibhiyāḥ kim || 1594 ||
kiyānapi syāt alpīyānvā bhavenmahīyānvā | atrānanyasāmānyatayā pratipāditasya bhagavata ātmarakṣābharavahanabhayābhāvarūpotkarṣasyāpātato ghaṭamānasyopapādanāyānantakoṭibrahmāṇḍākleśadhāraṇasamānādhikaraṇaḥ khedābhāvassubantamātrārthāviśeṣitassubantārtho hetutayopanibaddhaḥ | dhārayannityatra pūrvavadevākṛchrārthe śatā | imāni trīṇyapyudāharaṇāni tulyāni ||
yasya smaraṇāyāpi trasyanti caturmukhādayo lekhāḥ | tadbrahma hṛdi nibadhnannalajjayatko'pi tānasuraḍimbhaḥ || 1595 ||
smaraṇāya trasyanti smaraṇaṃ kartumapi bibhyati | ko'pi asuraḍimbhaḥ prahlāda ityarthaḥ | tān caturmukhādīn alajjayat | taddhyānāgocaramapi brahma svahṛdi nibadhnan tān trapāviṣṭānakaroditi bhāvaḥ | atra caturmukhādikarmakalajjāprayojakatvasiddhaye tathāvidhaparabrahmakarmakahṛdayādhikaraṇakabandhanaṃ kavinā nibaddhaṃ, taccetarasukaratvāttāddaśasiddhyasamarthaṃ viśeṣakāntaramapekṣata iti caturmukhādikartṛkaparabrahmānusmaraṇatrāsarūpavākyārthaḥ parabrahmarūpakarmadvārā viśeṣaka upāttaḥ, tadviśiṣṭaṃ ca tādṛśahṛdayādhikaraṇakabandhanamasuraḍimbhārūḍhaṃ tādṛśalajjāprayojakatvarūpakāryopapādanasamarthamiti bhavati hetuḥ ||
śrutiśikharadurgamādbhutavibhava hare tāvakān guṇānakhilān | bhaṇituṃ vijṛmbhitavato ḍimbhasyāgastvayaiva soḍhavyam || 1596 ||
atra ḍimbhatvaṃ śuddhaikasubantārthaḥ aparādhasoḍhavyatve hetuḥ | tathā adbhutavibhavatvamacintanīyaiśvaryatvaṃ subantārthaviśeṣitasubantārtharūpaṃ śrutiśikharadurgamatve | evaṃ tādṛśabhagavadguṇakarmakavarṇanamāgasi tādṛśavarṇane ca ucchṛṅkhalatayā vijṛmbhaṇamiti śuddhasubantārthodāharaṇe viśiṣṭasubantārthasyāpyudāharaṇam ||
dayito'pi tava mayā'mba tvadāśrayamadādudāsanamanāyi | yadimuñcasīmamadhunā rakṣeti tameva kimiti śaraṇamiyām || 1597 ||
he amba! tvadāśrayamadāt tvadavalambagarvāt tava dayito bhagavānapi udāsanaṃ upekṣaṇaṃ anāyi prāpyata | sarvaśaraṇyaṃ tamappupekṣitavāniti bhāvaḥ | adhunā tvadupekṣāvasare amuṃ māmiti bhāvaḥ | muñcasi yadi | adhunetyetat madhyamaṇinyāyādubhayato'nveti | adhunā tameva pūrvaṃ garvādupekṣitameva tava dayitaṃ kimiti śaraṇamiyāṃ, iyaṃ lokoktiḥ | kṛtāparādhastadagrataḥ kimānukūlyaṃ mayākṛtamiti śaraṇaṃ prāpnuyāmiti bhāvaḥ | atra imamityādinā abhivyakte vaktṛniṣṭhānanyaśaraṇatāhetuukāśrayāntarapārāṅmukhye ātmakartṛkabhagavatkarmakodāsīnatāprāpaṇarūpasubantārthaviśeṣitatiṅantārtha upapādakaḥ |
druhyanti ye jagadbhyastvaddayitāyāpi hanta tebhyo'pi | vitarasi vividhaṃ vibhavaṃ sutarāṃ mugdhā'si
dugdhasindhusute || 1598 ||
atra bhagavatyā jagajjananyā maugdhyopapādane tiṅantārthasya vitaraṇasya subantārthaviśeṣitasya tasyaiva vividhavibhavavitaraṇātmano vā asāmarthyājjagadādidrogdhṛtvarūpapūrvavākyārthaviśeṣitaṃ vitaraṇaṃ hetuḥ | viśeṣaṇatvaṃ ca saṃpradāne viśeṣaṇatvātparaṃparayā bodhyamiti ||
idaṃ sarvamapi prācīnālaṃkārikakalpitapadārthavākyārtharūpaprabhedasyaiva prakārāntareṇa kathanaṃ vyutpattivaicitryapradarśanāyaiveti dhyeyam | atra rasagaṅgādharakāraḥ  "kāvyaliṅgaṃ nālaṃkāraḥ, kavipratibhānirmitatvaprayuktacamatkāraviśeṣātmakavicchittivirahāt hetuhetumadbhāvasya lokasiddhatvāt, śleṣādisaṃmiśraṇajanyastu camatkāraḥ śleṣaprayuktatvāttadaṃśasyaivālaṃkāratāṃ kalpayati na tu kāvyaliṅgasya | tatprayojyakacamatkārāntaravirahāt" iti | atra vadanti  nedaṃ yuktaṃ, laukikatve'pi kavipratibhāmātragamyatayā camatkārādhāyakatvāt 'imaṃ taṃ māmamba tvamatha karuṇākrāntahṛdaye punānā sarveṣāmaghamathanadarpaṃ dalayasi' ityetadīyahetuhetumadbhāvasya lokasiddhatvābhāvācca | na hi ‘sukhālokocchedini mokṣanāmani mahāmohe' ityādau sukhālokocchedādīnāṃ mahāmohatādātmyādihetutvaṃ kvacitsiddham | kiṃca evamapyupamāderapyalaṃkāratā na syāt | sādṛśyasya vāstavatvena kavipratibhākaltitatvavirahāt | na ca tatrāpyupamāprayojakasādhāraṇadharmābhedāṃśe tatkalpitatvameveti vācyaṃ, ramaṇīyatvādinimittadharmasyāpi lokaprasiddhatvāt camatkārasya laukikasyāpyalaṃkāratve bādhakābhāvāt | anyathā sarvālaṃkārocchedāpatteśceti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre kāvyaliṅgasaro dviṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<13>
athārthāntaranyāsasaraḥ
samarthanaṃ viśeṣasya sāmānyenāsya tena vā | āhurarthāntaranyāsaṃ sādharmyeṇetareṇa vā ||
viśeṣasya sāmānyena sāmānyasya viśeṣeṇa vā samarthanamarthāntaranyāsaḥ | arthāntarasya viśeṣasya sāmānyasya vā samarthakatayā nyāsa ityanvartho'yam | samarthanaṃ ca idamevamanevaṃ vā syāditi saṃdehasya pratibandhakamidamitthamevetyavadhāraṇaṃ, niścaya iti yāvat | tacca dvividhamapi sādharmyeṇa vaidharmyeṇa ceti punardvividham | tatra prakṛtayossāmānyaviśeṣayossamarthyatvaṃ aprakṛtayorviśeṣasāmānyayossamarthakatvaṃ prāyaśo dṛśyate ||
yathā
syālo hareritīndurbālo'pi jaḍo'pi hanta kuṭilo'pi | dhriyate śivena śirasā mahadanubandho hi mānyatāhetuḥ || 1519 ||
atra prāthamikacaraṇatritayenokto viśeṣasturīyacaraṇoktasāmanyenāprakṛtena sādharmyeṇa samarthyate ||
yathāvā
ghanasārastilakasstānmṛganābhistilakitaḥ kathaṃ hariṇā | athavā prabhussvatantro yadicchati karoti tanniroddhā kaḥ || 1600 ||
ghanasāraḥ karpūraḥ tilakaḥ stāt bhavatu | hariṇā prakṛtena bhagavatā śrīnivāsena tasyaiva karpūrakastūrītilakitaphālatvaprasiddheḥ | atra ghanasārasya nāmnaivābhyarhaṇīyasya tilakīkaraṇamucitameva | mṛganābheḥ nāmnaiva jugupsanīyasya tilakīraṇamanucitamityākṣipya athavetyādinā sāmānyena samarthita ityākṣepālaṃkārasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
uddāmarāmatejojvalanairbhraṣṭo dharātalabhraṣṭro | niḥśrīko'rigaṇo'bhūdbhraṣṭasya ramāgamaḥ kuto vā syāt || 1601 ||
uddāmarāmatejāṃsyeva jvalanāḥ agnayaḥ taiḥ arigaṇaḥ dharātalameva bhāṣṭraṃ ambarīṣaṃ tasmin ‘klībe'mbarīṣaṃ bhrāṣṭro nā' ityamaraḥ | bhraṣṭaḥ bharjanaṃ prāpitassan niśśrīkaḥ alakṣmīkaḥ abhūt bhraṣṭasya bharjitasya ‘bhrasja pāke' ityasmātkarmaṇi ktaḥ | bhraṃśaṃ prāptasya ca, bhraṃśadhātoḥ kartari ktaḥ | ‘vraśca' iti ṣatve ṣṭutvam | ramāgamaḥ lakṣmīprāptiḥ | pakṣe ramityākāraka āgamaḥ kuto vā syāt | ‘bhrasjoropadhayoramanyatarasyām' iti ramāgamasya vaikalpikatvena bhraṣṭasyetyatra tadabhāvaḥ | nyathā bharṣṭasyeti syāditi bhāvaḥ | "bhrasjeḥ rephasya upadhāyāśca sthāne ramāgamo vā syādārdhadhātuke mittvādantyādacaḥ paraḥ sthānaṣaṣṭhīnirdeśādropadhayornnivṛttiḥ" iti sūtrārthaḥ | atrāpi pūrvapādatrayapratipāditasya viśeṣasya caramapādapratipādyasāmānyena samarthanam | rūpakaśleṣojjīvitatvādikaṃ viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvajjaṅghādāsyāptyā'hā jātā janani kāhalī kālī | śreyaskarīśvarāṇāṃ prāyassevā jaḍātmano'pi syāt || 1602 ||
kāhalī tvajjaṅghayoḥ dāsyasya āptyā īdṛśabhāgyeneti bhāvaḥ | kālī gaurī jātā ‘kālī haimavatīśvarī' ityamaraḥ | hā ityāścarye | pakṣe kāhalīti śabdavyaktiḥ ahā jāteti chedaḥ | ahā avidyamānahakārā satī kālī jātā kālīti niṣpanneti śabdārthatādātmyamūlakaśleṣadattahasto viśeṣasya sāmānyena samarthanarūpo'yam ||
yathāvā
śrīstanakalaśayugena spardhāyai yadi bhajeta mukhagatatām | gucchastucchassyādiha kaṭhinena mṛdorhi bhavati paribhūtiḥ || 1603 ||
gucchaḥ stabakaḥ śrīstanakalaśayugena saha spardhāyai spardhārthaṃ mukhagatatāṃ tatsaṃmukhīnatāṃ bhajeta yadi tadā tuccha eva syāt śauryarikta eva syāt 'śūnyaṃ tu vaśikaṃ tucchariktake' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe gucchaḥ gucchaśabdaḥ mukhe vidyamāno yo gaḥ tasmin tatāṃ takāratvaṃ bhajetacet tucchaḥ syāt | gatetyatra varṇadvaye'pyakāro mukhasukhoccāraṇārthaḥ | gakāramapanīya tatra takāranyāse tuccha iti niṣpadyetetyarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
glaustvanmukhamātsaryādyadāvilatvaṃ paraṃ gato nu bhavet | gaureva tadā'mba bhavenmatsariṇāṃ svasvarūpahānissyāt || 1604 ||
he amba! glauḥ candraḥ tvanmukhe mātsaryāt yadā paraṃ atiśayitaṃ āvilatvaṃ kaluṣatvaṃ gataḥ bhavennu | nurvitarke | pakṣe  glauḥ glauśabdaḥ yadā gataḥ gavarṇāt ādyāditvāttasiḥ | paraṃ anantaraṃ vilatvaṃ vigatalakāratvaṃ anubhavet prāpnuyāt tadā gaureva bhavet | utkṛṣṭatamalakṣmīvadanamātsaryeṇa kaluṣitahṛdayaścetsa paśureveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe glauśabdo lakāravirahe gaurityeva hi niṣpadyeteti bhāvaḥ | matsariṇāṃ parotkarṣāsahiṣṇūnāṃ svasvarūpahānissyāt svagauravakṣatireva syāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe glauśabdasyagauriti niṣpattau svarūpavyatyayassyāditi bhāvaḥ |
yathāvā
tvadgatibhāgyamavāptuṃ yatamāno'pi ca karī parābhūtaḥ | mandā eva bhaveyustuṅgā api devi pecakārūḍhāḥ || 1605 ||
tvadgatibhāgyaṃ tvatprāptibhāgadheyaṃ tvadgamanataulyaṃ ca avāptuṃ yatamāno'pica karī parābhūtaḥ | kuta ityata āha  mandā iti tuṅgā api mahānto'pi unnatā apica | pecakena ulūkena anyatra pucchamūlopāntena ārūḍhāḥ kariṇa iti yāvat | 'ulūke kariṇaḥ pucchamūlopānte ca pecakaḥ' ityamaraḥ | mandā eva bhāgyahīnā
eva, pakṣe mandasaṃjñā eva ‘mandotīkṣṇe ca mūrkhe ca svaire cābhāgyarogiṇoḥ | alpe ca triṣu puṃsi syāddhastijātyantare śanau' iti medinī | ulūkārūḍhānāmīpsitasiddhyarthaṃ yatamānānāmapi kuto mahābhāgyalābha iti bhāvaḥ | amīṣu śrīstanetyādiṣu triṣūdāharaṇeṣvapi śleṣottambhitaviṣamālaṃkārasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
puṣkaramamba padā tava niṣkāsitamapi rame tvadādhāratayā | puramāsīttava mahatā tiraskṛto'pyāśrito'mumayate hi śubham || 1606 ||
he amba rame! puṣkaraṃ kamalaṃ tava padā pādena niṣkāsitaṃ svavijitatayā sthānānnissāritamapi tvadādhāratayā tvadekāvalambanatayā tvadadhiṣṭhānatayā ca tava puraṃ gṛhaṃ āsīt ‘agāre nagare puram' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe puṣkaramiti padaṃ niṣkāsitaṃ nigataḥ ṣka iti varṇasamudayo yasmin karmaṇi tadyathā bhavati tathā āsitaṃ upaveśitaṃ puramāsīdityarthaḥ | spaṣṭamanyat | atrāpi viśeṣasya sāmānyena sādharmyeṇa samarthanam ||
yathā
tvayyatyādaravāṃścetpratyākhyāto'pi cetano bhagavan | sutarāṃ prakhyātassyāttvadādaro hyadvitīyatāṃ phalati || 1607 ||
he bhagavan! tvayeti śeṣaḥ | pratyākhyātaḥ abhibhūto'pi cetanaḥ kaścitprāṇī prāhlādirbaliriveti bhāvaḥ | tvayi atyā
daravān aviślathabhaktimāṃścet sutarāṃ asadṛśatayeti yāvat | prakhyātassyāt tvadāśrayaḥ advitīyatāṃ kīrtyādibhirasadṛśatāṃ, pakṣe dvitīyavarṇarāhityaṃ ca phalati hi | pratyākhyātaśabdaḥ atyādaravān tyā ityākārakavarṇe ādaravānna cet tadviśliṣṭaścediti yāvat prakhyātaśabdo bhavatītyarthāntaram ||
yathāvā
tava tejasi vidveṣānnātha mahāraśmayo raverlabdhvā | apyaśmadaśāmabhavanmahārayo vāsanā hi durmocā || 1608 ||
mahāraśmayaḥ aśmadaśāṃ prastarāvasthāṃ labdhvā'pi mahāraya eva mahāvidviṣa eva abhavan | pakṣe mahāraśmaya iti śabdaḥ avidyamānaḥ śma iti varṇasamudayo yasya sa tathābhūtaḥ aśmaḥ tasyadaśāṃ labdhvā, abhavat mahāraya iti chedaḥ | mahāraya iti niṣpanno'bhūdityarthaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam ||
yathāvā
samupanate'pi nadīnastava gāmbhīryāduparyupari bhaṅge | vyakriyata nāntarīdṛṅna vikṛtibhākprakṛtivaiparītyena || 1609 ||
he bhagavanniti labhyate, tasyaiva prakṛtatvāt | nadīnaḥ saritpatiḥ | adīna ityapyupaskāryam | tava gāmbhīryāt hetubhūtāt uparyupari padepade bhaṅge svasya paribhave, pakṣe uparyupari ūrdhvordhvabhāga eva bhaṅge taraṅge jātyabhiprāyakamekavacanam | samupanate'pi
prāpte'pītyarthaḥ | antaḥ manasi, pakṣe antaḥ pradeśe na vyakriyata na vikurute sma | hṛdayadaurbalyaṃ na prācīkaśaditi yāvat | karmakartari yak | yadvā na vyakriyata nadīnatāṃ nātyākṣīdityarthaḥ | pakṣe kṣubhito nāsīdityarthaḥ | īdṛk gabhīrasvabhavaḥ prakṛtivaiparītyena gabhīrimarūsvasvabhāvaviparyayeṇa vikṛtibhāk, lāghavarūpavikārabhāk na bhavati | āpatatsvapi hṛdayavikṣobhahetuṣu bhūyassu na gabhīraprakṛtissvasvabhāvavirodhilāghavādirūpavikāramāpnotīti bhāvaḥ ||
pakṣe nadīnaḥ nadīna iti śabdaḥ uparyupari agre'gre bhaṅge svādivibhaktīnāṃ parasparaṃ bhede upanate'pi | ‘bhaṅgo jayaviparyaye | bhedarogataraṅgeṣu' iti medinī | antaḥ vibhakyapekṣayā pūrvavartini prātipadikarūpe antarbhāge na vyakriyata | vibhaktīnāmanyonyabhedasadbhāve'pi prakṛteravikṛtatayaivāvasthānāditi bhāvaḥ | na kevalaṃ yathāvasthitaprātipadikāvikṛtatvameva, apitu tadvaiparītye'pyavikṛtatvamevetyāha  īdṛgiti | īdṛk nadīnaśabda eveti bhāvaḥ | prakṛtivaiparītyena prātipadikarūpaprakṛtervyatyastatayā pāṭhe na vikṛtibhāk pūrvāpekṣayā vibhinnavarṇānupūrvīkaḥ na bhavati | kiṃtu yathāpuraṃ nadīna ityevāvatiṣṭhata iti bhāvaḥ | atra samupanate'pītyādinā nāntarityantena pratipāditasya viśeṣasya īdṛgityādisāmānyenāprastutena samarthanam | samāsoktisaṃkīrṇatvādivicchittiviśeṣastu sugama eva ||
yathāvā
vikacasitāmbujamudbhaṭagandhaṃ tvannayanajitamathāpacitam | tava sarvadā''syalakṣmīmalabhata sūnasya vṛttiriyatī hi || 1610 ||
he bhagavan! vikacaṃ vyākocaṃ sitāmbujaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ udbhaṭaḥ gandhaḥ yasya tattathoktaṃ atisaundaryagarvitamityarthaḥ | atisaurabhaśālīti tu tattvam | atha tvannayanajitaṃ ata eva apacitaṃ apacayaṃ prāptaṃ tvannayanajitaṃ atha apacitamiti vā yojanā | pakṣe vikacasitāmbujamiti padaṃ apacitaṃ apagatacakāraṃ kṛtaṃ sat vikasitāmbujaṃ bhavaditi bhāvaḥ | tava sarvadāsyalakṣmīṃ sarvavidhakaiṃkaryaśriyaṃ, pakṣe sarvadā āsyalakṣmīṃ mukhaśobhāṃ alabhata | tathāhi, sūnasya suṣṭhu ūnasya vairaśuddhyaupayikaśauryadhairyādibhissarvathā'pi hīnasya prasūnasyeti tu tattvam | gatiḥ sthitiḥ iyatī etāvatī | yatpratīkārākṣamatayā svajaitropasarpaṇamiti bhāvaḥ | ayamapi sāmānyena viśeṣasya samarthanarūpa eva ||
tvatsaukumāryavijitaṃ matvā nijasaukumāryamakhilāmba | navanītamanasthitimadvanītamāsīnmṛdorhi gatiriyatī || 1611 ||
navanītaṃ anasthitimat anasi śakaṭe sthitimat 'śarpare khari vā visargalopaḥ' iti visargalope sthitītyatraikasakārakameva rūpamarthāntare śabdāvairūpyāyeti dhyeyam | deśāntaraprasthānāya śakaṭamārūḍhamiti bhāvaḥ | vanītaṃ vanīṃ gataṃ athavā vanī itā prāptā yeneti bahuvrīhiḥ | araṇyānīṃ praviṣṭamityarthaḥ | pakṣe navanītamiti padaṃ nasya nakārasya sthitiḥ sā'syāstīti nasthitimat tanna bhavatītyanasthitimat nakāravighaṭītaṃ vanītamāsīdityarthaḥ | tathāhi, mṛdoḥ komalasya atīkṣṇasya ca gatiḥ īdṛk | anyatsarvaṃ pūrvavat ||
yathāvā
tvatpadarucividveṣādvibhaṅgamabhyetya vidrumaśśaure | druma eva sarvadhā'bhūttvaddveṣasyedṛśo hi pariṇāmaḥ ||
vidrumaḥ pravālaḥ vidrumaśabdaśca | vibhaṅgaṃ viśeṣeṇa parābhavaṃ sarvatomukhaṃ paribhavamiti yāvat | pakṣe vi ityākārakavarṇasya nāśaṃ abhyetya | druma eva tarureva pakṣe drumaśabda eva abhūt tvayi tvadviṣaye dveṣasya pariṇāmaḥ paripākaḥ īdṛśo hi | sthāvarajanmaprāpaṇameva hi ||
yathāvā
phālena tava parāsto bālendurbhavati bahulagaladojāḥ | hālikasaṃgharṣī mṛdurayate kṣayameva devi haridayite || 1613 ||
bālenduḥ abhivṛddho bhavannapi bahulagaladojāḥ atimātrabhraśyattejāḥ bhavati bahule kṛṣṇapakṣe galadojāḥ bhavatīti vastusthitiḥ | halaṃ praharaṇamasya hālikaḥ tena saṃgharṣī mṛduḥ śarīrataśśauryataśca mṛdulaḥ | pakṣe hā alikasaṃgharṣīti chedaḥ alikaṃ lalāṭaṃ tena saṃgharṣītyarthaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam ||
yathāvā
paramapuruṣāśrito yaḥ paravākcharabādhito'pi dṛḍhabhāvāt | na pureva sa paramaruṣāśrito bhavennahi tathāvidhassīdet || 1614 ||
yaḥ pumān paramapuruṣāśritaḥ saḥ pareṣāṃ vairiṇāṃ vāgbhiśśaraiśca | pakṣe śaratulyābhirvāgbhiḥ bādhito'pi kṣato'pi dṛḍhabhāvāt bhagavadanugrahajanitaśarīradārḍhyāttadbhaktidārḍhyācca pureva bhagavadāśrayaṇātpūrvamiva paraṃ aruṣā ‘vraṇena vraṇo'striyāmarmimaruḥ' ityamaraḥ | śrito na bhavet | prahlādāderiva tasya bhagavatprasādātparaiḥ prayuktāśśarā na vyathāleśamapi vidhātumīśarinniti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe paramayā ruṣā krudhā śrito na bhavet ‘na krodho na ca mātsaryam' ityādyuktarītyā kṣodiṣṭhamarmasparśivāgbhistasya krodhānudayāditi bhāvaḥ | tathāhi tathāvidhaḥ mahadāśritaḥ na sīdet nāvasīdedityarthaḥ ||
pakṣe paramapuruṣāśritaśabdaḥ na vidyate puḥ puvarṇaḥ yasya napuḥ naśabdena bahuvrīhiḥ | puvarṇarahita eva paramaruṣāśrita iti jāyate | tathāvidhaḥ puvarṇavidhuratāṃ prāpto'pi paramapuruṣaśabdaḥ na hi sīdet nirarthakatayā nāvatiṣṭheta kiṃtu arthavattayaiva jāgṛyāditi bhāvaḥ |
yathāvā
induḥ kandurapi tvanmukhavasuhārī kṣayaṃ kamapi yātaḥ | yo dusthitimān svānte sa bhavati bhāvāntare'pi dusthitimān || 1615 ||
he bhagavan! induḥ tvanmukhavasuhārī vadanaprabhāharaṇaśīlaḥ tvatprabhṛtidhanaharaṇaśīla iti ca gamyate | tācchīlye ṇiniḥ | ataeva kamapi kṣayaṃ sakalakalāpacayarūpaṃ rājayakṣmāṇaṃ yātassan kandurapi kandurbhavannapi kandurnāma ayomaya uttāno vartulākāro
holakādyapūpanirvartanopakaraṇabhūto vastuviśeṣaḥ | ‘klibe'mbarīṣaṃ
bhrāṣṭro nā kandurvā svedanī striyām' ityamaraḥ | kanduśabdaḥ vā nā vā puṃsīti kośārthaḥ | svedanījanma bhajamāno'pi tadvannistejasko bhavannapīti tu hṛdayam | pakṣe hārī ikṣayamiti cchedaḥ | induḥ induśabdaḥ ikṣayaṃ ikāralopaṃ yātaḥ naitāvadeva, kamapi yātaḥ | apissamuccaye | ikārasthāne kakāraṃ ca yātaḥ kanduriti niṣpanna ityarthaḥ | evaṃ kandurūpaṃ prapanno'pi kamapi kṣayaṃ yāta ityāvṛttyā yojanīyam | indutvāvasthāyāmiva kandutvāvasthāyāmapi rājayakṣmāṇaṃ prāpta evāsīt na tu tamatyākṣīditi bhāvaḥ | vastutastu kamapi kṣayaṃ gṛhaṃ mahānasamiti bhāvaḥ | tatraiva kandoravasthānaucityāt ‘kṣayo rogāntare veśmakalpāntāpacayeṣu ca' iti medinī | yadvā cullīsthityavasaralagnadhūmamaṣīvyapanayārthakṛtasikatādigharṣaṇabhūyastayā apacayaṃ yāta ityarthaḥ | kathamasya janmāntare'pi kṣayānuvṛttirityata āha  ya iti | yaḥ svānte manasi ‘svāntaṃ hṛnmānasam' ityamaraḥ | dusthitimān nityaṃ parasvāpahārādidurāśayavān | nityayoge matup | tadanurūpakṣayādyupatāpī ca bhavati | saḥ bhāvāntare'pi ‘bhāvassattāsvabhāvābhiprāyaceṣṭātmajanmasu' ityamaraḥ | dusthitimāneva bhavati | na hi nityameva pāpaśīlānāmakṛtatanniṣkṛtīnāṃ janmātare'pi tatphalānubhavassukarāpanoda iti bhāvaḥ | vastustu yaḥ induśabdaḥ svānte svasya caramabhāge dusthitimān ikāralopena tatsthāne kakāraṃ prāpto'pi nityaduvarṇasthitimāniti bhāvaḥ | saḥ induśabdaḥ bhāvāntare'pi kanduriti rūpāntare prāpte'pi dusthitimāneva duvarṇasya
pūrvamapyanapanatitvāttadviśiṣṭa eva bhavati | indukanduśabdayorubhayorapyante duritivarṇasamudayasya taulyāditi bhāvaḥ | śabdārthatā
dātmyavijṛmbhitaśleṣamūlakābhedādhyavasāyānupraṇitassāmānyenāprastutena viśeṣasamarthanarūpa evārthāntaranyāsaḥ ||
viśesṣaya sāmānyena vaidharmyeṇa samarthanaṃ yathā
saṃkalpādeva jagadveṅkaṭanāthassṛjatyavatyatti | asamartho yaḥ kāryeṣvapekṣate sa paramanyasāhāyyam || 1616 ||
atra pūrvārdhoktasya viśeṣasya prakṛtasya uttarārdhoktena sāmānyenāprakṛtena vaidharmyeṇa samarthanam ||
yathāvā
nitarāṃ janani karābhyāṃ nijakaracaraṇekṣaṇapratispardhi | nalinaṃ bata lālayase na parābhavitā'rimapi mṛduprakṛtiḥ || 1617 ||
atra prāthamikacaraṇatritayapratipāditasya viśeṣasya caturthacaraṇoditasāmānyena vaidharmyeṇa samarthanam ||
yathāvā
lokeśe nākeśe kāke kīśe'pi tava kṛpā sadṛśī | na viśati hi mahodāro nanūttamādhamavibhāgakārpaṇyam || 1618 ||
atrāpi pūrvārdhokto viśeṣaḥ prakṛta uttarārdhoktena sāmānyenāprakṛtena vaidharmyeṇa samarthyate ||
yathāvā
hutavahamadhye taptvā drutamujjvalitaṃ bhavāntare bharma | varma tavācyuta samajani kalyāṇasya svataḥ kimaprāpyam || 1619 ||
hi acyuta! bharma svarṇaṃ kartṛ | hutavahamadhye pañcāgnimadhye agnimadhye ca taptvā tapaḥ kṛtvā taptaṃ bhūtvā ca ataeva drutaṃ śīghraṃ ujjvalitaṃ prakāśitaṃ pakṣe drutaṃ dravatāṃ gataṃ vilīnamityarthaḥ | ujjvalitaṃ puṭapākena śobhitamityarthaḥ | bhavāntare janmāntare rūpāntare ca tava varma kavacaṃ ajani | pakṣe bharma bharmeti padaṃ bhavāntare vena vakāreṇa antaraṃ antardhānaṃ bhasya bhakārasya vāntaraṃ bhavāntaraṃ tasmin sati vakārasya bhakāreṇa tirodhāne sati bhakārasthāne vakāroccāraṇe satīti yāvat 'antaramavakāśāvadhiparidhānāntardhibhedatādarthye' ityamara | varma ajani | bharmeti padaṃ varmeti niṣpannamityarthaḥ | svataḥ kalyāṇasya maṅgalakṛtaḥ suvarṇasya ca kimaprāpyaṃ sarvaṃ prāpyameva ‘na hi kalyāṇakṛtkaściddurgatiṃ tāta gacchati' iti gānāditi bhāvaḥ | ‘kalyāṇaṃ maṅgale svarṇe' iti ratnamālā | atrāpi pūrvavadeva samarthanam | śleṣādisaṃkīrṇatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ |
yathāvā
bhramaro'pi tava padābje lagno'bhramametya bhavati khalvamaraḥ | kiṃkimalabhyaṃ tvatpadapaṅkajasaktasya
veṅkaṭādrimaṇe || 1620 ||
bhramaro'pi tiryagjanturapi kāmukopīti vā ‘bhramaraḥ kāmuke bhṛṅge' iti medinī | tava padābje lagnassan abhramaṃ anyatra bhramaṇaṃ abhrāntiṃ vā etya amaro bhavati devo bhavati | khalviti prasiddhau | pakṣe bhramaraśabdaḥ abhramamityatra abhraṃ aṃ iti cchedaḥ| avidyamāno bhraḥ bhravarṇo yasya taṃ bhraṃśitabhravaṇamiti yāvat aṃ avarṇaṃ etyabhravarṇasthāne akāraṃ prāpyetyarthaḥ | amaraḥ abhūt uktarīyā bhramaraśabdaḥ amaraśabdo'bhūdityarthaḥ | atrāpi pūrvadeva sarvamanusandheyam ||
yathāvā
api pāmarastavāṅghrāvupajātarucistvayā kṛto'pāpaḥ | labhate'maratvamacyuta na bhajetkiṃ vā tvadāśrito'bhīṣṭam || 1621 ||
he acyuta! iyaṃ saṃbodhanaṃ vivakṣitapāpaharaṇasābhaprāyam | yathā rsmayate
atipāpaprasakto'pi dhyāyannimiṣamacyutam |
bhūyastapasvī bhavati paṅktipāvanapāvanaḥ ||
iti | tava aṅghrau upajātaruciḥ pāmaraḥ nīco'pi tvayā apāpaḥ kṛtaḥ nirdhūtākhilapūrvottarāghaḥ kṛtassan amaratvaṃ ‘yatra pūrve sādhyāssanti devāḥ' ityuktaprakāreṇa devatvaṃ muktatvamiti yāvat labhate | pakṣe pāmaraśabdaḥ tvayā paṃ āpnotīti pāpaḥ | karmaṇyaṇ | napāpaḥ apāpaḥ pavararṇarahitaḥ kṛta iti yāvat | amaratvaṃ amara śabdatvaṃ labhate ityarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
ādyantaprāptajayaṃ madhye'pica leśato'pyavidhvastam | jayati jaleśayamamba tvadāśritānāṃ kadā paribhavassyāt || 1622 ||
jaleśayaṃ kamalaṃ jaleśayamiti padaṃ ca | ādyantayossamarthayoḥ adyantabhāgayośca prāptaḥ jayaḥ utkarṣaḥ yasya tattathoktaṃ, prāptau jayau jakārayakārau yena taditi ca | madhye'pica madhyakāle'pi leśato'pi lavamātrato'pi avidhvastaṃ bhraṃśaṃ naprāptaṃ, pakṣe leśataḥ le śa iti varṇābhyāṃ avidhvasvaṃ adhvastaleśarṇamityarthaḥ | jayati sarvotkarṣe prāpnoti | śabdapakṣe jaleśayaśabdaḥ evaṃvidho jayatītyarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
nahuṣassa vāsavassannavalepātsvānta eva hasito'mba | vāhasabhāvamavindattvadapāṅgaviparyaye kimiva na syāt || 1623 ||
he amba! saḥ prasiddhaḥ nahuṣaḥ vāsavassan indro bhavan san avalepāt trailokyaiśvaryamadāt svānta eva manasyeva ‘svāntaṃ hṛnmānasaṃ manaḥ' ityamaraḥ | hasitaḥ mattulyaḥ ko'nyostīti hasanniti garvānubhāvoktiḥ | kartari ktaḥ | vāhasabhāvaṃ ajagaratvaṃ ‘ajagaraśśayurvāhasa ityubhau' ityamaraḥ| avindat | pakṣe vāsavaśabdaḥ vasya vakārasya lepaḥ saṃbandhaḥ na valepaḥ avalepaḥ tasmāt vakārasaṃbandhābhāvaṃ prāpyeti lyablope pañcamī | vakāralopena vāsa iti niṣpannaḥ svānta eva svasya ante madhyabhāga eva hasitaḥ hena hakāreṇa sitaḥ baddhassan madhye hakāraghaṭitassannityarthaḥ |
vāhasabhāvaṃ vāhasaśabdatvaṃ avindat vā sa iti varṇayormadhye hakāravinyāse tathā niṣpatteriti bhāvaḥ | spaṣṭamanyat ||
yathāvā
tvaccaraṇarucestaulye kisalayamudyuktameva cedamba | mukhabhaṅgātsalayaṃ syādīdṛgdarśayati kiṃ mukhaṃ bhagnam || 1624 ||
kisalayaṃ mukhabhaṅgāt parābhavāditi yāvat | layaḥ yatra kvāpi vā līnatā tena saha vartata iti salayaṃ syāt | kenāpyadṛśyamānatayā'vasthitaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe mukhavarṇavicyuteḥ salayaṃ syāt kisalayamiti śabdarūpaṃ mukhavarṇabhraṃśe salayamiti niṣpadyetetyarthaḥ | īdṛk evaṃ parābhūto yaḥko'pi vā bhagnaṃ mukhaṃ darśayati kiṃ ? janānāmiti śeṣaḥ | mukhabhaṅgaṃ prāptavānmānī kasyacidapi mukhaṃ na darśayatyeveti lokektiḥ | pakṣe īdṛk salayamiti niṣpannaṃ kisalayaṃ kisalayapadaṃ kartṛ, mukhaṃ ādibhāgavisthitamiti yāvat kiṃ ki ityākārakaṃ varṇaṃ bhagnaṃ darśayati cyutaṃ sūcayati | kisalayapadaṃ salayamiti śrūyetacet asya mukhe varṇaṃ srastamiti sūcayatīti bhāvaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | atra sāmānyamaprastutaṃ samarthakam ||
yathāvā
pulinamapuṃbhāvaṃ gatamamba bhavatyā nitambato vijitam | kathamapi sadagrabhavanaṃ pādadhutaṃ kiṃ pragalbhatāṃ klībam || 1625 ||
he amba! pulinaṃ apuṃbhavaṃ gataṃ svarūpata evāpuṃstvamāpannaṃ ‘toyotthitaṃ tatpulinam' iti klībatvānuśāsanāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe apuṃ puvarṇarahitaṃ bhāvaṃ sthitiṃ gataṃ linamityavaśiṣṭamityarthaḥ | ata eva bhavatyāḥ nitambataḥ puṃstvabhāja iti bhāvaḥ ‘paścānnitambaḥ' iti pulliṅgatānuśāsanāttasya | vijitaṃ atha kathamapi agre nitambasya samakṣaṃ bhavanaṃ sthitaḥ yasya tathābhūtaṃ sat, pakṣe agrabhavaḥ ādibhāgasthitiḥ naḥ nakāraḥ yasya tattathoktaṃ nalinaṃ saditi yāvat | tadā'pi klībatvamatyajadeveti bhāvaḥ | pādena puṃstvabhājeti bhāvaḥ | dhutaṃ kṣiptam | pulinabhāve nitambena nalinabhāve pādena ca tiraskṛtamiti nirgalito'rthaḥ | kuta evamityata āha  kimiti | klībaṃ napuṃsakam | kiṃ pragalbhatāṃ kimiti pumāniva prāgalbhyaṃ labheteti bhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
gotrapravare bhavanaṃ yavayorekatra mādhava tathā'pi | āsīdvivāhayogassvairāḥ kva nu vidhiniṣedhavaśagāssyuḥ || 1626 ||
he mādhava! idaṃ lakṣmīpatitvena saṃbodhanaṃ vivakṣitārthopaṣṭambhāya | lakṣmīśca tvaṃ ca yuvāṃ tayoḥ yuvayoḥ "tyadādīni sarvairnityam, tyadāditaśśeṣe punnapuṃsakato liṅgavacanāni" iti pulliṅgayuṣmacchabdaikaśeṣaḥ | ekatra ekasmin gotrapravare gotraṃ ca pravaraśca anayossamāhāraḥ gotrapravaraṃ tasmin smṛtiprasiddhe ekasminneva gotre pravare ceyarthaḥ | pakṣe gotrapravare giriśreṣṭhe śeṣādrāvityarthaḥ | bhavanaṃ janma gṛhaṃ ca bhavati | tathā'pi yuvayoḥ vivāhayogaḥ pariṇayarūpasaṃbandhaḥ | tathā'pītyanena
"samānapravarāṃ kanyāmekagotrāmathāpi vā |
vivāhayati yo mūḍhastasya vakṣyāmi niṣkṛtim" ||
"asagotrāmasamānapravarāṃ vindeta" ityādiśātātapaviṣṇuprabhṛtidharmaśāstravacanavirodhassūcitaḥ | pakṣe viḥ pakṣī garutmān sa eva vāhaḥ vāhanaṃ tasya yogaḥ ‘ajitākarṣaṇī nītirgaruḍā garuḍāsanā' iti, ‘vāhanaṃ vedātmā vihageśvaraḥ' ityādibhirlakṣmyā api tadvāhanātvokteriti bhāvaḥ | kuta evaṃ śāstraniṣiddhe pravṛttiratyatrāha  svairā iti | svairāḥ svecchācāriṇaḥ kvanu vidhiniṣedhavaśagāḥ syuḥ na bhavantyeveti bhāvaḥ 'aprameyo'niyojyaśca yatra kāmagamo vaśī' ityuktarītyā bhagavatassvairatvamiti hṛdayam ||
yathāvā
kacavijito'vadhvastaśśaivala iha śailatāṃ prapadya punaḥ | kucavijito'bhūllakṣmyāśśriyā jitaḥ kvanu jayāya kalpeta || 1627 ||
śaivalaḥ śaivālaṃ avadhvastaḥ avacūrṇitaḥ ‘avadhvasto'vacūrṇitaḥ' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe vakāreṇa dhvasta iti vā vakāro dhvasto yasmāditi vā vadhvastaḥ | śailatāṃ giritāṃ śailaśabdatāṃ ca prapadya | spaṣṭamanyat ||
yathāvā
devi prasūrna hi tvatpuṣṭā mlāyanti sumanasastāpaiḥ | mlāyanti tarugatāstu kvanu na mlāniḥ
prasūnatābhājām || 1628 ||
he prasūḥ he janani! sumanasaḥ vipaścitaḥ kusumāni ca | tāpaiḥ ādhyātmikādibhiḥ ravikarādijanyaiśca saṃtāpaiḥ | prasūnatā prasvā jananyā ūnatā hīnatvaṃ pakṣe kusumatvaṃ tāṃ bhajantīti tathoktāḥ | sugamamitarat ||
yathāvā
makarandassvakadalane'pyanujjhitasvārtha eva tava vacasaḥ | sakhyamupainna hi madhurā vairāyante nijāsuhṛdbhyo'pi || 1629 ||
he devi! makarandaḥ puṣparasaḥ makarandaśabdaśca | tava vacasaḥ hetubhūtādvacanāt svasya kaṃ svakaṃ tasya dalane'pi mastakaphālane'pīti lokoktiḥ | pakṣe svasya kaḥ kavarṇaḥ tasya dalane bhraṃśe satyapi anujjhitaḥ svaḥ nijaḥ arthaḥ prayojanaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ | svaprayojanaikasādhanadṛṣṭiḥ | pakṣe anujjhitasvārthaḥ aparityaktasvābhidheyaḥ makarandaśabdaḥ kavarṇabhraṃśe'pi maranda iti
niṣpannatayā aparityaktasvābhidheya eva | makarandamarandaśabdayostulyārthakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | vacasa ityetanmadhyamaṇinyāyenottaratrāpyanveti | tava vacasaḥ vacanasya sakhyaṃ sauhārdaṃ upait prāpat | svaśirovidalanahetubhūte'pi kuta eṣa na pratyakārṣīdityatrāha  madhurā iti | madhurāḥ nisargata eva priyaśīlāḥ svāduśīlāśca ‘svādupriyau tu madhurau' ityamaraḥ | nijāsuhṛdbhyo'pi svaprāṇahāribhyo'pi na vairāyante tānuddiśya vairaṃ na kurvantītyarthaḥ | ‘śabdavaira' ityādinā kyaṅ ‘tasmai kaḥ prathamāya māniṣu mahāvīrāya vairāyate' ityatreva ‘krudhadruha' ityādinā caturthī ||
yathāvā
pātālaṃ harimahila'pārahitaṃ sakhyametya tāvakanābheḥ | stanasakhyamapyagātkvanu sarvaguru sthitimayatsatāṃ na suhṛtsyāt || 1630 ||
he harimahile! pātālaṃ kartṛ | tava nābhessakhyametya tattulyaṃ bhūtveti hṛdayam | apāraṃ yathā syāttathā hitaṃ āptaṃ sat pakṣe pārahitamiti cchedaḥ pāvarṇavidhuraṃ sat stanayossakhyamapyagāt tālaṃ tālaphalaṃ sat kucataulyamapyalabhateti bhāvaḥ | śabdārthatādātmyaṃ na vismartavyam | tathā hi  sarvaguru sarveṣāmapi pūjyaṃ satāṃ mahatāṃ sthitiṃ maryādāṃ ayat prāpnuvat vastu kvanu kasminvastuni suhṛt na syāt īdṛśaṃ sarvasuhṛdeva bhavediti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe sarve varṇāḥ guravaḥ yasmin tattathoktaṃ satāṃ tākārasahitāṃ sthitiṃ avasthānaṃ prāptaṃ, pātālamiti padaṃ gurubhūtasarvavarṇaṃ tākārasahitaṃ ceti tathoktiḥ ||
