evaṃ sādharmyavaidharmyābhyāṃ viśeṣasya sāmānyena samarthane tayoḥ prastutatvāprastutatvavailakṣaṇyamanādṛtya samuditānyevodāharaṇāni yadyapi pradarśitāni | tathā'pi atra prathamaprabhedasya ‘syālo hareritīnduḥ' ityādiṣūdāharaṇeṣu prastutasya viśeṣasyāprastutena sāmānyena samarthanaṃ ‘tvayyatyādaravāṃścet | tvatpadarucividveṣāt' ityudāharaṇayoḥ prastutena sāmānyenāprastutasya viśeṣasya samarthanaṃ
dvitīyaprabhedasya tu ‘saṃkalpādeva jagat' ityādyudāharaṇeṣu prastutasya viśeṣasyāprastutena sāmānyena ‘bhramaro'pi tava padābje, ityādyudāharaṇacatuṣṭaye ‘kacavijito'vadhvastaḥ' ityādyudāharaṇe ca apra
stutaviśeṣasyaiva prastutena sāmānyena samarthanamiti vailakṣaṇyaṃ bodhyam ||
sāmānyasya viśeṣeṇa sādharmyeṇa samarthanaṃ yathā
tvādṛśasadīśvarajuṣāṃ svaguṇāśśrīnātha saṃprakāśeran | candrāśritasya sāndraśśyāmalimā bhāti kṛṣṇasārasya || 1631 ||
san praśastaḥ īśvaraḥ svāmī | satāṃ nakṣatrāṇāṃ īśvara iti ca tajjuṣām | atra pūrvārdhoktasya sāmānyasya prakṛtasyāprakṛtenottarārdhoktena viśeṣeṇa sādharmyeṇa samarthanam ||
yathāvā
niravadhikā dhanatṛṣṇā sphurati hi niratiśayasaṃpadāmeva | natajanadhanamādatte vitataṃ yasmādramānivāso'pi || 1632 ||
atra pūrvārdhapratipāditasya sāmānyasya prakṛtasya uttarārdhapratipāditena viśeṣeṇa prakṛtena sādharmyeṇa samarthanamiti pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
niyataṃ jagadīśena nyakkriyate jihmago dharābhṛdapi | adharīkṛtassarīsṛpagirīśvaro yadrameśvareṇāyam || 1633 ||
atrāpi pūrvavadevāprakṛtasya sāmānyasya prakṛtena viśeṣeṇa samarthanaṃ, śleṣasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
ṛddhimatāmevāśā mahatī yannandamandire nivasan | pītāmbaro'pi bhagavān bata pāṭaccaradaśāmupādatta || 1634 ||
paṭaccarasyeyaṃ pāṭaccarī tāṃ jīrṇavastrasaṃbandhinīṃ daśāṃ añcalaṃ, pakṣe pāṭaccarasya corasya daśāṃ avasthāṃ upādatta dadhinavanītādicauryalīlāyā darśitatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ‘paṭaccaraṃ jīrṇavastraṃṃ | pratirodhiparāṃskandipāṭaccaramalimlucāḥ' iti cāmaraḥ | ‘vartyavasthāṃśukānteṣu daśā' iti ratnamālā ||
sāmānyasya viśeṣeṇa vaidharmyeṇa samarthanaṃ yathā
śaraṇagamīśa bhavadṛkkaruṇātmā na tyajennihīnamapi | vinidhāyotsaṅge sve vidhuḥ kuraṅgaṃ śritaṃ hi lālayati || 1635 ||
atra pūrvārdhodīritasya sāmānyasya prakṛtasyāprakṛtena viśeṣeṇa vaidharmyeṇa samarthanam ||
yathāvā
yo hi nisargācchreyān sa jagati mūrdhanyatāṃ svayaṃ bhajate | kamiha puraskurvaṃstvaṃ mūrdhanyākārabhāgbhavasi kṛṣṇa || 1636 ||
he kṛṣṇa! kaṃ puraskurvan pūjayan āśrayannityarthaḥ | mūrdhanyaḥ sakalabhuvanaśiromānyaḥ yaḥ ākāraḥ svarūpaṃ taṃ bhajatīti tathokto bhavasi | na kasyacidapi puraskāreṇa tvaṃ mūrdhanyākārabhāk kiṃtu svabhāvata eveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe he kṛṣṇeti kṛṣṇaśabdasyaiva saṃbodhanam | tvaṃ kaṃ kavarṇaṃ puraskurvan mukhataḥ kuvan mūrdhanyāḥ mūrdhasthānakāḥ ṛkāraṣakāraṇakārāḥ ‘ṛ ṭu raṣāṇāṃ mūrdhā' ityanuśāsanāt | te ca akāraśca mūrdhanyākārāḥ tān bhajatīti tathoktaḥ bhavasi saṃbuddhyantakṛṣṇaśabdasya kakāraṛkāraṣakāraṇakārākārabhāktvāttathoktiḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva | śleṣacamatkārastu viśeṣaḥ ||
samarthyasamarthakayorubhayorapi prakṛtatve'pyayaṃ dṛśyate | yathā
durlabhamiha bhavitā śrīvallabha kimu me tvadanugṛhītasya | tvatkṛpayā yaddarvīkaro'pi bhūbhṛdvareṇya evāsīt || 1637 ||
darvīkaraḥ śeṣaḥ sūdaśca | bhūbhṛdvareṇyaḥ girirājaḥ rājamukhyaśca | atra samarthyasamarthakayordvayorapi prakṛtatvam | evamagre'pi ||
yathāvā
rakṣeriti varaṇakṛto'cyuta vidadhadvaṃśameva mama mukhyam | taṃ śaraṇakṛtaṃ racayerna hīyate jātvapi tvadīyajanaḥ || 1638 ||
he acyuta! rakṣeriti gopāyitā bhaveriti varaṇakṛtaḥ tvāṃ vṛtavataḥ mama vaṃśaṃ kulameva mukhyaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vidadhatsan ‘lakṣaṇahe
tvoḥ' iti hetvarthe śatā | taṃ vaṃśaṃ śaraṇakṛtaṃ bharanyasanarūpopāyānuṣṭhātāraṃ racayeḥ kuryā eva | madvaṃśasyaiva śraiṣṭhyavidhāne pravṛttastvaṃ taṃ prapannaṃ kathaṃ na racayeriti bhāvaḥ | tvadīyajanaḥ tvadanugṛhīto janaḥ jātvapi na hīyate | ‘kaunteya pratijānīhi na me bhaktaḥ praṇaśyati' iti gānāditi bhāvaḥ | atrārthāntaraṃ varaṇakṛtaḥ varaṇakṛcchabdasya mukhyaṃ mukhe bhavaṃ vaṃ vakāraṃ śaṃ śakāraṃ vidadhatsan te varaṇakṛcchabdaṃ śaraṇakṛtaṃ śaraṇakṛcchabdaṃ racayeḥ | varaṇakṛcchabdasyādimavakārasthāne śakāravinyāse śaraṇakṛditi niṣpatteriti ||
yathāvā
tava kṛpayā rāma purā valīmukho'pi ca caturmukho bhavati | paṅkajapalāśalocana kiṃkiṃ na karoti tava kṛpā svairā || 1639 ||
he rāma! valīmukhaḥ kapirapi hanūmāniti bhāvaḥ | tava kṛpayā rcatumukhaḥ purā bhavati bhaviṣyati | purāśabdayogena ‘yāvatpurā | iti bhaviṣyatyarthe laṭ | spaṣṭamanyat ||
yathāvā
tvamiva śrīvallabha te śrīvatsaśśrīnivāsatāmayate | sa śrīsadhryaṅ bhavati hi yastava hṛdyassulakṣaṇatvamiyāt || 1640 ||
he śrīvallabha ! te tava śrīvatsaḥ tannāmā lāñchanaviśeṣaḥ tvamiva śrīnivāsatāṃ śrīniketanatvaṃ ayate ‘śrīdaśśrīśaśśrīnivāsaḥ |
brahmaṇi śrīnivāse' iti bhavata iva tasyāpi śrīnivāsatvaprasiddheḥ | tathāca tanmantravarṇaḥ ‘śrīvatsāya śrīnivāsāya namaḥ' iti | lakṣmīnivāsasthānatvādeva hi tasya śrīnivāsatvavyavahāraḥ | yaḥ padārthaḥ tava hṛdyaḥ hṛdayapriyaḥ vakṣasi bhavaśca | sulakṣaṇatvaṃ śobhanāni lakṣaṇāni ‘śaṅkhacakrordhvapuṇḍrādicihnaiḥ priyatamairhareḥ' ityuktāni cihnāni yasya sa tathoktaḥ | pakṣe śobhanaṃ ca tallakṣaṇaṃ ca tattvaṃ iyāt | saḥ sahāñcatīti sadhryaṅ | śriyassadhryaj śrīsahacārī bhavati | bhagavanmanaḥpriyaśśubhalakṣaṇaśca cetanarūpaḥ padārthaḥ aparityaktajñānaiśvaryādisamṛddhirbhavatīti bhagavadvakṣasyavasthitaścihnarūpaḥ padārthaḥ nityaṃ śriyā sahaiva vasatīti ca nigalito'rthaḥ ||
tayoraprakṛtayorapyayaṃ dṛśyate | yathā
śubhakṛcchreyo vindettvadvaca iva saralasāratāmāptum | taptvā'dhaśśīrṣaṃ yadrasālarasa eva saralasāro'bhūt || 1641 ||
śubhakṛt śubhakārī śreyaḥ vindet | ‘śubhakṛcchubhamāpnoti' ityukteriti bhāvaḥ | rasālarasaḥ ikṣurasaḥ mākandaraso vā | tvadvaca iva saralasāratāṃ udārarasatāṃ āptuṃ adhaśśīrṣaṃ taptvā saralasāra eva abhūt | rasālarasaśabdasya vilomatayā paṭhane tathā niṣpatteriti bhāvaḥ | atra samarthyasamarthakayordvayorapyaprakṛtatvam ||
asminnalaṃkāre samarthyasamarthakabhāva ārthaśśabdaścālaṃkāratāprayojakaḥ | na tu kāvyaliṅge hetuhetumadbhāva ivārtha eva | hi yat yasmāt ityādeḥ pratipādakasyābhāve ārthaḥ | sa ca ‘asamartho yaḥ kāryeṣvapekṣate sa paramanyasāhāyyam' ityādau | tatsattve śābdaḥ | so'pi ‘na viśati hi mahodāraḥ |
tvatkṛpayā yaddarvīkaraḥ' ityādau darśitaḥ | atra vaktavyamanyatsarvamudāharaṇālaṃkāranirūpaṇāvasare darśitamiti nātra pratāyate | atha hi pratijñāhetvavayavayoriva ākāṅkṣākramaprāptaṃ samarthyasamarthakavākyayoḥ paurvāparyamiti na mantavyam | na hyatra samarthakaṃ samarthyānupapattyunmīlitāyāmākāṅkṣāyāṃ satyāmevābhidhīyata ityasti niyamaḥ | anupapatterabhāve'pi pratītivaiśadyārthaṃ kavibhistasyābhidhānāt | evaṃ kāvyaliṅge'pi | evaṃ ca tayorvaiparītye'pi na doṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
saridadhipe śikhariṇi vā sadṛśaṃ varṣati ghanāghanassalilam | avadhīritanimnonnatavibhāgamacyuta bhavādṛśaudāryam || 1642 ||
atra pūrvārdhagata eva viśeṣaḥ uttarārdhagate samānye prastute samarthakaḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre arthāntaranyāsasarastriṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<14>
atha vikasvarālaṃkārasaraḥ
sāmānyena viśeṣasya kriyate yatsamarthanam |
punastasya viśeṣeṇa sa vikasvara īryate ||
kasyacidviśeṣasya samarthanaṃ sāmānyena vidhāya tatprasiddhāvaparituṣyatā kavinā punarviśeṣeṇa tatsamarthanamupamānarītyā arthāntaranyāsavidhayā vā kriyate sa vikasvaro nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
tvatkṛpayā yaddarvīkaraḥ' ityādau darśitaḥ | atra vaktavyamanyatsarvamudāharaṇālaṃkāranirūpaṇāvasare darśitamiti nātra pratāyate | atha hi pratijñāhetvavayavayoriva ākāṅkṣākramaprāptaṃ samarthyasamarthakavākyayoḥ paurvāparyamiti na mantavyam | na hyatra samarthakaṃ samarthyānupapattyunmīlitāyāmākāṅkṣāyāṃ satyāmevābhidhīyata ityasti niyamaḥ | anupapatterabhāve'pi pratītivaiśadyārthaṃ kavibhistasyābhidhānāt | evaṃ kāvyaliṅge'pi | evaṃ ca tayorvaiparītye'pi na doṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
saridadhipe śikhariṇi vā sadṛśaṃ varṣati ghanāghanassalilam | avadhīritanimnonnatavibhāgamacyuta bhavādṛśaudāryam || 1642 ||
atra pūrvārdhagata eva viśeṣaḥ uttarārdhagate samānye prastute samarthakaḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre arthāntaranyāsasarastriṣaṣṭitamaḥ
atha vikasvarālaṃkārasaraḥ
sāmānyena viśeṣasya kriyate yatsamarthanam |
punastasya viśeṣeṇa sa vikasvara īryate ||
kasyacidviśeṣasya samarthanaṃ sāmānyena vidhāya tatprasiddhāvaparituṣyatā kavinā punarviśeṣeṇa tatsamarthanamupamānarītyā arthāntaranyāsavidhayā vā kriyate sa vikasvaro nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
nyastabharaṃ tvayi māmiha na stokaṃ vā'pi karma bādheta | kimapi vibādhitumīṣṭe na bhāgyavantaṃ yathā raviṃ dhvāntam || 1643 ||
kimapi vastu, idaṃ kartṛ | dhvāntaṃ tamaḥ, idamapi kartṛ | idamupamānarītyā viśeṣāntaranyasane udāharaṇam ||
arthāntaranyāsavidhayā yathā
satsvapi śikhariṣu bahuṣu śrīvāsa tvaṃ śrito'si vṛṣaśailam | na hi sarve bhāgyabhṛto miṣatsu vibudheṣvavāpa hi śrīstvām || 1644 ||
yathāvā
kṛpayā rājyaṃ prādāḥ kapaye mahatāṃ niraṅkuśā hi kṛpā | gopasyāpyacyuta tava jagadīśatvaṃ dadau hi jaladhisutā || 1645 ||
ayamalaṃkāraścandrālokānuyāyidīkṣitānurodhena darśitaḥ | jagannāthādayastu  prakṛtaviśeṣasya sāmānyena samarthanādeko'rthāntaranyāsaḥ | sāmānyasya viśeṣeṇa samarthanādapara iti bhedadvayasamāveśātmakatāyāḥ sphuṭatvāttatsaṃsṛṣṭyaiva gatārthatvānnālaṃkārāntarasvīkāra ucita ityāhuḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre vikasvarālaṃkārasaraścatuṣṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<15>
atha prauḍhoktisaraḥ
yadutkarṣānimittasya tannimittatvakalpanam |
prauḍhoktireṣā kathitā jayadevamukhairbudhaiḥ ||
kiṃcidutkarṣāhetubhūte vastuni taddhetutākalpanaṃ prauḍhoktirnnāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
sārasvatapūrodarasūrasamudbuddhasārasātyaruṇe | caraṇe subhagaṃkaraṇe smaraṇe stāṃ me hareḥ priyaṃkaraṇe || 1646 ||
atra sarasvatīpūragatāruṇimātiśayāhetostadudarasūrasamudbuddhatvasya taddhetutvakalpanam ||
yathāvā
śaradamṛtakarajyotsnācchuritābhrasaridvikāsikumudasitāḥ | muravairiyaśaḥpūrā haritā karpūralepamādadhate || 1647 ||
atrāpi kumudasitatvātiśayāhetośśaraccandrajyotsnācchuritatvāderhetutvakalpanam |
rasagaṅgādharakārastu "kasmiṃścidarthe kiṃciddharmakṛtātiśayapratipipādayiṣayā prasiddhataddharmavatā saṃsargasyodbhāvanaṃ prauḍhoktiḥ' iti asyālaṃkārasya lakṣaṇamāha | tadedamudāharaṇam indukaravyatikaritaiścandanagirigandhavāhasārāṃśaiḥ | jalanidhikanye manye jalaruhajanmā'sṛjattavāpāṅgān || 1648 ||
atrāpāṅgeṣu na kevalaṃ śaiśiryādayo malayānilakiśoramātraguṇā eva kaveḥ pratipipādayiṣitāḥ, kiṃtu sakalajagadujjīvayitṛtvādayo'nye'pīti malayānilasārāṃśeṣu candrakarasaṃsargo viśeṣaṇatayā'tiśayārthamupāttaḥ | utpādyotpādakabhāvaścātra na lokaprasiddhaḥ | kiṃtu kavipratibhāmātranibaddhaḥ | atra ca dharmiviśeṣasaṃsargādatiśayo dharmyantaragato yadi vyañjanāviṣayaḥ tadaivāyamalaṃkāraḥ | yadi vācyavṛttyā tattatprayuktatvenābhidhīyate tadā samālaṃkārasyaiva viṣayaḥ |
yathā
padme prādurabhūḥ kila padme padme bibharṣi pāṇibhyām | asi padmanābhahṛdayāvasatistvaṃ padmagandhiriti yuktam || 1649 ||
atra lakṣmyāssaurabhyātiśayastaddharmisaṃbandhaprayuktatvena varṇita iti sama evālaṃkāraḥ | evaṃ cāsya mate
‘ekonaviṃśasaṃkhyakapurāṇataḥ kiṃca ṣaṣṭhavedena | heyaguṇāstavabhagavan khyāpyante bhāvimātṛgarbhabhavaiḥ' ||
ityādāvekasya mithyātvasiddhyarthaṃ mithyābhūtavastvantarakalpanaṃ mithyādhyavasityalaṃkārāntaramiti na vaktavyaṃ, prauḍhoktyaiva gatārthatvāt | ‘kacāḥ kalindajātaritamālastomamecakāḥ' ityādau
prācīnakṛtaprauḍhoktyudāharaṇe yathā tamāleṣu śyāmalimātiśayārthaṃ śyāmalimādhikaraṇībhūtakālindīsaṃbandha udbhāvyate tathā heyaguṇeṣu mithyātvātiśayasiddhaye mithyātvādhikaraṇaikonaviṃśasaṃkhyākapurāṇādesaṃbandha ityasyāpi suvacatvāt | yadica mithyādhyavasitirevālaṃkārāntaraṃ syāt satyādhyavasitirapi tathā syādityāpādayatā rasagaṅgādharakṛtā
hariścandreṇa saṃjaptāḥ pragītā dharmasūnunā |
khelanti nigamotsaṅge mātargaṅge guṇāstava ||
ityādyudāhṛtya atra hariścandrayudhiṣṭhiranigamasaṃbandhādguṇānāṃ satyatvaṃ pratīyate | evaṃ
madhye sudhāsamudrasya sitāmayagṛhodare |
pūrṇenduviṣṭare deva sthātuṃ yogyāstavoktayaḥ ||
atrāpi sudhāsamudrādisaṃbandhāt uktiṣu mādhuryātiśayaḥ pratīyamānaḥ kasyālaṅkārasya gocaraḥ syāt | ato'laṃkārāntaraṃ syāt | mama tu prauḍhoktyaiva gatārthatetyuktam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre prauḍhoktisaraḥ pañcaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<16>
atha saṃbhāvanāsaraḥ
saṃbhāvanaṃ syādyadyevaṃ syādityūho'nyasiddhaye ||
yatra kiṃcittarkaṇamanyattarkaṇasādhanaṃ tatsaṃbhāvanam ||
yathā
mama koṭivadanatā syādyadi puruṣāyuṣamidaṃ ca koṭiguṇam | īśīya hare stotuṃ koṭitamāṃśaṃ tvadekaguṇabhūmnaḥ || 1650 ||
prācīnakṛtaprauḍhoktyudāharaṇe yathā tamāleṣu śyāmalimātiśayārthaṃ śyāmalimādhikaraṇībhūtakālindīsaṃbandha udbhāvyate tathā heyaguṇeṣu mithyātvātiśayasiddhaye mithyātvādhikaraṇaikonaviṃśasaṃkhyākapurāṇādesaṃbandha ityasyāpi suvacatvāt | yadica mithyādhyavasitirevālaṃkārāntaraṃ syāt satyādhyavasitirapi tathā syādityāpādayatā rasagaṅgādharakṛtā
hariścandreṇa saṃjaptāḥ pragītā dharmasūnunā |
khelanti nigamotsaṅge mātargaṅge guṇāstava ||
ityādyudāhṛtya atra hariścandrayudhiṣṭhiranigamasaṃbandhādguṇānāṃ satyatvaṃ pratīyate | evaṃ
madhye sudhāsamudrasya sitāmayagṛhodare |
pūrṇenduviṣṭare deva sthātuṃ yogyāstavoktayaḥ ||
atrāpi sudhāsamudrādisaṃbandhāt uktiṣu mādhuryātiśayaḥ pratīyamānaḥ kasyālaṅkārasya gocaraḥ syāt | ato'laṃkārāntaraṃ syāt | mama tu prauḍhoktyaiva gatārthatetyuktam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre prauḍhoktisaraḥ pañcaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
atha saṃbhāvanāsaraḥ
saṃbhāvanaṃ syādyadyevaṃ syādityūho'nyasiddhaye ||
yatra kiṃcittarkaṇamanyattarkaṇasādhanaṃ tatsaṃbhāvanam ||
yathā
mama koṭivadanatā syādyadi puruṣāyuṣamidaṃ ca koṭiguṇam | īśīya hare stotuṃ koṭitamāṃśaṃ tvadekaguṇabhūmnaḥ || 1650 ||
yathāvā
sitakiraṇakiraṇanikaravyatikarato drāvitastuṣāragiriḥ | ghanasārasāramasṛṇo yadi tulayennātha tava kṛpāsāram || 1651 ||
atrādye stotuḥ koṭivadanatvāditarkaṇaṃ bhagavadekaguṇamahimakoṭitamāṃśatarkaṇasādhanam | dvitīye tuṣāragireścandrakaradravībhūtatvāditarkaṇaṃ bhagavatkṛpāsāratulanārhatātarkaṇasādhanam ||
yathāvā
karmānuguṇaphalapradabhartranurodhaśramo'mba tava no cet | dhanidīnavibhāgakathādūramidaṃ śrīmadeva bhuvanaṃ syāt || 1652 ||
yathāvā
ahameva yadi vidhātā jātu bhaveyaṃ hare bhavatkṛpayā | khalavadanagahananipatanavisaṃsthulāṃ na racayeya nanu vāṇīm || 1653 ||
yathāvā
yadi tādṛśaśeṣatvaṃ sidhyenmama tarhi kathamapīśīya | lāvaṇyavibhavamacyuta tava vaktuṃ śrotumīkṣituṃ vā'pi || 1654 ||
tādṛśaśeṣatvamiti | śeṣatvaṃ tādṛśeti viśiṣato'yamabhiprāyaḥ  yadyapyadhunā'pi svābhāvikaṃ bhagavadananyārhaśeṣatvamasti tathā
'pi bhagavallāvaṇyavarṇanādipaṭutaraṃ sahasravadanatvarūpaṃ śeṣatvaṃ nāstīti | vaktuṃ dvisahasrarasanatvāditi bhāvaḥ | śrotumīkṣituṃ vā'pi dvisahasranayanatvāditi bhāvaḥ | sa hi cakṣuśśravāḥ ||
yathāvā
ujjhitvā bakavṛttiṃ nanu śiṣṭastattvavidbhavan stabakaḥ | sādhvagrabhavana eva syādyadi lakṣmyāstadā stanatvamiyāt || 1655 ||
stabakaḥ gucchaḥ stabakaśabdaśca | bakavṛttiṃ ‘adhodṛṣṭirnaikṛtikassvārthassādhanatatparaḥ, ityādyuktāṃ bakavṛttiṃ baketyārakavarṇadvayasthitiṃ ca | ujjhitvā tyaktvā śiṣṭaḥ ācāraśīlaḥ tattvavit tattvajñaśca bhavan | pakṣe śiṣṭaḥ urvaritaḥ yaḥ staḥ sta ityākārakavarṇasamudayo yasya tathābhūtassn ba ka varṇalope stakāramātrāvaśiṣṭa ityarthaḥ | sādhūnāṃ sāttvikānāṃ ramyavastūnāṃ vā agre bhavanaṃ sthitiḥ yasya sa tathokta eva | pakṣe sādhu yathā tathā agrabhavaḥ caramaśrūyamāṇaḥ naḥ nakāraḥ yasya sa tathokta eva syādyadi tadā lakṣmyāḥ stanatvaṃ iyāt stanajanma prāpnuyādityarthaḥ | stabakaśabdaḥ bakavarṇalopena avasāne navarṇaghaṭanena ca stana iti niṣpadyata iti bhāvaḥ | atra stabakasya bakavṛttiparityāgādipūrvakasādhvagrabhavanatvatarkaṇaṃ lakṣmīstanatvaprāptitarkaṇasādhanam | śleṣādikaṃ tu pūrvebhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
janani mama stutyā tvaṃ tanuyā yadi ca daramantarāmodam | siddhyeddāmodarapadamatha mama nāstyatra viśayaleśo'pi || 1656 ||
he janani! mama stutyā matkartṛkastavena daraṃ alpamapi antaḥ manasi āmodaṃ praharṣaṃ tanuyā yadi atha tadaiva mama dāmodarapadaṃ paramaṃ padaṃ siddhyet | pakṣe daraṃ daśca raśca anayossamāhāraḥ daraṃ antarā 'antarāntareṇayukte' iti dvitīyā | dakarārephayormadhye ityarthaḥ | āmodaṃ āmodaśabdaṃ tanuyā yadi dāmodara iti padaṃ śabdaḥ sidhyet ityarthaḥ | atra stoturyatkiñcicchrīhṛdayāmododayatarkaṇaṃ dāmodarapadasiddhitarkaṇasya sādhanaṃ śleṣasaṃkīrṇam | evamagre'pi ||
yathāvā
tvadudārakaravidhūtassurāgamo vītarāga eva bhavan | yadi sumatayā pariṇamettadā tu te'cyuta nakhena sadṛśassyāt || 1657 ||
he acyuta! surāgamaḥ kalpataruḥ tava udāreṇa kareṇa vidhūtaḥ ataeva vītarāga eva vidhūnanajanitadainyātiśayena virakta eva bhavan tapasvī bhavanniti yāvat | sumatayā śobhanā mā lakṣmīḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ sumaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ sumatā tayā śobhanalakṣmīviśiṣṭatvenetyarthaḥ | pariṇamedyadi jāyetacet | pakṣe puṣpatayā pariṇato bhavedyadītyarthaḥ | tadā tu te tava nakhena sadṛśassyāt | atra surāgamaśabdaḥ vītarāgaḥ vigatarāvarṇagavarṇaścet sumatayaiva pariṇaṃsyatītyartho'pi camatkārī | atra surāgamasya sumatāpariṇatitarkaṇaṃ nakhasādṛśyatarkaṇasādhanam ||
yathāvā
yadi jātu vijānīyācchrījāne tāvakīnamaudāryam | devatarussa tadātve nāsatyaṃ devadārutāṃ yāyāt || 1658 ||
devataruḥ acetana iti bhāvaḥ | jātu kadācit tāvakīnaṃ audāryaṃ janīyādyadi | cetanakāryasyācetane'saṃbhavādyadīti saṃbhāvanā | saḥ devataruḥ tadātve tatkāla eva devadārutāṃ bhadradārunāmakṣudrataruviśeṣatāṃ yāyāt yatrakvāpyaraṇye devadārutaruvadaudāryaprathāgandhadavīyastayā saṃkucito varteteti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe devataru devataruśabdaḥ tadātve takārasya dāvarṇatve takāramapanīya dākāre paṭhite iti yāvat | devadārutāṃ yāyāt devadāruśabdo bhavediti bhāvaḥ | atra devatarorbhagavadaudāryajñānasaṃbhāvanaṃ taddevadārutāsaṃbhāvanasādhanam ||
yathāvā
tvāṃ tattvena vṛṣācalakānta biḍaujā dviṣīta yadi jātu | sa bhavedeva gataujāssālo bhūtvā'ntato biḍālo'pi || 1659 ||
he vṛṣācalakānta! biḍaujāḥ indro'pi bhrātṛtvena vyapadeśyo'pi kimutānya iti bhāvaḥ | jātu tattvena vastutaḥ na tvāropitakopena tvāṃ dviṣīta yadi tadā tarhi biḍaujā gataujāssan bhagavadvidveṣamahimnā nistejāssan sālaḥ vṛkṣo bhūtvā sthāvarajanmalabdhvetyarthaḥ | antataḥ paryavasāne biḍālopi bhavet | sthāvarajanmanā vyapanītakiyatpāpastiryagjanmā'pi labheteti bhāvaḥ | bhagavadvidveṣaphalamīdṛgityabhiprāyaḥ | pakṣe biḍaujā iti śabdaḥ gatāḥ o jā ityākārakavarṇau yasya sa tathoktaḥ | antataḥ caramabhāge o jā ityākārakavarṇāpagame pariśiṣṭasya biḍetyākārakavarṇasya caramabhāge ityarthaḥ | sālaḥ āla ityākārakavarṇasamudayasahitasan biḍāla iti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atra viḍaujaso bhagava
dvidveṣasaṃbhāvanaṃ sālabiḍālatāsaṃbhāvanasādhanam | vicchittiviśeṣastu vyakta eva ||
yathāvā
hṛtvā'cyuta tava kāntiṃ kvacana grāvaṇi nilīnamasitābhram | darśayati kāntimattvaṃ yadi tarhi budhaiḥ kriyeta tadbhasma || 1660 ||
he acyuta! asitābhraṃ nīlajaladaḥ tava kāntiṃ hṛtvā kvacana kasmiṃcit grāvaṇi śaile | pakṣe prastare ‘grāvāṇau śailapāṣāṇau' ityamaraḥ | vilīnaṃ sat kāntimattvaṃ svasya dyutimattvaṃ pakṣe kaḥ kavarṇaḥ antimo yasya tattathoktaṃ tasya bhāvastattvam | asmin pakṣe antimattvamityatra takārasya ‘anaci ca' iti dvitvam | darśayati yadi tarhi budhaiḥ dharmaśāstrajaiḥ āyurvedajñaiśca tat abhrakaṃ bhasma kriyeta jagadīśvarapraśastavastuharaṇaduritāpanodanānurūpabhasmāntaprāyaścittagocarīkriyeteti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe bhasitaṃ kriyeta | kṛṣṇābhrakasyaiva bhasmaviṣaye jyāyastvasmaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāsitābhrasya kvacidgrāvaṇi kāntimatvaprakāśanatarkaṇaṃ bhasmīkaraṇatarkaṇaṃ prati sādhanam | camatkārātiśayastu pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
raghuvīra mahādevo mahāhavo yadi bhavettvayi kadācit | satyaṃ sa dehabhidayā vikṛtassyāttvaṃ hi dalitataddhanvā || 1661 ||
he raghuvīra! mahādevaḥ mṛtyuṃjayaḥ tvayi viṣaye kadācat mahāhavaḥ pravartitaprabalaraṇo yadi saḥ mahādevaḥ dehasya śarīrasya bhidayā
tvaccharakṛtavidāraṇena vikṛtaḥ syāt satyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ | tatra hetumāha  hi yasmāt tvaṃ dalitaṃ tasya śaṃbhoḥ dhanuḥ dhanva vā yena sa tathoktaḥ | tasmāt tvayā kṛtamahāraṇo'pi mahādevo dehavidāraṇena vikṛtassyādeveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe dehabhidayā dekārasya hakāreṇa bhedanena vikṛtassyāt | mahādevaśabdaḥ mahāhavaśabdatayā vikṛtassyādityarthaḥ | atra mahādevasya raghuvīraviṣayakamahāhavatvasaṃbhāvanaṃ taddehavidalanaprayojyavikāravattvasaṃbhāvanasādhanaṃ hetūpaṣṭambhena nibaddham ||
yathāvā
bhagavanmukuraḥ kusṛtessyāccettvadgaṇḍamaṇḍalajigīṣuḥ | śakalībhavetsa nūnaṃ tvaṃ muradalana iti sa kila nājñāsīt || 1662 ||
he bhagavan! mukuraḥ kusṛteḥ śāṭhyāt kutsitā sṛtiḥ kusṛtiḥ kumārgaḥ ‘kusṛtirnnikṛtiśśāṭhyam' iti kapaṭaparyāyeṣvamaraḥ | pakṣe ku iti varṇasya sṛteḥ apasaraṇāt 'sṛ gatau' ityasmāddhātoḥ ktin | mukuro mura iti niṣpanna iti bhāvaḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyam | tvadgaṇḍamaṇḍalajigīṣussyāccet śakalībhavet | nūnaṃ nātra saṃdegdhavyamiti bhāvaḥ | athaivaṃ muratvaṃ prāpto'pi mukuraḥ murabhidastava vijigīṣāyāṃ kathaṃ pravṛtta ityata āha  tvamiti | saḥ mukuraḥ tvaṃ muradalana iti nājñāsītkila | nājānāditi saṃbhāvaye iti bhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
avikulamapyavikalamiha mukhyenāśeṣapālakena
yadā | dṛśyeta tadā satyaṃ bhavetkavikulaṃ vimṛśyatā samyak || 1663 ||
avikulaṃ meṣavṛndamapi atra aviśabdaḥ avisadṛśe lākṣaṇikaḥ | atimātramūrkhāṇāṃ samūho'pītyarthaḥ | mukhyena agauṇena aśeṣapālakena sarveśvareṇa bhagavatā avikalaṃ yathā syāttathā yadā dṛśyeta kaṭākṣaviṣayīkriyate | tadā kavikulaṃ kāvyakṛdvidvadvṛndaṃ bhavet satyaṃ nātra saṃdehaḥ | yadi saṃdehaḥ tarhi samyagvimṛśyatām | bhagavatprabhāvavimarśe ukto'rthassamyajñāyata eveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe avikulamiti padaṃ avikalaṃ akāreṇa vikalaṃ sat mukhyeneti vakṣyamāṇatvādādyenākāreṇeti labhyate | he aśeṣapāla sarveśvara bhagavan mukhyena ādyena kena kavarṇena yadā dṛśyate cyāvitākareṇa ādyakavarṇena ghaṭitaṃ dṛśyatecet tadā kavikulamityeva bhavedityarthaḥ | atrāvikulasya bhagavatkaṭākṣagocaratvatarkaṇaṃ kavikulatvatarkaṇasādhanamityādikaṃ draṣṭavyam |
yathāvā
tulanāyālaṃ vacasassvaṃ tava yadi nāma kokilo'veyāt | kiṃ chinnamasya jānankolaṃ taṃ janani buddhimān brūyāt || 1664 ||
he janani! kokilaḥ tava vacasaḥ tulanāya sāmyāya svaṃ ātmānaṃ alaṃ samarthaṃ yadināma aveyāt jānīyāt ‘namassvasti' ityādinā caturthī | asya atiśrāvyatayā pratyakṣasya tava vacasaḥ kiṃ chinnaṃ kiṃ nyūnaṃ bhavet | kokilasya svairaṃ tathā samarthaṃmanyatve
tvadvacaso'pratidvaṃdvirāmaṇīyakasya kā hānirbhavediti vidanniti bhāvaḥ | kaḥ buddhimān taṃ kokilaṃ alaṃ tvadvacanatulāparyāptaṃ brūyāt | kiṃca taṃ kolaṃ sūkaraṃ brūyādityapyarthaḥ | svaparaguṇatāratamyavimarśanākuśalo yadi kaścidapakṛṣṭaṃ svamutkṛṣṭasadharmāṇaṃ jānīyāt taṃ nipuṇamatissthūlamatiṃ sūkarasadṛśameva manyeteti bhāvaḥ ||
pakṣe asya kokilasya kiṃ kīrtivarṇaṃ chinnaṃ nirastaṃ jānannityarthaḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyam | buddhimān taṃ kokilaṃ kolaṃ brūyāt kokilaśabdasya kivarṇalope kola iti niṣpatteriti bhāvaḥ | atra yadināmetyādyūhanaṃ kolaṃ tamidyādyūhaṃprati sādhanam | camatkārātiśayastu vyakta eva ||
yathāvā
tava caraṇāruṇalakṣmīṃ hṛtvā'pi yadāryameva manute svam | sūryakarastarhi bhavetsūkara evāmba tatra ko viśayaḥ || 1665 ||
āryaṃ pūjyameva | pakṣe aryaṃ avidyamānaryavarṇamityarthaḥ | atra cauryakarturapi sūryakarasyāryatājñanasaṃbhāvanaṃ tasya sūkaratāsaṃbhāvanasādhanamiti lakṣaṇānugatiḥ ||
yathāvā
yadi tava vāgrasamīpsetsvādimavidhuiro'rasattvabhāṅmakarandaḥ | mālakṣyo'gre mādhava vindatu mākandatāmiyātsaphalatvam || 1666 ||
he mādhava! idaṃ vakṣyamāṇārthopaskārakam | svādimnā svādutayā rahitaḥ tatra hatuḥ  arasattvabhāgiti | avidyamānaḥ rasaḥ
madhurādyuttamarasaḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | tasya bhāvaḥ tattvaṃ kenāpi ślāghyena vidhuratayā abhogya iti bhāvaḥ ||
rasassvāde jale vīrye śṛṅgārādau viṣe drave |
bhoge roge dehadhātau tiktādau pārade'pica ||
iti hemaḥ | makarandaḥ uktiviśeṣaṇamahimnā kṣudratamayatkiṃcitpuṣparasa iti labhyate | tvadvācaḥ rasaṃ āsvādaṃ īpsedyadi agre uttarasmin kāle mālakṣyaḥ yādṛcchikādisukṛtaparipākavaśena lakṣmīkaṭākṣaviṣayassan mākandatāṃ sahakāraphalatāṃ ‘mākandassahakāre'pi' iti hemamedinyau | māṃ śriyaṃ kandati āhvayati svarakṣaṇārthamiti mākandaḥ | tattāṃ lakṣmīśiśutāmiti ca gamyate | kadidhātorāhvānārthakātkarmaṇyaṇ | vindatu | tadā saphalatvaṃ svābhīpsitatvadvāgrasarūpaphalasahitatvaṃ tadbhāktvamityarthaḥ | iyāt | īdṛśamākandajanmaparigrahe satyeva mādhavasya tava vāgrasaṃ vindeta | natūktamakarandarūpeṇa svātmaneti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe svasya ādiḥ ādyaḥ yaḥ maḥ makāraḥ tadvidhuraḥ | sato bhāvaḥ sattvaṃ rasya sattvaṃ rasattvaṃ tadbhajatīti rasattvabhāk | sa na bhavatītyarasasattvabhāk | makārarephaśūnya iti viśeṣaṇadvayābhiprāyaḥ | makarandaḥ makarandaśabdaḥ kanda iti niṣannassanniti bhāvaḥ | agre ādau māḥ māvarṇaḥ tena lakṣyaḥ dṛśyassan | mākanda iti niṣpannaḥ | saḥ phalatvaṃ iti chedaḥ | saḥ mākandaśabdaḥ phalatāṃ phalavācakatāmiti yāvat | iyāt | atra makarandasya mākandatvasaṃbhāvanaṃ bhagavadvāgrasalābharūpaphalavattāsaṃbhāvanasya sādhanam | śabdārthatādātmyabhittikaśleṣamūlakābhedādhyavasāyojjīvitamiti dhyeyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre saṃbhāvanasaraḥ ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<17>
atha mithyādhyavasitisaraḥ
mithyārtho'nyaḥ kalpatecetkiṃcinmithyātvasidvaye |
mithyādhyavasitirnāma sā'laṃkṛtirudāhṛtā ||
kasyacidarthasya mithyātvasiddhyarthaṃ mithyābhūtārthāntarakalpanaṃ mithyādhyavasitirnāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
gaṇanātiśāyighṛṇinā payojasuhṛdā trayodaśena śucīnām | santamasaṃ hanta hṛdabhyantaragaṃ tvajjuṣāṃ nirāsi murāre || 1667 ||
he murāre! gaṇanātiśāyino ghṛṇayaḥ kiraṇāḥ yasya tena tathoktena trayodaśena payojasuhṛdā sahasramātrasaṃkhyākakiraṇatayā dvātdaśātmatayā ca prathitādbhāsvato'nyena bhāskareṇetyarthaḥ | tvajjuṣāṃ
tvayi pratirūpāpannadhyānabhājāṃ nyāsavidyayā tvāṃ sevamānānāṃ vā ‘juṣī prītisevanayoḥ' ityasmātkvip | śucīnāṃ 'kṣetrajñasyeśvarajñapnādviśuddhiḥ paramā matā' ityādyuktasarvaprakāraviśuddhibhājāmisyarthaḥ | agnīnāmityapyupaskāryam | hṛdabhyantaragaṃ antaraṃ saṃtamasaṃ tamoguṇarūpaṃ mahattimiraṃ nirāsi nirasyata | atra bhagavatprapannaśucihṛttamaso'tyantāsaṃbhāvitasya niṣpattaye trayodaśabhāskarakartṛkanirāso'tyantāsaṃbhāvito nibaddhaḥ | idaṃ śleṣasaṃkīrṇam ||
yathāvā
atasī paribhūtā tava rucyā taptvā vaneṣvadhaśśīrṣam | ante saṃdhessītā syāccettvadrucimiyādraghukulendo || 1668 ||
he raghukulendo! idaṃ vivakṣitārthopaskārāya | atasī kṣumākusumaṃ ‘puṣpamūleṣu bahulam' iti luk | tava rucyā tviṣā paribhūtā satī vaneṣu adhaśśīrṣaṃ taptvā | pakṣe vaiparītyena sthitvetyarthaḥ | ante paryavasāne saṃdheḥ balavadvirodho'nucita iti saṃdhiṃ kṛtvetyarthaḥ | pakṣe saṃhitātvavivakṣāyā iti yāvat | sītā syāccet jānakītvaṃ prāptācet tvadruciṃ tava dyutiṃ tvayyanurāgaṃ ca iyāt, anyathā tasyāstvadrucirna sulabheti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe atasīśabdavyaktiḥ vaiparītyenāvasthitācet sīta a iti syāt, tatastakārākārayossavarṇadīrgharūpassaṃdhiścet sītā iti niṣpadyeta | sa eva durghaṭaḥ ‘ato guṇe' iti pararūpalakṣaṇaikādeśe savarṇadīrghāpravṛtteḥ | tathāca sīta ityadanta evāyaṃ śabdo bhavet | na tu sīteti dīrghānta iti bhāvaḥ | atrātasyāḥ śrīraghunandanaruciprāpterativelāsaṃbhāvitasya nirvṛttyarthaṃ tasyāssītātvaprāptirūpātivelāsaṃbhāvitasya nirvṛttyarthaṃ tasyāssītātvaprāptirūpātivelāsaṃbhāvitatvakalpanam | śleṣacamatkārāntarādikaṃ vyaktameva ||
yathāvā
aṣṭamajaladhau janitaṣṣaṣṭho nirjaratarurvṛṣādrimaṇe | tvatto'pi vadānya iti sraṣṭā ṣaṣṭhena vadati vadanena || 1669 ||
idaṃ śuddhamudāharaṇam | evaṃ prauḍhoktyalaṃkāranirūpaṇe prasaṅgādupadarśitaṃ ‘ekonaviṃśasaṃkhyakapurāṇataḥ' iti padyamapi śuddhamevāsyā udāharaṇam ||
candrāloke tṛtīye kāvyalakṣaṇamayūkhe kāvyalakṣaṇatvena yāni akṣarasaṃhatiśobhābhimānahetupratiṣedhaniruktimithyādhyavasāyasiddhiyuktikāryāṇi daśa nirūpitāni; tāni kuvalayānande camatkāritayā'laṃkārapade niveśitāni dīkṣitaiḥ | teṣvekatameyaṃ mithyādhyavasitiriti dhyeyam | tatraitallakṣaṇaṃ lakṣyaṃ caivaṃ dṛśyate
syānmithyādhyavasāyaścedasatī sādhyasādhane |
candrāṃśu sūtragrathitāṃ nabhaḥpuṣpasrajaṃ vaha ||
iti | vaktavyāntaraṃ tattadavasare sphuṭībhaviṣyati ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre mithyādhyavasitisarassaptaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<18>
atha lalitālaṃkārasaraḥ
dharmiṇi prastute varṇyavṛttāntollekhanaṃ vinā |
tatra tatpratibimbasya varṇanaṃ lalitaṃ matam ||
prastutadharmiṇi prastutavyavahārānullekhena tatraiva nirūpyamāṇaḥ prakṛtavyavahārasaṃbandho lalitālaṃkāraḥ | ‘dorbhyāmabdhiṃ titīrṣantastuṣṭuvuste guṇārṇavam' ityādinidarśanāvāraṇāya tṛtīyāntam | aprastutapraśaṃsāvāraṇaya prastutadharmiṇīti ||
idaṃ śuddhamudāharaṇam | evaṃ prauḍhoktyalaṃkāranirūpaṇe prasaṅgādupadarśitaṃ ‘ekonaviṃśasaṃkhyakapurāṇataḥ' iti padyamapi śuddhamevāsyā udāharaṇam ||
candrāloke tṛtīye kāvyalakṣaṇamayūkhe kāvyalakṣaṇatvena yāni akṣarasaṃhatiśobhābhimānahetupratiṣedhaniruktimithyādhyavasāyasiddhiyuktikāryāṇi daśa nirūpitāni; tāni kuvalayānande camatkāritayā'laṃkārapade niveśitāni dīkṣitaiḥ | teṣvekatameyaṃ mithyādhyavasitiriti dhyeyam | tatraitallakṣaṇaṃ lakṣyaṃ caivaṃ dṛśyate
syānmithyādhyavasāyaścedasatī sādhyasādhane |
candrāṃśu sūtragrathitāṃ nabhaḥpuṣpasrajaṃ vaha ||
iti | vaktavyāntaraṃ tattadavasare sphuṭībhaviṣyati ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre mithyādhyavasitisarassaptaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
atha lalitālaṃkārasaraḥ
dharmiṇi prastute varṇyavṛttāntollekhanaṃ vinā |
tatra tatpratibimbasya varṇanaṃ lalitaṃ matam ||
prastutadharmiṇi prastutavyavahārānullekhena tatraiva nirūpyamāṇaḥ prakṛtavyavahārasaṃbandho lalitālaṃkāraḥ | ‘dorbhyāmabdhiṃ titīrṣantastuṣṭuvuste guṇārṇavam' ityādinidarśanāvāraṇāya tṛtīyāntam |
aprastutapraśaṃsāvāraṇaya prastutadharmiṇīti ||
yathāvā
kvāhaṃ bhavamarupānthaḥ kva ca tava sāyujyanityasāmrājyam | ita evācyuta sahasā sumeruśṛṅgāgramabhavamutpitsuḥ || 1670 ||
atra bhavamarupāntha eva sannahaṃ durāsadatvadīyanityasāmrājyaprepsurasmīti prastutavyavahāraṃ viṣayaṃ kaṇṭharaveṇāpratipādyaiva tatpratibimbabhūtaḥ durārohaṃ sumeruśṛṅgāgramita ekotpātenaivādhiṣṭhātumicchāmīti viṣayyupanibaddhaḥ | viṣayopādāne tu nidarśanaiva ||
yathāvā
mādṛṅmitavacanaḥ kva tvādṛṅnigamāgaṇeyasuguṇaḥ kva | bhūmiṣṭha eva kalpakasumanicayaṃ nikhilamapacicīṣāmi || 1671 ||
atrāpi mitaṃpacamatirahaṃ nigamāparicchedyabhagavadanantakalyāṇaguṇavivakṣurasmīti viṣayamakathayitvaiva bhūmiṣṭha eva sakalakalpatarukusumānyapacicīṣāmīti viṣayyeva nibaddhaḥ ||
yathāvā
yadyapi nāhaṃ pātraṃ hṛdyatamāyā hare tava kṛpāyāḥ | unmaryāda udanvānutsarpati cedamuṃ nirundhyātkaḥ || 1672 ||
atrāpi tvaṃ nīce'pi mayi nirargalakṛpāprasaraścedaparyanuyojyasvātantryavaibhavaṃ tvāṃ ko nāma niroddhumīṣṭāmiti bimbabhūtavā
kyārthānullekhena unmaryāda ityādipratibimbavākyārtha evopanyastaḥ ||
yathāvā
mahatāṃ satāṃ kule māṃ vihitakṛpastvaṃ vyajījanaśśaure | nibusīkāryaṃ dhānyaṃ nibiḍe vāte'nuvātyeva || 1673 ||
nibiḍe sāndre vāte'nuvātyeva anukūlatayā bahamāna eva dhānyaṃ tuṣāviviktamiti bhāvaḥ | nibusīkāryaṃ nistuṣīkāryaṃ ‘kaḍaṃgaro busaṃ klībe dhānyatvaci tuṣaḥ pumān' ityamaraḥ | nigataṃ busaṃ yasya tannibusam | atra yādṛcchikādisukṛtavaśātsatkulaprasavāvasara eva paramapuruṣārthassādhanīya iti viṣayavākyārthamapratipādya tatpratibimbavākyārthamātramullikhitam ||
yathāvā
śrutipaṭhitī vā bhavatu smṛtipaṭhitī vā hariṃ na vindedyaḥ | na hi kukkurasya raṭato bhedassyātkukkuṭasyāpi || 1674 ||
yaḥ hariṃ na vindet saḥ śrutipaṭhitī smṛtipaṭhitī vā bhavatu; ubhayatrāpi paṭhitamaneneti vigrahe iṣṭādibhyaśceti kartari iniḥ tadyoge śrutismṛtiśabdayoḥ ‘ktasyenviṣayasya karmaṇyupasaṃkhyānam' iti karmaṇi saptamī | tatassaptamīsamāsaḥ, trayīṃ dharmaśāstraṃ vā paṭhitavān
syādityarthaḥ | kukkurasya śunaḥ kukkuṭasya ca raṭataḥ śrutikaṭhu dhvanataḥ bhedo na hi syāt | ubhayorapi nairarthakyasya taulyāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe kukkura kukkuṭaśabdayoḥ, raṭato bheda ityatra abheda iti chedaḥ ||
raṭābhyāmabhedo na kiṃtu bheda evetyarthaḥ | atra bhagavadbhajanahīnaśruti smṛtipaṭhanaṃ nirarthakamiti varṇyamanuktvā tatpratibimbameva nibaddham |
yathāvā
ke'pi bhajante tvaditaramitare tvāmeva deva sevante | kākāḥ kākā eva hyadharitaśiraso'pi kiṃ nu haṃsāssyuḥ || 1675 ||
kākāḥ vāyasāḥ adharitaśiraso'pi | pakṣe kākā iti śabdaḥ viparīto'pītyarthaḥ | atra devatāntaraṃ bhajatāṃ mahatāpi prayāsena bhagavadbhaktajanasādharmyaṃ na ghaṭata iti viṣayavākyārthamapradarśya tatpratibimbavākyārtha eva varṇitaḥ camatkārāntaraṃ tūdāharaṇadvaye'pi vyaktameva ||
na cātra sarvatrāpi bhede'pyabheda ityatiśayoktyā cāritārthyamiti vācyam | tatra hi padārthena padārthasyaivābhedādhyavasānam' ‘kanakalatāyāṃ virājate candraḥ' ityādau dṛṣṭam, na tu vyavahāreṇa vyavahārasyetyaviṣaya evāyamatiśayokteḥ | nāpi sādṛśyamūlayā aprastutapraśaṃsayā, dharmyaṃśe prastutatvavirahāt | nāpi nidarśanayā, ekadharmigatavvayavahāradvayopādāna eva tasyā iṣṭeḥ | prakṛte ca prakṛtavyavahārasyānupāttatvādalaṃkārāntarameva | evaṃca
kva sūryaprabhavo vaṃśaḥ kva cālpaviṣayā matiḥ |
titīrṣurdustaraṃ mohāduḍupenāsmi sāgaram ||
ityatra kāvyaprakāśikākāro yannidarśanāmudāhārṣīt tadasaṃgatameva, lalitasyāvaśyābhyupagamyatvāt | nidarśanāyā aprāpteśca| tadevaṃ lalitasyālaṃkārāntaratvamurarīkurvatāmāśayaḥ ||
anye tu lalitaṃ nālaṃkārāntaraṃ, nidarśanayaiva gatārthatvāt | nanvekadharmigataprastutāprastutavyavahāradvayopādānajīvitā nidarśanā
kathamaprastutavyavahāramātropādāne padaṃ nidhattāmiti cet, śrūyatāmāyuṣmatā  iha tāvadalaṃkārāḥ prāyeṇa śrautā ārthāśca saṃbhavanti | tatra śrautebhya ārthā na pṛthagalaṃkāratayā saṃkhyāyante kiṃtu pṛthagbhedataryā | tadalaṃkārasāmānyalakṣaṇena kroḍīkaraṇāt | idaṃ punarvākyārthānidarśanāsvarūpam  vyavahāradvayavaddharmyabhedapratipādanākṣipto vyavahāradvayābhadeḥ | tatra vyavahāradvayavaddharmyabhedapratipādanaṃ śrautamevāpekṣitamiti na niyamaḥ | kintu pratipādanamātram | tena ‘paravittaṃ haran martyo viṣamevāttyasaṃśayam' ityatra vyavahāradvayavaddharmiṇorabhedasya śrutyā pratipādana iva ‘dhikparasvaṃ tathā'pyeṣa viṣamatti sa durmatiḥ' ityatra ārthaprakṛtavyavahāravaddharmiśrautāprakṛtavyavahāravaddharmiṇorārthābhedasya pratipādane'pi vākyārthanidarśanātvamakṣatam | ekatra śrautītvaṃ aparatrārthītvamiti tu viśeṣo na vāryate | padārthanidarśanāsvarūpaṃ tūpamānopameyadharmayorabhedādhyavasāyamūla upameyopamānadharmasaṃbandha iti pṛthageva | etadubhayānyataratvaṃ ca prācīnarītyā sāmānyalakṣaṇam | yadi tu lalitaṃ pṛthagalaṃkārassyāt luptopamādirapyupamādeḥ pṛthak syāt, tvaduktayuktestulyatvāt | nanvatiśayoktirevaṃ sati rūpaka eva vilīyeta | viṣayaviṣayiṇordvayorapyupādāne śrautaṃ rūpakaṃ, viṣayamātropādāne tvārthamityasyāpi suvacatvāt, satyaṃ, yatra hyalaṃkāraśarīramubhayatrāpyavilakṣaṇaṃ tatraivālaṃkāravyapadeśo yuktaḥ | yathā sādṛśyaṃ niṣpādyamānamupamāśarīraṃ luptopamādiṣvavilakṣaṇameveti tatrāpyupamayaiva vyapadeśo nyāyyaḥ | nālaṃkārāntareṇa | luptatvapūrṇatvādistu na taccharīraniviṣṭa iti svayaṃ vyāvartamāno'pi nopamātvavyāva
rtakaḥ | tathā'nyatrāpīti sthitiḥ | evaṃca viṣayatāvacchedakarūpeṇa bhāte viṣaye viṣayitāvacchedakāvacchinnābhedasya rūpakaśarīrasya viṣayatāvacchedakarūpeṇābhāsamānaviṣayātmakādatiśayoktisvarūpādvilakṣaṇatvena dvayorekālaṃkāratvaṃ na yuktam | nidarśanālalitayostu svarūpāvailakṣaṇyaṃ pradarśitamevetyekālaṃkāratvamevetyāhuḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre lalitālaṃkārasaro'ṣṭaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
<19>
atha praharṣaṇālaṃkārasaraḥ
vinā yatnādabhīṣṭārthasiddhissyāccetpraharṣaṇam ||
abhīṣṭārthasya tadupāyasaṃpādanayatnena vinā siddhiḥ praharṣaṇaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
kṛṣṇā''śleṣarasāyanamādhyāyantī gṛhakriyāvyagrā | tvaritā skhalitā ca haṭhāduddharatā tena gopikā''śliṣṭā || 1676 ||
ādhyāyantī utkaṇṭhāpūrvakamāśāsānā, gṛhakriyāyāṃ vyagrā ata eva tvaritā śīghraṃ saṃcarantī ata eva skhalitā sskhalitapadā ca gopikā haṭhāt akasmāt uddharatā tena kṛṣṇena āśliṣṭā | atra yatnasāmānyaśūnyāyā api gopikāyā abhīṣṭasiddhiḥ ||
abhīpsitārthādadhikalābhaścāpi praharṣaṇam ||
rtakaḥ | tathā'nyatrāpīti sthitiḥ | evaṃca viṣayatāvacchedakarūpeṇa bhāte viṣaye viṣayitāvacchedakāvacchinnābhedasya rūpakaśarīrasya viṣayatāvacchedakarūpeṇābhāsamānaviṣayātmakādatiśayoktisvarūpādvilakṣaṇatvena dvayorekālaṃkāratvaṃ na yuktam | nidarśanālalitayostu svarūpāvailakṣaṇyaṃ pradarśitamevetyekālaṃkāratvamevetyāhuḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre lalitālaṃkārasaro'ṣṭaṣaṣṭitamaḥ
atha praharṣaṇālaṃkārasaraḥ
vinā yatnādabhīṣṭārthasiddhissyāccetpraharṣaṇam ||
abhīṣṭārthasya tadupāyasaṃpādanayatnena vinā siddhiḥ praharṣaṇaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
kṛṣṇā''śleṣarasāyanamādhyāyantī gṛhakriyāvyagrā | tvaritā skhalitā ca haṭhāduddharatā tena gopikā''śliṣṭā || 1676 ||
ādhyāyantī utkaṇṭhāpūrvakamāśāsānā, gṛhakriyāyāṃ vyagrā ata eva tvaritā śīghraṃ saṃcarantī ata eva skhalitā sskhalitapadā ca gopikā haṭhāt akasmāt uddharatā tena kṛṣṇena āśliṣṭā | atra yatnasāmānyaśūnyāyā api gopikāyā abhīṣṭasiddhiḥ ||
abhīpsitārthādadhikalābhaścāpi praharṣaṇam ||
icchāviṣayabhūtādarthādadhikārthasya tadgocarayatnaṃ vinaiva siddhirdvitīyaṃ praharṣaṇam ||
yathā
dṛṣṭā cennandabhuvā hṛṣṭā syāmiti cirādabhilaṣantī | lebhe gopakiśorī rahasi tadīyāṅkapālisarvasvam || 1677 ||
atra bhagavatkartṛkātmakarmakadarśanamātreṇa svaharṣaṃ saṃbhāvayamānāyā gopakiśoryāstato'pyativelātiśāyiparirambhasarvasvasāmrājyarūpādhikārthalābhaḥ ||
yathāvā
saṃsāracārajanitāṃ saṃsādayituṃ vihastatāṃ ye tvām | śrīnāthākalayante te nāma kṛtāstvayā caturhastāḥ || 1678 ||
vihastatāṃ viklavatāṃ ‘vihastavyākulau samau' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe vigatau hastau yasya saḥ vihastaḥ tasya bhāvam | yathā kathaṃciddhastavattāsaṃpādanāya bhagavantamupāsīnānāṃ janānāṃ caturhastatāvāptirūpādhikārthalābho vihastaśabdagataśleṣanirvyūḍha iti pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvadanugrahāttanīyaśśrīrvā syāmityupetya śaure tvām | bata bahutapanīyaśśrīrabhūttava sakhā tatastvayā'pacitaḥ || 1679 ||
he śaure! tava sakhākucela iti bhāvaḥ | tvadanugrahāt tanīyaśśrīrvā syām ‘yāvadbhriyeta jaṭharam' ityādyuktarītyā atyalpatarasaṃpadvā syāṃ na tu tadatiśayalipsuriti bhāvaḥ | iti tvāmupetya tataḥ anantaraṃ tvayā apacitaḥ pūjitassan ‘pūjā namasyā'pacitiḥ' ityamaraḥ | bahvī tapanīyaśrīḥ suvarṇasaṃpat yasya sa tathoktaḥ abhūt | bateti vismaye harṣe vā | kiṃca alpataratejā api vā bhaveyamityabhilaṣamāṇaḥ bahutapanīyaśśrīḥ atiśayitabhānusaṃbandhiśrīrabhūdityartho'pyupaskāryaḥ | pakṣe tanīyaśśrīśabdaḥ tvayā tataḥ takārādanantaramiti yāvat | pacita iti cchedaḥ | pakāreṇa citaḥ ghaṭitassan | bahu iti bhinnaṃ padam | bahu yathā syāttathā prāgvidyamānavarṇāpekṣayā'dhikaikavarṇatayeti bhāvaḥ | tapanīyaśrīriti śabdo'bhūt | atra kucelasyāpekṣitādadhikārthalābho varṇitaḥ ||
bhūvāsavattvamīpsostvadvijayātkosalendra vidviṣataḥ | svarvāsavattvamadadā bhavataḥ kiṃ brūmahe mahaudāryam || 1680 ||
bhūvāsavattvaṃ bhūdevendranvaṃ, svaḥ svarge vāsavattvaṃ mahendratvaṃ vāso'syāstīti vāsavān tasya bhāvaṃ ca | adadāḥ sarvānarātīn saṃhṛtavāniti vastusthitiḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
jagatāṃ janani bhavatyāstaulyaṃ prepsuḥ kacaśriyāmekaḥ | taptvā'dhaḥkṛtaśīrṣaṃ kathamapi labhate sma mecakaḥ kacatām || 1681 ||
he jagatāṃ janani! mecakaḥ candrakaḥ andhakāro vā ‘mecakaśśyāmale kṛṣṇe timire barhicandrake' iti hemaḥ | bhavatyāḥ kacaśriyāṃ keśabhāsāṃ taulyaṃ prepsuḥ; ata eva ekassn adhaḥkṛtaśīrṣaṃ taptvā ‘ekastapāṃsi kurvīta' ityuktervivikte eka eva tapastaptveti bhāvaḥ | kathamapi bhavatyāḥ kacatāṃ labhate sma | pakṣe mecakaśabdaḥ kacaśriyā ameka iti chedaḥ | bhavatyāḥ kacaśriyā saha taulyaṃ prepsuḥ; avidyamāno me iti varṇo yasya sa tathoktaḥ, ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā' iti kap | mevarṇarahitaḥ caka ityavaśiṣṭa iti bhāvaḥ | tataḥ vilomatāṃ prapannaḥ kacaśabdatāṃ labhate smetyarthaḥ | atra mecakasya vāñchitaśrīkacataulyāpekṣayā adhikasya tatkacatvasya lābho varṇitaḥ |śleṣādicamatkṛtiviśeṣastu pūrvavadeva |
yathāvā
yāvanmṛgayati kāmapi marupānthaśśrāntiśāntaye chāyām | tāvadakasmādgraiṣmaṃ pidadhe caṇḍāṃśubimbamambumucā || 1682 ||
marupāntha ityanena sarvadā chāyādaurlabhyaṃ sūcyate | kāmapi chāyāmityanena śākoṭakādyatikṣudratarupraviralacchāyāmityuktam | atrāprastutamarupānthakartṛkayatkiṃcicchāyānveṣaṇākasmikamahājaladharakartṛkagraiṣmacaṇḍāṃśubimbapidhānapraśaṃsā yāvadeva bhavapathagatāgatakhedaśamanakṛte yaṃkaṃciccharaṇaṃ paryālocayati cetanastāvadeva yādṛchickaprāsaṅgikādisukṛtavaśātpratyakṣatāmupagatavatā bhagavatā
tattāpaśāntirnnirvartiteti prastute'rthe paryavasyatītyaprastutapraśaṃsārū
pe'tra vākyārthe abhīpsitārthādadhikārthalābhaḥ spaṣṭa eva | ayameva hi pūrvebhyo viśeṣo yadaprastutapraśaṃsāsaṃkīrṇatvam ||
upāyasiddhaye yatne kriyamāṇe yadṛcchayā |
sākṣātphalasya lābho'pi praharṣaṇamitīritam ||
phalānavāptyā tatprāptaye tadupāyagocaro yatno loke prasiddhaḥ | tādṛśayatnādatarkitatatphalasyaiva lābho yat so'pi praharṣaṇamityarthaḥ | ayaṃ tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ ||
yathāvā
tvatsevanena saṃpadamīpsustvacchikharisaṃmukho manujaḥ | tāvatpaśyati sindhurabandhuramīśa svamandirālindam || 1683 ||
he īśeti saṃbodhanam | atra gajāntaiśvaryalābhopāyabhūtabhagavatsevanasiddhyarthāccheṣācalābhimukhaprasthānāt svamandirālinda eva tādṛśaiśvaryalābha iti tṛtīyaṃ praharṣaṇam | idaṃ ca kāryakāraṇapaurvāparyavyatikramarūpātiśayoktyuttambhitam ||
yathāvā
yamunātīranikuñjaṃ kuṣṇavaśīkāramūlavicicīṣuḥ | prāpya haṭhāttatrāgatamamumeva vaśīcakāra gopavadhūḥ || 1684 ||
atra bhagavadvaśīkaraṇopāyabhūtamūlikārthādyamunātīranikuñjagamanāttatraiva bhagavadvaśīkāralābhaḥ | evaṃca sākṣāttaduddeśyakayatnamaṃ
tareṇāpyabhīṣṭārthalābhaḥ praharṣaṇamiti praharṣaṇatritayasādhāraṇaṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇamiti dhyeyam ||
candrāloke tu ‘vāñchitārthādhikaprāptirayatnena praharṣaṇam' iti dvitīyaprabheda eva lakṣitaḥ | na tu prathamatṛtīyau | anye tu  prathamapraharṣaṇodāharaṇe iṣṭārthānukūlaprayatnābhāve'pīṣṭaprāptivarṇanena na praharṣaṇālaṃkāro'bhyupeyaḥ, prayatnasya kāryamātraṃ prati kāraṇatayā iṣṭārthatadupāyagocaraprayatnābhāve'pīṣṭārthasiddhyā kāraṇaṃ vinā kāryotpattirūpavibhāvanāyā eva viṣayatvāt | ataḥ praharṣaṇasya bhedadvayameveti | iyāṃstu viśeṣo yattatra kāraṇābhāvaśśābdaḥ iha tvārtha ityāhuḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre praharṣaṇasara ekonasaptatitamaḥ
<20>
atha viṣādanālaṃkārasaraḥ
yadabhīṣṭaviruddhārthalābhastatsyādviṣādanam ||
asya ca abhīṣṭārthalābhārthaṃ kāraṇaprayogo yatra na kṛtaḥ kevalamicchaiva kṛtā jātaśca viruddhārthalābhaḥ saḥ; yatra ca iṣṭārthaṃ prayukte'pi kāraṇe tasmānna viruddhārthalābhaḥ; apitu svakāraṇavaśāt sa ca vivikto viṣayaḥ | yatra tu iṣṭārthaṃ prayuktātkāraṇādeva viruddhārthalābhaḥ tatra tādṛśakāraṇaviruddhārthayorutpādyotpādakabhāvalakṣaṇasaṃsargasyānanurūpatvādviṣamam | iṣyamāṇaviruddhārthalābhācca viṣādanamiti saṃkīrṇataiva | evaṃ cāsya na viṣamabhedairgatārthateti
tareṇāpyabhīṣṭārthalābhaḥ praharṣaṇamiti praharṣaṇatritayasādhāraṇaṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇamiti dhyeyam ||
candrāloke tu ‘vāñchitārthādhikaprāptirayatnena praharṣaṇam' iti dvitīyaprabheda eva lakṣitaḥ | na tu prathamatṛtīyau | anye tu  prathamapraharṣaṇodāharaṇe iṣṭārthānukūlaprayatnābhāve'pīṣṭaprāptivarṇanena na praharṣaṇālaṃkāro'bhyupeyaḥ, prayatnasya kāryamātraṃ prati kāraṇatayā iṣṭārthatadupāyagocaraprayatnābhāve'pīṣṭārthasiddhyā kāraṇaṃ vinā kāryotpattirūpavibhāvanāyā eva viṣayatvāt | ataḥ praharṣaṇasya bhedadvayameveti | iyāṃstu viśeṣo yattatra kāraṇābhāvaśśābdaḥ iha tvārtha ityāhuḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre praharṣaṇasara ekonasaptatitamaḥ
atha viṣādanālaṃkārasaraḥ
yadabhīṣṭaviruddhārthalābhastatsyādviṣādanam ||
asya ca abhīṣṭārthalābhārthaṃ kāraṇaprayogo yatra na kṛtaḥ kevalamicchaiva kṛtā jātaśca viruddhārthalābhaḥ saḥ; yatra ca iṣṭārthaṃ prayukte'pi kāraṇe tasmānna viruddhārthalābhaḥ; apitu svakāraṇavaśāt sa ca vivikto viṣayaḥ | yatra tu iṣṭārthaṃ prayuktātkāraṇādeva viruddhārthalābhaḥ tatra tādṛśakāraṇaviruddhārthayorutpādyotpādakabhāvalakṣaṇasaṃsargasyānanurūpatvādviṣamam | iṣyamāṇaviruddhārthalābhācca
viṣādanamiti saṃkīrṇataiva | evaṃ cāsya na viṣamabhedairgatārthateti
mantavyam | viṣamāsaṃspṛṣṭasyāpyetadviṣayasyānupadameva darśayiṣyamāṇatvāt ||
yathāvā
bhātyevameva bhānurbhāryāsukhamevameva vindeyam | dhyāyatyevaṃ koke sāyaṃ hantāmbudhau mamajja raviḥ ||
atra hi nāsti viṣamaprabhedasya viṣayaḥ, iṣṭārthaṃ kāraṇaprayogābhāvāt, iṣṭārthaprayuktakāraṇena saha viruddhārthasya utpādyotpādakabhāvalakṣaṇasaṃsargasyānanurūpatvaṃ hi taccharīram | tasmādviṣādanamevātrāprastutapraśaṃsāghaṭakatayā'vasthitam | atra hyaprastutaścakravākavṛttāntaḥ atimātrānityamaihikavibhavaṃ nityatamaṃ pratyetya śaśvatatamaniśśreyasasāmrājyasādhanopekṣayā samayamativāhayati tāvadeva prāptatādṛśavibhavabhraṃśe kasmiṃścidadhanye paryavasyatītyaprastutapraśaṃsā ||
yathāvā
kṛtametadidaṃ kāryaṃ kṛtākṛtamidaṃ sukhāya bhavitā naḥ | iti vimṛśanneva janastvadvimukho hanta mṛtyunā hriyate || 1686 ||
atrābhīṣṭasukhavirūddhamṛtyuharaṇarūpārthalābhaḥ | atra
idaṃ kṛtamidaṃ sajjamidamanyatkṛtākṛtam |
evamīhāsamāyuktaṃ mṛtyurādāya gacchati ||
iti mahābhāratavacanārtho'nusaṃhitaḥ ||
dvitīyaprakāro yathā
upagūhati nandasute trapayā pihitānanā'pi
gopavadhūḥ | ślathabandhakañculīkā harṣocchūnastanadvayatayā''sīt || 1687 ||
atrābhīṣṭasyānanapidhānasya viruddho'rthaḥ kañculikābandhaśaithilvaṃ kāraṇībhūtatrapāpratyanīkatvāt, tacca harṣocchūnastanadvayatārūpasvakāraṇādevotpannaṃ na tu pidhānānukūlayatnāt | nāpīṣṭasādhanatvena prayuktātkāraṇādiṣṭānutpattiratrāsti, ānanapidhānarūpasyeṣṭasyotpatteḥ | ato viṣādanamevātra, na viṣamam | ayamapyalaṃkāro dīkṣitopajñameva ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre viṣādanasarassaptatitamaḥ
<21>
athollāsālaṃkārasaraḥ
yadanyaguṇadoṣābhyāmanyasya guṇadoṣayoḥ |
ādhānaṃ varṇyate prāhurullāsālaṃkṛtiṃ tu tām ||
tacca guṇena guṇasya doṣeṇa doṣasya guṇena doṣasya doṣeṇa guṇasya veti caturdhā | krameṇodāharaṇāni
api mayi patedapāṅgaḥ kṛpayā śiśiro'mbujākṣabhaktijuṣām | api dhanyassyāmahamiti sa pitāmaha eva vāñchati kimanyaḥ || 1688 ||
atra śrīnivāsabhaktirūpabhāgavataguṇena tadapāṅgasaṅgātpitāmahādīnāmapi dhanyatvaguṇādhānam | ādhānaṃ ca tadvattābuddhiḥ ||
gopavadhūḥ | ślathabandhakañculīkā harṣocchūnastanadvayatayā''sīt || 1687 ||
atrābhīṣṭasyānanapidhānasya viruddho'rthaḥ kañculikābandhaśaithilvaṃ kāraṇībhūtatrapāpratyanīkatvāt, tacca harṣocchūnastanadvayatārūpasvakāraṇādevotpannaṃ na tu pidhānānukūlayatnāt | nāpīṣṭasādhanatvena prayuktātkāraṇādiṣṭānutpattiratrāsti, ānanapidhānarūpasyeṣṭasyotpatteḥ | ato viṣādanamevātra, na viṣamam | ayamapyalaṃkāro dīkṣitopajñameva ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre viṣādanasarassaptatitamaḥ
athollāsālaṃkārasaraḥ
yadanyaguṇadoṣābhyāmanyasya guṇadoṣayoḥ |
ādhānaṃ varṇyate prāhurullāsālaṃkṛtiṃ tu tām ||
tacca guṇena guṇasya doṣeṇa doṣasya guṇena doṣasya doṣeṇa guṇasya veti caturdhā | krameṇodāharaṇāni
api mayi patedapāṅgaḥ kṛpayā śiśiro'mbujākṣabhaktijuṣām | api dhanyassyāmahamiti sa pitāmaha eva vāñchati kimanyaḥ || 1688 ||
atra śrīnivāsabhaktirūpabhāgavataguṇena tadapāṅgasaṅgātpitāmahādīnāmapi dhanyatvaguṇādhānam | ādhānaṃ ca tadvattābuddhiḥ ||
yathāvā
tvatkṛpayā'paribhāvyo lokasyāgrata udāritākāraḥ | sarvasyoparibhāvyo varvarti tvatpadāśrito bhagavan || 1689 ||
lokasya aparibhāvyaḥ atiraskāryaḥ agrataḥ udāritaḥ udārīkṛtaḥ ākāraḥ yasya saḥ uvarṇena dāritaḥ bhinnaḥ akāro yasya saḥ tathoktaḥ iti ca | sarvasya lokasya upari bhāvyaḥ śreṣṭhatayā mānanīyassan varvarti | pakṣe aparibhāvyaśabdaḥ ādau avarṇacyāvanena tatraiva uvarṇanyāsena ca uparibhāvya iti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atra bhagavatkṛpārūpaguṇena tatpadāśritasyāparibhāvyādiguṇādhānam | pūrvaṃ śuddhaṃ, idaṃ tu śleṣasaṃkīrṇamiti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
jalajantukule jātastvaddṛṣṭaścedyadṛcchayā jātu | hanta kulīro'pi naro netassyānnanu kulīna eva hare || 1690 ||
he netaḥ! sarveśvara | jalajantūnāṃ jaḍajantūnāṃ ca kule jātaḥ kulīraḥ karkaṭako'pi jātu yadā kadā vā yadṛcchayā na tvaidaṃparyeṇa tvayā dṛṣṭaścet yādṛcchikādisukṛtapariṇativaśāt kaṭākṣitaścedityarthaḥ | kulīnaḥ sadvaṃśya ityarthaḥ | ‘mahākulakulīnāryasabhyasajjanasādhavaḥ' ityamaraḥ | ‘kulātkhaḥ' iti khaḥ | nara eva mānuṣa eva syānnanu bhavedeva | hanteti bhagavatkaṭākṣaprabhāvaparicintanajanitaharṣe | pakṣe kulīraśabdaḥ naraḥ repharahitassan netaḥ tatraiva nakāreṇa itaḥ saṃyutaścet kulīna iti niṣpadyetetyartho'pi camatkārī | atrāpi bhagavatkaṭākṣaprabhāvarūpaguṇena kulīrasyāpi kulīnanaratvarūpaguṇādhānam | śleṣasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ camatkāraviśeṣaśca pūrvavadeva | saṃbhāvanāgarbhitatvaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvatto bahuprasādassārahitatvādbahupradaśca pumān | cāturvarṇyaślāghyo bhavetsvayaṃ janani dāntimattvamayan || 1691 ||
he janani! svayaṃ dāntimato bhāvaḥ dāntimattvaṃ dāntatāṃ ayan prāpnuvan | pakṣe dakāraḥ antimo yasya tasya bhāvaṃ ayan pumān puruṣaḥ idaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ bahuprasāda ityasya | tvattaḥ tvadanugrahāditi yāvat | bahuprasādaḥ tvatkṛpākṛtabahuprasādaviṣayassannityarthaḥ | sāraścāsau hitaśca sārahitaḥ tasya bhāvaṃ sārahitatvaṃ tasmāt arthināmuttamahitatvaṃ prāpyetyarthaḥ | lyablope pañcamī | yadvā tvattaḥ tvatkaṭākṣāditi yāvat | bahuḥ prasādaḥ prasannatā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ayaṃ hi ‘yaddātussaumyatā seyaṃ pūrṇendorakalaṅkatā' ityukto dāturmahān guṇaḥ, ata eva sārahitatvāt ukta evārthaḥ| bahupradaḥ sthūlalakṣo bhūtvā | pakṣe bahuprasādaśabdaḥ sārahitatvaṃ sāvarṇarāhityaṃ prāpya bahuprada iti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | ataeva cāturvarṇyena brāhmaṇādivarṇacatuṣṭayena ślāghyo bhavet | pakṣe pañcavarṇātmako bahuprasādaśabdassāvarṇarāhitye caturvarṇatvenaiva ślādhyo bhavedityarthaḥ | prāthamikārthapakṣe catvāro varṇāścāturvarṇyamiti vigrahaḥ ‘catuvarṇādīnāṃ svārthai' iti svārthe ṣyañ | dvitīyasmin pakṣe tu catvāro varṇā yasyeti bahuvrīhiḥ | tataḥ
'guṇavacanabrāhmaṇādibhyaśca' iti bhāve ṣyañ | atra bhagavatprasādaprabhāvaguṇena tadbhaktasya sārahitatvādiguṇādhānam | bhagavatkaṭākṣaprabhāvādbahuprasādatvādyādhānaṃ vā ||
yathāvā
durgativaśātpumānyo bahupramādo'pi tava dṛśāṃ viṣayaḥ | mānitatāṃ prāpya bhavedbahupradassa khalu kalaśajaladhisute || 1692 ||
he kalaśajaladhisute! durgativaśāt dāridryavaśāt bahupramādaḥ bahuvipadapi | pakṣe bahupramādaśabdo yaḥ pumān tava dṛśāṃ kaṭākṣāṇāṃ viṣayaḥ bhavet sa pumān puruṣaḥ pulliṅgo bahupramādaśabdaśca mānitatāṃ jagatpūjitatāṃ | pakṣe māvarṇena anitatāṃ ayuktatāṃ ca prāpya bahupradaḥ dānaśauṇḍaḥ bhavetkhalu | pakṣe bahuprada iti niṣpadyetetyarthaḥ | atra lakṣmīkaṭākṣaprasaraguṇena durgaterapi mahodāratādiguṇādhānam | ekasminnādhāre bahupramādatvabahupradatvarūpānekādheyavarṇanarūpaparyāyālaṃkāragarbhitatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ| śleṣādisaṃkīrṇatvaṃ tu pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
ādau vaiśvānugato'pyagryanaro yassvabhāvataḥ kaścit | dṛṣṭaḥ krameṇa bhavatā vauśvānara eva bhavati ko viśayaḥ || 1693 ||
he bhagavan! agryanaraḥ agrajanmā manujaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ vai iti prasiddhau | ādau śvābhiḥ anugataḥ śvānugataḥ "śvakrīḍī śyenajīvī
ca' ityādyuktaniṣiddhācāravān bhavannapi yaḥkaścit bhavatā krameṇa yādṛcchikādisukṛtaparipākakrameṇa tadvaśāditi yāvat | dṛṣṭaścet vaiśvānaraḥ agniḥ tadvacchucistejasvī ca bhavati, pakṣe ādau vaiśvā iti varṇābhyāmanugataḥ agryau narau nakārarephau yasya sa tathoktaḥ | yaḥkaścicchabdaḥ krameṇa uktavarṇānupūrvyeṇa dṛṣṭaścet vaiśvānara eva bha ti nānyaśabda ityarthaḥ | atrāpi bhagavanmahimātiśayaguṇena taddṛṣṭivaśādaśucerapi śucitvaguṇādhānam | ekasminnādhāre aśucitvaśucitvarūpānekādheyavarṇanātmakaparyāyeṇa saṃbhāvanena ca saṃkīrṇatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
ajahatsvāmin prakṛtiṃ sadmāntassthānabhāgyato bhavataḥ | api pakṣī padmākṣībhaviṣyati tataḥ kimatra vaktavyam || 1694 ||
he svāmin! prakṛtiṃ svasvabhāvaṃ ajahadeva svaprakṛtyanuguṇaṃ ceṣṭamāna eva na tūpāsanādi kurvanniti bhāvaḥ | bhagavataḥ sadmanaḥ divyālayasya antaḥ sthānaṃ sthitiḥ tadrūpaṃ yat bhāgyaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ tasmāt sadmāntassthānabhāgyataḥ sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | pakṣyapi vihago'pi tiryagjanturapīti bhāvaḥ | padmākṣo bhagavān apadmākṣaḥ padmākṣassaṃpatsyate padmākṣībhaviṣyati | cviḥ | upāyābhimukhyagandhasyāpi davīyān pakṣimātro'pi bhavaddivyālayavāsabhāgyavaśāt 'brahma veda brahmaiva bhavati' ityuktarītyā brahmavidiva bhagavatsārūpyayogyo bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | tataḥ tadapekṣayā vaktavyaṃ kimasti kathanīyaṃ tanmāhātmyaṃ tato'pi nānyadavaśiṣyata iti bhāvaḥ ||
pakṣe svāṃ inprakṛtiṃ iti chedaḥ | svāṃ svakīyāṃ inprakṛtiṃ matvarthīyena inpratyayena yuktāṃ prakṛtiṃpakṣīti prakṛtiṃ ajahadeva | inpratyayāntaprakṛtikatvāttasyeti bhāvaḥ | pakṣī pakṣītiśabdaḥ sadmāntasthānabhāk yataḥ iti chedaḥ | yataḥ yasmātkāraṇāt dmāvarṇena saha vartata iti sadmaṃ antassthānaṃ madhyabhāgaḥ tadbhajatīti tathoktaḥ | tataḥ tasmātkāraṇāt padmākṣī bhaviṣyati | padmākṣīti niṣpatsyate | atra vaktavyaṃ kiṃ vaktavyaprakiyā sarvāp'yuktaiva nānyatkiṃcidavaśiṣyata iti bhāvaḥ | atra bhagavanmandiramahimaguṇena tannivāsena pakṣiṇo'pi bhagavatsārūpyaguṇādhānam | anyatsarvaṃ pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
ādyamaratvaṃ gatamapyatha cātitarāntakatvamāptamapi | haritatvavittvabhūmnā tatsādharmyeṇa maratakaṃ bhāti || 1695 ||
maratakaṃ gārutmataṃ maratakapadaṃ ca, tayostādātmyam | ādyamaratvaṃ pūrvadevatvaṃ daityatvamityarthaḥ | gatamapi karmavaśādvṛtrādivaditi bhāvaḥ | atha ca anantaraṃ ca atitarāntakatvaṃ ativelajagannāśakatvaṃ daityatvānuguṇamiti bhāvaḥ | āptamapi | pakṣe ādyau prāthamikau marau mavarṇarephau yasya tat ādyamaraṃ tasya bhāvaḥ tattvaṃ gataṃ atha ca maravarṇānantaraṃ ca atitarāṃ ativelaṃ takau takārakakārau
yasya tat atitarāṃtakaṃ tasya bhāvaṃ āptamityarthaḥ | maratakaśabdasyaivaṃ varṇacatuṣṭayātmakatvādityabhiprāyaḥ | hareḥ bhagavataḥ tatvaṃ yāthātmyaṃ vettīti vā harirūpaṃ paraṃ tatvaṃ vettīti vā haritatvavit tasya bhāvaḥ haritattvavittvaṃ tasya bhūmnā tatsādharmyeṇa bhāti |
idaṃ jñānamupāśritya mama sādharmyamāgatāḥ |
sarge'pi nopajāyante pralaye na vyathanti ca ||
ityuktarītyā bhagavatsāmyaṃ prāpya prakāśata ityarthaḥ ||
pakṣe  haritatvaṃ haridvarṇatvaṃ vindatīti haritatvavit | vindateḥ kvip | tadbhūmnā tatsādharmyeṇa bhāti | maratakamaṇerharitavarṇatvena bhagavatsāvarṇyāttathoktiḥ | nīlaharidvarṇayorabhedaḥ kavisamayasiddhaḥ, atra makārarephādivarṇavattvaṃ maratakaśabdagataṃ haritattvabhāktvaṃ tadarthagatamapi śabdārthatādātmyasāmrājyādabhedenādhyavasitamiti rahasyam | bhagavanniṣṭhamahimaguṇena tattattvavidi maratake tatsādharmyaguṇādhānam | haritattvavittvādiśabdakroḍīkṛtārthadvayābhedādhyavasāyanirvyūḍhapratibandhakasadbhāve'pi kāryotpattilakṣaṇavibhāvanāviśeṣeṇa pūrvoktarītyā paryāyālaṃkāraviśeṣeṇa copaskṛtamityavadheyam ||
yathāvā
acalāmarasthitiḥ prāgyenāptā'nte pratīpavarṇagati | bhūtvā'pi marakataṃ taddharirucyā svārthato na vibhraṣṭam || 1696 ||
yena marakatena gārutmatamaṇinā marakatapadena ca prāk ādau acalāmarasthitiḥ acalāmaro bhūdevaḥ tasya sthitiḥ maryādā āptā brāhmaṇyaṃ labdhamityarthaḥ | pakṣe acalā niścalā marasthitiḥ makārarephayoravasthānaṃ āptā ‘sthitisstriyāmavasthāne maryādāyāṃ ca sīmani, iti medinī | tat marakataṃ tatpadaṃ ca | ante avasānasamaye pratīpavarṇaḥ brāhmaṇapratikūlavarṇaḥ śūdravarṇa iti yāvat | gatiḥ saṃbandho yasya tattathoktaṃ | ajāmilādivadvṛṣalasaṃsargadūṣitamiti
bhāvaḥ bhūtvā; pakṣe ante svacaramabhāge pratīpā pratilomā varṇagatiḥ varṇayoḥ kakāratakārayoḥ sthitiḥ yasya tattathoktaṃ bhūtvā'pi | marakatamiti padaṃ maratakaṃ bhūtvā'pītyarthaḥ | harau bhagavati rucyā niratiśayaprītyā ‘yamevaiṣa vṛṇute' ityuktarītyā bhagavatkṛtasvaviṣayakaprītyā vā | pakṣe bhagavattulyarucā upalakṣitamiti śeṣaḥ | svārthataḥ svābhīpsitaniśśreyasarūpaniratiśayapuruṣārthāt na vibhraṣṭaṃ na pracyutaṃ | kiṃtu sādhitavadeveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe svābhidheyādgārutmatamaṇilakṣaṇāt na vibhraṣṭaṃ marakatapadasya yādṛśārthapratyāyakatā maratakamityasyāpi padasya tādṛśārthapratyāyakatāyā eva prasiddhatvānna svārthavibhraṃśa iti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvodāharaṇavadeva sarvamanusandheyam ||
yathāvā
ādimavarṇatritayatyakto'pi tvadrucerbalāḍhyatayā | varṇāntarago'pi jahau na svārthaṃ vārivāhako bhagavan || 1697 ||
he bhagavan! vārivāhakaḥ ambuvāhaḥ vārivāhakaśabdaśca ādau bhavaṃ ādimaṃ yat varṇatritayaṃ brāhmaṇādivarṇatrayaṃ | pakṣe vārivā iti varṇatrayaṃ tena tyakto'pi; tvadruceḥtvayi prītisadbhāvāddhetoḥ
tvadanugrahasadbhāvādvā | balāḍhyatayā vivekādisādhanasaptakāntargatānavasādalakṣaṇamanobalavattvena īdṛśabalāsadbhāve vivakṣitapuruṣārthalābhāyogāt | yacchrūyate 'nāyamātmā balahīnena labhyaḥ' itīti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe | tvadruceḥ tvattulyadyutisadbhāvādityarthaḥ | yadvā | tvadabhiprāyānurodhādityarthaḥ | balāḍhyatayā balā iti varṇadvayayuktatayā tvadicchānusārāt' ‘na girā girā, iti nyāyāt vārivā
iti varṇatrayaniṣedhena tatsthāne vidhīyamānaṃ yat balā iti varṇadvayaṃ tādṛśavarṇavattvena varṇāṃntarago'pi avaravarṇaṃ prāpto'pi pakṣe valā ityākārakavarṇābhinnavarṇāntarasaṃbanddho'pītyarthaḥ | balāhaka iti niṣpanno'pītyabhiprāyaḥ | pṛṣodarādigaṇe vārivāhakaśabda eva balāhaka iti paṭhitaḥ | svārthaṃ svabhīṣṭaṃ paramapuruṣārthaṃ na jahau | bhagavatprītisadbhāve puruṣārthasādhanaviṣaye avaravarṇasaṃbandho'pi na pratibandhaka iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe svābhidheyaṃ na jahau vārivāhaka śabdasya balāhakaśabdatve'pi tulyārthakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāpi bhagavadguṇena balāhakasya guṇadhānam | anyatprāgvat ||
anyadoṣeṇānyadoṣādhānaṃ yathā
bahudhā'pi ca yatamānāstava dhāmagatiṃ kadā'pyalabhamānāḥ | maśakatayā vā'tra svānasṛṣṭavantaṃ vidhiṃ vinindanti || 1698 ||
atra bhagavadbhaktānāṃ śrīnivāsanivāsagamanābhāvadoṣeṇa taddivyālaye maśakatayā vā'pi svānasṛṣṭavato vidhernnindārūpadoṣādhānam ||
yathāvā
tuṣṭūṣustavaṃ bhagavannanantamadbhutatamaṃ ca mahimānam | ekāmeva rasajñāṃ nirmitavantaṃ vidhi vinindāmi || 1699 ||
atra teuṣṭūeṣārakarasajñatvadoṣeṇa anekarasanā anirmitavato vidhernindyatvarūpadoṣādhānam ||
acyutapadanigalitamapyasāraviṣayeṣu madaviditameva | dhāvadadāntaṃ hṛnmama dhātrā sṛjatā pratārito'smi mudhā || 1700 ||
idaṃ bhūyasā'pi yatnena duśśakamanonigrahatayā nirvidyamānasya kasyacitprapannasya vacanam | tathāhi  acyutasya bhagavataḥ pade caraṇe nigalitamapi niścalatayā sthāpitamapi, pakṣe durmocakārārūpasthānaviśeṣaśṛṅkhalitamapi madaviditaṃ mayā ajñātameva yathā syāttathā asāreṣu viṣayeṣu deśeṣu ca ‘viṣayo gocare deśe tathā janapadepi ca' iti medinī | dhāvat rucibhedādviṣayarasānevāsvādayaditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe kārāgṛhādviśṛṅkhalatayā svairaṃ deśāddeśaṃ dhāvamānamiti bhāvaḥ | adāntaṃ svarūpato'śikṣitaṃ mama hṛt cetaḥ sṛjatā dhātrā, anena svataḥ poṣakasvabhāvenāpīti vyajyate | mudhā pratarito'smi vyarthameva vañcito'smi | īdṛgatidurdāntacapalacetassarjanapūrvakamadvañcanena na kiṃcidasya prayojanaṃ vimṛśannapi paśyāmīti bhāvaḥ | atra prapannamanasaścāpalyarūpadoṣeṇa tatsraṣṭurdhātuḥ pratārakatvarūpadoṣādhānam | prakṛte prapannamanovṛttānte ucchṛṅkhalakārāgṛhasvairanissṛtacetovṛttāntapratītirūpasamāsoktyupaskṛtamidam | pūrvodahāraṇe tu ubhe api śuddhe iti vivekaḥ ||
yathāvā
adhinātho bhavatu vidhirnanu nātha tvayi manākparācīne | bhagavannanātha eva hi bhavitā kaścinna ko'pi viśayo'tra || 1701 ||
nanu nātha he bhagavan! kaścit adhināthaḥ yaḥ kaścidaiśvaryaśālī janaḥ vidhirbhavatu brahmaiva bhavatviti lokoktiḥ | kiṃ tāvateti bhāvaḥ | tvayi manāk parācīne parāṅmukhe sati anātha eva bhavitā | tvayyudāsīne ko'nyo nāthassyāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe adhināthaśabdaḥ vidhiḥ vigatadhivarṇaścet anātha iti saṃpadyetetyarthaḥ | atra manāgbhagavatpārāṅmukhyadoṣeṇādhināthasya vidherapi kasyacidanāthatvarūpadoṣādhānam | upadarśitaśleṣacamatkārastu pūrvebhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
anyaguṇenānyasya doṣādhānaṃ yathā
ativelasahanaśīlā vipuleva tavāntaraṅgarītiriti | vāraṃvāraṃ manujāssvairaṃ vidhadati kilāṃhasāṃ bhāram || 1702 ||
atra ātyantikabhagavatkṣamāguṇena durdāntānāṃ svairaduritabhārakaraṇarūpadoṣādhānam ||
yathāvā
ādyantākṣaravairaṃ tvayyakhilasuhṛttame rameśa vibho | vaiśvānaramapyantaśśvānaṃ ko nāma bhuvi na jānāti || 1703 ||
he vibho rameśa! akhilasuhṛdi 'sarvasya śaraṇaṃ suhṛt' iti śrutyudghoṣitasarvasauhārde tvayi ādyantāvabhivyāpya ādyantaṃ yāvaccharīrapātamityarthaḥ | akṣaraṃ akṣayyaṃ vairaṃ virodhaḥ yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ vaiśvānaramapi vahnitulyamapīti yāvat, atipavitramapīti
bhāvaḥ | śvānaṃ sārameyasadṛśaṃ antaḥ manasi ko nāma bhuvi na
jānāti | dākṣiṇyena prakāśaṃ śunakatulyaṃ vaktumanīśo'pi cetasi tathā jānīyādeveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe ādyante prathamacarame akṣare varṇe vairarūpe yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ ādau vai iti varṇena ante ra iti varṇena ca yuktamityarthaḥ | vaiśvānaraṃ vaiśvānaraśabdaṃ antaśśvānamiti samastaṃ padam | antaḥ vaira ityākārakavarṇadvayamadhye śvāna ityākārakavarṇadvayaṃ yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ ko nāma na jānāti; sarvo'pi vaiśvānaraśabdaḥ īdṛgiti jānātyeveti bhāvaḥ | atra bhagavato'khilasuhṛttvādiguṇena tadvairasya doṣatvānuvarṇanam ||
yathāvā
tvadapāṅgaviparyāsānnahuṣo nāke mahāśayo'pi bhavan | antecyuta ūrjitatāṃ prāpto'pi mahāśayurbabhūvāmba || 1704 ||
he amba! nahuṣaḥ nāke mahāśayaḥ mahecchaḥ bhavannapi trailokyaiśvaryaṃ bhajannapīti yāvat 'mahecchastu mahāśayaḥ' ityamaraḥ | apratihateccho bhavannapītyarthaḥ | ūrjitatāṃ sarvaprakārabalavattvaṃ prāpto'pi tvadapāṅgaviparyāsāt ante paryavasāne cyutaḥ svargādbhraṣṭassan mahāṃścāsau śayuśca mahāśayuḥ ajagaro babhūva | ‘ajagare śayurvāhasa ityubhau' ityamaraḥ | anyatra mahāśayaśabdaḥ ante acyutaḥ cyutākāraḥ ūrjitatāṃ uvā ukāreṇa ūrjitatāṃ prāṇitatāṃ ukāraghaṭitatāmiti yāvat | prāpto'pi apissamuccaye | na kevalamante akāracyutatāmeva kiṃtu tatraiva ukārasaṃbandhatāṃ ca prāpta ityarthaḥ | mahāśayuriti nirapadyateti bhāvaḥ | atra śrīkaṭākṣaniṣṭhamahodāratvādiguṇena tadviparyāsahetukamahāśayutvadoṣo nahuṣasya nibaddhaḥ ||
anyadoṣeṇānyaguṇadhānaṃ yathā
daṇḍadharasttvamiti hare caṇḍakarādissvakṛtyamadhikurvan | bhadrāya bhavati jagatāṃ nidrātyalase prabhau janassvairam || 1705 ||
prabhau alase sati janaḥ svairaṃ nidrātīti yojanā | atra bhagavato daṇḍadharatvasūcitakrauryadoṣeṇa caṇḍakarādeḥ svasvādhikāranirvartanamūlakajagatkṣemaniṣpādanalakṣaṇaguṇādhānam | ayaṃ ca vyatirekamukhapravṛttena turīyapādagatārthāntaranyāsena samarthitaḥ | atra ‘bhīṣā'smādvātaḥ pavate bhīṣodeti sūryaḥ' ityādiśrutyartho'nusaṃdheyaḥ | idaṃ bhagavato daṇḍadharatvaṃ krauryamiti lokadṛṣṭyoktam | vastutastu tasyāpi guṇatvamevetyabhāṣi bhagavadbhāṣyakāraiḥ | evaṃ satyanyaguṇenānyaguṇādhānasyaivodāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati ||
yathāvā
cakreṇa vyalunāstvaṃ caidyasya śirodharāṃ sarojākṣa | kiṃ chinnamasya tāvattvatsāyujyaṃ paraṃ tvanenāptam || 1706 ||
atra bhagavataścakrasya caidyasya śirodharācchedahetubhūtakrauryadoṣeṇa tasya tatsāyujyalābharūpaguṇādhānam | idaṃ śuddham ||
yathāvā
tvadanugrahasya viṣayaśśarīrabhṛddharaṇibhāsvaraḥ prabhavet | tvannigrahasya viṣayo nissvo'cyuta dharaṇibhāra eva bhavet || 1707 ||
dharaṇyā dharaṇau vā bhāsvaraḥ tejasvī san prabhavet prabhurbhavet nissvaḥ durgatassan dharaṇibhāraḥ bhuvo bhārabhūta eva bhavet | na ca kiṃcidapi prayojanaṃ tena bhavediti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe dharaṇibhāsvaraśabdaḥ nissvaḥ svavarṇarahitassan dharaṇibhāra iti niṣpanno bhavedityarthaścamatkārī | atra bhagavato'nugraho guṇaḥ tena śarīrabhṛto dharaṇibhāsvaraprabhutvarūpaguṇādhānaṃ pūrvārdhe | tannigraho doṣaḥ tena tasya nissvatāvāptipūrvakabhūbhāratvarūpadoṣādhānamuttarārdhe ityubhāvapi prakārau darśitāviti pūrvebhyo vicchittiviśeṣaḥ | evaṃ pradarśayiṣyamāṇeṣvapyudāharaṇeṣu ||
śarvassthāṇussannapi sarvajñastvatkṛpāvaśādāsīt | syāditaradhā sa paśupaḥ patannadhaśśīrṣamapi paśupa eva || 1708 ||
atra bhagavatkṛpāmahimaguṇena sthāṇorapi sarvajñatvarūpaguṇaḥ, tadvaiparītyena doṣeṇa paśupasya paśupatvarūpadoṣaścāhita ityubhayam ||
yathāvā
kṛpayā tvayekṣito yadi bhavatyasabhyo'pi khalu sabhāstāraḥ | ata eṣa sabhāsāro viparītatve bhavedrasābhāsaḥ || 1709 ||
he bhagavan ! tvayā kṛpayā īkṣitaḥ dṛṣṭo yadi asabhyo'pi sabhānarhaḥ anāryo'pi 'māhākulakulīnāryasabhyasajjanasādhavaḥ' ityamaraḥ | sabhāstāraḥ sabhyo bhavati khalu | ataḥ asmādeva kāraṇāt eṣaḥ sabhāsāraḥ bhavatītyanuṣajyate | sabhāyāṃ śreṣṭho
bhavatītyarthaḥ | ‘sāro bale sthirāṃśe ca nyāyye klībaṃ vare triṣu' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe eṣaḥ sabhāstāra iti śabdaḥ ataḥ avidyamānatakārassan sabhāsāra iti niṣpadyate viparītatve tvatkaṭākṣavaiparītye vilomatayā paṭhane ca rasābhāso bhavet | avidyamānaḥ bhāsaḥ bhāsanaṃ yasya saḥ abhāsaḥ rasena abhāsaḥ rasābhāsaḥ nīrasa ityarthaḥ | yadvā rasāyāṃ bhuvi abhāsaḥ aprakāśa ityarthaḥ | yadvā asyate nirasyata ityāsaḥ karmaṇi ghañ rasaśca ābhā ca rasābhe tābhyāṃ nirasta ityarthaḥ | pakṣe sabhāsāraśabdaḥ rasābhāsa iti pariṇamatītyarthaḥ | atra bhagavatkaṭākṣasya kṛpārdratāguṇena asabhyasyāpi sabhāstāratādiguṇādhānaṃ, tadvaiparītyena sabhāsārasyāpi rasābhāsatvarūpadoṣādhānaṃ ca ||
yathāvā
sadyastvayekṣito'gre sa vīśvaro'pi dyavīśvaro'mba bhavet | ādau dyavīśvaro'pi hyupekṣito vīśvarodyassyāt || 1710 ||
he amba! vigataḥ īśvaro yasya saḥ vīśvaraḥ anātho'pītyarthaḥ | tvayā agre purastāt īkṣitaścet saḥ pumān sadyaḥ tatkṣaṇameva dyavi divi dyośabdasya saptamyekavacanam | īśvaraḥ adhināthaḥ bhavet ‘svāmīśvarādhipati' ityādinā īśvaraśabdayogāddyośabdasya saptamī | indro bhavedityarthaḥ | yadvā sadya ityatra sat yaḥ iti chedaḥ | yaḥ tvayā sat praśastaṃ yathā syāttathā īkṣitaḥ iti yojanā | anyattulyam | athavā dyavi 'tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divi' iti śruteḥ parame vyomni īśvaraḥ ‘sa svarāḍbhavati' ityuktarītyā
akarmavaśyaḥ muktyaiśvaryabhāk bhavedityarthaḥ | kiṃca ādau prathamaṃ dyavīśvaro'pi indrobhavannapi adya adhunā tvayā upekṣitaścet vīśvaraḥ anātha eva syāt | tvadīkṣaṇānvayavyatirekāveva aiśvaryadāridryaprayojakāviti bhāvaḥ | syādityatra sakārasya ‘anaci ca' iti vaikalpikaṃ dvitvam | pakṣe vīśvaraśabdaḥ ādau prathamavarṇātpūrvaṃ sadyaḥ dyavarṇasahitaścet dyavīśvara iti niṣpadyate | na kevalametāvadeva | ādau dyavīśvaraḥ dyavīśvaraśabdassannapi adyaḥ dyavarṇarahitaḥ vīśvara iti bhavatītyartho'pi camatkārī | atra lakṣmīkaṭākṣasya sānugrahatvarūpaguṇena tadīkṣitasya svārājyaiśvaryalakṣaṇaguṇaḥ | tadupekṣaṇalakṣaṇadoṣaprayuktaḥ indrasyāpi anāthatvarūpadoṣaścetyubhayam ||
yathāvā
vyāsavibhīṣaṇabalivattvadupagatau nātha vardhate pūrṇāyuḥ | pūrṇāyurapyapagatāvūrṇāyuriveha vadhyate bata manujaḥ || 1711 ||
nāthyate āyurārogyādisarvapuruṣārthān janairiti nāthaḥ, tasya saṃbuddhiḥ he nātha! tvadupagatau tvaccharaṇāgatau satyāṃ vyāsavibhīṣaṇabalivat pūrṇāyussan vardhate |
aśvatthāmā balirvyāso hanumāṃśca vibhīṣaṇaḥ |
kṛpaḥ paraśurāmaśca saptaite cirajīvinaḥ ||
ityuktaṃ teṣāṃ cirajīvitvaṃ bhagavatprapadanamahimnaiveti bhāvaḥ | pūrṇāyurapi nimittāntareṇa pūrṇāyurapi apagatau tvatto vaimukhye sati ūrṇāyuḥ meṣa iva vadhyate hanyate | durgādibalipradairmāṃsārthibhirdveṣibhirvā janairiti śeṣaḥ | tvadvaimukhyaṃ brahmaṇo'pyāyurbhaṅgaheturiti
bhāvaḥ 'āyuḥ kṣiṇotyupacinoti ca sarvadoṣāndveṣo mukundaviṣayo viṣayogatulyaḥ' ityukteḥ | bhagavadupagataḥ pūrṇāyurvardhate tadapagatastu ūrṇāyuriva vadhyata iti saṃpiṇḍito'rthaḥ | pakṣe pūrṇāyuśśabdo'pi pasya pakārasya gatiḥ prāptiḥ na pagatiḥ apagatiḥ tasyāṃ pakārarāhitye ityarthaḥ | ūrṇāyussanvadhyate bavayorabhedāt badhyate padasamudāye grathyata ityarthaḥ | ivaśabdo vākyālaṃkāre | atra bhagavatassakalaphalapradatvādiguṇena tatprapannasya pūrṇāyuṣṭvaguṇādhānaṃ tadapagatyā tadanyasya ūrṇāyuvadvadhyatvarūṣadoṣādhānaṃ ca nibaddham | upamāsaṃkīrṇatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
tava padi lagnā janatā pūrṇā lakṣyeta sarvathā'pi hare | pūrṇā'pi paccyutā cedūrṇālakṣyā bhavenna sandehaḥ || 1712 ||
he hare! tava padi lagnā tvaccaraṇe saktā janatā sarvadhā sarvaprakāreṇāpi pūrṇā satī lakṣyate janairiti śeṣaḥ | tava paccyutā padaḥ caraṇāt cyutā janatā svataḥ pūrṇā'pi ūrṇālakṣyā ūrṇeva meṣādilomeva alakṣyā anādaraṇīyā bhavet | yadvā ūrṇā tatprāyā lakṣyā dṛśyā bhavet | 'ūrṇā meṣādilomni syāt' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe pūrṇā'pi pūrṇeti śabdavyaktirapi paccyutā pakāreṇa cyutā | paḥ pakāraḥ cyutā yasyā iti ghā' pakāravidhurā satī ūrṇā ūrṇetyānupūvīmatī lakṣyā dṛśyā bhavati | atra bhagavato māhātmyaguṇena tatpadalagnāyā janatāyāḥ pūrṇāyuṣṭvalakṣaṇaguṇādhānam | tādṛśamahimavattvaṃ bhagavato guṇaḥ | tena tatpādacyutihetukorṇālakṣyatvarūpo doṣa ityubhayaṃ pūrvebhyo vicchittiśāli ||
yathāvā
ghanasārassmitadāsyāttavāghavānprāṅnasāra evāmba | kathamapyano'tha sāro rasānadhaśvāsa sakalaviparītaḥ || 1713 ||
he amba! ghanasāraḥ karpūraḥ tava smitadāsyāt prāk mandahasitadāsyalābhātpūrvaṃ aghavān dāsyālābhaprayuktaduḥkhavān 'aṃhoduḥkhavyasaneṣvagham' ityamaraḥ | ataeva nasāra eva | asāra eva naśabdena nañarthakena samāsaḥ | kathamapi atiprayāsena anitītyanaḥ 'ana prāṇane' pacādyac | jīvannityarthaḥ | atha smitadāsyalābhānantaraṃ sakalaviparītaḥ pūrvoktāghavattvādisarvaguṇaviparyāsaṃ gatassan rasānaghaḥ rasāyāṃ bhuvi anaghaḥ rasena anaghaśca san sāraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ balavānvā āsa didīpe | asyateḥ daivādikāddīptyarthakālliṭ | aghavattvanasāratvayorvaiparītyena sāro rasānaghaśceti sāratvarasānaghatvayoḥ pratidvaṃdvinornnirdeśaḥ | pakṣe ghanasāraśabdaḥ prāk prathamaṃ ghavānna bhavatītyaghavān ghavarṇavidhurassan nasāra eva nasāra iti saṃpadyamānaḥ atha anaḥ navarṇarahitaḥ sāra iti niṣpannaḥ | evaṃ varṇavaikalyahetukaṃ śabdasvarūpamuktam | atha sarvavarṇasākalyena vaiparītye śabdasvarūpamāha  atheti | atha sakalaviparītaḥ varṇavaikalyābhāvena vilomapaṭhitasarvavarṇaḥ rasānagha iti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atra śrīsmitagatadhāvalyatāpahāritvādiguṇena ghanasārasya tadalābhamūlakāghavattvarūpadoṣaḥ | taddāsyalābhaprayojyasāratvādiguṇaścetyubhayaṃ pūrvodāharaṇebhyo vailakṣaṇyena nibaddham | śabdārthatādātmādicamatkṛtistu vyaktaiva ||
yathāvā
amba prāktvadadṛṣṭeramarakadambamapi viṣṭapatyaktam | naṣṭamadhunā tvanantodayaṃ tava dayādṛśā vipatyaktam || 1714 ||
he amba ! prāk pūrvaṃ amarakadambamapi kimuta martyakadambamiti bhāvaḥ | tvadadṛṣṭeḥ tvatkaṭākṣālābhāddhetoḥ viṣṭapena tyaktaṃ viṣṭapaṃ tyaktaṃ yena tattathoktamiti | visṛṣṭasvasvabhuvanāvasthānamiti yāvat | naṣṭaṃ kvāpyadarśanaṃ prāptaṃ nilīnamityarthaḥ | adhunā tu tava dayādṛśā vipattyaktaṃ āpadā parityaktam | na kevalamaniṣṭanirāsa eva, kiṃtvativelamiṣṭalābhaścāsyetyāha anantodayaṃ aparicchinnasarvavidhābhyudayaṃ ca bhavati | atra
tvayā devi parityaktaṃ sakalaṃ bhuvanatrayam |
vinaṣṭaprāyamabhavattvayedānīṃ samedhitam ||
iti śrīviṣṇupurāṇānusāriṇā ‘īṣattvatkaruṇanirīkṣaṇasudhāsaṃdhukṣaṇādrakṣyate naṣṭaṃ prāktadalābhatastribhuvanaṃ saṃpratyanantodayam' iti śrīyāmunāryaśrīsūktiranusaṃhitā ||
pakṣe viṣṭapatyaktamiti padaṃ naṣṭaṃ na vidyate ṣṭa iti varṇaḥ yasya tat naśabdapūrvako bahuvrīhiḥ | bhraṣṭaṣṭavarṇaṃ sat vipatyakaṃ vipatyaktamiti padaṃ anantāt śeṣāvatārātpatañjaleḥ udayaḥ āvirbhāvo yasya tattathoktaṃ bhavati | sarveṣāmapi śabdānāṃ tanniṣpāditatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atra śrīkaṭākṣamahimaguṇena tadaviṣayasyāmarakadambasya viṣṭapatyāgādidoṣa ityeka ullāsaḥ, tatkaṭākṣamahimaguṇena tadviṣayasya tasya vipattiparityāgādiguṇa ityanya ullāsaḥ pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
vānaraniśicararājau tayoravarajau ca tava ravikulendo | kopakṛpābhyāṃ nṛpaterājyādarametya hanta nirvavatuḥ || 1715 ||
he raghukulendo! vānaraniśicararājau vālidaśānanau nṛpatestava kopāt ājyāḥ yuddhāt 'samityājisamidyudhaḥ' ityamaraḥ | daraṃ bhayaṃ etya nirvavatuḥ nirvāṇaṃ prāptau astaṃ gatāvityarthaḥ | tadavarajau sugrīvavibhīṣaṇau, nṛpate iti saṃbuddhyantaṃ padaṃ he raghukulendo ityasya viśeṣaṇam | rājyādaraṃ rājye ādaraṃ etya kulakramāgatarājyalābhāttatrātimātrādaraṃ prāpyeti bhāvaḥ | nirvavatuḥ nirvāṇaṃ sukhaṃ prāptāvityarthaḥ| niḥpūrvakādvāterupapāditobhayārthakatāyā darśanāt | tathāca medinī 'nirvāṇamastaṃgamane nirvṛtau gajamajjane | saṅgame'pyapavarge ca' iti | atra śrīraghunandanagatābhyāṃ kopakṛpābhyāṃ doṣaguṇābhyāṃ vālidaśānanayossugrīvavibhīṣaṇayośca ājyādarasya rājyādarasya ca prāptipūrvakaṃ vināśasukhalakṣaṇadoṣaguṇayorādhānamityubhayaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaśleṣatulyayogitāsaṃkīrṇamiti pūrvebhyo vailakṣaṇyam ||
atra prathamacaturthayorullāso'nvarthaḥ | madhyayośchatrinyoyana lākṣaṇikaḥ | kāvyaliṅgena caritārtho'yaṃ nālaṃkārāntarabhūmimāḍhaukata ityeke | laukikārthamayatvādanalaṃkāra evetyapare ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre ullāsasara ekasaptatitamaḥ
<22>
athāvajñālaṃkārasaraḥ
na syātāṃ yadi te tābhyāṃ sā'vajñālaṃkṛtirbhavet ||
tābhyāṃ anyaguṇadoṣābhyāṃ te anyaguṇadoṣādhāne kasyacidguṇena kasyacidguṇādhānaṃ doṣeṇa doṣādhānaṃ vā na syātāṃ cet avajñālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
karuṇājaladhiriti tvāṃ śrito'smi karuṇālavo'pi mayi na kṛtaḥ | pāpī gacchatu jaladhiṃ pādatalaṃ vā'pyamuṣya nārdraṃ syāt || 1716 ||
atra bhagavanniṣṭhāśritavatsalatvajaladhiniṣṭhajalapauṣkalyarūpaguṇābhyāṃ śaraṇāgatapāpinoḥ kṛpāpādatalārdratayorguṇayoranādhānam || atra mahāvākyārtho dṛṣṭāntarūpaḥ bimbapratibimbavākyārthābhyāṃ avajñārūpābhyāṃ saṃkīrṇaḥ ||
yathāvā
amṛtamaye nivasannapi sumanonivahe jano bhavadvimukhaḥ | viśadatvaṃ naiti hare śaśadharabimbe ciraṃ kalaṅka iva || 1717 ||
atra vidvannivahaśaśadharabimbayoramṛtamayatvalakṣaṇaguṇena bhagavadvimukhakalaṅkayorviśadatvarūpaguṇasyānādhānaṃ śleṣakṛtasamānadharmavadupamāsaṃkīrṇo'yamiti pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
gacchatu gayāṃ vitanutāṃ vilomatannāmavācyamapi yadivā | vindedaravindekṣaṇa kathaṃ tava padaṃ tvayā vivadamānaḥ || 1718 ||
yathāvā
vilomatannāmavācyaṃ pratilomagayāśabdavācyaṃ yāgamityarthaḥ | gayāgantā vā yaṣṭā vā bhavatu | sa pumān he aravindekṣaṇa ! tvayā vivadamānaḥ ‘tvaṃ me'haṃ me' ityuktarītyā vivādakartā kathaṃ tava padaṃ vindeta |
yamo vaivasvato rājā yastavaiṣa hṛdi sthitaḥ |
tena cedavivādaste mā gaṅgāṃ mā kurūn gamaḥ ||
iti tatpadabharārpaṇarūpāvivādavata eva niśśreyasaprāptyabhidhānāditi bhāvaḥ | atra vaktavyāṃśassarvo'pi samāsoktiprakaraṇe saprapañcaṃ nyarūpyata | atra gayāyāgayoḥ pavitratādiguṇena bhagavatpadalābhaguṇānādhānam ||
doṣeṇa doṣānādhānaṃ yathā
kā tava hānirbhagavan kaścidabhāgyaḥ parāṅmukhastvayi cet | nalinasuhṛdo bhavetkiṃ nayanarujā cennimīlitaḥ kaścit || 1719 ||
nayanarujetyupalakṣaṇatṛtīyā | atra bhāgyahīnanayanarogiṇoḥ parāṅmukhatvanimīlitatvābhyāṃ doṣābhyāṃ bhagavannalinasuhṛdorhānirūpadoṣānādhānam | iyamapi dṛṣṭāntasaṃkīrṇā ādyodāharaṇavat ||
yathāvā
yaśśrīnivāsa iti bhuvi vidito'higirau cakāstyasau kaiścit | ukto'pi vipratīpaṃ kadā'pi bhavitā sa vā niśrīḥ || 1720 ||
yaḥ bhagavān śrīnivāsa iti bhuvi viditaḥ ahigirau cakāsti | saḥ vā niśrīḥ iti chedaḥ | so'sau kaiścit bhagavatpāramyāsahiṣṇubhiśśaivaiḥ vipratīpaṃ viruddhaṃ yathā tathā uko'pi nāyaṃ śrīnivāsaḥ kiṃtu kumāra ityuktopīti bhāvaḥ | kadā'pi yadākadācidvā niśrīḥ śrīvidhuraḥ 'nivātapadma' ityādāviva nītyupasargapūrvako bahuvrīhiḥ| bhavitā vā iti yojanā syātkimityarthaḥ | śriyo vakṣasyevāvasthānātkadā'pi nityaśrītvaṃ na jahātītyarthaḥ | yathocyate śrīmati rāmāyaṇe  'śrīvatsavakṣā nityaśrīḥ' | iti | pakṣe śrīnivāsaśabdaḥ kadā'pi yadākadāvā vipratīpamuktaḥ prātilomyena paṭhitaḥ savāniśrīriti bhavitetyarthospi camatkārī | atra nāyaṃ śrīnivāsa iti śaivoktidoṣeṇa bhagavato niśrītvarūpadoṣānādhānam | camatkṛtiviśeṣastu vyakta eva ||
yathāvā
sarveśvaraṃ hare tvāṃ bhūyaśśrutyaikakaṇṭhyato vidmaḥ | parvatamātrapatiṃ tvāṃ sthaviragavī vadatu kā'pi kā hāniḥ || 1721 ||
bhūyasīnāṃ śrutīnāmaikakaṇṭhyāt ekārthapratipādanāt | sthaviragavī vṛddhapralapitam | sthaviretyanena tasya matibhraṃśo dyotyate | jaradgavī vā | vastutastu sthaviragavī ‘viṣṇuḥ parvatānāmadhipatiḥ'
iti śrutiḥ | kā'pītyanena tasyāḥ śrutyantarānaikakaṇṭhyaṃ dyotyate | atra udāhṛtaśrutyā bhagavataśśrīnivāsasya parvatamātrādhipatyaprayuktanyūnatārūpadoṣavarṇanena hānirūpadoṣānādhānavarṇanam ||
yathāvā
asthirajayo'navana ityabhidhattāṃ vipratīpameva janaḥ | navatoyajalocana iha nacalojayatovanaśca na bhavetkim || 1722 ||
janaḥ pāmara ityarthaḥ | 'jano loke maharlokātparaloke'pi pāmare' iti viśvamedinyau | ayaṃ sthiraḥ nityaḥ jayo yasya saḥ sthirajayaḥ sa na bhavatītyasthirajayaḥ | anavanaḥ arakṣakaḥ ābhyāṃ viśeṣaṇābhyāmaniṣṭanivāraṇeṣṭaprāpaṇākṣamatvaṃ sūcitam | iti vipratīpaṃ bhagavatsvabhāvaviruddhameva abhidhattāṃ kathayatu ityanādaroktiḥ | navatoyajalocanaḥ abhinavanalinanayano bhagavān jayataḥ jayāt nacalaḥ acalaḥ sthirajaya iti yāvat | avanaḥ rakṣakaśca na bhavetkim | ābhyāṃ pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇābhyāṃ pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇapratidvaṃdvibhyāmaniṣṭanirākaraṇeṣṭaprāpaṇadhurīṇatvaṃ sūcitam | ayamasthirajayaḥ anavana iti kaścitpāmaro brūyāccet tāvataivāyaṃ sthirajayo rakṣakaśca kathaṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe navatoyajalocana iti śabdaḥ pratīpaṃ kathitaścet nacalojayatovanaḥ kathaṃ na syāt tathaiva tanniṣpatteriti bhāvaḥ | atra pāmarajanakartṛkavipratīpavarṇanadoṣeṇa bhagavati tādṛśadoṣānādhānamupapāditacamatkāropaskṛtam ||
yathāvā
mūrkhastvāṃ nindati cecchātaṃ kiṃ tava hare tadidamāstām | taṃ tvaṃ nādriyase cecchātaṃ tasyāpi kimapi bata na bhavet || 1723 ||
kiṃ chātaṃ kiṃ chinnaṃ ‘cho chedane' ktaḥ | 'adecaḥ' ityātvam | 'chinna chātaṃ lūnam' ityamaraḥ | taṃ ninditāraṃ mūrkhaṃ tvaṃ nādriyase cet tasyāpi na kimapi chātaṃ chinnamityāścaryam | parihārastu cet śātaṃ iti uttarārdhe chedaḥ | kimapi śātaṃ sukhaṃ na bhavatīti | 'śarmaśātasukhāni ca' ityamaraḥ 'śo tanūkaraṇe' daivādikādasmādbāhulakāttan | 'ādecaḥ' ityatvam | śyatyaśubhamiti vigrahaḥ | atra mūkhīkṛtanindanaprayuktasya bhagavannyanatārūpadoṣasyānādhānam | bhagavatkartṛkānādaradoṣaprayojyamūrkhadoṣānādhānamiti bhavatyavajñālaṃkāradvayam | atrādyaṃ śuddhaṃ, dvitīyaṃ tu śleṣojjīvitamiti vivekaḥ ||
atra kecit avajñodāharaṇeṣu 'svalpamevāmbu labhate prasthaḥ prāpyāpi sāgaram, mīlanti yadi padmāni kā hāniramṛtadyuteḥ' ityādiṣu nāvajñākhyamalaṃkārāntaramabhyupeyaṃ, tatrānyaguṇadoṣayoranyaguṇadoṣajananasamarthayossatorapi tadajananavarṇanena viśeṣoktereva yuktatvādvaktum | sāgarasalilasamṛddhisattve'pi prasthe tatsamṛddhyabhāvasya padmasaṃkoce'pi cadragatadoṣābhavasya ca varṇanena tallakṣaṇasyāvikalatvāditi vadanti | vastutastu nātra viśeṣoktissuvacā | 'hṛdisnehakṣayo nābhūt smaradīpe jvalatyapi' ityāditadudāharaṇeṣu dīpajvālāsnehakṣayayorivātra sāgarasalilasannidhānaprasthagamanāmbusamṛddhyabhāvayoḥ kārthakāraṇabhāvasya lokasiddhasyābhāvenoktaviśeṣoktilakṣaṇavirahādityāhuḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre avajñāsaro dvisaptatitamaḥ
<23>
athānujñāsaraḥ
anujñā sā guṇautsukyāddoṣasyāpyarthanā yadi |
utkaṭaguṇaviśeṣalālasayā doṣatvena prakhyātasyāpi vastunaḥ prārthanamanujñālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
bhavahara phaṇiśikharīśvara bhavatāttava viprakarṣa eva mama | sa hyādarasya kāraṇamanādarasyaiva sannikarṣastu || 1724 ||
atra śrīveṃkaṭagirināthaviprakarṣo doṣaḥ | tadabhyupagame heturādaraṇakāraṇatvaṃ guṇaḥ | sa ca sāmānyena viśeṣasamarthanarūpeṇārthāntaranyāsena darśitaḥ ||
yathāvā
tāvakaśaile'cyuta me sthāvaratā jaṅgamatvamapi vā'stu | kiṃ muktido'si na purā kosalajanapadacarācarāṇāṃ tvam || 1725 ||
atra tarulatādirūpasthāvaratvena vā bhujagavihagamṛgāditiryagjantutayā vā janmanaḥ prārthanaṃ doṣaḥ | tadāśāsane tādṛśarūpeṇa veṃkaṭādryavasthānasyāpi muktihetutvaṃ guṇaḥ | sa ca kosalajanapadacarācaraniśśreyasavitaraṇodāharaṇena darśitaḥ ||
yathāvā
mā bhūttvayyanurāgo mama caidyāderivāstu vairaṃ vā | yena tvayyacchinnā smṛtisantatiracyutāvatiṣṭheta ||
atra caidyāderiva vaktuḥ bhagavadviṣayakavairarūpadoṣābhyarthanama vicchinnatatsmṛtisaṃtānakāraṇatvalakṣaṇaguṇalālasayeti dhyeyam || atra 'dveṣāccaidyādayo nṛpāḥ' ityetadanusaṃdheyam ||
yathāvā
yatra kvavā nihākā tāvakatīrthe bhavatvasāvātmā | tāvakayogādacyuta kā hānissyāttataḥ prasavyagateḥ || 1727 ||
he acyuta ! asāvātmā ayaṃ cetanaḥ yatrakvavā tāvake tīrthe svāmipuṣkariṇyādau nihākā godhikākhyo jalajantuviśeṣaḥ ‘nihākā godhikā same' ityamaraḥ bhavatu | tataḥ nihākātvaprāptāvapi tāvakayogena tvadīyadhyānena tvadīyānāṃ tvadīyena vā saṃbandhena vā tanmahimneti yāvat | prasavyagateḥ anukūlasadgateḥ | ‘prasavyaṃ vācyaliṅgaṃ syāt pratikūlānukūlayoḥ' iti medinī | ātmana iti śeṣaḥ | kā hānissyāt na kā'pi hāniḥ kiṃtu śreya eva bhavediti bhāvaḥ | tvatsaṃbandhaprabhāva īdṛśa iti tātparyam | pakṣe nihākāśabdaḥ tāvakena yogena upāyena yuktyā vā prasavyā pratikūlā gatiḥ sthitiḥ tasyā iti pañcamī | yuktyā upāyena vā prātilomyena paṭhanāditi yāvat | kāhāni kāhānītyānupūrvīmat syāt | asmin pakṣe syādityatra ‘anaci ca' iti dvitvam | atrāpyanujñālaṃkāraḥ pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
saṃsārameva ditsasi kaṃsāre yadi tathaiva bhūyānnaḥ | api jātu sa eva syātsāro nanu devasaṃpadaviyuktaḥ || 1728 ||
he kaṃsāre! tvaṃ naḥ saṃsārameva anādikālādanubhūtameveti bhāvaḥ | ditsasi yadi itaḥparamapi dātumicchasicet tathaiva bhūyāt | anatilaṅghanīyaṃ hi niyantuśśāsanaṃ niyantavyavargeṇeti bhāvaḥ | tadā'pi na kiṃcidaniṣṭamityāhāpi jātviti | sa eva saṃsāra eva devasaṃpadā
"dvau bhūtasargau loke'smin daiva āsura eva ca |
viṣṇubhaktiparo daivo viparītastathā''suraḥ ||
ityuktaprakārā daivī saṃpat | yā hi 'abhayaṃ sattvasaṃśuddhiḥ' ityādinā 'daivī saṃpadvimokṣāya' ityagīyata | tayā aviyuktaḥ saṃyuktaḥ api jātu sārassyāt jātusārassyādapīti vā yojanā | apissaṃbhāvanāyām ||
karmabhirbhrāmyamāṇānāṃ yatrakvāpīśvarecchayā |
maṅgalācaritairdānairmatirnaḥ kṛṣṇa īśvare ||
ityādyuktarītyā yadākadāvā yatrakvavā tādṛśayādṛcchikādisukṛtapariṇativaśāttvayi bhaktirudiyādeveti tasyāpi sāratvaṃ saṃbhavedeveti bhāvaḥ| sa hyapavargatulyakakṣyatayocyate
tvaṃ cetprasīdasi tavāsmi samīpataścet
tvayyasti bhaktiranaghā kariśailanātha|
saṃsṛjyate yadi ca dāsajanastvadīyaḥ
saṃsāra eṣa bhagavannapavarga eva ||
iti | pakṣe he deveti saṃbodhanam | sa eva saṃsāraśabda eva saṃpadaviyuktaḥ saṃ ityākārakapadena vighaṭitaḥ sāraśabda eva syādityartho'pi camatkṛterupaskurute | atra doṣarūpasaṃsārābhyarthanaṃ tatra yadā
kadāvā daivasaṃpadabhijātatvādiguṇasaṃbhavasaṃbhāvanayeti dhyeyam | iyamapyalaṃkṛtirdīkṣitopajñameva ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre anujñālaṃkārasarastrisaptatitamaḥ
<24>
atha tiraskārasaraḥ
guṇasya doṣasaṃbandhāddveṣaścetsā tiraskṛtiḥ ||
doṣaviśeṣānubandhādguṇatvena prasiddhasyāpi dveṣaścet sa tiraskāro nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
madhusūdana mama jātvapi māhendryassaṃpado'pi mā bhūvan | yāsu nimagno narchati yadṛcchayā vā'pi tāvakasmaraṇam || 1729 ||
atra | bhagavadvismaraṇadoṣasaṃbandhānmāhendrasaṃpatsu guṇatvena ṣasiddhāsvapi dveṣaḥ | ayamalaṃkāraḥ paṇḍitarājopajñam | sa hi "doṣaviśeṣānubandhādityādyuktarītyā amuṃ lakṣayitvā amuṃ ca tiraskāramalakṣayitvā anujñāṃ lakṣayataḥ kuvalayānandakṛto vismaraṇameva śaraṇaṃ, anyathā
bhajema bhavadantikaṃ prakṛtimetya paiśācikīṃ
kimityamarasaṃpada pramathanātha nāthāmahe |
kadāvā daivasaṃpadabhijātatvādiguṇasaṃbhavasaṃbhāvanayeti dhyeyam | iyamapyalaṃkṛtirdīkṣitopajñameva ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre anujñālaṃkārasarastrisaptatitamaḥ
atha tiraskārasaraḥ
guṇasya doṣasaṃbandhāddveṣaścetsā tiraskṛtiḥ ||
doṣaviśeṣānubandhādguṇatvena prasiddhasyāpi dveṣaścet sa tiraskāro nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
