madhusūdana mama jātvapi māhendryassaṃpado'pi mā bhūvan | yāsu nimagno narchati yadṛcchayā vā'pi tāvakasmaraṇam || 1729 ||
atra | bhagavadvismaraṇadoṣasaṃbandhānmāhendrasaṃpatsu guṇatvena ṣasiddhāsvapi dveṣaḥ | ayamalaṃkāraḥ paṇḍitarājopajñam | sa hi "doṣaviśeṣānubandhādityādyuktarītyā amuṃ lakṣayitvā amuṃ ca tiraskāramalakṣayitvā anujñāṃ lakṣayataḥ kuvalayānandakṛto vismaraṇameva śaraṇaṃ, anyathā
bhajema bhavadantikaṃ prakṛtimetya paiśācikīṃ kimityamarasaṃpada pramathanātha nāthāmahe |
bhavadbhavanadehalīvikaṭataṇḍudaṇḍāhatisphuṭanmakuṭakoṭibhirmaghavadādibhirbhūyate ||
iti tadudāhṛte padye tiraskārasya sphuraṇānāpatteḥ" ityabhāṇīt ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre tiraskārasaraścatussaptatitamaḥ
<25>
atha leśālaṃkārasaraḥ
guṇo doṣatayā doṣo guṇatvenāthavā yadi |
varṇyate tamalaṃkāraṃ leśamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ ||
yatra guṇo'niṣṭasādhanatayā doṣatvena, doṣaśceṣṭasādhanatayā guṇatvena varṇyate sa leśo nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
bhūyassu samullāsiṣu bhūbhṛtsu dharātale nirātaṅkam | haridhṛtiphalamidamahidhara tava yatsarve śiro'dhirohanti || 1730 ||
atra prathamārdhe śailāntarāṇāṃ bhagavaddhāraṇābhāvadoṣasya nirātaṅkollāsānukūlatayā guṇatvaṃ kalpitam | dvitīyārdhe śrīnivāsadhāraṇaguṇasya sarvajanakartṛkaśirodhirohaṇakāraṇatayā doṣatvaṃ kalpitam | ayaṃ ca nirguṇeṣu bhūyassu bhūpāleṣu sukhavattayā'vasthiteṣu niratiśayaudāryādisuguṇaśālino rājñaḥ arthijanakartṛkasaṃmardādikleśo bhaviṣyatītyaprastutavyavahāraparisphūrtirūpasamāsoktyāstutyā…
bhavadbhavanadehalīvikaṭataṇḍudaṇḍāhatisphuṭanmakuṭakoṭibhirmaghavadādibhirbhūyate ||
iti tadudāhṛte padye tiraskārasya sphuraṇānāpatteḥ" ityabhāṇīt ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre tiraskārasaraścatussaptatitamaḥ
atha leśālaṃkārasaraḥ
guṇo doṣatayā doṣo guṇatvenāthavā yadi |
varṇyate tamalaṃkāraṃ leśamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ ||
yatra guṇo'niṣṭasādhanatayā doṣatvena, doṣaśceṣṭasādhanatayā guṇatvena varṇyate sa leśo nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
bhūyassu samullāsiṣu bhūbhṛtsu dharātale nirātaṅkam | haridhṛtiphalamidamahidhara tava yatsarve śiro'dhirohanti || 1730 ||
atra prathamārdhe śailāntarāṇāṃ bhagavaddhāraṇābhāvadoṣasya nirātaṅkollāsānukūlatayā guṇatvaṃ kalpitam | dvitīyārdhe śrīnivāsadhāraṇaguṇasya sarvajanakartṛkaśirodhirohaṇakāraṇatayā doṣatvaṃ kalpitam | ayaṃ ca nirguṇeṣu bhūyassu bhūpāleṣu sukhavattayā'vasthiteṣu niratiśayaudāryādisuguṇaśālino rājñaḥ arthijanakartṛkasaṃmardādikleśo bhaviṣyatītyaprastutavyavahāraparisphūrtirūpasamāsoktyāstutyā
nindābhivyaktirūpayā nindayā stutyabhivyaktirūpayā vyājastutyā ca pūrvottarārdhagatayā saṃkīryate ||
asahanameva jyāyassahiṣṇutāvaibhavaṃ dhigastu rame | sukhino'nye tava tu sakhī sarvaissarvaṃsahetyadhaḥ kriyate || 1731 ||
atrāpi doṣaguṇayogurṇadoṣatvena varṇanaṃ, arthāntaranyāsapariṣkṛtatvaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
sarvaguṇānāṃ nidhiriti garvaṃ mā gāḥ payonidhestanaye | yaireva svajanayitussvacchatamasyāpi mathanaheturabhūḥ || 1732 ||
atra guṇasya doṣatvena varṇanaṃ śuddham | na cāyamalaṃkāraḥ vyājastutyā ubhayarūpayā gatārtha iti śaṅkyaṃ, mukhapratipāditārthavaiparītyenātra sarvatra paryavasānavirahāt | na hi 'sarvaguṇānāṃ nidhiḥ' ityasmin padye dāridryāt khinnasya kasyacillakṣmīguṇopālambharūpe śriyasstutau tasya tātparyam | apitu svajanakamathanahetutvena nindāyāmeva | evaṃ 'asahanameva jyāyaḥ' iti śloke bhūmītareṣāṃ nindā na vivakṣitā kiṃtu sukhāvasthānaṃ, sarvaṃsahākhedadarśanaduḥkhitasya vākye'smin tadanyeṣāṃ nindāyā avaktavyatvāt | pratyutastutereva vaktumucitatvāt | 'bhūyassu samullāsiṣu' iti ślokevyājastutirasticedastu nāma, tasyāssāvakāśatvena etadbādhanāyogāt ||
priyatama eva bhavatyāstribhuvanabhikṣau sthite kucelasakhe | sarvajagaddurvidhatānirvāpaṇato'mba garvitā'si katham || 1733 ||
atra paryante pratīyamānasya bhagavadatiriktasakalajagaddāridryanirvāpaṇarūpasya guṇasya doṣatvenāvarṇanāt doṣatvena varṇayamānasya bhagavatastrailokyabhikṣutvakucelasakhatvānirākaraṇadoṣasya guṇatvenākalpanādguṇadoṣayorbhinnaviṣayatayā'vasthānena leśasparśaleśarahitā vyājastutiriti vyaktameva sāvakāśatvam | ataeva leśo'pi na vyājastuterbādhaka iti prāgudāhṛte ‘bhūyassu samullāsiṣu' iti padye dvayorapi samāveśa ityavadhātavyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre leśasaraḥ pañcasaptatitamaḥ
<26>
atha mudrālaṃkārasaraḥ
prakṛtārthaparaiśśabdairmudrā sūcyārthasūcanam |
vivakṣitārthapratipādanaparaiśśabdaissūcanīyasyārthasya sūcanaṃ mudrālaṃkāraḥ | ayamapi dīkṣitopajñameva | prakṛtārthapareṇa padena tadekadeśena vā padasamudāyena padaikadeśasamudāyena vā tadubhayasamudāyena vā yāthākathaṃcit jñāpanīyārthaviśeṣasya jñāpanaṃ mudreti nirgalito'rthaḥ | padaikadeśastu prakṛte arthavānvā nirarthakovā'stu | sūcyārthaśca prakṛto'prakṛtovā'stu | na tatrābhiniveṣṭavyam ||
bhavadbhavanadehalīvikaṭataṇḍudaṇḍāhatisphuṭanmakuṭakoṭibhirmaghavadādibhirbhūyate ||
iti tadudāhṛte padye tiraskārasya sphuraṇānāpatteḥ" ityabhāṇīt ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre tiraskārasaraścatussaptatitamaḥ
atha leśālaṃkārasaraḥ
guṇo doṣatayā doṣo guṇatvenāthavā yadi |
varṇyate tamalaṃkāraṃ leśamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ ||
yatra guṇo'niṣṭasādhanatayā doṣatvena, doṣaśceṣṭasādhanatayā guṇatvena varṇyate sa leśo nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
bhūyassu samullāsiṣu bhūbhṛtsu dharātale nirātaṅkam | haridhṛtiphalamidamahidhara tava yatsarve śiro'dhirohanti || 1730 ||
atra prathamārdhe śailāntarāṇāṃ bhagavaddhāraṇābhāvadoṣasya nirātaṅkollāsānukūlatayā guṇatvaṃ kalpitam | dvitīyārdhe śrīnivāsadhāraṇaguṇasya sarvajanakartṛkaśirodhirohaṇakāraṇatayā doṣatvaṃ kalpitam | ayaṃ ca nirguṇeṣu bhūyassu bhūpāleṣu sukhavattayā'vasthiteṣu niratiśayaudāryādisuguṇaśālino rājñaḥ arthijanakartṛkasaṃmardādikleśo bhaviṣyatītyaprastutavyavahāraparisphūrtirūpasamāsoktyāstutyā
nindābhivyaktirūpayā nindayā stutyabhivyaktirūpayā vyājastutyā ca pūrvottarārdhagatayā saṃkīryate ||
asahanameva jyāyassahiṣṇutāvaibhavaṃ dhigastu rame | sukhino'nye tava tu sakhī sarvaissarvaṃsahetyadhaḥ kriyate || 1731 ||
atrāpi doṣaguṇayogurṇadoṣatvena varṇanaṃ, arthāntaranyāsapariṣkṛtatvaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
sarvaguṇānāṃ nidhiriti garvaṃ mā gāḥ payonidhestanaye | yaireva svajanayitussvacchatamasyāpi mathanaheturabhūḥ || 1732 ||
atra guṇasya doṣatvena varṇanaṃ śuddham | na cāyamalaṃkāraḥ vyājastutyā ubhayarūpayā gatārtha iti śaṅkyaṃ, mukhapratipāditārthavaiparītyenātra sarvatra paryavasānavirahāt | na hi 'sarvaguṇānāṃ nidhiḥ' ityasmin padye dāridryāt khinnasya kasyacillakṣmīguṇopālambharūpe śriyasstutau tasya tātparyam | apitu svajanakamathanahetutvena nindāyāmeva | evaṃ 'asahanameva jyāyaḥ' iti śloke bhūmītareṣāṃ nindā na vivakṣitā kiṃtu sukhāvasthānaṃ, sarvaṃsahākhedadarśanaduḥkhitasya vākye'smin tadanyeṣāṃ nindāyā avaktavyatvāt | pratyutastutereva vaktumucitatvāt | 'bhūyassu samullāsiṣu' iti ślokevyājastutirasticedastu nāma, tasyāssāvakāśatvena etadbādhanāyogāt ||
priyatama eva bhavatyāstribhuvanabhikṣau sthite kucelasakhe | sarvajagaddurvidhatānirvāpaṇato'mba garvitā'si katham || 1733 ||
atra paryante pratīyamānasya bhagavadatiriktasakalajagaddāridryanirvāpaṇarūpasya guṇasya doṣatvenāvarṇanāt doṣatvena varṇayamānasya bhagavatastrailokyabhikṣutvakucelasakhatvānirākaraṇadoṣasya guṇatvenākalpanādguṇadoṣayorbhinnaviṣayatayā'vasthānena leśasparśaleśarahitā vyājastutiriti vyaktameva sāvakāśatvam | ataeva leśo'pi na vyājastuterbādhaka iti prāgudāhṛte ‘bhūyassu samullāsiṣu' iti padye dvayorapi samāveśa ityavadhātavyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre leśasaraḥ pañcasaptatitamaḥ
atha mudrālaṃkārasaraḥ
prakṛtārthaparaiśśabdairmudrā sūcyārthasūcanam |
vivakṣitārthapratipādanaparaiśśabdaissūcanīyasyārthasya sūcanaṃ mudrālaṃkāraḥ | ayamapi dīkṣitopajñameva | prakṛtārthapareṇa padena tadekadeśena vā padasamudāyena padaikadeśasamudāyena vā tadubhayasamudāyena vā yāthākathaṃcit jñāpanīyārthaviśeṣasya jñāpanaṃ mudreti nirgalito'rthaḥ | padaikadeśastu prakṛte arthavānvā nirarthakovā'stu | sūcyārthaśca prakṛto'prakṛtovā'stu | na tatrābhiniveṣṭavyam ||
yathāvā
bhajamanasāgopetā niyatarṣigaṇāḥ padorbhajante yām | bhṛgujagaṇajāṃ yatīḍyāṃ vidyāmaviṣamajayāṃ sugītimimām || 1734 ||
atra lakṣmīvarṇanapare padye gītivṛttaparatayā'rthāntaramapi vivakṣitam | tatra lakṣmīparatve evamartho niyatarṣigaṇāḥ niyatāḥ niyamitendriyā ekāgramanasā iti yāvat | ekāntina iti vā | ye ṛṣigaṇāḥ āgo'petāḥ āgasaḥ aparādhāt apetāḥ niraparādhāssanta ityarthaḥ | 'apetāpoḍha' iti pañcamīsamāsaḥ | yāṃ śriyaṃ padoḥ caraṇayoḥ bhajante sevante | bhṛgujagaṇajāṃ bhṛgujaḥ bhṛgunāmnaḥ ṛṣeḥ jātaḥ yo gaṇaḥ dhātṛvidhātṛprabhṛtitatputrapautrapramukhassaṃtānavargaḥ tasmin jātāṃ prādurbhūtāṃ
devau dhātṛvidhātārau bhṛgoḥ khyātirasūyata |
śriyaṃ ca devadevasya patnī nārāyaṇasya yā ||
iti viṣṇupurāṇokteḥ | yatīḍyāṃ yatibhiḥ maskaribhiḥ īḍyāṃ stavyāṃ aviṣamajayāṃ aviṣamaḥ nyāyyaḥ sarvasaṃpratipanna iti yāvat | jayaḥ utkarṣo yasyāstāṃ ataeva sugītiṃ gīyata iti gītiḥ gānaṃ śobhanā gītiḥ yasyāstāṃ praśastakīrtimiti yāvat | imāṃ vidyāṃ ātmavidyāṃ śriyamityarthaḥ | 'ātmavidyā ca devi tvaṃ vimuktiphalasādhanī | vidyāsahāyavantaṃ mām' ityādibhirvidyāśabdena śriyo'bhidhānāt | vidyetyatra kāryakāraṇayorabhedopacāraḥ | manasā bhaja upāsveti kasyacidguroḥ kaṃcidantevāsinaṃ pratyanuśāsanam ||
gītivṛttapakṣe tu  yāṃ gītiṃ gopetāḥ gasaṃjñakena guruṇā upetāḥ yuktāḥ asya ante ityetadādiḥ avasāne guruṇā upetā ityarthaḥ | te ca aniyatāḥ kramaniyamarahitāḥ yathāyathaṃ prayuktā ityarthaḥ |
jñeyāssarvādimadhyāntaguravo'tra catuṣkalāḥ |
gaṇāścaturlaghūpetāḥ pañcāryādiṣu saṃsthitāḥ ||
iti sarvādimadhyāntagurūṇāṃ caturlaghūpetānāṃ caturlaghurūpagaṇāntarasahitānāmaniyamenāryāvṛtteṣu prayogasyānuśāsanāt | atra dviguruka eko gaṇaḥ | ādimadhyāntaguravaḥ bhagaṇajagaṇasagaṇāstrayaḥ caturlaghuka eka ityevaṃ pañcagaṇā yathāsaṃbhavamāryādiṣu prayojyā iti
kārikārthaḥ | te ca katītyatrāharṣīti | ṛṣisaṃkhyākā gaṇāḥ saptagaṇā ityarthaḥ | gopetāśca te aniyatarṣigaṇāśceti karmadhārayaḥ | te ca ke ityata āha  bhajamanasā iti | bhakārādisaṃjñakāḥ | kva ca te prayojyā ityata āha  padoriti | padoḥ ślokacaturthāṃśayoḥ pūrvottarārdharūpayordvaryoḥ pādayorityarthaḥ | `paddan' iti pādaśabdasya padādeśaḥ | bhajante śrayante | atha catuṣkalānāṃ gaṇānāmaniyamena yathāyathaṃ prāptau niyamayati  bhṛgujeti | bhṛgujaḥ paraśurāmaḥ sa ca matsyādibhagavadavatāreṣu ṣaṣṭhatayā gaṇitaḥ | tena praṣṭha iti labhyate | bhṛgujaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ gaṇaḥ jaḥ jasaṃjñakaḥ yasyāstāṃ bhṛgujagaṇajāṃ jagaṇātmakaṣaṣṭhagaṇāmityarthaḥ | anena ṣaṣṭhena jagaṇena bhāvyamiti niyama uktaḥ | aviṣamajayāṃ viṣame viṣamāṃśe viṣamagaṇasthāne jaṃ jagaṇaṃ yātīti viṣamajayāḥ yāteḥ kvip | sā na bhavatītyaviṣamajayā tāṃ tathoktāṃ prathamatṛtīyādiviṣamāṃśeṣu jagaṇaśūnyāmityarthaḥ | anena viṣamasthāneṣu jagaṇo na prayojya iti niyama uktaḥ | yatibhiḥ vicchedaiḥ 'vicchedo yatisaṃjñakaḥ' ityanuśāsanāt | īḍyāṃ
ramaṇīyavicchedayutāmityarthaḥ | 'kṛtayatiśobhām' iti tallakṣaṇe vakṣyamāṇatvāt | imāṃ sugītiṃ śobhanāṃ gītiṃ gītyākhyāmāryāmityarthaḥ | vidyāṃ jānīyām | viderlīṅuttamaikavacanam | atra
lakṣmaitatsaptagaṇā gopetā bhavati neha viṣame jaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhoyaṃ na laghū vā prathame'rdhe niyatamāryāyāḥ ||
ityuktāryāprathamārdhalakṣaṇasyaiva uttarārdhe'pi vidyamānatvāt |
aryāprathamadaloktaṃ yadi kathamapi lakṣaṇaṃ bhavedubhayoḥ |
dalayoḥ kṛtayatiśobhāṃ tāṃ gītiṃ gītavān bhujaṅgeśaḥ ||
ityuktaṃ gītilakṣaṇaṃ sarvamapi suspaṣṭam ||
atra śrīlakṣmīvarṇanapareṇa sugītimiti padena asya vṛttasya gītināmatāyāḥ bhajamanasā ityādinā tadgaṇaniyamādīnāṃ ca sūcyānāmarthānāṃ sūcanamiti mudrālaṃkāraḥ prakṛtāprakṛtaśleṣojjīvitaḥ | na cātra prakṛtāprakṛtaśleṣa eva na mudrālaṃkāra iti vācyaṃ, śleṣasadbhāve'pi sūcyārthasūcanasyāpi sphuṭatayā mudrālaṃkārasya nirargalatvāt | nāpyasya mudrālaṃkārasya śleṣato viviktaviṣayadaurlabhyabhramaḥ kāryaḥ, anupadamave viviktasya viṣayasya darśayiṣyamāṇatvāt | tasmādatra śleṣamudrālaṃkārayossaṃkara eveti dik ||
yadyapyatrālaṃkāragranthe vṛttanāmno'vaśyaṃ nāsti sūcanīyatvaṃ, tathā'pyasya padyasya evaṃvidhalakṣaṇābhinnalakṣyaghaṭitacchandaśśāstramadhyapāṭhe tasya sūcanīyatvaṃ saṃbhavatīti lakṣaṇānugatiḥ kāryā | ata eva
sūcyārthasūcanaṃ mudrā prakṛtārthaparaiḥ padaiḥ |
nityambagurvī taruṇī dṛgyugmavipulā ca sā ||
atra nāyikāvarṇanapareṇa yugmavipulāpadane asyānuṣṭubho yugmavipulānāmatvarūpassūcyārthasūcanaṃ mudrā | yadyapyatra granthe vṛtta
nāmno nāsti sūcanīyatvaṃ, tathā'pyasyottarārdhasya lakṣyalakṣaṇayuktacchandaśśāstramadhyapātitve tasya sūcanīyatvamastīti tadabhiprāyeṇa lakṣaṇaṃ yojyam" iti kuvalayānandagranthassaṃgaccheta | atra chandaśśāstramadhyapātitveneti tṛtīyāntatayā keṣucitkośeṣvarvāktaneṣu dṛśyamānaḥ pāṭho'svacchaḥ, bhūyassu prācīnakośeṣu madhyapātitve iti saptamyantapāṭhasyaiva darśanāt tathaiva tadvyākhyāyāṃ rasikarañjanyāṃ vyākhyātatvācca | tathāca tadgranthaḥ  "nanvatra taruṇīvṛttāntavarṇanaprastāve vṛttasyālakṣaṇīyatayā tannāmnassūcanīyatvābhāvānneha mudrālaṃkāra ityākṣipati  yadyapīti | satyaṃ, nātra vṛttanāma sūcanīyaṃ, tathā'pi ‘yasyāṃ lassaptamo yugme sā yugmavipulā matā' iti vipulālakṣaṇottarārdhe yadi 'nitambagurvī taruṇī' ityuttarārdhamudāharaṇaṃ bhavet tadā tava vṛttasya sūcanīyatvamastītyabhipretyātra mudrālaṃkārodāharaṇamiti pariharati tathā'pīti" | etena chandaśśāstramadhyapātitveneti tṛtīyāntapāṭhameva bahumanyamānāḥ ‘nitambagurvī' ityādyuttarārdhasya lakṣyalakṣaṇayuktacchandaśśāstramadhyapātina evātra lakṣyatayodāhṛtatvāttādṛśasyaiva prastutasūcyārthasūcino mudrālaṃkāratvaṃ na tu tadanyakāvyāntargatasyāprastutasūcyārthasūcanasyāpītyāgṛhṇānāḥ kecitparāstāḥ | sati ca tṛtīyāntatayā pāṭhe upadarśitarītyā vyākhyānavyākhyeyayoranyonyamukhānavalokanāpatteḥ |
yeṣāṃ candrāloke dṛśyante lakṣyalakṣaṇaślokāḥ |
prāyasta eva teṣāmitareṣāmabhinavā viracyante ||
iti jayadevapraṇītacandrālokādṛśyamānānāmalaṃkārāṇāṃ kuvalayānandakāraireva lakṣyalakṣaṇayuktaślokapraṇayanasya pratijñātatayā atra
granthāntaragatapadyaghaṭanāvācoyukterayuktatvāt | tādṛśalakṣyalakṣaṇayuktacchandrogranthasyāprasiddheśca | vastutastu prakṛtārthasyaiva sūcanaṃ mudrālaṃkāra iti paramāgrahaḥ | prakṛtasya vā aprakṛtasya vā sūcyasyārthasya sūcanaṃ mudrālaṃkāra ityeva grāhyam | 'sucyārthasūcanaṃ mudrā prakṛtārthaparaiḥ padaiḥ' iti hi taccharīram | tathāca kavi saṃrambhānurodhenatādṛśalakṣyalakṣaṇayuktaprabandhānantaḥpātitve'pi tadatiriktakāvyeṣu yatrayatra 'kṛtvā hāṭakatāṭakeyahariṇe śārdūlavikrīḍitam | paryāyeṇa stanakalaśayoraṅgulīyaṃ madīyam' ityādau aprakṛtavṛttālaṃkārādisūcane'pyakṣata evāyamalaṃkāraḥ | anyathā rāmādicaritaṃ grathayitṝṇāṃ kavikulāgrayātṝṇāṃ mahatā prayatnena yatrakvavā vṛttālaṃkārādisūcakaśabdaprayogavaicitryavaiyarthyāpatteḥ | alaṃkārakaustubhakṛto bukkapattanaveṃkaṭāryāstu
prakṛtārthaparaiśśabdaiḥ kavinā'prakṛtaṃ yadi |
sūcyaṃ sūcyeta jagadustadā mudrāmalaṃkṛtim ||
ityaprakṛtasūcyārthasūcanameva mudretyamumalaṃkāraṃ lakṣayantaḥ
'nīlaśrīralakeṣu śaṅkhagarimā kaṇṭhe kuce mākarī
rekhā kundavaradyutissmitasudhākānteṣu danteṣu ca |
pādau kacchapabhūṣitau karayugaṃ yasyāssapadmaṃ mahā
padmā''nandamayī mukundaramaṇī sadyaḥ prasadyānmama ||
ityudāhṛtya 'atra nīlādiśabdairnidhinavakasūcanam' ityaprastutaṃ nidhinavakamevāsūsucan | na hi lakṣmīvarṇanaprastāve nidhinavakasūcanaṃ prastutam | anyatsarvaṃ prapañcitaṃ haṃsasaṃdeśarasāsvādinyāṃ 'paryāyeṇa stanakalaśayoḥ' ityatrāsmābhiḥ ||
yathāvā
hārasameto bhogī rāhoḥ kālo ravipravekeṣu | gaganacarāṇāṃ vāreṣvāste'ntara eṣa santatamariṣṭaḥ ||
hāreṇa vaijayantīrūpeṇa sametaḥ bhogī niratiśayabhogavān bhūmni praśaṃsāyāmatiśayane vā matvarthīyaḥ | rāhoḥ kālaḥ śatruḥ bhagavān ravipravekeṣu ādityādiṣu gaganacarāṇāṃ diviṣadāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca vāreṣu samūheṣu vāsareṣu ca | antaraḥ antassthitaḥ 'ya eṣo'ntarāditye | ya āditye tiṣṭhannādityādantaraḥ | sarvāntaraḥ' ityādiśruteḥ | āste | eṣaḥ sarvāntaryāmitayā'vasthito'pi saṃtataṃ nityaṃ riṣṭaṃ aśubhaṃ tannāstyasyetyariṣṭaḥ apahatapāpmetyarthaḥ | sarvāntaryāmitve'pi tadgatadoṣagandhāsaṃspṛṣṭa iti bhāvaḥ | atrādityādivāsareṣu rāhukālahārasametobhogīti kacaṭādivarṇajñāpyasaṃkhyākrameṇāṣṭamadvitīyādyardhayāmaparimāṇa āste | tadyathā ravivāre aṣṭamo'rdhayāmo rāhuḥ kālaḥ | induvāre dvitīyo'rdhayāmassa ityādi draṣṭavyam | sa ca saṃtatamariṣṭaḥ kāryeṣvaśubhada iti jyotiśśāstramaryādāyāssūcyāyāssūcanam ||
yathāvā
natasamanavanirnidrāṃ nalinekṣaṇahṛdayavasatimakṣudrām | kāmapi karuṇāmudrāṃ pathyāmāryāṃ śraye bhadrām || 1736 ||
atra pathyāmāryāmiti padābhyāmarthavadbhyāmasya padyasya
triṣvaṃśakeṣu pādo dalayorādyeṣu dṛśyate yasyāḥ |
pathyeti nāma tasyāśchandovidbhissamākhyātam ||
ityuktalakṣaṇapathyāryānāmatvarūpasūcyārthasūcanam ||
evaṃ ‘kāmapi karuṇāmudrām' ityatra mudrāpadenāsmin padye pratipāditasyālaṃkārasyoktalakṣaṇamudrānāmatvasūcanamarthavatpadaikadeśenetyanyā'pi mudrā ||
yathāvā
tvaddīptidhūtarocistaṭillatā | kvāpi hanta jīmūte | līnā'pi bhītibhārātpayodhinandinyabhūccapalā || 1737 ||
kvāpi jīmūte jalade parvate ca 'jīmūtau meghaparvartau' ityamaraḥ | atra capaletyarthavatā śliṣṭena padena asya padyasya
ubhayārdhayorjakārau dvitīyaturyau gamadhyagau yasyāḥ |
capaleti nāma tasyāḥ prakīrtitaṃ nāgarājena ||
iti lakṣitagurudvayamadhyagajagaṇātmakadvitīyacaturthagaṇavadubhayārthaśālicapalākhyāryānāmatālakṣaṇasūcyārthasūcanam ||
yathāvā
yasya na karuṇāleśastasyānantaṃ vṛṣācaleśa sukham | bhajasi kṛpāvivaśastvaṃ paraduḥkhanirācikīrṣayā kleśam || 1738 ||
atra karuṇāleśavaidhuryarūpadoṣasyānantasukhavattvarūpaguṇatvakalpanaṃ kṛpāpāravaśyarūpaguṇasya paraduḥkhanirācikīrṣānimittakakleśavattārūpadoṣatvakalpanaṃ cetyanupadoktalakṣaṇaleśālaṃkāraḥ | sa ca
anarthakapadaikadeśarūpaleśaśabdena tannāmno jñāpanīyasya jñāpanam ||
yathāvā
navanalinākṣaḥ phaṇigiridurganivāse nisargataḥ kutukī | kṛṣṇaghanajiṣṇurucirākṛtissa lakṣmīpatiściraṃ jīyāt || 1739 ||
atra pādacatuṣṭayādyavarṇayugmarūpārthavatpadairmilitaiḥ
'navadurgakṛṣṇakṛtiṃḥ' iti prabandhakartṛnāmnassūcanīyasya sūcanam ||
atra nakṣatramālādistutivatkenāpyakṣuṇṇacarī kā'pi śrīhayagrīvadevadivyamahiṣīviṣayā alaṃkaraṇamālikākhyā bavabālavādikaraṇanāmasūcanī asmābhireva praṇītā stutirudāhriyate | tasyā ime ādye padye
kamalavanāvalibhogyaṃ kalaśapayorāśirājasaubhāgyam | kimapi hayānanabhāgyaṃ kalayatu māṃ svaguṇavinutikṛtiyogyam || 1740 ||
haraṇāyākhilavipadāṃ karaṇānyekādaśāpi viniyamya | ślokairekādaśabhirlokeśitrīṃ stuve prasīdatu sā || 1741 ||
ekādaśasaṃkhyākāni karaṇāni indriyāṇi | pakṣe bavabālavetyādicarasthirasaṃjñakāni karaṇāni tānyapyekādaśasaṃkhyākānyeva | viniyamya viṣayebhyaḥ pratyāhṛtya | padyānāmekadeśeṣu grathittvā ca | atra karaṇānyekādaśāpi viniyamyeti padaiḥ śliṣṭairarthavadbhiḥ vakṣyamāṇastutipadyeṣu krameṇa karaṇanāmnāṃ sūcanaṃ bhaviṣyatīti sūcitam ||
aṃba vadhūmaṇi bhavatīmamburuhākṣo'pi vakṣasi bibharti | kiṃ bahunā'khilajagatāṃ tvaṃ bahumānyā sudhāmbunidhikanyā || 1742 ||
atra aṃbavadhū itipadaikadeśābhyāṃ anarthakābhyāṃ bavakaraṇanāmarūpasūcyārthasya prasyāyanam ||
nirupamavaratamasuguṇāṅkuranikuruṃbālavālabhūtāṃ tvām | turagavadanasya taruṇīṃ taruṇāmbujalocanāṃ śraye śaraṇam || 1743 ||
atra nikurumbālavāletyanarthakapadaikadeśābhyāṃ bālavakaraṇanāmasūcanam ||
śubhadāyakau lavaṇimaprabhavau nanu janani tava viditavibhavau | pādau vitīrṇamodau śrīdau syātāṃ mamābjadāyādau || 1744 ||
lavaṇimā lāvaṇyaṃ 'lavaṇimaguṇanāṇīyastarāmbhodhiveṇī' iti śṛṅgārataraṅgiṇīprayogāt | atra dāyakau lavaṇimetyanarthakapadaikadeśābhyāṃ kaulavakaraṇanāmasūcanam ||
haridarśanasavikasanaṃ hāri mamāmodi ṣaṭpadollasanam | hṛdayābmujaṃ śaraṇyā hayamukhadayitaitu lalitalāvaṇyā || 1745 ||
hareḥ bhagavataḥ bhānośca darśanena savikasanaṃ unnidraṃ ullāsi ca | hāri manoharaṃ āmodi pramodavat surabhi ca | ulasantyasminni
yullasanam | ṣaṭpadasya śaraṇāgatimantrasya ṣaṭpadānāṃ bhramarāṇāṃ ca ullasanam | anyatsugamam | atra dayitaitu lalitetyarthavannirarthakapadaikadeśasamudāyena taitulakaraṇanāmasūcanam ||
śiśiramayūkhāvarajaṃ vaśitahayagrīvamamṛtasāgarajam | bhajatāṃ bhavabharatāpaṃ nayatāṃ naḥ kimapi dhāma tallopam || 1746 ||
śiśiramayūkhaḥ avarajaḥ yasya tat, bhavabharatāpaṃ lopaṃ nayatāmityanvayaḥ | atra sāgarajamityanarthakena padaikadeśena garajakaraṇanāma sūcitam ||
kaścit vaṇigjana iva nyāsadhanī maugdhyamamba tava vīkṣya | tvayi vikrīyāṇutamaṃ svamaho labhate mahāvibhūtiṃ tvat || 1747 ||
nyāsadhanī bharanyāsadhanavān nyāsarūpadhanavāṃśca | nyāso nāma kenacit rakṣaṇāyānyavaśe upanihitaṃ vastu ‘pumānupanidhirnyāsaḥ' ityamaraḥ | nyasyata iti nyāsaḥ karmaṇi ghañ | maugadhyaṃ saundaryaṃ mauḍhyaṃ ca aṇutamaṃ aṇīyassvarūpaṃ atyalpaṃ ca | svaṃ ātmānaṃ dhanaṃ ca | vikrīya vinimitaṃ kṛtvā ca mahāvibhūtiṃ śrīnivāsaṃ mahatīṃ sampadaṃ ca labhate | atra vaṇigjetyanarthakena padaikadeśena vaṇigjakaraṇanāmasūcanam ||
bhuvanāvananirnidrā savanāśananāthakṛtanutikṛpārdrā | dhuvatātkācinmudrā bhavatāpaṃ bhavyadāyinī bhadrā || 1748 ||
atra bhadrāśabdenārthavatā bhadrakaraṇanāmasūcanam | mudreti mudrālaṃkārasyāpi sūcanamiti viśeṣaḥ ||
śakunidhvajāvibhājyaṃ sakalaṃ tatkimapi tasya sāmrājyam | dattāṃ vibhavaṃ prājyaṃ tattādṛśamakhilavibudhaparipūjyam || 1749 ||
atra śakunītyarthavatpadaikadeśena śakunikaraṇanāmnassūcanam ||
sucaturamatirapi yaste na caraṇakamaladvayaṃ namejjātu | acatuṣpātkhara eva sa na ca viśayo'trāsti vacananicayaiḥ kim || 1750 ||
atra catuṣpāditi padaikadeśenārthavatā catuṣpātkaraṇanāmasūcanam ||
nāgavarabhogabāhau rāgavatī bhagavatīha vāgīśe | drāgavatātpracurabhavodvegavato'smān kṛpājharī kā'pi || 1751 ||
atra nāgavareti padaikadeśabhūtena nāgaveti varṇatrayeṇa nāgavākhyakaraṇanāmasūcanam ||
bhavabharatapartutāpā bhavanti te'mī ciraṃ na sāṭopāḥ | kamalāpāṅgā mānasakekiṃstu ghnantyamūn ghanasmerāḥ || 1752 ||
he mānasakekin ! cittamayūreti saṃbuddhiḥ | te'mī bhavabharatapartutāpāḥ grīṣmāgamoṣmāṇaḥ ciraṃ sāṭopāḥ sāvaṣṭambhāḥ na bhavanti | kuta ityata āha  kamaleti | ghanasmerāḥ prāvṛṣeṇyajaladamanoharāḥ | idaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ tāpāṭopalopanaipuṇyābhiprāyagarbham |
kamalāyāḥ śriyaḥ apāṅgāḥ kaṭākṣāḥ amūn tāpān ghnanti | tuśabdo'vadhāraṇe ‘syurevaṃtupunarvaivetyavadhāraṇavācakāḥ' ityamaraḥ | ghnantyevetyarthaḥ | ataste na ciraṃ sthāsyantīti bhāvaḥ | atra kekiṃstughnantītyānupūrvyā arthavannirarthakapadaikadeśobhayasamudāyarūpayā kiṃstughneti karaṇanāmasūcanam ||
kalyāṇaguṇasyūtāṃ kāntyāśleṣeṇa santataṃ sphītām | tanutāṃ hṛdyāṃ jagatāṃ mātā'laṃkaraṇamālikāmetām || 1753 ||
jagatāṃ mātā śrīḥ kalyāṇaguṇaiḥ suvarṇatantubhiḥ pakṣe maṅgalaiḥ śriyaḥ guṇaiḥ sauśīlyavātsalyādibhiḥ syūtāṃ kānteḥ āśleṣeṇa pakṣe kāntyā ‘atyujjvalatvaṃ bandhasya kāvye kāntiritīṣyate' ityuktalakṣaṇena śleṣeṇa ‘mithassaṃśliṣṭapadatā śleṣa ityabhidhīyate' ityuktalakṣaṇena ca kavyotkarṣahetubhūtena guṇena spītāṃ etāṃ alaṃkaraṇamālikāṃ bhūṣaṇamālātvenādhyavasitāṃ stutiṃ hṛdyāṃ vakṣassthāṃ hṛdayapriyāṃ ca tanutām | atra alaṃkaraṇamālikāmiti padena asyā stuteḥ bavabālavādikaraṇamālānāmaghaṭitatvarūpasūcyārthasūcanam ||
asyāḥ stuterayamantimaḥ ślokaḥ
eṣā mahiśūreśvarakṛṣṇakṣmāpālaratnavaṃśaguroḥ | uditā kṛtirvijayatāṃ śrīkṛṣṇabrahmatantraparakālāt || 1754 ||
yathāvā
saṃyogo'gre pāde bhavato varṇasya kasyacidvā
yassyāt | yukto'yamahigirīśvara kramo'tra paratastu gururapi laghuśca syāt || 1755 ||
he ahigirīśvara! agre parārthye ‘parārthyāgraprāgraharaprāgryāgryāgrīyamagriyam' ityamaraḥ | kasyacidvā yasyakasyāpi varṇasya brāhmaṇādeḥ bhavataḥ pāde caraṇe yaḥ saṃyogaḥ prapattikṛtassaṃbandhaḥ syāt | ayaṃ atra asmin loke yuktaḥ ucitaḥ kramaḥ ślāghanīyā paripāṭiḥ ‘śaktau caraṇavikṣepe paripāṭyāmapi kramaḥ' iti ratnamālā | ayaṃ kramaḥ yuktaḥ iti vā yojanā | paratastu tvaditarasmin deve saṃbandhastu gururapi śramādhikyena mahānapi laghuḥ alpaśca syāt | caśabdo'vadhāraṇe | aślāghya eva bhavedityarthaḥ | pakṣe pāde padyacaturthāṃśe agre ādau bhavataḥ vidyamānasya kasyacidvā yasyakasyāpi vā varṇasya kakārāderakṣarasya yassaṃyogaḥ 'halo'nantarāssaṃyogaḥ' ityuktalakṣaṇaṃ saṃyuktatvaṃ syāt bhavet | yuktaḥ saṃyuktaḥ ayaṃ saṃyogaḥ kramaḥ kramasaṃjñakaḥ | atra asmin kramasaṃjñake varṇe paratastu paratra vidyamāne tu gururapi 'saṃyoge guru' ityuktalakṣaṇasaṃyuktavarṇaparakatvahetukagurutvaśālī pūrvavarṇo'pi laghuśca ‘hṛsvaṃ laghu' ityuktalakṣaṇalāghavaśālyapi syāt | laghuścetyanena kvacideveti jñāpyate | yathoktaṃ
pādādāviha varṇasya saṃyogaḥ kramasaṃjñakaḥ |
parasthitena tena sthāllaghutā'pi kvacidguroḥ ||
iti | yathā atraiva padye caturthapādādimavarṇe saṃyogarūpe vidyamāne'pi tatpūrvavarṇasya laghutvamavaśyameṣṭavyam | 'alpavyayena sundari grāmyajano mṛṣṭamaśnāti' itivat | anyadhā chandobhaṅgāditi
draṣṭavyam | atra krama iti padena caturthapādādimabhūtena svādibhūtakakārarephasaṃyogasya etatpadyapradarśitalakṣaṇalakṣitasya krama iti saṃjñeti sūcanīyo'rthassvānatiriktodāharaṇena tādṛśasaṃjñāprayojanena ca saṃha sūcitaḥ | iyaṃ ca prakṛtāprakṛtagocaraśleṣeṇa prāguktollāsena ca saṃkīryata iti viśeṣaḥ | turīyapādādimavarṇasya laghutaraprayatnenoccāraṇe padyasya visaṃṣṭhulatā'pi na dṛśyata iti dhyeyam | evaṃ navaratnamālāprabandhe tattadratnanāmaniveśanena tattadratnajātisūcanam | nakṣatramālāprabandhe agnyādidevatānāmabhirnakṣatranāmasūcanamityādāvayamevālaṃkāraḥ | evaṃ nāṭakeṣu vakṣyamāṇārthasūcaneṣvapīti dīkṣitāḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre mudrālaṃkārasaraṣṣaṭsaptatitamaḥ
<27>
atha ratnāvalīsaraḥ
prasiddhasahapāṭhānāmarthānāṃ nyasanaṃ yadi |
ratnāvalī sā vikhyātā sakramākramatābhidā ||
lokavedādiprasiddhasahapāṭhānāmarthānāṃ nyasanaṃ ratnāvalīnāmālaṃkāraḥ | sā ca sakramatvākramatvābhyāṃ dviprakārā | sakramatvaṃ ca prasiddhasahapāṭhānāmarthānāṃ krameṇa nyasanam | akramatvaṃ ca prasiddhakrame keṣāṃcidarthānāṃ vaikalyena kathanaṃ vaiparītyena kathanaṃ sahapāṭhasadbhāve'pi kramaprasiddhirāhityaṃ ca | prakāradvaye'pi kramikāṇāmarthānāṃ prakṛtānvayitvatadabhāvābhyāṃ pratyekaṃ dvaidhī | prakṛtānvayitvaṃ
draṣṭavyam | atra krama iti padena caturthapādādimabhūtena svādibhūtakakārarephasaṃyogasya etatpadyapradarśitalakṣaṇalakṣitasya krama iti saṃjñeti sūcanīyo'rthassvānatiriktodāharaṇena tādṛśasaṃjñāprayojanena ca saṃha sūcitaḥ | iyaṃ ca prakṛtāprakṛtagocaraśleṣeṇa prāguktollāsena ca saṃkīryata iti viśeṣaḥ | turīyapādādimavarṇasya laghutaraprayatnenoccāraṇe padyasya visaṃṣṭhulatā'pi na dṛśyata iti dhyeyam | evaṃ navaratnamālāprabandhe tattadratnanāmaniveśanena tattadratnajātisūcanam | nakṣatramālāprabandhe agnyādidevatānāmabhirnakṣatranāmasūcanamityādāvayamevālaṃkāraḥ | evaṃ nāṭakeṣu vakṣyamāṇārthasūcaneṣvapīti dīkṣitāḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre mudrālaṃkārasaraṣṣaṭsaptatitamaḥ
atha ratnāvalīsaraḥ
prasiddhasahapāṭhānāmarthānāṃ nyasanaṃ yadi |
ratnāvalī sā vikhyātā sakramākramatābhidā ||
lokavedādiprasiddhasahapāṭhānāmarthānāṃ nyasanaṃ ratnāvalīnāmālaṃkāraḥ | sā ca sakramatvākramatvābhyāṃ dviprakārā | sakramatvaṃ ca prasiddhasahapāṭhānāmarthānāṃ krameṇa nyasanam | akramatvaṃ ca prasiddhakrame keṣāṃcidarthānāṃ vaikalyena kathanaṃ vaiparītyena kathanaṃ sahapāṭhasadbhāve'pi kramaprasiddhirāhityaṃ ca | prakāradvaye'pi kramikāṇāmarthānāṃ prakṛtānvayitvatadabhāvābhyāṃ pratyekaṃ dvaidhī | prakṛtānvayitvaṃ
ca yathākathaṃcitprakṛtopamānoparañjakatādiprakāreṇa | tadananvayitvaṃ ca krameṇa tattannāmnāṃ yathākathaṃcit śleṣabhaṅgyā mudrālaṃkāravidhayā vā niveśanena bhavati | sarvamidamudāharaṇeṣu spaṣṭībhaviṣyati iyamapi dīkṣitopajñameva ||
tatra prakṛtānvayavatī sakramā yathā
vidhiriva sakalāgamavān haririva pītāmbaro vibhāti tarām | giriśa iva śirasi vahate sarasijanayanāṅghrisaritamahiśailaḥ || 1756 ||
sakalāḥ agamā taravaḥ āgamāḥ vedāśca tadvān | pītaṃ ambaraṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ ‘nisargodagreṇa prasabhamurasā pītagaganaḥ' itivat svābhogenākrāntagagana ityarthaḥ | sarasijanayanaḥ hariḥ tadaṅghrijāṃ saritaṃ ākāśagaṅgāṃ gaṅgāṃca | spaṣṭamanyat | atra sṛṣṭisthitilayakartṛtayā purāṇādau prasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāṇāṃ trayāṇāmupamānavidhayā prakṛtānvayināṃ krameṇa nyasanam ||
yathāvā
kṣitiriva titikṣurāsīssalilamivāpyāyano'nala iva tvam | tejasvānanila iva vyāpī viyadiva vinirmalaśśaure || 1757 ||
atra upamānarītyā prakṛtānvayināṃ ‘kṣityaptejomarudvyoma' iti purāṇādau sahapaṭhitānāṃ pañcānāṃ mahābhūtānāṃ krameṇa nyāsaḥ ||
saiva uparañjakatayā prakṛtānvayinī yathā
aṅkaritā sukṛtavaśātpallavitā deva tava guṇaśravaṇāt | sumitā ca tadāmananātphalitā dṛṣṭyā ca māmikā bhaktiḥ || 1758 ||
sumitā puṣpitā | dṛṣṭyā darśanena | atra bhaktau latātvāropaḥ | atrāṅkuritetyādinā aṅkurapallavakusumaphalānāṃ caturṇāṃ lokasiddhakramāṇāmuparañjakatayā vinyāsaḥ ||
svāmī tvaṃ śrīsacivassakalasuhṛtsarvahṛdyakośasthaḥ | cakradhara tadapi tava padamatidurgaṃ tvatprasādabalavidhuraiḥ || 1759 ||
he cakradhara ! cakraśabdena rāṣṭramapi gṛhyate ‘cakraṃ rāṣṭre'pi' ityamaraḥ | tvaṃ svāmī sarveśvaraḥ 'eṣa sarveśvaraḥ' ityādiśruteḥ | anena paratvamuktam | pakṣe kaścidrājetyarthaḥ | śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ sacivā sahāyo yasya sa tathoktaḥ ‘mantrī sahāyasacivau' ityamaraḥ | lakṣmyāssaciva iti vā | pakṣe śrīyuktasacivo mantrī yasya sa tathoktaḥ | anena puruṣakārasāṃnidhyena paramadayālutā sūcitā | sakalasuhṛt | ‘nivāsaśśaraṇaṃ suhṛdgatirnārāyaṇaḥ' iti śruteḥ | anena saulabhyamuktam | pakṣe sakalāḥ kalāsahitāḥ rājanayanipuṇā ityarthaḥ | tādṛśāḥ suhṛdo mitrāṇi yasya sa ityarthaḥ | sarveṣāṃ hṛdyaḥ hṛdayasthitaḥ kośaḥ tasmin tiṣṭhatīti tathoktaḥ 'padmakośapratīkāśam' ityārabhya ‘tasmin sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam' iti śruteḥ | anena vātsalyaṃ vyañjitam | sa eṣa vātsalyākhyo guṇaḥ kaṭhavallyāmuktaḥ
aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo madhya ātmani tiṣṭhati |
īśāno bhūtabhavyasya na tato vijugupsate ||
iti | asyāśśruterarthaśca uktaviśeṣaṇaiḥ pratyabhijñāpyate | vātsalyaṃ nāma doṣatiraskāriṇī prītiḥ | pakṣe sakalajanahṛdayaṃgamahiraṇyādikośamadhyavartītyarthaḥ | evaṃ sakalacetanahṛdayastho'pi tvaṃ durdarśa ityāha  tadapītyādi | tadapi tathā'pi tava padaṃ svarūpaṃ tvatprasādabalavidhuraiḥ janaiḥ durgaṃ adhigantumaśakyaṃ 'taṃ durdarśaṃ gūḍhamanupraviṣṭaṃ, tadyathā hiraṇyanidhiṃ nihitamakṣetrajñā uparyupari saṃcaranto na vindeyuḥ' ityādiśrutibhyaḥ | pakṣe tat prasiddhaṃ tava padaṃ sthānamapi tvatprasādena balena sainyena ca vidhuraiḥ durgaṃ gantumaśakyamityarthaḥ | atra ‘svāmyamātyasuhṛtkośarāṣṭradurgabalāni ca' iti prasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ svāmyādīnāmuparañjakatayā prakṛtānvayināṃ saptānāṃ kramiko nyāsaḥ ||
yathāvā
harireṣa śacirdharmaśśritarakṣaśśrīḥ patiśca bhuvanānām | paripāti jagatprāṇaḥ puṇyajaneḍyaśśivaśca mādṛkṣān || 1760 ||
eṣa hariḥ indraḥ bhagavāṃśca| śuciḥ vahniḥ apahatapāpmā ca | dharmaḥ yamaḥ | pakṣe ‘kṛṣṇaṃ dharmaṃ sanātanam' ityuktarītyā dharmasvarūpaḥ | śritā rakṣasāṃ śrīḥ yena saḥ naiṛta ityarthaḥ | pakṣe śritān rakṣatīti śritarakṣī | karmaṇyaṇi ‘ṭiḍḍha' iti ṅīp | tādṛśī śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | anadīsaṃjñakatvānna kap | yadvā bhagavatpakṣe dharmaśritarakṣaśśrīrityekameva padam | dharmaṃ nyāsarūpaṃ śritān rakṣatīti dharmaśritarakṣī tādṛśī śrīḥ yasya saḥ | asmin pakṣe dharmaśritetyatra śakārasya 'anaci ca' iti vaikalpikaṃ dvitvam |
bhuvanānāṃ apāṃ patiḥ varuṇaḥ | pakṣe lokānāmadhināthaḥ | jagatprāṇaḥ samīraṇaḥ jagadujjīvayitā ca |
na prāṇena nāpānena martyo jīvati kaścana |
itareṇa tu jīvanti yasminnetāvubhau śritau ||
iti śruteḥ | puṇyajaneḍyaḥ kuberaḥ sukṛtijanastutaśca | śivaḥ śarvaḥ maṅgalamūrtiśca 'śāśvataṃ śivamacyutam' iti śruteḥ | atra prasiddhasahapāṭhānāmaṣṭānāmindrādilokapālānāmuparañjakatayā prakṛtānvayināṃ krameṇa nyasanam | uparañjakatā cāropyamāṇatā | taduktaṃ 'uparañjakatāmeti viṣayī rūpakaṃ tadā' iti | atra hi śrīnivāsasya indrāditayā uparañjanam ||
yathāvā
bhāsvān rājā pārthiva eṣa budho vākyapatiśca kāvyaprathitaḥ | maitryākhyātaścottama ucchritajayaketurāśritastvāṃ bhagavan || 1761 ||
pṛthivyāṃ viditaḥ pārthivaḥ ‘tatra viditaḥ' ityadhikāre ‘sarvabhūmipṛthivībhyāmaṇañau' ityañ | pakṣe pṛthivyāṃ jātaḥ pārthivaḥ | bhauma ityarthaḥ | kāvyena prathitaḥ svaviṣayakaprabandhaprakhyātakīrtirityarthaḥ | svapraṇītena kāvyena prakhyāta iti vā pakṣe kāvya iti prathitaḥ | maitryā sarvabhūtasuhṛttayā khyātaḥ | anyatra mitrasya bhānoḥ apatyaṃ maitriḥ maitririti ākhyātaḥ ravisutaśśanirityarthaḥ | uttamaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ | pakṣe unnataṃ ca tat tamaḥ rāhurityarthaḥ | 'tamastu rāhuḥ' ityamaraḥ | ucchritaḥ jayaketuḥ jayadhvajo yasya saḥ | pakṣe ucchritajayaścāsau ketuśca | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | atra bhāsvadādipadaiḥ varṇanīyasya
bhagavadāśritasya bhāsvadādyātmatā pratīyata iti prasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ bhāsvadādīnāṃ navānāṃ grahāṇāṃ krameṇa prakṛtānvayitayā nyāsaḥ ||
yathāvā
jahi kāmaṃ krodhaṃ jaya jahīhi lobhaṃ jahāhi mohaṃ ca | jahihi madaṃ matsaramapi visṛja harernanu kaṭākṣapātraṃ syāḥ || 1762 ||
atra prasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ kāmādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ samuccayālaṃkāravidhayā prakṛtānvayināṃ krāmikatvam ||
prakṛtānvayaśālinī akramā yathā
yamamārjaya niyamaṃ śraya jitāsano vindase sakhe brahma | prāṇāyāmapratyāhārī dhyāyan sadhāraṇasamādhiḥ || 1763 ||
atra 'yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhayo'ṣṭāvaṅgāni' iti pātañjalasūtroditakramāṇāṃ yamādīnāṃ samuccayavidhayā prakṛtānvayināṃ kiṃcitkramaviparyāsena nyasanam ||
pṛthvī saṃpattejo niravadhikaṃ sarvatomukhaṃ ca yaśaḥ | bhāti śubhasparśanatā tava guṇajātaṃ tvanantameva hare || 1764 ||
pṛthvī mahatī ‘oto guṇavacanāt' iti ṅīṣ | pakṣe bhūrityarthaḥ | tejaḥ prabhāvaḥ, pakṣe agniḥ | sarvatomukhaṃ viśvataḥ prasṛmaraṃ pakṣe jalam | śubhasparśanatā śubhaṃ sparśanaṃ dānaṃ yasya
sa tathoktaḥ | tasya bhāvaḥ | anyatra śubhaḥ yaḥ sparśanaḥ vāyuḥ
tasya bhāvaḥ | anantaṃ aparicchinaṃ, anyatra ākāśaḥ | atra pṛthivyādimahābhūtānāṃ pañcānāṃ kiṃcitkramavyatyayena pratipādanamuparañjakatayā prakṛtānvayitvaṃ ca ||
yathāvā
dhṛṣṭiṃ jayantamacyuta vijayaṃ siddhārthamarthasādhakatām | gatamāhuraśokaṃ tava girā sumantraṃ ca mantrapālaṃ ca || 1765 ||
he acyuta! dhṛṣṭiṃ dhairyaṃ jayantaṃ abhibhavantaṃ samarārambhe svajanān yoddhumavasthitānabhivīkṣya śokena tyaktadhairyamityarthaḥ | idaṃ ca ‘sīdanti mama gātrāṇi' ityādau spaṣṭam | vijayaṃ arjunaṃ tava girā gītārūpayā 'na tvevāhaṃ jātu nāsaṃ na tvaṃ neme janādhipāḥ' ityārabhya ‘mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ' ityantayā aśokaṃ tyaktaśokam | śobhano mantraḥ ālocanaṃ yasya tathābhūtaṃ mantraṃ caramaślokarūpaṃ pālayatīti tathoktaṃ 'nidhipāyāpramādine' ityuktarītyā mantraṃ gopāyitāram | arthasya śatruvijayarūpaprayojanasya sādhakatāṃ gataṃ prāptaṃ ata eva siddhaḥ arthaḥ prayojanaṃ yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ āhuḥ janā iti śeṣaḥ | gītopadeśena tyaktamohatayā yathāvat kṣatradharmamanuṣṭhāya labdhavijayamityarthaḥ | atra
dhṛṣṭirjayanto vijayassiddhārtho hyarthasādhakaḥ |
aśoko mantrapālaśca sumantraścāṣṭamo'bhavat ||
iti śrīrāmāyaṇe prasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ raghukulasacivabhūtadhṛṣṭyādīnāmaṣṭānāṃ yatkiṃcitkramaviparyāsena prakṛtānvayināṃ varṇanamuparañjakatayeti dhyeyam ||
indra iva pālitasvasthemā śamana iva dakṣiṇānugataḥ | dhanada iva rājarājo bhavadīyo varuṇa iva ghanaraseśaḥ || 1766 ||
pālitaḥ svasthemā svargasthairyaṃ svasya dhanasya sthairyaṃ ca | dakṣiṇā dakṣiṇadik tayā anugataḥ tadīśa iti yāvat | dakṣiṇaiḥ udārairjanaiḥ anugataśca | rājarājaḥ rājarājanāmā rājādhirājaśca | ghanarasasya salilasya īśaḥ | rasāyāḥ īśaḥ bhūpatiḥ ghanaścāsau raseśaśceti vigrahaḥ | spaṣṭamanyat | atrendrādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ lokapālānāṃ kiṃcitkramavyatyayena kathanaṃ upamānavidhayā prakṛtānvayitvaṃ ca | idamevāṣṭānāṃ lokapālānāṃ madhye caturṇāmeva nibandhanāt kramavaikalyasyāpyudāharaṇam ||
dviradamukhasattvahṛdyo bhujagāhvayabhūṣaṇo bhuvi girīśaḥ | vanamālī ca vicitraṃ bhāti sarojātabhāvitābhyudayaḥ || 1767 ||
dviradamukhaiḥ gajaprabhṛtibhissattvaiḥ jantubhiḥ | anyatra dviradamukhaḥ vighnarājaḥ tasya satvena sthityā hṛdyaḥ | bhujaga iti āhvayaḥ nāma bhūṣaṇaṃ yasya | anyatra bhujagāhvayāni phaṇirūpāṇi bhūṣaṇāni yasya sa tathoktaḥ | girīśaḥ śailarājaḥ śivaśca | vanamālī kānanapaṅktimāṃśca bhagavānapīti ca | sarojātena kāsāranivahena pakṣe sarojāte padme bhāvitaḥ abhyudayaḥ ucchrayaḥ āvirbhāvaśca yasya sa tathoktaḥ | atra prasiddhakramāṇāṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivānāṃ sarvathā vaiparītyena kathanam | uparañjanavidhayā prakṛtānvayitvaṃ ca ||
gārutmatakarasevyaṃ muktālambaṃ pravālasukumāram | paśyema padmarāgaṃ caraṇaṃ śrīśasya vajrapāṇinutam ||
atra sahapāṭhasadbhāve'pi kramaprasiddhirahitānāṃ gārutmatādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ ratnānāṃ uparañjanādividhayā yathākathaṃcitprakṛtānvayināṃ varṇanamitīyamapi ratnāvalī akramarūpaiva ||
yathāvā
ghanaśṛṅgasaṅgatāṅgāścaṭulakaliṅgāstathā pṛthulavaṅgāḥ | pracuramahācchavidarbhāssusaṃniveśāḥ phaṇīśagirideśāḥ || 1769 ||
ghanāni śṛṅgāṇi taiḥ | pakṣe ghanaṃ śṛṅgaṃ prādhānyaṃ tena saṃgataṃ aṅgaṃ yeṣāṃ te | pakṣe ghanaśṛṅgasaṃgatāḥ aṅgāḥ tannāmāno deśāḥ yeṣāṃ te ‘śṛṅgaṃ prādhānyasānvośca' ityamaraḥ | deśeṣu madhye aṅgasya prathamaparigaṇanātprādhānyam | caṭulāḥ kaliṅgāḥ khagaviśeṣāḥ kaliṅgadeśāśca yeṣu te pṛthavaḥ mahāntaḥ lavaṅgāḥ lavaṅgataravo yeṣu te | pṛthulāḥ viśālāḥ vaṅgāḥ tannāmāno deśaviśeṣāśca yeṣu te | spaṣṭamanyat | atrāṅgādideśatādātmyaṃ phaṇigirideśānāṃ pratīyata iti kramaprasiddhirahitānāmaṅgādideśānāṃ nyasanaṃ prakṛtānvayi ||
prakṛtānanvayavatī sakramā ratnāvalī yathā
ghanaghṛṇimaṇiprabhāvati kāmaṃ trātuṃ jaganti tāpebhyaḥ | āvirabhūrharibhūbhṛti yatrotsā hastivarada bhānti himāḥ || 1770 ||
he hastivarada! ghanāḥ sāndrāḥ ghṛṇayaḥ kiraṇāḥ yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ maṇīnāṃ prabhā asminnastīti tathokte jaladacchaviratnaprabhāvatīti vā haribhūbhṛti veṃkaṭādrau jaganti kāmaṃ tāpebhyaḥ trātuṃ āvirabhūḥ | yatra girau himāḥ śiśirāḥ utsāḥ prasravaṇāni 'utsaḥ prasravaṇam' ityamaraḥ | bhāntīti tadgirau bhagavata āvirbhāvasya tāpanivartanaupayikatvamuktam | atra prabhāvamantrotsāhānāṃ trayāṇāṃ prasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ mudrālaṃkāravidhayā prakṛtānanvayināṃ krameṇa nyasanam | evamuttarodāharaṇeṣvapi ||
yathāvā
nalinākṣa yaḥ phaṇīśvaraśikharisthānanta tāvakīnānām | caraṇābjareṇuviśamitakaluṣo nīvṛddhi sa khalu vāsārhaḥ || 1771 ||
nīvṛt janapadaḥ | atra kṣayasthānavṛddhīnāṃ prāguktarītyā krameṇa nyāsaḥ ||
yathāvā
ānarcābjabhavo'yaṃ vinayajuṣā mānasena sāmnāyam | so'vyādṛgādirūpo bhavyātmā divyasaindhavo bhagavān || 1772 ||
sāmnāyaṃ sasaṃpradāyaṃ ‘athāmnāyassaṃpradāye' ityamaraḥ | ṛgādirūpaḥ 'ṛgyajussāmarūpāya' iti tanmantravarṇāt | divyasaindhavaḥ śrīhayagrīvaḥ atra ‘ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni yo'dhīte sakṛdañjasā' ityādiśāstrapratītasahapaṭhanānāmṛgyajussāmnāṃ krameṇa nyasanam | ṛgādirūpapadena uktavidhakramanibandho'tra sūcanīyassūcita iti mudrālaṃkāraśiraskatā tu viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
aridamanadakṣiṇāhava nīyante sma dviṣo'pi tava dhāma param | ahameva gārhapatye bhava iha kiyadavadhi nātha bhavatā''yāsye || 1773 ||
aridamane dakṣiṇaḥ caturaḥ āhavaḥ yuddhaṃ yasya tasya saṃbuddhiḥ he nātha! dviṣo'pi caidyādayo'pi bhavatā tava paraṃ dhāma nīyante sma | ahameva sarvātmanā tvāṃ prapanna eva | iha bhave saṃsāre gārhapatye gṛhasthadharmopalakṣite tattadāśramakarmaṇi gṛhapateḥ karma gārhapatyamiti vigrahaḥ | 'patyantapurohitādibhyo yak' iti karmarūpārthe yak | kiyadavadhi bhavatā āyāsye āyāsito bhavāmi vā na jāne iti bhāvaḥ | atra dakṣiṇāhavanīyagārhapatyānāṃ kramikatvam ||
yathāvā
kṛtamacyuta tīrthagaṇaiḥ kṛtaṃ ca nastretayā'pi sevitayā | syādvā paro'tra bhavato bhavatoyadhitāraṇe kalitadīkṣaḥ || 1774 ||
kṛtaṃ alam | atra kṛtādīnāṃ yugānāṃ caturṇāṃ krameṇa mudrālaṃkārarītyā prakṛtānanvayināṃ nyasanamiti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
devobhirāmalakṣmaṇavakṣā rakṣārthamahigirau jagatām | avatīrya kṛpābharataḥ sphurati harirvinatanityaśatrughnaḥ || 1775 ||
abhirāmaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ lakṣmīrasminnastīti lakṣmaṇaṃ, pāmāditvānmatvarthīye napratyaye ‘lakṣmyā acca' iti lakṣmīśabdekārasya atvaṃ tato ṇatvaṃ ca | īdṛśaṃ vakṣo yasya sa tathoktaḥ | kṛpābharataḥ dayābhārāt | sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | vinatānāṃ nityaśatravaḥ kāmādayaḥ tān hantīti vinatanityaśatrughnaḥ mūlavibhujāditvāddhanteḥ kapratyayaḥ | atra rāmādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ śleṣottambhitamudrālaṃkārarītyā prakṛtānanvayināṃ krameṇa nyasanam ||
yathāvā
lasati hasitātasītā pavanollalanormilāgrasarasīkā | brahmāṇḍavyāpiśirā viśrutakīrtirvṛṣādrivaramūrtiḥ || 1776 ||
atra pratītapāṭhasāhityānāṃ sītormilāmāṇḍavīśrutakīrtīnāṃ pūrvavadeva grathanam ||
yathāvā
nityavibhavāsu devāsuranarasaṃkarṣaṇo'higiribhūṣu | pradyumnado natebhyo viharatyaniruddhaśāsanaśśauriḥ || 1777 ||
devāsuranarāṇāṃ saṃkarṣaṇaḥ guṇabhūmnā ākarṣakaḥ natebhyaḥ pradyumnadaḥ prakṛṣṭavibhavapradaḥ aniruddhaśāsanaḥ apratihatājñaḥ śauriḥ
nityavibhavāsu ahigiribhūmiṣu viharati | atra vāsudevasaṃkarṣaṇapradyumnāniruddhānāṃ nyasanam ||
yathāvā
dhanyaścātakadharmā yastatprītyarthameva kṛtakarmā | kāmaṃ tamambujākṣaṃ dhyāyati sānandabāṣpaparimokṣam || 1778 ||
cātakasya dharma iva dharmaḥ aikāntyarūpo yasya sa tathoktaḥ | pumān cātakavṛttiḥ prapanna ityarthaḥ | ‘prapannaścātako yadvat' iti smaraṇāt | dhanyaḥ | anyatsugamam | atrāpi caturṣu caraṇeṣu krameṇa dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ caturṇāṃ puruṣārthānāṃ pūrvavadeva pratipādanam ||
yathāvā
sāmajavarado dānavabhedapaṭurditisuteṣu dhṛtadaṇḍaḥ | bhagavannupāyabhūto bhava tvamevādarāttava prāptau ||
atrāpi sāmadānabhedadaṇḍānāṃ prakṛtānvayaṃ vinā krameṇa nyasanam | upāyabhūta iti prakṛtārthapareṇa padenāsmin padye upadarśitopāyanāmāni nibaddhānīti sūcyārthasyāpi sūcanānmudrālaṃkāraśceti tacchekhariteyaṃ ratnāvalīti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
brahmanuto nalinākṣa trāsaharasphāṭikatviṣā vai śyetaḥ | āśūddrāvitaduritaścāturvarṇyaṃ dharādharaḥ prathayati te || 1780 ||
he nalinākṣa! brahmaṇā caturmukhena nutaḥ | trāsaharaḥ bhayahārī | sphāṭikatviṣā śyetaḥ śvetaḥ | ‘śuklaśubhraśuciśvetaviśadaśyeta
pāṇḍurāḥ' ityamaraḥ | āśu uddrāvitaṃ pradrāvitaṃ ‘pradrāviddrāva sandrāvāḥ' ityamaraḥ | duritaṃ yena saḥ |
veṃ pāpamiti saṃproktaṃ kaṭastasya tu dāhakaḥ |
pāpānāṃ dāhakatvāddhi veṃkaṭādriritīryate ||
iti tasya pāpahāritvaprasiddheḥ | te bhavataḥ dharādharaḥ śeṣādrirityarthaḥ | cāturvarṇyaṃ caturakṣaratvaṃ dharādharaśabdasya tathāvidhatvāditi bhāvaḥ | prathayati | atra brahmakṣatravaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ krameṇa nyasanam | cāturvarṇyapadena upapāditabrahmakṣatrādisūcanānmudrāca pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
tvaddivyacaritavarṇī nyāsena tvāṃ gṛhītavānmatimān | tvadbhāvanasthito'yati paramaṃ dhāmāśramānvito bhagavan || 1781 ||
he bhagavan! taddivyacaritaṃ varṇayatīti tathoktaḥ | nyāsena bharanyasanena tvāṃ gṛhītavān vaśīkṛtavān gṛhītavānityanena upayāma gṛhīto'si brahmaṇe tvā mahasa omityātmānaṃ yuñjīta' iti nyāsakaraṇamantragataṃ gṛhītapadaṃ pratyabhijñāpyate | tava bhāvane sthitaḥ matimān āśrameṇa upāyāntarānuṣṭhānaprayāsarāhityenaiva paramaṃ dhāma ayati prāpnoti 'iṭakiṭakaṭi' ityatra praśliṣṭasya idhātoḥ laṭ | atra brahmacārigṛhasthavanasthayatīnāmāśramiṇāṃ smṛtyādiṣu
sahapaṭhitānāṃ kramiko nyāsaḥ | ayamevāśrameṇeti padena sūcyorthassūcita iti pūrvavadeva sarvam | varṇigṛhiśabdābhyāṃ brahmacārigṛhasthau gṛhyete ||
yathāvā
bhūyāṃsamanvayaṃ tvayi dadhataṃ bhavabhāvirodhaparihṛtyai | śubhasādhanaphalalipsuścaturadhyāyī śraye hariṃ kamale || 1782 ||
he kamale ! tvayi bhūyāṃsaṃ anvayaṃ saṃbandhaṃ dadhataṃ hariṃ śubhānāṃ sādhanaṃ yatphalaṃ niśśreyasarūpaṃ prayojanaṃ lipsussan ataeva bhavena saṃsāreṇa janmanā vā bhāvī yo rodhaḥ nirbandhastaya parihṛtyai śāntyai caturaṃ nipuṇaṃ yathātathā dhyāyatīti caturadhyāyī san śraye | atra samanvayāvirodhasādhanaphalānāṃ brahmamīmāṃsācaturadhyāyīpratipādyānāṃ krameṇa sūcanam | caturadhyāyīti sūcyārthasūcanānmudrā ca | tacchiraskā ratnāvalīyaṃ pūrvavadeva | kiṃca bhūyāṃsamanvayaṃ tvayi dadhataṃ harimityanena prāpyasya brahmaṇassvarūpaṃ, śraye ityanena prāptuḥ pratyagātmanassvarūpaṃ, caturadhyāyīti prāptyupāyaḥ, śubhasādhanaphaleti phalasvarūpaṃ, bhavabhāvirodha ityanena svarūpopāyaphalaprāptivirodhasvarūpaṃ ca kiṃcitkramavaiparītyena varṇitamiti viśeṣaścātra vibhāvanīyaḥ ||
satyaṃ dharmaṃ vindati sa bhīmaseno raṇe vijayaśālī | vrajati suśobhanakulatāṃ lakṣmyā saha deva sevate yastvām || 1783 ||
atra śleṣopaśliṣṭamudrālaṅkārarītyā yudhiṣṭhirādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ pāṇḍavānāṃ prasiddhakrameṇa pratipādanamiti pūrvodāharaṇebhyo vicchittiviśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
kāmaṃ dārādimajuṣamapārijātaṃ śritaṃ sasantānasukham | sāmodaṃ kalpayase haricandana daivataruciraśrīdastvam || 1784 ||
he haricandana! harireva candanaḥ tasya saṃbuddhiḥ | tvaṃ daivatānāmapi rucirā ramaṇīyā tairapi ślāghyeti yāvat | yā śrīḥ saṃpat śobhā vā tāṃ dadātīti tathoktassan | haricandanetyanena devataruviśeṣo'pi gṛhyate | tadā daivatānāṃ rucirāṃ śriyaṃ dadātīti tathokta ityapyupaskāryam | śritaṃ svāśritaṃ janaṃ kāmaṃ paryāptaṃ yathātathā dārādimān juṣatīti taṃ tathoktaṃ ādimaśabdena bandhvādirgṛhyate | apāstaṃ arijātaṃ vairivṛndaṃ yena taṃ apārijātaṃ saṃtānasukhena saha vartata iti sasaṃtānasukhaṃ ataeva sāmodaṃ sānandaṃ sasaurabhaṃ ca | hareścandanatayā rūpitatvāditi bhāvaḥ | kalpayase racayasi | svopāsitāraṃ sarvaprakāravibhavajuṣaṃ tanoṣīti bhāvaḥ | atra mandārapārijātādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ devatarūṇāṃ krameṇa nyasanam | daivataru iti padaikadeśena ślokaghaṭitadevatarurūpasūcyārthasyāpi sūcanānmudrālaṅkārāvataṃsiteyamiti pūrvavadevānusaṃdheyam ||
yathāvā
caraṇāravindaśaraṇagamaśokayannātmacaramavacasāpārtham | kutasahakāraśrīmallīlo nīlotpalojjvalo'vatu sa pumān || 1785 ||
caraṇāravindaśaraṇagaṃ pārthaṃ ātmacaramavacasā 'sarvapāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ' iti vacanena aśokayan kṛtaḥ saha
kāraḥ sācivyaṃ yayā sā tathoktā śrīmatī līlā sārathyalīlā yasya sa tathoktaḥ nīlotpalojjvalaḥ sa pumān avatu | māmiti śeṣaḥ | atra ‘aravindamaśokaṃ ca' ityādiprasiddhakramapañcabāṇanyasanam ||
yathāvā
prāṇayitāraṃ svakathāpānavidhātṝnpiśaṅgasaṃvyānam | kalayeya mudā'nantam samānayan hṛdayapuṇḍarīkaṃ tam || 1786 ||
svakathāpanavidhātṝn janān praṇayitāraṃ | piśaṅgasaṃvyānaṃ pītāmbarottarīyaṃ ‘saṃvyānamuttarīyaṃ syāt' ityamaraḥ | taṃ sakalalokavedaprasiddhaṃ anantaṃ bhagavantaṃ hṛdayapuṇḍarīkaṃ samānayan prāpayan san mudā kalayeya iti yojanā | atra śrutyādiprasiddhānāṃ prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānānāṃ kramiko nyāsaḥ ||
yathāvā
śabdaikagamyamujjhitadoṣasparśaṃ nirūpaṇātigasuguṇam | tāmarasanayana tanuyāṃ subhagaṃ dharmeṇa cetaso viṣayaṃ tvām || 1787 ||
he tāmarasanayana! ujjhitadoṣasparśaṃ nirūpaṇātigāḥ vāṅmanasāviṣayāḥ suguṇāḥ yasya taṃ 'yato vāco nivartante' iti śruteḥ | anena padatritayena ‘tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevamakṣiṇī sa eṣa sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhya uditaḥ | sa eṣa ye cāsmātparāñco lokāsteṣāṃ ceṣṭe devakāmānām' ityantarādityavidyoktaṃ divyamaṅgalavigra
hopalakṣakaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣatvaṃ apahatapāpmatvaṃ sarvalokakāmeśitṛtvādiguṇākaratvaṃ coktaṃ bhavati | atra ‘apahatapāpmā' ityādi 'satyasaṃkalpaḥ' ityantāḥ niravadyaṃnirañjanam | yassarvajñaḥ' ityādiśrutayo'pyanusaṃdheyoḥ | ataeva subhagaṃ śobhanaḥ bhagaḥ
aiśvaryasya samagrasya vīryasya yaśasaśśriyaḥ|
jñānavairāgyoścaiva ṣaṇṇāṃ bhaga itīraṇā ||
ityuktaṃ ṣāḍguṇyaṃ yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ bhagavacchabdavācyamiti yāvat | śabdaikagamyaṃ 'taṃ tvaupaniṣadaṃ puruṣam' iti śruteḥ 'śāstrayonitvāt' iti sūtrācca | upadarśitamasya rūpādikaṃ śāstraikasamadhigamyamiti bhāvaḥ | īdṛśaṃ tvāṃ dharmeṇa
sarveṣāmeva dharmāṇāmuttamo vaiṣṇavo vidhiḥ |
eṣa me sarvadharmāṇāṃ dharmo'dhikatamo mataḥ |
yadbhaktyā puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ stavairarcennarassadā ||
ityuktena arcanavandanastavanādyuttamadharmeṇa cetasaḥ viṣayaṃ tanuyāṃ viśuddhena manasā gṛhṇīyāṃ ‘manasā tu viśuddhena' iti śruteḥ | atra śabdasparśarūparasagandhānāṃ lokavedaprasiddhānāṃ pañcānāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ prakṛtānanvayināṃ kramika upanyāsaḥ | viṣayamityanena kramavinyastā ime viṣayā iti sūcyasyārthasya sūcanānmudrālaṃkāraśca | taduttaṃsiteyaṃ ratnāvalīti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
śubhakṛttaddhitadāyī samāsahasraṃ tvadekaśeṣaṃ mām | kalyāṇavṛttimacyuta kalaya sanādyantadhāturūpastvam || 1788 ||
he acyuta! śubhakṛt maṅgalakāri tat śāstraprasiddhaṃ yat hitaṃ upāyarūpaṃ tat dadātīti tathoktaṃ sakalakalyāṇāspadabhaktiprapattirūpaṃ tvatprāptyupāyaṃ vitarannityarthaḥ | ‘dadāmi buddhiyogaṃ taṃ yena māmupayānti te' iti gānāt | na vidyete ādyantau yasya saḥ nādyantaḥ naśabdena samāsaḥ | utpattināśavidhuraḥ yaḥ dhātuḥ dhātā 'sitanigamimasisacyavidhāñkruśibhyastun' ityanena auṇādikastunpratyayaḥ | udanto'yaṃ śabdaḥ | etacca ‘dhātuprasādānmahimānam' iti śrutivyākhyānāvasare śrutaprakāśikopaniṣadbhāṣyayorvyaktam | tadrūpaḥ janmanāśopalakṣitabhāvavikārarāhityeta sarvajagatāṃ dhartā poṣṭā cetyarthaḥ | saḥ tvaṃ samānāṃ vatsarāṇāṃ sahasraṃ bahutithaṃ kālamiti yāvat | tvadekaśeṣaṃ yāvadātmabhāvi tavaiva śeṣabhūtamiti bhāvaḥ | māṃ kalyāṇī śreyaskarī vṛttiḥ kaiṃkaryarūpā yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ kalaya | atra svarūpopāyapuruṣārthāḥ yathāsthānamanusandheyāḥ | atra ‘kṛttiddhatasamāsaikaśeṣasanādyantadhāturūpāḥ pañca vṛttayaḥ' iti śābdikagoṣṭhīprasiddhānāṃ pañcānāṃ vṛttīnāṃ krameṇa kathanam | kalyāṇavṛttiśabdena upāsāditavṛttayo'tra nibaddhā iti sūcyasyārthasya sūcanaṃ ca pūrvavadeva | atra sanādyantetyādessautranirdeśānuvādarūpatvānna ṅamuḍāgamaprasaktirityavadheyam | ataeva "dvādaśāmī sanādayaḥ | etacchāstramuṇādiṣu | dhātusūtragaṇoṇādivākyaliṅgānuśāsanam' ityadayo ṅamuḍaghaṭitāḥ prayogāssaṃgacchante ||
yathāvā
yattu svārthaṃ dravyaṃ nāliṅgati tava padābjamabjākṣa | saṃkhyātigamapi nṝṇāṃ tanna syātprītikārakaṃ viduṣām || 1789 ||
he abjākṣa! nṝṇāṃ yaddravyaṃ svārthameva sat tava padābjaṃ nāliṅgati tvaccaraṇāmbhojārpitaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ | taddravyaṃ saṃkhyātigaṃ aparimitamapi viduṣāṃ brahmavidāṃ prītikārakaṃ na syāt | vyarthameva taditi bhāvaḥ | atra svārthadravyaliṅgasaṃkhyākārakāṇāṃ prātipadikārthānāṃ śābdikagoṣṭhīprasiddhyanusārataḥ kramikatvam ||
yathāvā
aviṣayamaviśayamāgamavacasāmapi pūrvapakṣamiha tamasām | lokottaraphaladaṃ tvāṃ jagatāmadhikaraṇamāśraye bhagavan || 1790 ||
he bhagavan! āgamavacasāmapi aviṣayaṃ agocaraṃ tamasāṃ ajñānānāmeva timirāṇāṃ pūrvapakṣaṃ śuklapakṣaṃ tannirmūlayitāramiti bhāvaḥ | lokottaraṃ yatphalaṃ niśśreyasarūpaṃ taddadātīti tathoktaṃ jagatāṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ādhāraṃ tvāṃ aviśayaṃ nissaṃdehaṃ yathā tathā''śraye | atra
viṣayo viśayaścaiva pūrvapakṣastathottaram |
prayojanaṃ ca pañcaite prāñco'dhikaraṇaṃ viduḥ ||
iti pūrvottaramīmāṃsāprasiddhādhikaraṇāṅgapañcakasya krameṇa nyāsaḥ | adhikaraṇamityanena sūcanīyārthasūcanaṃ ca ||
yathāvā
kāmaṃ jitaśakro dhanavibhavaissukalo bhayaṃ kṣamo hartum | bhānumadaṃśuśrīmatsarojalocana bhavedbhavaddṛṣṭaḥ || 1791 ||
sukalaḥ dātā bhoktā ca ‘sulako dātṛbhoktari' ityamaraḥ | bhayaṃ svasya svāśritānāṃ ca trāsaṃ hartuṃ kṣamaḥ śaktaḥ bhītābhayadātetyarthaḥ | bhānumadaṃśubhiḥ śrīmat ujjvalaṃ yatsarasijaṃ tadiva locanaṃ yasya tasya saṃbuddhiḥ | atra kāmakrodhalobhamohamadamatsarāṇāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ krameṇa nyāsa iti pūrvobhyo vicchittiśālinīyam ||
yathāvā
kāmamavakro dharmairvimalo bhagavan bhavettamohatiśīlaḥ | śramadamana namatsarasijabhava tava bhakto jitāriṣaḍvargaśca || 1792 ||
śramadamanaḥ sāṃsārikakhedavimocanaḥ tasya saṃbuddhiḥ | naman sarasijabhavaḥ brahmā yasya saḥ tasya saṃbuddhiḥ he bhagavan ! tava bhaktaḥ kāmaṃ avakraḥ akuṭilaśīlaḥ | dharmaiḥ sāmānyairviśeṣaiśca dharmaiḥ vimalaḥ nirmalaḥ ‘dharmeṇa pāpamapanudati' iti śruteḥ | ataeva tamasaḥ tamoguṇasya hatiḥ nirāsaḥ tasyāṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | jitaḥ ariṣaḍvargaḥ kāmādiḥ yena sa tathoktaśca bhavet |
na krādho na ca mātsaryaṃ na lobho nāśubhā matiḥ |
bhavanti kṛtapuṇyānāṃ bhaktānāṃ puruṣottame ||
ityukteriti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi prakārāntareṇa kāmakrodhādīnāmeva krameṇa grathanam | īdṛśaviśeṣarūpasūcyārthasya jitāriṣaḍvarga ityanena sūcanānmudrā ceti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
suśliṣṭasaṃdhivigrahamibhapatiyānaṃ śarāsanasanātham | rāmamabhivīkṣya tena prepsanto'dvaidhamāśrayanmunayaḥ || 1793 ||
suśliṣṭāḥ saṃdhayaḥ saṃdhibandhāḥ yasya tādṛśo vigraho yasya tam | anena vakṣyamāṇamunijanamānasākarṣakassaundaryātiśayassūcitaḥ | yathocyate
aṅgapratyaṅgakānāṃ syātsaṃniveśo yathocitam |
susnigdhasandhibandho yastatsaundaryamitīryate ||
iti | ibhapateḥ yānamiva yānaṃ yasya tam | śarāsanasanāthaṃ rāmaṃ abhivīkṣya munayaḥ tena rāmeṇa saha advaidhaṃ gāḍhāliṅganenaikībhāvaṃ prepsantassantaḥ | taṃ āśrayan daṇḍakāraṇyaṃ praviṣṭasya bhagavato dāśarathessaundaryeṇa mohitāḥ vanitā iva munayastamāliṅgitumutsukā babhūvuriti padmapurāṇakathā'trānusaṃdheyā | yadvā tena advaidhaṃ avivādaṃ prepsantastamāśrayan 'avivādastu tasyaiva padayorātmano'rpaṇam' ityuktarītyā taccaraṇāravinde atmarakṣābharārpaṇamabhīpsanta āśrayannityarthaḥ | atra ‘saṃdhirnā vigraho yānamāsanaṃ dvaidhamāśrayaḥ' iti prasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ saṃdhyādīnāṃ krameṇa ṣaṇṇāṃ nyasanam ||
yathāvā
surabhītiharabhujoṣmā śaravarṣāsāraviśamitāśaradāvaḥ | paramahimānīko yo diśidiśi śirasā namāmi taṃ dāśarathim || 1794 ||
yaḥ surabhītiharaḥ bhujoṣmā bāhupratāpaḥ yasya śaravarṣāsāraḥ bāṇavarṣāsāra eva jalavarṣāsāraḥ tena viśamitaḥ āśarāḥ niśicarā eva dāvā yasya paramahimā anīkaḥ sainyaṃ yasya pakṣe paramā himānī himasaṃhatiḥ yasya saḥ paramahimānīkaḥ | ‘nadṛyataśca' iti kap | taṃ dāśarathiṃ diśidiśi śirasā namāmi 'namaḥ
purastādatha pṛṣṭhataste namonamassarvata eva sarva' ityuktaprakriyayā pratidiśaṃ praṇamāmītyarthaḥ | atra surabhītyādipadaikadeśaiḥ ūṣmetyādiśabdaśleṣeṇa ca vasantādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāmṛtūnāṃ krameṇa varṇanam ||
yathāvā
sṛṣṭyādikarmakartā karaṇaniyantā tvayā vinā'cyuta ko'nyaḥ | sakalaśubhasaṃpradānaṃ kurute pādānatādhikaraṇahatiṃ ca || 1795 ||
he acyuta ! sṛṣṭyādīnāṃ jagatsarjanādīnāṃ karmaṇāṃ kartā anena ‘yo vai bālāka eteṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ kartā yasya caitatkarma sa vai veditavyaḥ' iti śrutiḥ pratyabhijñāpyate | karaṇānāṃ indriyāṇāṃ niyantā ‘śrotrasya śrotram' ityādiśruteḥ | pādānatānāṃ caraṇapraṇatānāṃ ādhiḥ manovyathā tasya karaṇāḥ kartāraḥ karaṇāni sādhanāni vā teṣāṃ hatiṃ kṣatiṃ, sakalaśubhasaṃpradānaṃ teṣāmeva sarvakalyāṇavitaraṇaṃ ca | ābhyāminiṣṭanirāseṣṭaprāpaṇe abhihite | tvayā vinā ko'nyaḥ kurute iti yojanā | atra
karma kartā ca karaṇaṃ sapradānaṃ tathaiva ca |
apādānādhikaraṇe ityāhuḥ kārakāṇi ṣaṭ ||
iti śābdikoktānāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ kārakāṇāṃ krameṇa nyasanam ||
yathāvā
lavaṇāśālī kṣubhitāsurendrarājyodadhīśabhujadaṇḍaḥ | nalinākṣīrājitahṛjjalajākṣo jayatu saptabhuvananidhiḥ || 1796 ||
lavaṇāśabdaḥ svābhāvyāt strīliṅgaḥ lāvaṇyavācī | 'lavaṇaiva lāvaṇyam' iti 'lāvaṇyaśriya iva śeṣasaṅganāyāḥ' ityādau mallināthādibhistatratatra vyākhyātatvāt | 'lavaṇo rasarakṣo'bdhibhedeṣu lavaṇā tviṣi, iti viśvaḥ | tayā śālata iti lavaṇāśālī | kṣubhitaḥ asurendrāṇāṃ hiraṇyakaśipunarakaprabhṛtīnāṃ rājyameva udadhīśaḥ | samudrarājaḥ yena tādṛśo bhuja eva daṇḍaḥ manthānadaṇḍo yasya saḥ nalinākṣyā śriyā rājitaṃ hṛt vakṣaḥ yasya saḥ saptasaṃkhyākānāṃ bhuvananāṃ bhūrādilokānāṃ nidhiḥ jalajākṣaḥ jayatu | atra 'lavaṇekṣusurāsarpirdadhikṣīrajalārṇavāḥ' iti purāṇādiprasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ saptānāmudanvatāṃ krameṇa pratipādanamiti pūrvodāharaṇebhyo viśeṣaḥ | saptabhuvananidhiriti padena sūcyasamudrasūcanānmudrā ca ||
yathāvā
bhūridayaṃ jaladhibhuvaḥ priyaṃ suvarṇābhamaharadhipabimbastham | sujanogratapogamyaṃ vyāhṛtayo no'bhidadhatu satyaṃ devam || 1797 ||
naḥ asmākaṃ vyāhṛtayaḥ vyāhārāḥ bhūridayaṃ niravadhikakṛpaṃ, tatra hetuḥ jaladhibhuvaḥ priyamiti | atra 'nityaśrīrbradhnabimbe' ityācāryaśrīsūktissmartavyā | ataeva suvarṇābhaṃ aharadhipabimbasthamiti tadupaskārakam | atra ‘ya eṣo'ntarāditye hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣaḥ' ityantarādityavidyoktaṃ divyātmavigraharūpamanusaṃhitam | sujanānāṃ satāṃ ugreṇa tīvreṇa tapasā gamyaṃ prāpyaṃ, satyaṃ nirupādhikasattāyoginaṃ 'satyaṃ jñānam' iti śruteḥ | muktāmuktaniyantāraṃ vā ‘atha yattatsadamṛtaṃ atha yatti tanmartyaṃ atha yadyaṃ tenobhe yacchati'
iti daharavidyāyāṃ śravaṇāt | devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ abhidadhatu vadantu
asmadvacanāni saṃtataṃ divyamaṅgalavigrahaṃ paraṃ brahma puruṣottamaṃ śrīnivāsameva paricinvatām | tadviṣayakaprabandhāneva praṇayantāmiti bhāvaḥ | atra śrutiprasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ bhūrādisaptavyāhṛtīnāṃ krameṇa vinyāsaḥ | vyāhṛtaya ityanena tādṛśasūcyārthasūcanaṃ ceti pūrvavadeva mudrāśiraskatvam ||
yathāvā
aruṇābjavarṇamaṅgalasaumyāṅgirasāvabhāsita nuhasite | asitākṣi mātarāhurloke'tulyā'navagraheti tvām || 1798 ||
arūṇābjasya varṇa iva varṇo yasya tat ‘padmavarṇām' iti śruteḥ | tathoktaṃ maṅgalaṃ saumyaṃ suṃndaraṃ 'saumyaṃ tu sundare somadaivate' ityamaraḥ | tādṛśaṃ aṅgaṃ yasyāssā tathoktā tasyāssaṃbuddhiḥ 'aṅgagātra' iti ṅīṣ | rasena śṛṅgārarasena dayārasena vā avabhāsi prakāśamānaṃ na tu 'smayanniva nṛpo hanti' itivatparātisaṃdhāyakaṃ, tanuhasitaṃ mandahāso yasyāssā tathoktā | anena bhagavanmandahasitādapyasyā mandahasitaṃ guṇavattaramiti dyotitam | taddhi hāso janonmādakarī ca māyā' ityuktarītyā janonmādakārimāyārūpaṃ tasyāssaṃbuddhiḥ rasāvabhāsitanuhasite | he asitākṣi! 'taṃ ceyamasitekṣaṇā' ityukteḥ | he mātaḥ! tvāṃ atulyā asadṛkṣā anavagrahā apratibandhā sarvavyāpinītyarthaḥ | 'avagraho dṛṣṭirodhe pratibandhe gajālike' iti medinī | yadvā anavagrahā niraṅkuśasvātantryā aparyanuyojyasya bhagavatsvātantryasyāpi tadadhīnatvāt | yathoktaṃ ‘yasyā vīkṣya mukhaṃ tadiṅgitaparādhīno vidhatte'khilam' ityādi | iti vadanti santa iti śeṣaḥ|
atra sūryādīnāṃ navānāṃ grahāṇāṃ prakṛtānvayaṃ vinā krameṇa nyāsaḥ | navagrahetyanena sūcyārthasūcanānmudrā ca | prāgudāhṛtaṃ ‘bhāsvān rājā' ityādipadyaṃ tu prakṛtānvayi bhagavadviṣayaśceti vailakṣaṇyam ||
yathāvā
bhujagācalaśṛṅgāro vīraḥ karuṇādbhuto mahāsyaruciḥ | svabhayā na kaṃ nu vaśayedbībhattitaraudraripurayaṃ śāntaḥ || 1799 ||
karuṇayā adbhutaḥ vismayāvahakṛpa ityarthaḥ | mahatī āsyaruciḥ mukhaprabhā yasya bībhatsitāḥ vikṛtāḥ kutāḥ raudrāḥ dāruṇāḥ ripavaḥ yena saḥ | śāntaḥ ūrmiṣaṭkapratibhaṭaḥ svabhayā nijaprabhayā kaṃ nu na vaśayet sarvamapi svatejasaiva vaśīkuryādityarthaḥ | atra
śṛṅgāravīrakaruṇādbhutahāsyabhayānakāḥ |
bībhatsaraudraśāntāścetyete nava rasāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
iti kaścitprasiddhakramāṇāṃ navānāṃ rasānāṃ krameṇa nyāsaḥ |
kvacittu
śṛṅgārahāsyakaruṇā raudravīrabhayānakāḥ |
bībhatsādbhutaśāntāśca rasāḥ pūrvairudāhṛtāḥ ||
iti kramaḥ paṭhyate | tadanusāre tu vakṣyamāṇakramavaiparītyāderudāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati ||
yathāvā
madhuhara mādhava tanuyāśśukraśuciṃ nanu nabhassadāmapi mānyam | bhādrāśvayujorjasvalasahāyataiṣaṃ
samāghanatapasyaṃ tvām || 1800 ||
idaṃ kasyacidaihikāpavargikapuruṣārthaṃ prārthayiturbhaktasya vacanam | he madhuhara madhuvairin nanu mādhava he śrīnivāsa | saḥ tvaṃ ghanā tapaścaryā yasya taṃ mā māṃ bhadrāṇāṃ gajaviśeṣāṇāṃ samūho bhādraṃ aśvānāṃ samūhaḥ āśvaṃ ubhayatra sāmūhiko'ṇ | tābhyāṃ yujyata iti bhādrāśvayujaḥ igupadhalakṣaṇaḥ kaḥ | ūrjasvalaḥ baliṣṭhaśca sahāyaḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | tasya bhāvaḥ tattātāṃ iṣyate gacchatīti bhādrāśvayujorjasvalasahāyataiṣaḥ taṃ ‘iṣagatau' divādiḥ tasmātkarmaṇyaṇ | caturaṅgasenāyuktamahāsahāyaśālinamityarthaḥ | etena aihikasamṛddhirarthitā | śukraḥ vahni 'śukassyādbhārgave jyeṣṭhamāse vaiśvānare pumān' iti medinī | tamiva śuci śuddhaṃ anenāpavargasādhanasarvaprakāraśuddhimattā sūcitā | 'śuciśśuddhe'nupahate śṛṅgārāṣāḍhayossite' iti viśvaḥ | nabhaḥ ssadāṃ arcirādikānāmātivāhikānāṃ devānāṃ mānyaṃ paryavasāne taissevyamapi tanuyāḥ | 'bhuktvā ca bhogānakhilānante haripadaṃ vrajet' ityukarītyā aihikasāmrājyānubhavapūrvakamante niśśreyasasāmrājyānubhavaśālinamapi kurvīthā iti bhāvaḥ | atra 'madhuśca mādhavaśca śukraśca śuciśca' ityādiśrutismṛtyādipaṭhitānāṃ madhumādhavādīnāṃ dvādaśānāṃ māsānāṃ kramo nibaddhaḥ ||
yathāvā
syāmeṣa vṛṣādristhaṃ mithunamakarkyaṅgajāriramaṇīḍyamaham | atulālikaṃ vicāpalamasamakaraṃ vīkṣya vinatasakalaśamīnam || 1801 ||
atra jyautiṣādiprasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ meṣavṛṣādimīnāntānāṃ dvādāśānāṃ rāśīnāṃ mudrālaṅkāravidhayā krameṇa varṇanam | aṅgajāriḥ
kāmavairī tasya ramaṇī gaurī tayā īḍyaṃ stutyaṃ atulaṃ asadṛśaṃ alikaṃ lalāṭaṃ yasya tat vicāpalaṃ acañcalaśīlaṃ vīkṣyeti kriyāyā viśeṣaṇaṃ vā | asamau asadṛśau karau pāṇī yasya tat | śama eṣāmastīti śaminaḥ teṣāṃ ināḥ prabhavaśśāntāgraṇya ityarthaḥ | vinatāḥ sakalā śamīnāḥ yasya yasminvā tattathoktaṃ vṛṣādristhaṃ mithunaṃ śrīśrīnivāsarūpaṃ strīpuṃsātmakaṃ dvaṃdvaṃ vīkṣya dṛṣṭyā dhyātvā vā karkaṃ kalkaṃ ralayorabhedātpāpaṃ ‘kalko'strī śamalainasoḥ' ityamaraḥ | tadasyāstīti karkī sa na bhavatītyakarkī, vinaṣṭāśliṣṭapūrvottarāghaḥ eṣaḥ ahaṃ syāmiti yojanā | bhaveyamityarthaḥ ||
prakṛtānanvayavatī akramā yathā
madhurāmā yāstāsāṃ dātā kāṃcicchucaṃ vijitakāśī | sadayo dhyāto'stu vahan hṛdi natasadvārakāmavantīṃ lakṣmīm || 1802 ||
yāḥ madhoḥ tannamno'surasya rāmāḥ striyaḥ tāsāṃ kāṃcit śucaṃ vaidhavyaprayuktaṃ śokaṃ dātā | dadātestṛn | madhudaityasaṃharteti bhāvaḥ | vijitena vijayena bhāve ktaḥ kāśata iti vijitakāśī kāśaterṇiniḥ ‘vānarā jitakāśinaḥ' itivat | natāḥ satāṃ vārāḥ nivahāḥ yasyāṃ sā natasadvārakā tāṃ avantīṃ sarvalokapālayitrīṃ lakṣmīṃ hṛdi vahan ataeva sadayaḥ pumān paramapuruṣaḥ śrīnivāsa iti bhāvaḥ | dhyātaḥ astu | mayeti śeṣaḥ| atra
ayodhyā madhurā māyā kāśī kāñciravantikā |
purī dvāravatī caiva saptaitā mokṣadāyikāḥ ||
iti prasiddhakramapāṭhānāṃ kramavyatikrameṇa nyasanam ||
yathāvā
puṣkalaśālmalijambūplakṣaḥ krauñcakuśameduraḥ phaṇiśailaḥ | ākrāntamahāśākassaptadvīpādhiko jayatyayamekaḥ || 1803 ||
puṣkalāḥ atiśobhanāḥ 'puṣkalassyātsattamaścātiśobhane' ityamaraḥ | śālmalijambūplakṣāḥ vṛkṣaviśeṣāḥ yasmin saḥ | krauñcaiḥ pakṣiviśeṣaiḥ kuśaiḥ darbhaiḥ jalairvā meduraḥ ākrāntāḥ svābhogena vyāptāḥ mahatyaḥ āśāḥ diśaḥ yena sa tathoktaḥ 'śeṣādvibhāṣā' iti kap 'ke ṇaḥ' iti prāptasya hṛsvasya 'āpo'nyatarasyām' iti vikalpitatvādāśāśabdasya na hṛsvaḥ | ayaṃ phaṇiśailaḥ eka eva saptadvīpādhiko jayati | atra 'jambūplakṣakuśakrauñcaśākaśālmalipuṣkalāḥ' iti purāṇādiṣu sahapaṭhitasya dvīpasaptakasya kramātikrameṇa nyasanam | saptadvīpapadena sūcyārthasūcanaṃ ca ||
yathāvā
stuvatāṅgirassadā me marīcimattriyugasapulaharṣakaram | vipulastyānakratubhugvasiṣṭhasukhadaṃ tavāmba nayanayugam || 1804 ||
he amba ! marīcimat aṃśumat triyugasya bhagavataḥ sapulaharṣakaraṃ sapulakapramodāvahaṃ 'pulassyātpulake puṃsi vipule vācyaliṅgakaḥ' iti medinī | vipulaṃ viśālaṃ styānānāṃ snigdhānāṃ 'styānaṃ snigdhe pratidhvāne ghanatvālasyayorapi' iti medinī | kratubhujāṃ vasiṣṭhāḥ vasīyāṃsaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ teṣāṃ lokapālānāmityarthaḥ | vasuśabdaḥ praśastavācī | tata ātiśāyanike iṣṭhani vasiṣṭha iti rūpam |
etacca ‘śvaso vasīyaśśreyasaḥ' iti sūtre kaumudīgranthe spaṣṭam | sukhadaṃ ‘lokeśeśavibhūtīnāṃ kāraṇaṃ yannirīkṣaṇam' ityukteḥ | vipulaṃ ca tat styānakratubhugvasiṣṭhasukhadaṃ ceti viśeṣaṇobhayapadakarmadhārayaḥ | yadvā vipulaṃ atiśayitaṃ styānaṃ ālasyaṃ durvāsaśśāpajanitasvārājyabhraṃśaprayuktamapāṭavaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ kratubhugvasiṣṭhaḥ indraḥ tasya sukhadamiti tava nayanayugaṃ me mama giraḥ sadā stuvatā stuvantu | atra
marīcimatryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum |
vasiṣṭhaṃ ca mahātejāsso'sṛjjatsapta mānasān ||
iti purāṇoktānāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ kiṃcitkramātilaṅghanena nyasanam ||
yathāvā
yaśasaiṣa sārvabhaumenairāvatakumudapuṣpadantarucā te | loko'ñjananāyaka supratīka vāmana supuṇḍarīkākṣa sitaḥ || 1805 ||
he vāmana ! svecchāgṛhītavāmanāvatāra | he supratīka 'ā praṇakhātsarva eva suvarṇaḥ' ityuktaprakāradivyamaṅgalavigraha ! he supuṇḍarīkākṣa ! śobhanaṃ yatpuṇḍarīkaṃ tadiva akṣi yasya tasya saṃbuddhiḥ | suśabdena gambhīrāmbhassamudbhūtasumṛṣṭanālaravikaravikasitatvarūpaḥ ‘tasya yathā kapyāsam' iti śrutisthakapyāsaśabdārthassarvo'pi
saṃgṛhītaḥ | añjananāyaka añjanākhyagirinetaḥ airāvataḥ kumudapuṣpaṃ dantaḥ gajadantaḥ teṣāṃ rugiva ruk prabhā yasya tathoktena sārvabhaumena sarvabhūmau viditena te yaśasā jagat sitaṃ śubhramiti yojanā | atra 'airāvataḥ puṇḍarīkaḥ' ityuktānāṃ diggajānāṃ kramātilaṅghanam ||
pralayasarasvati vaṭadalaśayitastvamadīdṛśassvamahimānam | mārkaṇḍeyamuneśśrutisubhagaṅgeyaṃ purā phaṇigirīndo || 1806 ||
idamupākhyānamaraṇyaparvaṇi prasiddham | atra ‘imaṃ me gaṅge yamune sarasvati' iti saṃbuddhyantatayā krameṇa śrutau paṭhitānāṃ gaṅgāyamunāsarasvatīnāṃ sarvātmanā vyutkrameṇa nyasanaṃ pūrvobhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
duritopasaṃhṛticaṇā ravimarśavikāsipadmagarbhendhe | taṭagānprati mukharamukhabhramarā sausnātikī vṛṣādrinadī || 1807 ||
raveḥ marśaḥ sparśaḥ tatkarasparśa iti yāvat | taṭagān tīrasthān prati sausnātikī susnānapraṣṭī ‘pṛcchatau susnātādibhyaḥ' iti ṭhak | susnātān pṛcchatīti vigrahaḥ | atra 'mukhaṃ pratimusaṃ garbhassāvamarśopasaṃhṛtiḥ ' iti prasiddhakramapāṭhānāṃ mukhādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ nāṭakasandhīnāṃ sarvātmanā vyutkrameṇa nyasanam | gamyotprekṣāsaṃkīrṇamiti ca viśeṣaḥ ||
yamaniyamāsanadakṣaprāṇāyāmā hare bhavatpāriṣadāḥ | pratyāharanti yamabhaṭagṛhītamapi nāmadhāraṇādeva || 1808 ||
he hare ! anena
brahmāṇamindraṃ rudraṃ ca yamaṃ varuṇamevaca |
prasahya harate yasmāttasmāddhariritīryate ||
ityuktaprakāreṇa yamopasaṃharaṇaśaktimattā prakṛtārthopaskāriṇī dyotitā | ‘harirharati pāpāni' ityuktapāpanirākaraṇaśaktatā ca | tava pāriṣadāḥ sunandanandaprabhṛtayaḥ pāriṣadyāḥ | tānviśinaṣṭi  yametyādinā | yamasya samavartinaḥ niyamaḥ niyamanaṃ ‘pātakino matsakāśamāneyāḥ' ityājñā tasya asane nirāse dakṣaḥ prāṇasya śakteḥ ‘śaktiḥ parākramaḥ prāṇaḥ' ityamaraḥ | āyāmaḥ dairghyaṃ yeṣāṃ te kṛtāntakṛtājñānirākaraṇadhurīṇavipulaparākramā ityarthaḥ | ata eva yamabhaṭaiḥḥ gṛhītamapi naraṃ ajāmilatulyamiti bhāvaḥ | tava nāmnaḥ dhāraṇādeva grahaṇādeva evakāreṇa taditaropāyaleśasparśaḥ tasyāpi buddhipūrvakatvaṃ ca vyudasyate | pratyāharanti yamabhaṭahastādācchidya svavaśaṃ nayantīti bhāvaḥ | atra
ayaṃ hi kṛtanirveṣo janmakoṭyaṃhasāmapi |
yadvyājahāra vivaśo nāma svastyayanaṃ hareḥ ||
ityādipramāṇaśataṃ smartavyam | atra yogaśāstraprasiddhasahapāṭhānāṃ yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇānāṃ dhyānasamādhivikalānāṃ krameṇa nyasanam ||
yathāvā
madhurāṃ giraṃ ramāyāśśṛṇvankāṃcidatha bhavadavaṃ tīvraṃ tvam | suguṇamayo dhyāturme śamaya dṛśā nātha kālikāśītalayā || 1809 ||
madhurāṃ ‘kimevaṃ nirdoṣaḥ ka iha jagati' ityādyuktaprakāratayā'tiramaṇīyāṃ kāṃcit yāṃkāṃcit na tu bhūyasīmiti bhāvaḥ | tāvataiva tatparantrasya tava cittaprasādassukara iti bhāvaḥ | ramāyāḥ
tvadavāptipuruṣakārabhūtāyā iti bhāvaḥ | giraṃ śṛṇvan atha śrava
ṇānantarameva he nātha! suguṇamayaḥ kāruṇyavātsalyādiguṇapracuraḥ tvaṃ kālikā megharekhā seva śītalā tayā dṛśā dhyātuḥ me tīvraṃ ugraṃ bhavadavaṃ śayama | tīvraṃ śayameti vā yojanā | atra ‘ayodhyā madhurā' ityādiprasiddhasya purīsaptakasya kiṃcidvaikalyena kramātikrameṇa ca varṇanam ||
yathāvā
śubhabhā ratīśajananī jaladhibhavā nīrajātavanabhavanā | krodhavaśacīravāsaśśāpadhvastendravibhavadā jayatāt || 1810 ||
atra purāṇādau sahapaṭhitānāmapi bhāratībhavānīśacīnāṃ kramaprasiddhirahitānāṃ nyasanam ||
yathāvā
vidhivāmadevavanitāvinutau subhagau tamovijayanipuṇau | caraṇau mūrdhni nidhattāṃ mamābdhijā bālikā'tisukumārau || 1811 ||
atra
vasiṣṭho vāmadevaśca jābāliratha kāśyapaḥ |
kātyāyano gautamaśca suyajño vijayastathā ||
iti prasiddhasahapāṭhānāmapi kramaprasiddhividhurāṇāṃ vāmadevagautamavijayajābālīnāṃ caturṇāṃ varṇanam ||
ityalaṃṅkāramaṇihāre ratnāvalīsaraḥ saptasaptatitamaḥ
<28>
atha tadguṇasaraḥ
svaguṇasya parityāgāttadguṇo'nyaguṇagrahaḥ |
svaguṇasya parityāgapūrvakaṃ svasannihitavastvantarasaṃbandhiguṇagrahaṇaṃ tadguṇaḥ | tasya guṇo'syāstītyanvarthasaṃjño'yam ||
yathāvā
indīvarasuṣumābharamandīkṛtinipuṇatāvakāṅgarucā | rañjita iha vṛṣagirirayamañjananāmānurūpamullasati || 1812 ||
yathāvā
marakatanibhanijarucivicchuritaṃ garuḍaṃ janāssamārūḍham | sakalaṃ jagadvijetuṃ śukamadhirūḍhaṃ smaraṃ harimajānan || 1813 ||
yathāvā
adhararucā'dho'ruṇatamamasitamuparyakṣitārakārucibhiḥ | nāsāmauktikametadbhāsā guñjāyate'mba bhavadīyam || 1814 ||
yathāvā
sarasi niviśamānāyāṃ salilavihāre hiriṇyavarṇāyām | tvayyāpaḥ praṇayanti śrutikathitaṃ svaṃ
hiraṇyavarṇātvam || 1815 ||
‘hiraṇyavarṇā'śśucayaḥ' ityādiśrutyuktaṃ hiraṇyavarṇātvamityarthaḥ | eṣūdāharaṇeṣu svaguṇatyāgapūrvakānyaguṇagrahaṇaṃ spaṣṭameva | ādye samālaṃkāreṇa dvitīye bhrāntimatā tṛtīye upamayā turīye gamyotprekṣayā cāyaṃ pariṣkṛta iti parasparaṃ vailakṣaṇyaṃ bodhyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre tadguṇasaro'ṣṭasaptatitamaḥ
<29>
atha pūrvarūpasaraḥ
svaguṇasya punaḥ prāptiḥ pūrvarūpamitīryate |
pūrvaṃ svaguṇatyāgena gṛhītānyaguṇasya punarnijaguṇaprāptiḥ pūrvarūpaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
jaladheradhirūḍhāyā hṛdayaṃ svarucā śriyotinailyajuṣaḥ | vailakṣyaṃ vīkṣya harirvyadhādyathāpuramimāṃ svamaṇibhāsā || 1816 ||
vailakṣyaṃ hiraṇyavarṇāyā api mama vallabhahṛdayapariṣvaṅgasaṃbhrameṇa niratiśayo nīlimā samajanīti trapāviśeṣaḥ | svamaṇeḥ kaustubhasya bhāsā imāṃ śriyaṃ yathāpuraṃ pūrvavadeva hiraṇyavarṇāṃ vyadhāt | na cātra hiraṇyavarṇatvena śrutāyāśśriyaḥ pītavarṇatayā kaustubhabhāso'ruṇatayā ca vibhinnatvātkathaṃ pūrvarūpaprāptiriti vācyaṃ, pītāruṇavarṇayoraikyavarṇanasya kavisamayasiddhatvāt | tathācoktamalaṃkāraśekhare
‘hiraṇyavarṇā'śśucayaḥ' ityādiśrutyuktaṃ hiraṇyavarṇātvamityarthaḥ | eṣūdāharaṇeṣu svaguṇatyāgapūrvakānyaguṇagrahaṇaṃ spaṣṭameva | ādye samālaṃkāreṇa dvitīye bhrāntimatā tṛtīye upamayā turīye gamyotprekṣayā cāyaṃ pariṣkṛta iti parasparaṃ vailakṣaṇyaṃ bodhyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre tadguṇasaro'ṣṭasaptatitamaḥ
atha pūrvarūpasaraḥ
svaguṇasya punaḥ prāptiḥ pūrvarūpamitīryate |
pūrvaṃ svaguṇatyāgena gṛhītānyaguṇasya punarnijaguṇaprāptiḥ pūrvarūpaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
jaladheradhirūḍhāyā hṛdayaṃ svarucā śriyotinailyajuṣaḥ | vailakṣyaṃ vīkṣya harirvyadhādyathāpuramimāṃ svamaṇibhāsā || 1816 ||
vailakṣyaṃ hiraṇyavarṇāyā api mama vallabhahṛdayapariṣvaṅgasaṃbhrameṇa niratiśayo nīlimā samajanīti trapāviśeṣaḥ | svamaṇeḥ kaustubhasya bhāsā imāṃ śriyaṃ yathāpuraṃ pūrvavadeva hiraṇyavarṇāṃ vyadhāt | na cātra
hiraṇyavarṇatvena śrutāyāśśriyaḥ pītavarṇatayā kaustubhabhāso'ruṇatayā ca vibhinnatvātkathaṃ pūrvarūpaprāptiriti vācyaṃ, pītāruṇavarṇayoraikyavarṇanasya kavisamayasiddhatvāt | tathācoktamalaṃkāraśekhare
kavisaṃpradāyaratne ṣoḍaśamarīcau ‘kamalāsaṃpadoḥ' ityupakramya 'nīlalohitayossvarṇaparāgāgniśikhādiṣu' ityuktvā ‘aikyamevābhisaṃhitam' ityantena ||
yathāvā
nīlāyitaṃ nijarucā vyālācalanātha mauktikākalpam | urasi tava dhavalayantī sarasijanayanā smitaiḥ kriyātkuśalam || 1817 ||
udāharaṇadvaye'pi pūrvaṃ parityaktasya svaguṇasya punaḥ prāptisspaṣṭaiva || ayameva tadguṇa iti kecidvyavāhārṣuḥ | tanna caturaśraṃ, atra tadguṇānavakāśāt | svaguṇatyāgenānyadīyaguṇaparigrahe hi tasya pravṛttiḥ | na ceha tatsaṃbhavaḥ śriyo mauktikākalpasya ca prathamaṃ svaguṇatyāgenānyadīyaguṇagrahaṇasadbhāve'pi paścāt svaguṇatyāgānyadīyaguṇagrahaṇayorabhāvāt | ataeva svaguṇatyāgāditi tadguṇalakṣaṇe svaguṇetyatra samāsāntarhitaguṇaguṇisaṃbandhamātramāgantukānāgantukasādhāraṇaṃ vivakṣitam | tataśca ‘vibhinnavarṇā garuḍāgrajena' ityādau gṛhītasyāruṇavarṇasyāpi svaguṇatayā tatparityāgenānyadīyaguṇasvīkārasadbhāvāttadguṇa eveti nirastam | tathā'pi svakīyasyaiva guṇasya prāptyā anyadīyaguṇābhāvena tadguṇasya durvacatvāt | kiṃ ca ayameva tadguṇa ityukte ‘padmarāgāyate nāsāmauktikā te'dharatviṣā' ityādiprasiddhatadguṇālaṃkārodāharaṇeṣu tadguṇālaṃkāro na syāt | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, anubhavasiddhāyāścamatkṛterniravalambanatāpatteriti dik ||
yattu syādvikṛte'pyarthe pūrvāvasthānuvartanam |
pūrvarūpaṃ tamapyāhuralaṃkṛtiviśāradāḥ ||
pūrvāvasthāpekṣayā vijātīyatāṃ prāpte'pyarthe pūrvāvasthānuvṛttirapi pūrvarūpamalaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
citramidaṃ tvatkṛpayā bandhavimokṣaṃ gato'pi padmākṣa | prāpnoti mahāśuddhaprakṛtiguṇāmocanaṃ yadātmā'yam || 1818 ||
ayamātmā jīvaḥ tvatkṛpayā bandhasya prakṛtisaṃbandhasya vimokṣaṃ gato'pi mahāśuddhāḥ atimātrāśucayaḥ ye prakṛterguṇāḥ tamaḥprabhṛtayaḥ teṣāṃ āmocanaṃ bandhaṃ prāpnotīti citram | tattvaṃ tu mahāśuddhāḥ ativelanirmalāḥ ye prakṛtiguṇāḥ svabhāvaguṇāḥ apahatapāpmatvādayaḥ svābhāvikā guṇā ityarthaḥ | ‘prakṛtirguṇasāmye syādamātyādisvabhāvayoḥ' iti medinī | taiḥ amocanaṃ avinābhāvaṃ āmocanaṃ saṃbandhaṃ vā prāpnoti āvirbhūtasvābhāvikaguṇāṣṭako bhavatītyarthaḥ | atra bandhamokṣeṇa pūrvāvasthāto vilakṣaṇasya pratyagātmanaḥ śleṣamahimnā mahāśuddhaprakṛtiguṇāmocanarūpapūrvāvasthānuvṛtterlakṣaṇānugatiḥ ||
yathāvā
jagatāṃ savitastāvakadivyālokena tamasi galite'pi | bhūyo'pi sattamaśśrīrbhajatāmanuvartate vicitramidam || 1819 ||
he jagatāṃ savitaḥ jagajjanaka! pakṣe jagatāmityetat bhajatāmityasya viśeṣyatayā yojyam | he savitaḥ bhāno ! tāvakena divyena ālokena darśanena tejasā ca | tamasi ajñāne tadupalakṣitaprakṛtau vā | anyatra timire vigalite'pi bhūyo'pi bhajatāṃ sattamasaḥ adhi
kājñānasya atiśayitatimirasya ca śrīḥ vibhavaḥ anyatra sattamā atiśobhanā ‘sattamaścātiśobhane' ityamaraḥ | śrīḥ jñānādisaṃpat muktaiśvaryaṃ vā anuvartate | atrāpi śleṣamahimnā tamasi galite'pi tadanuvṛttirdarśitā ||
yathāvā
prāgamba tava kaṭākṣādyeṣāṃ kaṇṭheṣu sataralāhārāḥ | paścādapi hantāsaṃsteṣāṃ kaṇṭheṣu sataralāhārāḥ || 1820 ||
he amba! tava kaṭākṣātprāk kaṭākṣaprasaraṇātpūrvaṃ yeṣāṃ janānāṃ kaṇṭheṣu galabileṣu sataralāḥ taralyā yavāgvā sahitāḥ āhārāḥ nihīnābhyavahārā ityarthaḥ | āsan paścādapi tvatkaṭākṣaprasareṇa saṃpadutkarṣalābhādanantaramapi teṣāṃ kaṇṭheṣu sataralāhārāḥ uktābhyavahārā eva | pakṣe grīvāsu sataralāḥ madhyamaṇisahitāḥ hārāḥ muktāvalyaḥ āsan | hantetyāścarye ‘taralaṃ cañcale ṣidge bhāsvare'pi triliṅgakam' | hāramadhyamaṇau puṃsi yavāgūsurayosstriyām' iti medinī | atrāpi sataralāhārā iti śleṣeṇa pūrvāvasthānuvṛttirdarśitā ||
yathāvā
daśamukhaśamanāttridivikṣobhe daśarathasutena śamite'pi | saṃkulitā dyaurbata madaviśṛṅkhalairāvaṇodbhaṭavilāsaiḥ || 1821 ||
dyauḥ svargaḥ madena viśṛṅkhalaiḥ nirargalaiḥ rāvaṇasya dāśānanasya udbhaṭavilāsaiḥ saṃkulitā saṃbādhitā | pakṣe madena viśṛṃkhalaḥ viga
litanigalaḥ airāvaṇaḥ airāvataḥ tasya udbhaṭavilāsaiḥ daśamukhāvasthānāvasare taddordaṇḍapratāpaśoṣitamadatayā'vasthita airāvatastasminnastamite prarūḍhamadatayā nirargalapracāro'janīti bhāvaḥ | atra daśamukhaśamane'pi divaḥ pūrvāvasthānuvṛttiśleṣojjīvitā ||
yathāvā
asamādhika eva bhavan purā murāre'tha tava padaṃ prepsuḥ | dadhadapi samādhimuccairasamādhika eva bhavati bata manujaḥ || 1822 ||
asamaḥ ādhiryasya saḥ avidyamānassamārdhyisya sa iti ca | asamādhika eva asadṛśādhiyukta eva | samādhirahita eva | nissamābhyadhika eveti ca | atrāpi asamādhikatvarūpapūrvāvasthānuvṛttiśleṣagarbhitā ||
yathāvā
pravisarati tava kaṭākṣe viśrāṇayate'nnamamba suguṇo yaḥ | api tasminvikale bata viśrāṇayate'nnameṣa suguṇatayā || 1823 ||
he amba ! tava kaṭākṣe pravisarati sati yaḥ pumān suguṇaḥ
sa ślāghyassa guṇī dhanyassa kulīnassa buddhimān |
sa śūrassa ca vikrānto yaṃ tvaṃ devi nirīkṣase ||
ityuktarītyā kalyāṇaguṇavān san annaṃ viśrāṇayate vitarati ‘śraṇa dāne' caurādikādasmātsvārthe ṇyāntāllaṭ | eṣa pumān tasmin tava kaṭākṣe vikale'pi svasmin parāvṛtte'pi suguṇatayā
annaṃ viśrāṇayate | batetyāścarye | parihārastu guṇasya sūdasya bhāvaḥ guṇatā suṣṭhu guṇatā suguṇatā | pākaśikṣitasūdatvamityarthaḥ tayā 'guṇo maurvyāmapradhāne rūpādau sūda indriye | tyāgaśauryādisattvādisaṃdhyādyāvṛttirajjuṣu' iti medinī | annaṃ viśrāṇayati visrāvitā śrāṇā yāvāgūḥ yasya tattathoktaṃ karoti paragṛhe annavisrāvayitā pācako bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | 'yavāgūruṣṇikā śrāṇā' ityamaraḥ | atrāpi pūrvāvasthā'nuvṛttiśleṣeṇa varṇitā ||
yathāvā
kaściddvijāgragaṇyo manuṣyadharmādhikaśriyaṃ śaure | avalambya tava kaṭākṣādbhūyassukhago'bhavatkila vicitram || 1824 ||
dvijāgragaṇyaḥ vihagaśreṣṭhaḥ viprāgraṇīḥ kucela iti tatvam | manuṣyadharmaiḥ manujadharmaiḥ adhikāṃ kuberādapyadhikāmiti vastusthitiḥ bhūyaḥ punaḥ sukhagaḥ śobhano vihagaḥ bhūyaḥ bhūri yat sukhaṃ tadgacchatīti tathokta iti vāstavārthaḥ | abhavat | kiletyaitihye | spaṣṭamanyat | atrāpi pūrvāvasthā'nuvṛttirdarśitā ||
vidhvastadaśamukhaśrīvibhīṣaṇavaśaṃgatā'pi bata laṅkā | abhivṛddhabahumukhaśrīrāsīdraghuvīra tejasā bhavataḥ || 1825 ||
abhivṛddhā bahumukhasya daśānanasya śrīḥ yasyāḥ nānāvidhā samṛddhirasyā iti tu tattvam | sarvaṃ prāgvat ||
yathāvā
viparītadaśāyāmapi viśiṣṭavarṇā girāṃ tava samānāḥ | sārasarasāssudhāmbudhisute na jahati svavarṇaparipāṭīm || 1826 ||
he sudhāmbudhisute! idaṃ tadvācomādhuryātiśayaṃ dyotayitum | viśiṣṭaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ varṇaḥ yeṣāṃ te brāhmaṇavarṇā ityarthaḥ | praśastākṣarā iti tu tattvam | ataeva tava girāṃ samānāḥ manasahitāḥ tvadvācā bahumānitā iti yāvat | mādhuryeṇa tvadvāktulyā iti tattvam | sārasarasāḥ pañcarasāḥ makarandāḥ, sārāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ sarasāścetyapyupaskāryam | ataeva viparītadaśāyāṃ dharmaviparyāsasamaye'pi svavarṇaparipāṭīṃ brāhmaṇavarṇocitakramaṃ na jahati | yathāpurameva vartanta iti bhāvaḥ | vastutastu prātilomyena paṭhanāvasare'pi svavarṇānāṃ nijākṣarāṇāṃ paripāṭīṃ anukramaṃ na jahati | sārasarasā ityasyānulomavilomapaṭhanayostulyānupūrvīkatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atra pūrvāvasthānuvṛttiśśabdārthatādātmyamūlakaśleṣojjīviteti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
nikhilajagaccheṣiṇa iha saṃprati śaurerbhavan sadādāsaḥ | naikyaṃ bhajetkadā'pi pratīkamukto'pi tādṛgeva syāt || 1827 ||
nikhilajagaccheṣiṇaḥ śaureḥ saṃprati adhunā etaddehasaṃbandhitādaśāyāmiti yāvat | sadādāso bhavan janaḥ
dāsabhūtāssvatassarve hyātmānaḥ paramātmanaḥ |
ato'hamapi te dāsa iti matvā namāmyaham ||
ityādipramāṇāditi bhāvaḥ | pratīkena avayavena śarīreṇeti yāvat | mukto'pi pratīkānmukto'pīti vā | etaddehaprahāṇapūrvakaṃ niśśreyasaṃ prāpto'pīti yāvat | aikyaṃ tasya bhagavataḥ abhedaṃ kadācidapi na bhajet na prāpnuyāt | kiṃtu tādṛgeva tatsadṛśa eva syāt | ‘nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti' iti paramasāmyaśravaṇāt |
anena
yathodakaṃ śuddhe śuddhamāsiktaṃ tādṛgeva bhavati |
evaṃ munervijānata ātmā bhavati gautama ||
iti śrutyartho varṇitaḥ | asyāśśruterayamarthaḥ  śuddhe śuddhaṃ jalaṃ prakṣiptaṃ tatsadṛśameva bhavati na tu kiṃcidapi visadṛśam | evaṃ manananididhyāsanaśīlasyātmā'pi paramātmasadṛśa eveti na tvabhedaṃ prāpnoti, tādṛkchabdaśravaṇāt | tādṛkchabdastatsadṛśavācī | sādṛśyaṃ ca bhedaghaṭitameveti | kiṃca tādṛgeva sadādāsa eva syāt na tu svayaṃ sarvaśeṣīti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe sadādāsaśabdaḥ pratīkaṃ pratipratilomaṃ yathāsyāttathā ‘pratikūle pratīkaṃ syāt' ityamaraḥ | ukto'pi paṭhato'pi tādṛgeva sadādāsa eva syāt na tu vibhinnānupūrvīka iti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvāvasthānuvartanaṃ pūrvavadeva | idaṃ pūrvarūpaprakāradvayamapi jayadevopajñam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre pūrvarūpasara ekonāśītitamaḥ
<30>
athātadguṇasaraḥ
atadguṇaṃ saṃgatānyaguṇānaṅgīkṛtiṃ viduḥ |
guṇavatsannidhāvapi tadīyaguṇānaṅgīkāro'tadguṇālaṃkāraḥ ||
ityādipramāṇāditi bhāvaḥ | pratīkena avayavena śarīreṇeti yāvat | mukto'pi pratīkānmukto'pīti vā | etaddehaprahāṇapūrvakaṃ niśśreyasaṃ prāpto'pīti yāvat | aikyaṃ tasya bhagavataḥ abhedaṃ kadācidapi na bhajet na prāpnuyāt | kiṃtu tādṛgeva tatsadṛśa eva syāt | ‘nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti' iti paramasāmyaśravaṇāt |
anena
yathodakaṃ śuddhe śuddhamāsiktaṃ tādṛgeva bhavati |
evaṃ munervijānata ātmā bhavati gautama ||
iti śrutyartho varṇitaḥ | asyāśśruterayamarthaḥ  śuddhe śuddhaṃ jalaṃ prakṣiptaṃ tatsadṛśameva bhavati na tu kiṃcidapi visadṛśam | evaṃ manananididhyāsanaśīlasyātmā'pi paramātmasadṛśa eveti na tvabhedaṃ prāpnoti, tādṛkchabdaśravaṇāt | tādṛkchabdastatsadṛśavācī | sādṛśyaṃ ca bhedaghaṭitameveti | kiṃca tādṛgeva sadādāsa eva syāt na tu svayaṃ sarvaśeṣīti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe sadādāsaśabdaḥ pratīkaṃ pratipratilomaṃ yathāsyāttathā ‘pratikūle pratīkaṃ syāt' ityamaraḥ | ukto'pi paṭhato'pi tādṛgeva sadādāsa eva syāt na tu vibhinnānupūrvīka iti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvāvasthānuvartanaṃ pūrvavadeva | idaṃ pūrvarūpaprakāradvayamapi jayadevopajñam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre pūrvarūpasara ekonāśītitamaḥ
athātadguṇasaraḥ
atadguṇaṃ saṃgatānyaguṇānaṅgīkṛtiṃ viduḥ |
guṇavatsannidhāvapi tadīyaguṇānaṅgīkāro'tadguṇālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
vidalitavalaripumaṇivarakānte phaṇiśailanāthahṛdayānte | api satatakṛtanivāsā nāsādayase'mba kiṃcidapi nailyam || 1828 ||
yathāvā
śucitaraphaṇivararucibharaśoṇaphaṇāmaṇimarīcinicayena | niculitarucirapi nitarāṃ phaṇiśayana jahāsi nāsitatvaṃ svam || 1829 ||
yathāvā
parihitamapyanavarataṃ pītāmbaramambujākṣa bhavatedam | na jahāti pītatāṃ svāṃ paridadhadapi tadbhavānasitatāṃ ca || 1830 ||
yathāvā
api kamalānilaya hare tvadrucikamalārucī mitho milite | pravivikte eva bhṛśaṃ bhāto yamunāsarasvatītulye || 1831 ||
atrādyodāharaṇe paraguṇāgrahaṇaṃ śābdam | svaguṇatyāgābhāva arthaḥ | anantarayorudāharaṇayoḥ svaguṇatyāgābhāvaśśābdaḥ| paraguṇāgrahaṇaṃ tvārtham | anayorapi dvitīyodāharaṇe pītāmbarabhagavatoḥ te parasparamiti viśeṣaḥ | turīyodāharaṇe tūbhayamapyārthamityavadheyam ||
na cānyaguṇenānyatra guṇodayānudayarūpābhyāmullāsāvajñālaṃkārābhyāṃ tadguṇātadguṇayorgatārthateti vācyam | ullāsāvajñālakṣaṇaghaṭako guṇaśabdo doṣapratyanīkavācī | anyaguṇenānyatra guṇodayānudayau ca na tasyaiva guṇasya saṃkramāsaṃkramau | kiṃtu sadācāryopadeśena sadasacchātrayorbodhodayānudayavattadguṇajanyatayā saṃbhāvitayorguṇāntarayorudayānudayau | tadguṇātadguṇayostu guṇaśabdo rūparasagandhādivācī | tatrānyadīyaguṇagrahaṇāgrahaṇe ca raktasphaṭikavasanamālinyādinyāyenānyadīyaguṇenaivānurañjanānurañjane vivakṣite tathaiva ca darśitānyudāharaṇāni | evaṃca pradarśayiṣyamāṇe udāharaṇe nātadguṇālaṃkāraḥ | kiṃtvavajñālaṃkāra eva ||
śatakoṭito'pi śatakoṭyadhikakaṭhore phaṇīndragiriśikhare | nivasannapyaviratamiha naiṣṭhuryaṃ nātha nāśnuṣe kvāpi || 1832 ||
atra kaṭhoratārūpaguṇasyānuparañjakatvāttadanaṅgīkāro nātadguṇaḥ ||
yathāvā
nihitā'pi vidhau prasabhaṃ na hi tādṛśaśuddhimeti capalā dhīḥ| vṛddhā'pi samānatayā na sairibhī bhajati saurabhīvṛttim || 1833 ||
vidhau bhagavati candre ca | nihitā'pi dhīḥ tādṛśīṃ tatprāptyanuguṇāṃ vidhusadṛśīṃ ca śuddhiṃ nairmalyaṃ vijātīyapratyayāsaṃvalitatvamiti yāvat | pakṣe dhavalimānaṃ naiti hi | kuta ityatrāha  capaleti | capalatvādeveti bhāvaḥ | samānatayā vṛddhā'pi saura
