bhībhissahābhivṛddhā'pi sairibhī mahiṣī | jātilakṣaṇo ṅīṣ | saurabhī surabhiḥ gauḥ suradhenurvā 'surabhirgavi ca striyām' ityamaraḥ tasyā iyaṃ saurabhī, aṇṇantalakṣaṇo ṅīṣ | tatsaṃbandhinī vatsatarī tasyāḥ vṛttiṃ sthitiṃ na bhajati | vatsataryā eva vṛttiṃ na bhajati kiṃpunassākṣātsurabhivṛttimiti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe samānatayā saurabhīśabdena saha tulyatayā vṛddhā vṛddhasaṃjñāṃ prāptā'pi ubhayorapyacāmādervṛddhatvāt ‘vṛddhiryasyācāmādistadvṛddham' itihyanuśāsanam | sairibhī sairibhīti śabdaḥ | arthagatastrītvasya śabde āropaḥ | sairabhīvṛttiṃ saurabhīśabdasya sthitiṃ na bhajati | ubhayorapi śabdayorvṛddhatve'pi tayoranyonyaṃ vijātīyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | kiṃca sairibhīśabdaḥ saurabhīśabdasya vṛttiṃ na bhajati | surabheriyaṃ saurabhīti taddhitavṛttimatvāttasya, svasya ca kevalarūḍhatayā tādṛśavṛttimattvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntarūpo mahāvākyārthaḥ, bimbapratibimbabhāvāpannatvāt | tatra dārṣṭāntikavākyārthaḥ vidhuprabhṛtiśabdapratipādyabhagavaccandrādirūpārthadvayaśleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyānuprāṇitātadguṇālaṃkārarūpa eva | dṛṣṭāntavākyārthastu pūrvoktarītyā'vajñālaṃkārarūpa iti pūrvodāharaṇebhyo vicchittiviśeṣaḥ ||
atra guṇagrāhakāpekṣayā sannihitasya guṇavata utkṛṣṭatvasamatvābhyāṃ dvaividhyamiti sarvasvakāraḥ | tasyāyamāśayo’pakṛṣṭasaṃbandhiguṇāgrahaṇasya svābhāvikatvena vaicitryānādhāyakatvādanalaṃkārataivetyapakṛṣṭatvena tṛtīyaḥ prakārastu na saṃbhavatīti | anye avāntaracamatkāraviśeṣavirahāt dvaividhyamāpi neti vadanti | yadyapi guṇavadvastusāṃnidhyalakṣaṇakāraṇasadbhāve'pi guṇānudayasyāvajñālaṃkārātadguṇayossadbhāvādimau
viśeṣoktito nātiricyete 'kāryā
janirviśeṣoktissati puṣkalakāraṇe' iti tatsāmānyalakṣaṇākrāntatvāt | tathā'pi ullāsatadguṇapratidvaṃdvinā viśeṣākāreṇālaṃkārāntaratayā parigaṇitāvityāhuḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre tadguṇasaro'śītitamaḥ
athānuguṇasaraḥ
svasajātīyaguṇavadanyasāṃnidhyato yadi |
utkarṣaḥ pūrvasiddhasya guṇasyānuguṇaṃ hi tat ||
svasamānaguṇavadanyasannidhānātprāgvidyamānasvakīyaguṇotkarṣavarṇanamanuguṇālaṃkāraḥ | ayaṃ ca jayadevopakramam ||
yathāvā
saṃsiddhita indīvarasaṃsaddhitaruci tato'ñjanenāktam | triguṇāñjanamiva ramaṇāvalokane tadrucāmba tava nayanam || 1834 ||
saṃsiddhitaḥ svabhāvataḥ 'saṃsiddhiprakṛtī same' ityamaraḥ | indīvarāṇāṃ saṃsat sabhā samūhaḥ tasyāḥ hitaṃ tatsadṛśamiti yāvat | atra prakṛtyaiva siddhasya lakṣmīnayanaśyāmalimnaḥ añjanādinā utkarṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
navapadmarāgakāñcīchavimasṛṇitakanakavasanajanitarucā | churitaṃ tava jagadamba sphurati vapurdivyamatipiśaṅgatayā || 1835 ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre anuguṇasara ekāśītitamaḥ
<31>
janirviśeṣoktissati puṣkalakāraṇe' iti tatsāmānyalakṣaṇākrāntatvāt | tathā'pi ullāsatadguṇapratidvaṃdvinā viśeṣākāreṇālaṃkārāntaratayā parigaṇitāvityāhuḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre tadguṇasaro'śītitamaḥ
athānuguṇasaraḥ
svasajātīyaguṇavadanyasāṃnidhyato yadi |
utkarṣaḥ pūrvasiddhasya guṇasyānuguṇaṃ hi tat ||
svasamānaguṇavadanyasannidhānātprāgvidyamānasvakīyaguṇotkarṣavarṇanamanuguṇālaṃkāraḥ | ayaṃ ca jayadevopakramam ||
yathāvā
saṃsiddhita indīvarasaṃsaddhitaruci tato'ñjanenāktam | triguṇāñjanamiva ramaṇāvalokane tadrucāmba tava nayanam || 1834 ||
saṃsiddhitaḥ svabhāvataḥ 'saṃsiddhiprakṛtī same' ityamaraḥ | indīvarāṇāṃ saṃsat sabhā samūhaḥ tasyāḥ hitaṃ tatsadṛśamiti yāvat | atra prakṛtyaiva siddhasya lakṣmīnayanaśyāmalimnaḥ añjanādinā utkarṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
navapadmarāgakāñcīchavimasṛṇitakanakavasanajanitarucā | churitaṃ tava jagadamba sphurati vapurdivyamatipiśaṅgatayā || 1835 ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre anuguṇasara ekāśītitamaḥ
<32>
atha mīlitasaraḥ
na dṛśyate bheda eva sādṛśyādyadi mīlitam |
sadṛśayorvastunossādṛśyādbhedānabhivyaktau mīlitaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
kāñcanyanulepanamiha kāñcanagaure muhustava kapole | vidadhānaṃ tadadarśanakhinnaṃ svinnaṃ patiṃ hasasi padme || 1836 ||
he padme ! kāñcanī haridrā 'niśākhyā kāñcanī pītā haridrā' ityamaraḥ | taccūrṇamiti yāvat | tasyāḥ anulepanaṃ pariṇayotsavādāviti bhāvaḥ | kāñcanagaure tava kapole muhuḥ vidadhānaṃ tasyānulepanasyādarśanena sāvarṇyādvivicyāgrahaṇena khinnaṃ vṛthaiva mama prayāso'janīti khedaśālinaṃ ataeva svinnaṃ patiṃ hasasi | atra kamalākapolaphalakaharidrācūrṇānulepanayorgauraguṇasāmyādbhedānadhyavasāyaḥ ||
yathāvā
śyāmalavikasitakuvalayakomalarūpe nisargasurabhiṇi te | alike murahara mṛgamadatilakamidaṃ kena nāma jānīyām || 1837 ||
atra śyāmalimasaurabhābhyāṃ śrīnivāsaphālamṛgamadatilakayossāmyādbhedānadhyavasāyaḥ ||
prācāṃ mate tvevaṃ lakṣaṇam
vastutulyena liṅgena nijenāgantukena vā | pidhīyate vastunā cenmīlitaṃ tadudāhṛtam ||
yatra prabalena kenacidvastunā svābhāvikenāgantukena vā svanigūhyavastvantarasādhāraṇena svagatena tulyena liṅgena cihlena vastvantaraṃ pidhīyate nigūhyate tatrobhayatrāpi mīlitaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ | vastudvayasya lakṣaṇasāmyāttayoḥ kenacidbalīyasā tadanyasya svarūpatirodhānaṃ mīlitamiti niṣkarṣaḥ | na cātra ‘tāṭaṅkavajrarucibhara' ityādivakṣyamāṇātsāmānyānna vailakṣaṇyamiti vācyaṃ, yatra vastudvayasya liṅgasāmye sati tayormadhye kenacidvalinā tadanyasvarūpameva tirodhīyate tatra mīlitaṃ; yatra tu svarūpapratītāvapi guṇasāmyādbhedatirodhānaṃ tatra sāmānyamiti vailakṣaṇyāt ||
yathā
kākutsthasya nisargādaruṇimakānte vilocanayugānte | krodhodayaṃ nadīnāṃ nātho nāvidadataściraṃ stabdhaḥ || 1838 ||
yathāvā
lakṣmyāḥ kapolaphalake nisargagaure hiraṇyavarṇāyāḥ | praṇayādroṣo yo'janyathokṣajenāpi sa kila nājñāyi || 1839 ||
yathāvā
smitalalitaṃ tava vadanaṃ svata eva vikāsitāmarasanayanam | kṛtapadamapyamba tvayi kuta eva nateṣvanugrahaṃ vidyām || 1840 ||
ataeva stabdhaḥ svātmānamapradarśayannavasthita ityarthaḥ | atrādye kākutsthadṛgantena svasya naisargikeṇa svanigūhyakrodhodayasādhāraṇena cāruṇimarūpeṇa liṅgena krodhodaya āgantuko nigūhyate | dvitīye tu śrīkapolaphalakena svasya svatassiddhena svagopanīyaroṣasādhāraṇenāruṇyena liṅgenāgantuko roṣo nigūhyate | tṛtīye tu śriyo vadanena svasya svābhāvikena svanigūhitavyānugrahasādhāraṇena smitalalitatvādinā liṅgena svābhāviko'nugraho nigūhyata iti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
dhanuṣi ravāvuṣasi hariṃ vṛṣaśikhariṇyañcatāṃ saromāñcam | bhaktiḥ kṛtāspadā'pi vyaktiṃ nopaiti hṛdi sakampānām || 1841 ||
atrāgantukahemantasamayajanitapulakakampalakṣaṇanigūhitavyasādhāraṇadharmarūpeṇa liṅgena bhaktiḥ svarūpata eva tirodhīyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre mīlitasaro dvyaśītitamaḥ
<33>
atha sāmānyālaṃkārasaraḥ
na gṛhyate | viśeṣaścetsāmyātsāmānyamīritam ||
anekasya vastunassāmyena yat vyāvartakadharmānupalambhaḥ tatsāmānyaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
tāmarasanayanam | kṛtapadamapyamba tvayi kuta eva nateṣvanugrahaṃ vidyām || 1840 ||
ataeva stabdhaḥ svātmānamapradarśayannavasthita ityarthaḥ | atrādye kākutsthadṛgantena svasya naisargikeṇa svanigūhyakrodhodayasādhāraṇena cāruṇimarūpeṇa liṅgena krodhodaya āgantuko nigūhyate | dvitīye tu śrīkapolaphalakena svasya svatassiddhena svagopanīyaroṣasādhāraṇenāruṇyena liṅgenāgantuko roṣo nigūhyate | tṛtīye tu śriyo vadanena svasya svābhāvikena svanigūhitavyānugrahasādhāraṇena smitalalitatvādinā liṅgena svābhāviko'nugraho nigūhyata iti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
dhanuṣi ravāvuṣasi hariṃ vṛṣaśikhariṇyañcatāṃ saromāñcam | bhaktiḥ kṛtāspadā'pi vyaktiṃ nopaiti hṛdi sakampānām || 1841 ||
atrāgantukahemantasamayajanitapulakakampalakṣaṇanigūhitavyasādhāraṇadharmarūpeṇa liṅgena bhaktiḥ svarūpata eva tirodhīyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre mīlitasaro dvyaśītitamaḥ
atha sāmānyālaṃkārasaraḥ
na gṛhyate | viśeṣaścetsāmyātsāmānyamīritam ||
anekasya vastunassāmyena yat vyāvartakadharmānupalambhaḥ tatsāmānyaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
varṣāvanavihṛtau ghanavalaye tvāṃ vṛṣagirīśa jaladhisutā | tāṃ ca na kāñcanavarṇāṃ vidyutsu bhavānvivicya nāgṛhṇāt || 1842 ||
mīlitālaṃkāre sarvātmanā bhedasyaiva sāmānyadharmanibandhanasya tattadvyaktitvādiviśeṣanibandhanasya cānupalambhaḥ, sāmānye tu vyaktīnāṃ parasparabhedāvabodhe'pi parasparavyāvartakadharmānuphalambhamātraṃ sādṛśyādupanibadhyata iti bhidā | mīlitālaṃkārodāharaṇe hi nisargagaurāllakṣmīkapolaphalakādervastvantaratvenāgantukaṃ haridrānulepanagauratvādikaṃ na bhāsate | sāmānyodāharaṇe tu bhagavallakṣmyorghanavidyutośca vyaktyantaratvena bhānamastyeva | kiṃtu ayaṃ bhagavān ayaṃ ghanaḥ iyaṃ lakṣmīḥ iyaṃ vidyuditi viśeṣastu nopalakṣyate | ataeva bhedatirodhānānmīlitaṃ tadatirodhāne'pi sāmyena vyāvartakānavabhāse sāmānyamityubhayorapyanvarthatā ||
yathāvā
madhumathana ślathamalike tavālakaṃ tilakamapi jano bhinnam | atimātrabhidāyāmapi sahasā bahuvarṇasāmyato nāvait || 1843 ||
he bhagavan! janaḥ tava alike lalāṭe ślathaḥ alakaṃ tilakaṃ mṛgamadatilakaṃ ca atimātrabhidāyāṃ satyāmapi ativelabhede satyapi pakṣe avarṇativarṇābhyāṃ bhede satyapītyarthaḥ | bahu yat varṇasya nīlavarṇasya sāmyaṃ tasmāt pakṣe bahūnāṃ lakārādīnāṃ varṇānāṃ sāmyāt bhinnaṃ viśeṣavat nāvait | atrālakatilakayorbhinnayoreva satorvarṇasāmyānna viśeṣagrahaṇam | camatkārāntaraṃ tu pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
nailyaṃ ghane'sti mādhava taulyaṃ tasyāsti vigrahe bhavataḥ | naitau viśeṣabhājau jñātau tāvantatassadṛśavarṇau || 1844 ||
iyaṃ ghanāgame vane viharantaṃ bhagavantaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ prati kasyaciduktiḥ | he mādhava! ghane meghe nailyaṃ śyāmalimā asti ata eva tasya ghanasya taulyaṃ bhavataḥ tava vigrahe vapuṣi asti ata eva sadṛśavarṇau tulyavarṇau tāvetau ghanabhagavadvigrahau antataḥ dūraṃ paryālocane iti yāvat | viśeṣabhājau vailakṣaṇyabhājau na na bhavataḥ dvayorapi nīlatvāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe tāvetau nailyaṃ taulyamiti śabdau tāvetāviti pulliṅganirdeśo viśeṣyakaṭākṣeṇa | antataḥ ante sadṛśaḥ varṇaḥ lyamityākārakavarṇaḥ yayostau | nai tau iti varṇābhyāṃ viśeṣabhājau vailakṣaṇyayujau jñātau ityarthaḥ | nailyataulyaśabdayostābhyāmeva viśeṣadarśanādityartho'pi camatkārī | atrāpi pūrvavadeva ||
prācāṃ mate tvevaṃ lakṣaṇam
guṇataulyavivakṣātaḥ parasya prastutena yat | aikātmyamucyate yogātsāmānyaṃ tadudīritam ||
yatra vastutaḥ prastutāt bhinnamasya prastutaṃ guṇasāmyavivakṣayā prastutātpṛthagapratīyamānatayā nibadhyate tatra yogāt samānaguṇasaṃbandharūpāt sāmānyaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ | vastudvayasya pṛthak svarūpapratītāvapi guṇasāmyādbhedatirodhānaṃ sāmānyamiti niṣkarṣaḥ | asya ca mīlitādvailakṣaṇyaṃ tatraiva nirūpitam | tadguṇe
tu svaguṇaparityāgapūrvako'nyaguṇaparigrahaḥ | iha tu svaguṇamaparityajyaiva tulyaguṇādanyasmāt pṛthagavibhāvyamānateti tato bhidā ||
yathā
keyūrayugalakhacitā māyūrarucirharinmaṇīśreṇī | haridivyavigraharucā labdhādvaitā vivicya nāgrāhi || 1845 ||
yathāvā
tāṭaṅkavajrarucibharakabalitarūpā tavāmba radanālī | nipuṇanirūpaṇakuśalairapi naiva pṛthaktayā janairjajñe || 1846 ||
atrādye udāharaṇe prastutabhagavaddivyavigraharucyapekṣayā haritaguṇasāmyādaprastutagārutmatamaṇīśreṇyāḥ pṛthagavibhāvyamānatoktā | dvitīye tu aprastutāttāṭaṅkavajrarucibharāt dhavalaguṇasāmyātprastutāyā lakṣmīradanāvalyāḥ pṛthagapratīyamānatā nibaddheti bhedaḥ | evaṃca etanmate ‘padmākarapraviṣṭānāṃ mukhaṃ nālakṣi subhruvām' ityanyadīye padye ‘varṣāvanavihṛtau' ityāsmākīne padye ca mukhapadmādīnāṃ bhedāvabhāse'pi vyāvartakāsphuraṇādalaṃkārāntareṇa bhāvyam | svarūpātirodhānena mīlitāsaṃbhavāt bhedātirodhānena
sāmānyasyāpyasaṃbhavāt | yadvā guṇasāmyādviśeṣāgraha iti sāmānyālaṃkārasāmānyalakṣaṇam | viśeṣāgrahaśca kvacidbhede gṛhyamāṇe kvaciccāgṛhyamāṇe iti kalpayitvā tadavāntarabhedena bhāvyam | prāthamike 'na dṛśyate bheda eva sādṛśyādyadi mīlitam' iti mate
apāṅgatarale dṛśau taralavakravarṇā gīro
vilāsabharamantharā gatiratīva kāntaṃ mukham |
iti sphuritamaṅgake mṛgadṛśāṃ svato līlayā
tadatra na madodayaḥ kṛtapado'pi saṃlakṣyate ||
iti prācīnapadye tanmīlitodāharaṇe 'dhanuṣi ravāvuṣasi harim' ityāsmākīne padye ca svarūpatirodhānasthale svarūpato jñāyamāne vastudvaye sādṛśyādbhedāgrahaṇe mīlitamiti lakṣaṇe abhyupagamyamāne alaṃkārāntareṇa bhāvyam | sādṛśyādbhedāgrahaṇamityetāvanmātraṃ mīlitamiti lakṣaṇāṅgīkāre mīlitāvāntarabhedena bhāvyamiti dhyeyam | sarvamidaṃ kuvalayānande spaṣṭam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre sāmānyasarastryaśītitamaḥ
<34>
athonmīlitasaraḥ
sphūtā samānaguṇayorbhedasyonmīlitaṃ viduḥ |
guṇasāmyena bhedānadhyavasāye prāpte kenāpi nimittena bhedasphūrtau mīlitapratidvaṃdvi unmīlitam ||
yathā
tīre vihṛtijuṣastava pūre gātradyutessarati śaureḥ | tanmilitāṃ dyumaṇisutāṃ śaiśiryeṇaiva jānate gopyaḥ || 1847 ||
apāṅgatarale dṛśau taralavakravarṇā gīro
vilāsabharamantharā gatiratīva kāntaṃ mukham |
iti sphuritamaṅgake mṛgadṛśāṃ svato līlayā
tadatra na madodayaḥ kṛtapado'pi saṃlakṣyate ||
iti prācīnapadye tanmīlitodāharaṇe 'dhanuṣi ravāvuṣasi harim' ityāsmākīne padye ca svarūpatirodhānasthale svarūpato jñāyamāne vastudvaye sādṛśyādbhedāgrahaṇe mīlitamiti lakṣaṇe abhyupagamyamāne alaṃkārāntareṇa bhāvyam | sādṛśyādbhedāgrahaṇamityetāvanmātraṃ mīlitamiti lakṣaṇāṅgīkāre mīlitāvāntarabhedena bhāvyamiti dhyeyam | sarvamidaṃ kuvalayānande spaṣṭam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre sāmānyasarastryaśītitamaḥ
athonmīlitasaraḥ
sphūtā samānaguṇayorbhedasyonmīlitaṃ viduḥ |
guṇasāmyena bhedānadhyavasāye prāpte kenāpi nimittena bhedasphūrtau mīlitapratidvaṃdvi unmīlitam ||
yathā
tīre vihṛtijuṣastava pūre gātradyutessarati śaureḥ | tanmilitāṃ dyumaṇisutāṃ śaiśiryeṇaiva jānate gopyaḥ || 1847 ||
atra nīlaguṇasāmyena bhagavaddivyavigraharucipūrabhedānadhyavasāye prāpte śaiśiryeṇa nimittena yamunāyāstato bhedasphūrterunmīlitam ||
tadguṇarītyā bhedānadhyavasāyaprāptāvapīdaṃ dṛśyate | yathāvā
śītadyutividyotaiḥ śvetadvīpati carācare nikhile | rāsavilāse mādhavameṇadṛśo veṇunādato'nvabudhan || 1848 ||
atra bhagavatassvanīlimaparityāgapūrvakacandrajyotsnāviśadimaguṇagrahaṇalakṣaṇatadguṇālaṃkāraprāpitabhedānadhyavasāyopanatau veṇunādena nimittena bhagavatastato bhedasphuraṇam | anvabudhannityatra ‘budhir bodhane' ityasmādbhauvādikāllaṅ | idittvāt cleraṅ | prayogaśca 'sa ca kākutstha nābudhat' iti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre unmīlitasaraścaturaśītitamaḥ
<35>
atha viśeṣakasaraḥ
dvayortvaidharmyasaṃsphūrtau tulyayossyādviśeṣakaḥ |
bhedatirodhānābhāve'pi sādṛśyādviśeṣāsphuraṇe prāpte kenacinnimittena viśeṣasphūrtī sāmānyapratidvaṃdvī viśeṣakaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvatkṛpayā muktānāṃ caturbhujatvādilakṣmaṇi samāne | vakṣassthitayā lakṣmyā tvāṃ kila niścinvate'bhinavamuktāḥ || 1849 ||
atra nīlaguṇasāmyena bhagavaddivyavigraharucipūrabhedānadhyavasāye prāpte śaiśiryeṇa nimittena yamunāyāstato bhedasphūrterunmīlitam ||
tadguṇarītyā bhedānadhyavasāyaprāptāvapīdaṃ dṛśyate | yathāvā
śītadyutividyotaiḥ śvetadvīpati carācare nikhile | rāsavilāse mādhavameṇadṛśo veṇunādato'nvabudhan || 1848 ||
atra bhagavatassvanīlimaparityāgapūrvakacandrajyotsnāviśadimaguṇagrahaṇalakṣaṇatadguṇālaṃkāraprāpitabhedānadhyavasāyopanatau veṇunādena nimittena bhagavatastato bhedasphuraṇam | anvabudhannityatra ‘budhir bodhane' ityasmādbhauvādikāllaṅ | idittvāt cleraṅ | prayogaśca 'sa ca kākutstha nābudhat' iti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre unmīlitasaraścaturaśītitamaḥ
atha viśeṣakasaraḥ
dvayortvaidharmyasaṃsphūrtau tulyayossyādviśeṣakaḥ |
bhedatirodhānābhāve'pi sādṛśyādviśeṣāsphuraṇe prāpte kenacinnimittena viśeṣasphūrtī sāmānyapratidvaṃdvī viśeṣakaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvatkṛpayā muktānāṃ caturbhujatvādilakṣmaṇi samāne | vakṣassthitayā lakṣmyā tvāṃ kila niścinvate'bhinavamuktāḥ || 1849 ||
atra muktānāṃ bhagavataśca bhedatirodhānābhāve'pi caturbhujatvena sādṛśyādviśeṣāsphuraṇe prāpte lakṣmīsphuraduraskatvena bhagavato viśeṣasphūrterviśeṣakaḥ ||
yathāvā
samamasitāsu svarucā kamalādiṣu nātha divyamahilāsu | sarasijasaurabhavibhavābhāve padmā tvayā'pi duravagamā || 1850 ||
atra bhagavatyāḥ padmāyāḥ svaguṇatyāgapūrvakabhagavacchyāmalimagrahaṇarūpatadguṇālaṅkārarītyā divyamahilāntarasādhāraṇe nīlimani taddhṛtakamalasaurabheṇa viśeṣasphūrtiriti pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ | imau dvāvapyalaṃkārau dīkṣitopakrame eva ||
prāñcastu  "bhedavaiṣamyayoḥ sphūrtau nālaṃkāradvayaṃ kalpyaṃ kiṃtūktālaṃkārābhāvamātraṃ, bhedavaijātyasphuraṇayoḥ svatassiddhatayā kavipratibhāgocaratāmūlakavaicitryavirahāt | na ca mīlitasāmānyarītibhyāṃ bhedaviśeṣasphūrtau tadapavādavarṇanakṛtavaicitryasadbhāvādalaṃkārāntarakalpanaṃ yuktamiti vācyaṃ, tāvatā vaicitryābhāvatādavasthyāt" ityāhuḥ | paṇḍitarājastu imāmanumāne'ntarbhāvayati sma ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre viśeṣakasaraḥ pañcāśītatamaḥ
<36>
athottarālaṃkārasaraḥ
unnītapraśnamathavā nibaddhapraśnamuttaram |
sākūtamucyate yatra tatrottaramudīritam ||
yatra uttarātsākūtānnibadhyamānātpraśna unnīyate tatredamuttaram | na cedamanumānaṃ, pakṣadharmatvādinirdeśavirahāt | asakṛtpraśnapratipādanapūrvakaṃ sākūtamuttaraṃ dvitīyam | idameva gūḍhottaramiti vyavahriyate | yathā lakṣitaṃ kuvalayānande ‘kiṃcidākūtasahitaṃ syādgūḍhottaramuttaram' iti | neyaṃ parisaṃkhyā, tadvadatra vyavacchedyavyavacchedakabhāvasyāvivakṣitatvāt ||
tatrādyaṃ yathā
tata ita iha saṃbhramataḥ kuta iva vicinoṣi nandasuta gāṃ tvam | kālindītaṭakuñje kāmaṃ dīvyati hi tatra sulabhaiṣā || 1851 ||
idaṃ svayūdhāccyutāṃ gāṃ vicinvantaṃ bhagavantaṃ nandanandanaṃ prati taṃ kāmayamānāyā gopakāminyā uttaraṃ kalindītīranikuñje'stu svācchandyamāvayorityākūtagarbham | gopapraśno'tronnītaḥ | gāmapṛṣṭāyā gavāvasthānakathanasyāyogāt ||
dvitīyaṃ yathā
kiṃ cintayase bhadre kiṃ tvayi kathanena śāmyati mamaitat | brūhi tathā'pica śṛṇuyāmavidanniva kṛṣṇa pṛcchasi vicitram || 1852 ||
kiṃ cintayase bhadre iti kāṃcidgopalalanāṃ prati bhagavataḥ kṛṣṇasya praśnaḥ | kiṃ tvayītyādi tasyā uttaram | atrādyasya praśnasya cintāhetuṃ cedvadasi tadā pratikariṣyāmīti vyaṅgyam | uttarasya tu upabhogena vinā kevalapraśnottarābhyāṃ kaḥ pratīkārassyāditi | dvitīyasya tu praśnasya jñāta eva tvadabhiprāyaḥ | athāpi tvadvadanāttaṃ śuśrūṣeyetyabhisaṃdhyudghāṭanam | uttarasya tu jñāta eva tvayā madabhiprāyaḥ kiṃ punaḥpunaḥ praśnakālayāpaneneti | unnītapraśne sakṛduttarasya cārutvaṃ, nibaddhapraśne tu praśnottarayorasakṛdupanyāse tat | ayaṃ cottarālaṃkāro dvividho'pi praśnottarayoranyatarasyobhayorapi sābhiprāyatvena nirabhiprāyatvena ca caturvidha ityaṣṭadheti prāñcaḥ | tatra prāthamike ‘tata itaḥ' iti padye unnītaḥ praśnaḥ kiṃ gaustvayā madīyā dṛṣṭetyākārakaścaturasārvabhaumeṇa bhagavatā kṛṣṇena vaktrā vaiśiṣṭyātsaṃbhogarūpeṇābhiprāyeṇa garbhitaḥ | uttaramapi tenaivābhiprāyeṇa garbhitam | evaṃ nibaddhapraśne ‘kiṃ cintayase' iti padye'pi praśnottarayorubhayorapi sābhiprāyatvam | evamanyatarasābhiprāyaprabhedā apyudāhāryāḥ ||
praśnottarayorubhayorapi nirabhiprāyatve unnītapraśnamuttaraṃ yathā
iha vā'muṣminvā tvaṃ bahusukhamadhigamya vastumicchasicet | vṛjinapathaṃ vijahadimaṃ bhujagagirīnduṃ bhajasva niśśaṅkam || 1853 ||
vṛjinapathaṃ kuṭilamārgaṃ duritasaraṇiṃ vā | atra kasyaciddhitaiṣiṇaḥ puruṣasya kaṃcitpumāṃsaṃ pratyuttareṇa tatkartṛkasvahitānuśāsanapraśna unnīyate | tādṛśapraśnamantareṇa hitānuśāsanāyo
gāt | atra praśnottarayorubhayorapi nirabhiprāyatā, yathā'vasthitārthajñānanivedanaparatvāt ||
yathāvā
bhagavadbhāgavatārcanahetostṛṇamapi na jātu vitareyam | iti yadi kṛtaḥ paraḥ paṇa eṣa hi kṛpaṇaḥ kadaryaparyāyaḥ || 1854 ||
iti paraḥ atiśayitaḥ paṇaḥ kṛto yadi yenaivaṃ pratijñā kṛtetyarthaḥ | eṣa hi ayameva kṛpaṇaḥ ito'pyanyaḥ kaḥ kṛpaṇa iti bhāvaḥ | amumeva viśinaṣṭi kadaryeti | aryo vaiśyaḥ ‘aryassvāmivaiśyayoḥ' iti nipātito'yam | kutsitaścāsāvaryaśca kadaryaḥ 'koḥ kattatpuruṣe'ci' iti koḥ kadādeśaḥ | tasya paryāyaḥ sa evāyaṃ rūpāntaramāpanna iti bhāvaḥ | vaiśya eva nisargataḥ kṛpaṇaḥ tatrāpi kutsitatve tasya kārpaṇyaṃ kimu vaktavyamiti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe paṇaḥ paṇaśabdaḥ kṛtaḥ kṛvarṇāt ādyāditvātpañcamyāstasiḥ | paraḥ anantarabhāvī yadi eṣa eva kṛpaṇaḥ kṛpaṇaśabdaḥ kadaryaparyāyaḥ kadaryaśabdaparyāyatayā paṭhitaḥ | ‘kadarye kṛpaṇakṣudrakiṃpacānamitaṃpacāḥ' iti | atra kaḥ kṛpaṇa iti praśna unneyaḥ | praśnottarayordvayorapyanākūtatvaṃ ca pūrvavadeva | upadarśitacamatkārastu viśeṣaḥ ||
tatraiva nibaddhapraśnamuttaraṃ yathā
gantavyaḥ ko'higirirmantavyaṃ kiṃ vṛṣādripaticaritam | nantavyaḥ ko'bjākṣo rantavyaṃ kvaitadabhimate deśe || 1855 ||
yathāvā
dhyeyaṃ kiṃ brahma paraṃ jñeyaṃ kiṃ tatsvarūparūpaguṇam | geyaṃ kiṃ tasya yaśaḥ peyaṃ kiṃ tadabhidhānanibhamamṛtam || 1856 ||
svarūparūpaguṇamiti samāhāradvaṃdvaḥ | abhidhānamiti nibho vyājo yasya tat | atra sarve praśnā nibaddhāḥ | praśnottarayorubhayorapi pūrvavannirabhiprāyataiva ||
kā tvaṃ kā vā kiṃ tava gacchasyekākinīti pṛcchāmi | tvaṃ kila bahusāhyakaraḥ pṛcchasi govinda vedmi tava vacanam || 1857 ||
atra kā tvamiti kṛṣṇasya praśnassvopabhogārhatābhiprāyagarbhaḥ | kā vā kiṃ taveti gopakāminyā uttaraṃ kiṃ vṛthāpraśnamātreṇetyabhiprāyagarbham | gacchasyekākinīti pṛcchāmīti punaḥ kṛṣṇasya praśnastu svācchandyasaukaryābhiprāyakaḥ | tvaṃ kiletyādisopālambhāottaraṃ tu tvaṃ kevalavāṅmātracapalaḥ na tu svairavihārasāhasika ityabhiprāyagarbham | idaṃ ca padyaṃ caturṇāmapi nibaddhapraśnabhedānāmudāharaṇatāmarhati vaktṛvaidagdhyāvaidagdhyavyavasthayeti ||
atra jagannātho’alaṃkāre hyasmin praśnottaragatamasakṛdupanibaddhatvaṃ jīvātuḥ, tathaiva camatkārodayāt | tena sakṛtpraśnasya sakṛduttaraṃ nālaṅkārabhūmiḥ | na connītapraśnottare avyāptiḥ | unnītasya praśnasyaikatvādanupanibandhāccottarasyāpyekatvāditi vācyam|
prakṣagatamunnītatvamatrottareṇākṣiptatvaṃ na vivakṣitam | kiṃtu praśnottaraparaṃparāyāṃ prācīnottaraśravaṇajanyatvamātramityabhāṇīt ||
yathā
kva gamiṣyasyahiśailaṃ kiṃ tatrāstyadbhutaṃ paraṃ tattvam | tatkīdṛk saśrīkaṃ kā śrīryasyā vibhūtirakhilamidam || 1858 ||
atraḥ kiṃ tatrāstīti praśnaḥ ahiśailamityuttaraśravaṇādudgata ityunnīta ucyate | ādyaḥ praśnatsvanunnīto'pi uttarotthāpanārthaṃ nibaddha iti | evaṃcāsminmate prāgdarśitānyunnītapraśnodāharaṇānyanudāharaṇānyeva | alaṃkārasyāsya dvaividhyamapi na praśnasyonnītatvanibaddhatvābhyāṃ, kiṃtūnnītatvānunnītatvābhyāṃ jñeyam | vastutastu praśnottarayorākūtagarbhatve tāvataiva camatkārānnāsakṛdupādānāpekṣā | ākūtavirahe tu asakṛdupādānakṛtaścamatkāro'pekṣyate nibaddhapraśne | ākṣiptapraśne tu praśnākṣepakṛtaṃ camatkāraṃ yadi manyante sahṛdayāḥ tadā sakṛdupādāne'pyalaṃkāratvamastu ||
tatra nibaddhapraśne praśnasya sākūtatve sakṛdupādāne'pi camatkāro yathā
sallapasi cetsumukhi te vadanātkiṃ mauktikāni vigalanti | sallāpaṃ nāgarakaṃ vallavyo vayamimāḥ kva vidmo'ṅga || 1859 ||
atra sallapasi cedityādipraśnaḥ bhagavatā nandanandanena kṛtassaṃbhogābhimukhyasaṃpādanākūtagarbhaḥ | sallāpaṃ nāgarakamityādikamuttaraṃ tu ṛjubuddhyā vallavyā dattatayā nirākūtameva | vadanātkiṃ mauktikāni vigalantīti lokoktiḥ ||
tatraivottarasya sākūtatve sakṛdupādāne camatkṛtiryathā
labhyeta kimaṅga sumaṃ dyumaṇitanūjātaṭadrukuñjeṣu | sulabhatamaṃ tatra sumaṃ bhavādṛśāṃ mṛgadṛśāmapi natāṅgi || 1860 ||
atra labhyeta kimaṅgetyādipraśnaḥ kayācidvrajalalanayā ṛjubuddhyā bhagavantaṃ yaśodānandanaṃ prati kṛto nirākūtaḥ | sulabhatamamityādi bhagavata uttaraṃ tu tatra saṃbhogasvācchandyākūtagarbham | ākūtavirahe asakṛdupādānakṛtaścamatkāraḥ kva gamiṣyasītyatra gadita eva | prakārāntareṇāpyasya bhedāssaṃbhavanti | padyāntarvartitvena padyabahirvartitvena tāvaddvaividhyam | tatrādyasyābhinnavākyodgīrṇatvabhinnavākyodgīrṇatvābhyāṃ punardvaividhyam | padyāntarvartipadyabahirvartinordvayorapyuttarayossakṛcchabdaśrutiparyāyatvena śabdāvṛttiparyāyatvenānekeṣāṃ praśnānāmekapadaniveditottaratvena prakārāntaraiśca bahuprabhedatvamityālaṃkārikaśekharo rasagaṅgādharakāraḥ | ayameva prabhedaścitrottaramiti dīkṣitaiḥ pṛthaguktaḥ ||
citrottaramalaṃkāraḥ praśnābhinnottaraṃ bhavet |
yaccottarāntarābhinnamuttaraṃ tadupīṣyate ||
praśnābhinnamuttaraṃ uttarāntarābhinnamuttaraṃ ca citrottaramityucyate ||
yathā
kaṅkamanaiṣīdbhagavān saṃkalpenaiva śāśvataiśvaryam | iti pṛcchate tadevottaramiti kasmaicidāha kavirekaḥ || 1861 ||
bhagavān saṃkalpamātreṇaiva kaṅkaṃ cetanaṃ, vismaye vīpsā | tathācoktaṃ
pratijñāyāṃ praśaṃsāyāṃ pralāpe tarjane'pi ca |
bhaye ca vismaye caiva paunaḥpunyamalaṃkṛtiḥ ||
iti | śāśvataiśvaryamanaiṣīditi praśnaḥ kaṅkaṃ gṛdhraviśeṣaṃ jaṭāyuṣamanaiṣīdityuttaraṃ praśnābhinnaṃ nibaddham | kaṅkamityatra ‘vā padāntasya' iti vaikalpikaḥ parasavarṇaḥ ||
yathāvā
mṛdvī kā paribhūtā mṛdvyā svādvyā mukundavāceti | pṛcchāmevottaratāmanayata kasyāpi ko'pi kavirājaḥ || 1862 ||
atra mṛdvī mṛduḥ kā paribhūteti praśnasya mṛdvīkā drākṣā paribhūteti tadabhinnamuttaram ||
yathāvā
kamalaṃ karoti haste kamalā kamalālayā pariṣkāram | ityanuyuktā kācana sakhyā citrottaraṃ tadevāha || 1863 ||
kamalālayā padmagehā kamalā śrīḥ haste alaṃ kaṃ pariṣkāraṃ alaṃkāraṃ karotīti praśnaḥ | kamalaṃ padmaṃ karotīti tadabhinnamuttaraṃ padmaṃ pariṣkāraṃ karotītyarthaḥ | citrottaramityalaṃkārasya sūcyasya sūcanānmudrālaṃkārastvatra viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
puruṣaḥ phaṇadharaśikhariṇi kastūrīkṛtalalāmako bhāti | brūhi sakhe kiṃ brūyāmuttaramatrānuyoga eva syāt || 1864 ||
kaḥ tu ūrīkṛtalalāmakaḥ iti praśnapakṣe chedaḥ | tuśabdaḥ praśaṃsāyāṃ bhede vā | ‘tu pādapūraṇe bhede samuccaye'vadhāraṇe | pakṣāntare viyoge ca praśaṃsāyāṃ vinigrahe' iti medinī | phaṇadharaśikhariṇi eteṣu puruṣeṣu kaḥ puruṣaḥ punaḥ ūrīkṛtalalāmakaḥ praśastatayā bhātīti praśnaḥ | kastūryā kṛtalalāmakaḥ viracitaviśeṣakaḥ puruṣo bhagavān śrīnivāsa eva bhātītyuttaraṃ tadabhinnaṃ nibaddham | 'lalāmaṃ pucchapuṇḍrāśvabhūṣāḥ prādhānyaketuṣu' ityamaraḥ ||
yathāvā
hariṇā dvirasanakulapatigiridhāmnā'nāyi kā nijaṃ vakṣaḥ| iti yaḥ praśnastasya svayamuttaratāṃ sa eva bata dhatte || 1865 ||
dvirasanakulapatigiridhāmnā hariṇā nijaṃ vakṣaḥ kā anāyīti praśnaḥ | nāyikā'nāyītyuttaramabhinnavākyodgīrṇam | nāyikā dayitā śrīḥ vakṣaḥ anāyi prāpyatetyarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
satyaṃ brūyātpuruṣastribhuvanajananāya kaḥ pragalbheta | kiṃnu brūyāṃ puruṣastribhuvanajananāyakaḥ pragalbheta || 1866 ||
tribhuvanajananāya trilokīsarjanāya kaḥ puruṣaḥ pumān pragalbheteti praśnaḥ | kiṃ nu brūyāmiti ajānata ivottaram | tribhuvanajanānāṃ nāyakaḥ adhīśaḥ puruṣaḥ ‘atha puruṣo ha vai nārāyaṇo'kāmayata prajāssṛjeyeya' iti śrutaḥ bhagavannārāyaṇaḥ pragalbhetetyuttarāntaraṃ ca ||
yathāvā
kānte prītiṃ vidadhātvātmanyayamiti kṛtāṃ svadayitena | pṛcchāmevottarayadviḍambayantīva sasmitaṃ lakṣmīḥ || 1867 ||
ayaṃ tvadvaśavartīti sābhinayaṃ svasya nirdeśaḥ | te ātmani manasi kāṃ prītiṃ vidadhātviti praśnārthaḥ | kānte vallabhe ātmani tvayi viṣaye ayaṃ tvamiti bhāvaḥ | prītiṃ mama vidadhātu ityuttarārthaḥ | tvadviṣayakaprītyā vinā nānyā mama manasi prītirapekṣaṇīyeti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi praśnottarayorabhinnavākyanibaddhatvam ||
yathāvā mamaiva raṅgarājavilāse
modaṃ vaha sakhi me vada ko daṇḍadharo daśānanakulasya | iti kāciccaturā nijapatinā pṛṣṭottaraṃ tadevāha || 1868 ||
daśānanakulasya daṇḍadharaḥ ka iti praśnaḥ kodaṇḍadhara ityuttaraṃ cābhinnavākyagatam ||
yathāvā
saumitriṇā daśāsyasvasurmukhe brūhi kā'sinā
lūnā | jānāsi paṭha vilomaṃ pṛcchāyāṃ kāsineti varṇāṃstvam || 1869 ||
atra kāsineti praśno vilomapaṭhita uttaratāṃ prapadyata iti vaicitryaṃ viśeṣaḥ | īdṛśānyudāharaṇāni mamaiva śrīnivāsavilāsanṛsiṃhavilāsādiṣu prabandheṣu bhūyāṃsi draṣṭavyāni ||
evaṃ praśnābhinnottararūpaścitrottarālaṃkāro nirūpitaḥ | uttarāntarābhinnamuttaraṃ yathā
svārājye kīdṛkṣaḥ punarasthopi śriyā sahasrākṣaḥ | atha paryabhavandaityāṃstadvimukhānkā nigadyatāṃ vipadaḥ || 1870 ||
atra praśnadvayasyāpi vipada ityekamevottaram | vigataṃ padaṃ svārājyasthānaṃ yasyetyekavacanāntavipadaśabdena prathamapraśne indraviśeṣaṇatayā yojitenottaram | dvitīye tu vipadaḥ āpadaḥ iti bahuvacanāntena vipacchabdenottaramiti dhyeyam ||
anilātmaṃbharivaragirinilayasya cakāsti kīdṛśo vibhavaḥ | tattanurucikālimnā nuttamadāḥ ke nigadyatāmalayaḥ || 1871 ||
atra śrīnivāsasya vibhavaḥ kīdṛśa iti tadvigrahaśyāmalimnā nirastamadāḥ ke iti ca praśnadvayasyāpi alaya iti uttarābhinnamuttaram | ādye alayaḥ layarahitaḥ nitya ityarthaḥ | dvitīye alayaḥ bhramarā ityarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
siṃhagirināthavāhanaraṃhasi kīdṛgvibhāti pavamānaḥ | taddordaṇḍavidhūtā vepante ke'bhyudīryatāmarayaḥ || 1872 ||
atrāpi praśnadvayasyottaraṃ araya ityabhinnameva | arayaḥ vegahīna iti prathamasyottare'rthaḥ | arayaḥ śatrava iti dvitīyasyeti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
śrīvāsasyāśrayaṇādbhaveyurarthārthino'tra kīdṛkṣāḥ | ārtānāṃ kiṃ bhavitā jñānī kīdṛktato bhavedvibhavaḥ || 1873 ||
atra praśnatrayasyāpi vibhava ityekamevottaramiti pūrvebhyo viśeṣaḥ | arthārthinaḥ apūrvaiśvaryakāmāḥ vibhavaḥ prabhavaḥ bhaveyuriti prathamasya praśnasyottaram | ārtānāṃ bhraṣṭaiśvaryakāmānāṃ vibhavaḥ aiśvaryaṃ bhaviteti dvitīyasya | jñānī bhagavantameva paramaprāpyaṃ prāpakaṃ ca matvā upāsīnaḥ vibhavaḥ vigatasaṃsṛtirbhavatīti tṛtīyasyeti bodhyam ||
yathāvā
hariyaśasā kā viśadāḥ kīdṛśyastattanutviṣastābhiḥ | vṛṣaśailaḥ kīdṛksyātkutaśca niśśreyasaṃ bhaveddharitaḥ || 1874 ||
atra praśnacatuṣṭayasyāpi harita ityekamevottaramiti pūrvasmādvailakṣaṇyam | hariyaśasā kā viśadā iti prathamasya praśnasya
haritaḥ diśa ityuttaram | kīdṛśyaḥ tattanutviṣaḥ harivigraharuca iti dvitīyasya haritaḥ haridvarṇā ityuttaram | nīlaharidvarṇayorabhedaḥ kavisamayasiddha ityavocāma vakṣyāmaśca | tābhiḥ haritanurucibhiḥ vṛṣaśailaḥ kīdṛk syāditi tṛtīyasya haritaḥ haritavarṇa ityuttaram | 'pālāśo harito harit' ityamaraḥ | kutaḥ kasmācca niśśreyasaṃ bhavediti turīyasya haritaḥ hareḥ iti sārvavibhaktikatasyantapadenottaram ||
yathāvā
kiṃ vitarati phaṇigiripatiraṅgadyutivaibhavena kiṃ jayati | kaṃ dhvanayati ripuvijaye kaṃdharayā kaṃ dhunoti vada śaṃkham || 1875 ||
kiṃ vitarati phaṇigiripatiriti praśnasya śaṃ sukhaṃ ityuttaram | aṅgadyutivaibhavena kiṃ jayatītyasya khaṃ gaganaṃ iti, kaṃ dhvanayati ripuvijaye ityasya kaṃdharayā kaṃ dhunotītyasya ca śaṅkhamiti cottaracatuṣṭayaṃ praśnacatuṣṭayādabhinnam | atrādyayoḥ padabhedena antyayostu tadabhedeneti pūrvato bhedaḥ ||
padyabahirvartino yathā
phaṇiśikhariṇi ko nivasati ko dhūpo ghrāṇatarpaṇastasya | kimahāri rahasi hariṇā vidvannuttaramihaikameva vada || 1876 ||
atra praśnatrayasyāpyekamevottaraṃ śrīvāsa iti padyabahirbhūtam | ādye praśne śrīvāsaḥ śrīnivāso bhagavānityarthaḥ | dvitīye śrīvāsaḥ devadārvādiniryāsaviniṣpādito dhūpaviśeṣa ityarthaḥ | 'śrīvāso
vṛkṣadhūpo'pi śrīveṣṭasaraladravau' ityamaraḥ | tṛtīye śrīvāsaḥ lakṣmyā aṃśukamityarthaḥ | evaṃjātīyakā anye bahavaḥ prabhedā buddhimadbhirūhanīyāḥ | ayaṃ padyabahirvartyuttararūpaprathamaprabheda uttarābhinnottararūpaprabheda eva saṃbhavati | na tu praśnābhinnottararūpaprathamaprabhede, praśnenaivottarasyāpi nibaddhatayā padyādbahiṣṭhatāyā asaṃbhavāt ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre uttarasaraṣṣaḍaśītitamaḥ
<37>
atha citrapraśnālaṃkārasaraḥ
praśnaḥ praśnāntarābhinno yadivā'nyārthagarbhitaḥ |
nibadhyate taṃ katiciccitrapraśnākhyamūcire ||
praśnasya praśnāntarābhinnatve vā arthāntaragarbhatve vā citrapraśnākhyamalaṃkāraṃ katicidāhuḥ | ayaṃ kaustubhakṛdupakramam ||
yathāvā
kā kāruṇyanidhirnanu kākodaraśailapatihṛdayavṛttiḥ | śāradaravikaravikasitanīrajarucirā padoryugī cāsya || 1877 ||
atra kākodaraśailapateḥ hṛdayavṛttiḥ cittavṛttirityuttarasya śāradetyādyuttarasya ca kākāruṇyanidhiriti praśnāntarābhinna ekaeva praśnaḥ | kā kāruṇyanidhiḥ dayānidhiriti prāthamikyapraśnasyārthaḥ | kā kā āruṇyanidhiḥ śoṇimāśraya iti dvitīyasya | kāketi praśaṃsāyāṃ vīpsetyavocāma kaṅkamiti padye ||
vṛkṣadhūpo'pi śrīveṣṭasaraladravau' ityamaraḥ | tṛtīye śrīvāsaḥ lakṣmyā aṃśukamityarthaḥ | evaṃjātīyakā anye bahavaḥ prabhedā buddhimadbhirūhanīyāḥ | ayaṃ padyabahirvartyuttararūpaprathamaprabheda uttarābhinnottararūpaprabheda eva saṃbhavati | na tu praśnābhinnottararūpaprathamaprabhede, praśnenaivottarasyāpi nibaddhatayā padyādbahiṣṭhatāyā asaṃbhavāt ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre uttarasaraṣṣaḍaśītitamaḥ
atha citrapraśnālaṃkārasaraḥ
praśnaḥ praśnāntarābhinno yadivā'nyārthagarbhitaḥ |
nibadhyate taṃ katiciccitrapraśnākhyamūcire ||
praśnasya praśnāntarābhinnatve vā arthāntaragarbhatve vā citrapraśnākhyamalaṃkāraṃ katicidāhuḥ | ayaṃ kaustubhakṛdupakramam ||
yathāvā
kā kāruṇyanidhirnanu kākodaraśailapatihṛdayavṛttiḥ | śāradaravikaravikasitanīrajarucirā padoryugī cāsya || 1877 ||
atra kākodaraśailapateḥ hṛdayavṛttiḥ cittavṛttirityuttarasya
śāradetyādyuttarasya ca kākāruṇyanidhiriti praśnāntarābhinna ekaeva praśnaḥ | kā kāruṇyanidhiḥ dayānidhiriti prāthamikyapraśnasyārthaḥ | kā kā āruṇyanidhiḥ śoṇimāśraya iti dvitīyasya | kāketi praśaṃsāyāṃ vīpsetyavocāma kaṅkamiti padye ||
yathāvā
tāpatrayāturāṇāṃ bhavennarāṇāṃ bhuvīha kārākā | asatāṃ saṃgatirekā sāravidāṃ saṃgatissatāmanyā || 1878 ||
atra uttarārdhapratipāditayoruttarayoḥ kārāketi praśnāntarābhinna eka eva praśnaḥ | kārā bandhanālayaḥ tadvadatiśayitaduḥkhahetuḥ keti prathamapraśnasyārthaḥ | kā rākā pūrṇaniśākarā pūrṇimā tadvattāpahartrī keti dvitīyapraśnasyārthaḥ ||
yathāvā
kālīkā vṛṣagiripatitanuratha tadvimukhavadanavīkṣā ca | kāśaṅkā nyakcakre tatkīrtistatparatvaviṣayā ca || 1879 ||
atra pūrvārdhe vṛṣagiripatitanuḥ tadvimukhavadanavīkṣā cetyuttaradvayasya kāliketyeka eva praśnaḥ | kālī śyāmalā kā ityekasya praśnasyārthaḥ | kā alīkā apriyetyanyasya | uttarārdhe tu tatkīrtiḥ tatparatvaviṣayā cetyuttaradvayasya kāśaṅkā nyakcakre ityeka eva praśnaḥ | kāśaṃ kāśakusumaṃ kā nyakcakre iti prathamapraśnasya | dvitīyasya kā śaṅkā saṃśayaḥ nyakcakre tiraskṛtā ityarthaḥ | bhagavatkīrtiḥ kāśaṃ nyakkaroti sma | bhagavatparatvaviṣayā śaṅkā avadhīritā'smābhirityuttarayorabhiprāyaḥ | cakre iti prathame praśne
kartari liṭ | dvitīye tu karmaṇi iti dhyeyam | pūrvatra praśnāntarābhinnaḥ praśna ekaḥ | atra tu dvau praśnāvitiviśeṣaḥ ||
anyārthagarbhitapraśnarūpo dvitīyaścitrapraśno yathā
bhāmāmaṇi vyapanayan kāmākulatāṃ mudaṃ tava vidadhyām | kāmapi gopīmevaṃ pṛcchan sākūtamavatu nandasutaḥ || 1880 ||
atra ākulatāṃ vyapanayan tava kāṃ mudaṃ vidadhyāmiti praśnaḥ tava kāmākulatāṃ kandarpavyākulatvaṃ vyapanayan mudaṃ vidadhyāmityarthāntaragarbhaḥ ||
yathāvā
sutarāṅgavibho'gaṃ tvaṃ svāmin kamitaḥ paraṃ vrajasyuccaiḥ | māmehīti sabhāvaṃ mādhavamabrūta kā'pi gopavadhūḥ || 1881 ||
atra prāthamikārthe  sutarāṅga sutara aṅga iti vā vibho agaṃ tvaṃ svāmin kaṃ itaḥ paraṃ vrajasi uccaiḥ mā mā ihi iti chedaḥ | sutarāṅga he śobhanagātra sundaretyarthaḥ | athavā sutara kalyāṇatara | aṅgetyavyayaṃ saṃbodhane | vibho vyāpanaśīla svāmin ! itaḥparaṃ asmāt śailāt sugamāditi bhāvaḥ | anyaṃ uccaiḥ unnataṃ durārohamiti bhāvaḥ | kaṃ agaṃ śailaṃ vrajasi gacchasi | mā mā ihīti yojanā |
mā mā sma gamaḥ mā metivīpsā sarvātmanā niṣedhārthā | arthāntare tu sutarāṃ gavi bhogaṃ tvaṃ svāmin kamitaḥ paraṃ vrajasi uccaiḥ māṃ ehi iti chedaḥ | he gavi svāmin gavāmadhīśa | gopalotyarthaḥ | ‘svāmīśvarādhipati' ityādinā svāmiśabdayogāt gośabdasya saptamī | he kamitaḥ he kamana! kamestṛci saṃbuddhiḥ | ārdhadhātukatvena āyādeśasya vaikalpikatvādiha tadabhāvaḥ | sutarāṃ atyantaṃ paraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ uccaiḥ
niravadhikaṃ sukhaṃ tvaṃ vrajasi | tatra hetumāha māṃ evamabhilaṣamāṇāṃ māṃ ehi prāpnuhi | atrāpyupapāditarītyā praśnasyārthāntaragarbhatā draṣṭavyā | kvacitpraśnottarayorubhayorapyarthāntaragarbhatve cārutātiśaya iti taireva kaustubhakāraiḥ pratyapādi | tasyedamudāharaṇam
kāntāramayetkā māṃ tatsaraṇiṃ vada yayā sukhaṃ yāyām | iti pṛcchati nandasute sugameyamiheti kā'pi gopyāha || 1882 ||
kāntāraṃ mahāvanaṃ prati kā saraṇiḥ ayet gacchet māṃ tatsaraṇiṃ tāṃ padavīṃ vada 'ciñbrūñśāsuji' iti dvikarmakatā | yayā saraṇyā sukhaṃ nirbādhaṃ yathā syāttathā yāyāṃ gaccheyaṃ iti mārgaviṣayakaḥ praśnaḥ | kā kāntā māṃ ramayet tatsaraṇiṃ tasyāḥ prāptyupāyaṃ vada | yayā kāntayā upāyena vā sukhaṃ ratisukhaṃ yāyāmityarthāntaragarbhaḥ | iyaṃ saraṇiḥ sugamā sukhena gamyeti mārgapraśnasyottaram | arthāntarasya tu iyaṃ yāṃ tvaṃ mārgaṃ pṛcchasi seyaṃ ahamityarthaḥ | iha atraiva sugamā sukhena gamyā satī ramayati niśśalākatvādasya pradeśasyeti ||
yathāvā
kānte'navaratasaṃpadamabhīpsitāṃ komalaṅgi tanavāni | ghanabhāvirataṃ bhāgyaṃ vitarasi me'bhīpsitaṃ yadi kṛtārthā || 1883 ||
ayaṃ śrīkṛṣṇagopikayoḥ praśnottararūpaḥ ślokaḥ | atra he
komalāṅgi! abhīpsitāṃ kāṃ anavaratasaṃpadaṃ avicchinnāṃ saṃpa
ttiṃ te tava tanavānīti praśnaḥ, he kānte! abhīpsitāṃ navaratasaṃpadaṃ te tanavānītyarthāntaragarbhitaḥ | he ghanabha! meghābha avirataṃ satataṃ me abhīpsitaṃ bhāgyaṃ yadi vitarasi tarhi kṛtārthetyuttaram | he ghana! me abhīpsitaṃ cirakālaprārthitaṃ bhāvi bhaviṣyat rataṃ suratarūpaṃ bhāgyaṃ yadi vitarasi tarhi kṛtārthā nirvṛttābhīpsitaprayojanetyabhiprāyagarbham ||
atredamavadheyam prāktanamatānurodhenāsmadupadarśitottarālaṃkāraprakārabhedeṣu satsvapi bahuṣu citrottaragūḍhottararūpau tadalaṃkāraprakāraprabhedau dvāveva kuvalayānandakārairupāttau | tanmatānurodhenālaṃkārānnibadhnadbhiḥ kaustubhakārairveṅkaṭāryaistatpratidvandvitayā citrapraśnaprakāradvayamabhinavaṃ parikalpitam | tadidamasmābhirapi pradarśitam | yaditvatra anyārthagarbhapraśnarūpo dvitīyaḥ prakāro nibaddhasābhiprāyanirabhiprāyānyatarottarassyāt tarhi tat prāgupadarśite prācāṃ mate uttarālaṃkārodāharaṇameva bhavet | evaṃca 'bhāmāmaṇi vyapanayana, sutarāṅgavibho'gaṃ tvam' ityupadarśitayoḥ padyayoruttarānupanibandhātpraśnamātrasya sābhiprāyatvācca citrapraśnodāharaṇatvameva | asya prāthamikaprabhede pradarśiteṣu 'kā kāruṇyanidhiḥ' ityādipadyeṣūttaropanibandhamantareṇa ekasya praśnasya praśnāntarābhinnapraśnatāyā durvijñānatayā tadupanibandhāvaśyaṃbhāve'pi praśnamātrasyaiva nirākūtatve'pi śleṣavaśena vaicitryaprakāśanātprācīnopadarśitottarālaṃkāravyāvṛttaścitrapraśnālaṃkāra eva | ‘kāntāramayetkāmām' ityādipadyadvaye tu sābhiprāyayoḥ praśnottarayorubhayorapinibaddhatvāduttarālaṃkāraprabheda eva | prathamataraṃ prācīnarītyā pradarśiteṣūdāharaṇeṣu sābhiprāyatvaṃ vyaṅgyamaryādayā, anayostu śleṣamahimneti vailakṣaṇyam | atha matam uttarālaṃkāre praśnottarayorā
kūtaṃ vyañjanāmūlameveti, tarhi tayoḥ śleṣamūlakārthāntaragarbhatve citrapraśnaviśeṣa eva nottarālakāra ityeṣṭavyaṃ, vicchittiviśeṣasadbhāvāt | ihāpyuttaranibandhastu uttarālaṃkāre praśnanibandhavaccārutātiśayaheturiti dik ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre citrapraśnasarassaptāśītitamaḥ
<38>
atha sūkṣmālaṅkārasaraḥ
anyāśayajñasākūtaceṣṭitaṃ sūkṣmamīryate ||
anyābhiprāyamiṅgitādinā jñātavato'nyasya sākūtastadviṣayako vyāpārassūkṣmaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
purato nandakiśore karakalitamanojñakanduke miṣati | sundaramauktikaradanā mandaṃ hasati sma gopaśaśivadanā || 1884 ||
atra gṛhītakandukanandanandanasandarśanalakṣaṇeneṅgitena tadīyapayodharagrahābhisaṃketasamayānuyogābhiprāyaṃ viditavatyā gopasudatyā tadānukūlyena prasarantyāṃ candrikāyāṃ saṅketasamaya iti taduttara svahṛdayagataṃ vyañjayituṃ mandahāsavyāpāro vyaracīti lakṣaṇānugatiḥ ||
kūtaṃ vyañjanāmūlameveti, tarhi tayoḥ śleṣamūlakārthāntaragarbhatve citrapraśnaviśeṣa eva nottarālakāra ityeṣṭavyaṃ, vicchittiviśeṣasadbhāvāt | ihāpyuttaranibandhastu uttarālaṃkāre praśnanibandhavaccārutātiśayaheturiti dik ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre citrapraśnasarassaptāśītitamaḥ
atha sūkṣmālaṅkārasaraḥ
anyāśayajñasākūtaceṣṭitaṃ sūkṣmamīryate ||
anyābhiprāyamiṅgitādinā jñātavato'nyasya sākūtastadviṣayako vyāpārassūkṣmaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
purato nandakiśore karakalitamanojñakanduke miṣati | sundaramauktikaradanā mandaṃ hasati sma gopaśaśivadanā || 1884 ||
atra gṛhītakandukanandanandanasandarśanalakṣaṇeneṅgitena tadīyapayodharagrahābhisaṃketasamayānuyogābhiprāyaṃ viditavatyā gopasudatyā tadānukūlyena prasarantyāṃ candrikāyāṃ saṅketasamaya iti taduttara svahṛdayagataṃ vyañjayituṃ mandahāsavyāpāro vyaracīti lakṣaṇānugatiḥ ||
yathāvā
svakaradhṛtaṃ likhiti nakhairlikucaphalaṃ nandanandane purataḥ | vallavalalanā mauktikavallīmāmallate sma dhammille || 1885 ||
idamapi bhagavataśśrīkṛṣṇasya svairavihārasaṅketasamayānuyogabhāvaṃ jānānāyāḥ vrajalalanāyāśceṣṭitaṃ tamasi tārakollāse saṅketasamaya ityākūtagarbham ||
yathāvā
vyālācalaśailendau nīlākucakalaśayugalaloladṛśi | sā ramaṇī smeramukhī śrīramaṇoraḥprasārinayanā''sīt || 1886 ||
atra svakucayugalanirīkṣaṇabhagavacceṣṭitena svopabhogautsukyarūpaṃ tadākūtaṃ jānānāyā nīlāyāḥ śrīnivāsavakṣassthalavīkṣaṇarūpaṃ ceṣṭitaṃ tatra śrīsāṃnidhyarūpapratibandhakasadbhāvapradarśanākūtagarbham ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre sūkṣmasaro'ṣṭāśītitamaḥ
<39>
atha pihitālaṃkārasaraḥ
paravṛttajñasākūtaceṣṭitaṃ pihitaṃ matam ||
parakīyavyāpāravettussākūtaceṣṭitaṃ pihitaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathāvā
svakaradhṛtaṃ likhiti nakhairlikucaphalaṃ nandanandane purataḥ | vallavalalanā mauktikavallīmāmallate sma dhammille || 1885 ||
idamapi bhagavataśśrīkṛṣṇasya svairavihārasaṅketasamayānuyogabhāvaṃ jānānāyāḥ vrajalalanāyāśceṣṭitaṃ tamasi tārakollāse saṅketasamaya ityākūtagarbham ||
yathāvā
vyālācalaśailendau nīlākucakalaśayugalaloladṛśi | sā ramaṇī smeramukhī śrīramaṇoraḥprasārinayanā''sīt || 1886 ||
atra svakucayugalanirīkṣaṇabhagavacceṣṭitena svopabhogautsukyarūpaṃ tadākūtaṃ jānānāyā nīlāyāḥ śrīnivāsavakṣassthalavīkṣaṇarūpaṃ ceṣṭitaṃ tatra śrīsāṃnidhyarūpapratibandhakasadbhāvapradarśanākūtagarbham ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre sūkṣmasaro'ṣṭāśītitamaḥ
atha pihitālaṃkārasaraḥ
paravṛttajñasākūtaceṣṭitaṃ pihitaṃ matam ||
parakīyavyāpāravettussākūtaceṣṭitaṃ pihitaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
svinnānanamāyātaṃ prātarnijasadma vīkṣya nandasutam | laghulayalalitaṃ vyajanaṃ karakisalayato'grahīddrutaṃ rādhā || 1887 ||
laghulayaṃ lāmajjakam | sarvarātraṃ nāyikāntarasaṃbhogena śrānto'sīti vījayāmīti lāmajjakatālavṛntaprahaṇākūtam ||
yathāvā
parataruṇīhāridracchuritakapolaṃ nirīkṣya yadubālam | pihitākārā bhaiṣmī pāṇau maṇidarpaṇaṃ dadau tasya || 1888 ||
atra harikapole haridrācūrṇalepanenānumitaṃ rajanyāṃ tava sapatnīsadane svairavihāraṃ jānāmyahamityākūtagarbhaṃ tatkarakamale rukmiṇyā darpaṇadānarūpaṃ ceṣṭitaṃ nibaddham | pihitākāretyanena sūcanīyālaṃkāranāmasūcanarūpamudrālaṃkāropaskṛtatvaṃ pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
idaṃ kuvalayānandānurodhena | sarvarsvakārādayastu 'saṃlakṣitasūkṣmārthaprakāśanaṃ sūkṣmam' iti sthūlamatibhirasaṃlakṣaṇīyaḥ kuśāgramatibhissaṃlakṣyate yassūkṣmārthaḥ tasya vidagdhaṃ prakāśanamiti sūkṣmārthatvena parāśayavṛttāntayoḥ kroḍīkāreṇa lakṣayitvā
saṃketakālamanasaṃ viṭaṃ jñātvā vidagdhayā |
āsīnnetrārpitākūtaṃ līlāpadmaṃ nimīlitam ||
vaktrasyandisvedabinduprabandairdṛṣṭvā bhinnaṃ kuṅkumaṃ kā'pi kaṇṭhe |
puṃstvaṃ tanvyā vyañjayantīva tasyāssmitvā pāṇau khaḍgalekhāṃ lilekha ||
ityudāharaṇayorlakṣaṇasattāmupapādayantaḥ pihitanāmānamalaṃkārāntaraṃ nānumenire | yuktaṃ caitad alaṃkārāntarakalpanāvaicitryahetossākūtaceṣṭitavinyasanasya bhedābhāve āśayavṛttāntarūpaprakāśyabhedamātreṇa bhedānupapatteriti vadanti | atra 'yatsūkṣmasyārthasya saṃlakṣaṇamātraṃ prakāśanamātraṃ vā'pyayamevālaṃkāraḥ' ityalaṃkārabhāṣyakārāśayaṃ vyavṛṇuta vimarśinīkāraḥ | tadedamudāharaṇam
līlāsadanakavāṭe lole'pāṅge payodhibālāyāḥ | vallabhamātrasahāyā yathā ramā syāttathā'bhavan sakhyaḥ || 1889 ||
atra sakhībhiḥ śriyo vallabhopabhogautsukyaṃ saṃlakṣitaṃ kelīgṛhakavāṭe kaṭākṣavisāraṇena prakāśitaṃ cetyubhayārthasahitatvam | tadevamādau sukṣmālaṃkāra eva vācyaḥ, sūkṣmasyaivārthasya saṃlakṣyamāṇatvādinā'vasthānāt | yadyapi saṃlakṣitamātramapi lakṣmyā autsukyaṃ sakhībhirviviktatayā bahirnirgamanena ceṣṭitena prakāśitam | tathā'pi sakhīnāṃ tadautsukyasaṃlakṣaṇamātramevātra camatkāri | na tu sakhībahirnirgamanaceṣṭitamityasyālaṃkāratollāsaḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre pihitasara ekonanavatitamaḥ
<40>
atha vyājoktyalaṅkārasaraḥ
hetva taroktyā vyājoktiryadākārasya gūhanam ||
nigūḍhasya vastunaḥ kenacitprakaṭatāṃ gatasya hetvantaroktyā yadgopanaṃ tat vyājoktirnāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
ityudāharaṇayorlakṣaṇasattāmupapādayantaḥ pihitanāmānamalaṃkārāntaraṃ nānumenire | yuktaṃ caitad alaṃkārāntarakalpanāvaicitryahetossākūtaceṣṭitavinyasanasya bhedābhāve āśayavṛttāntarūpaprakāśyabhedamātreṇa bhedānupapatteriti vadanti | atra 'yatsūkṣmasyārthasya saṃlakṣaṇamātraṃ prakāśanamātraṃ vā'pyayamevālaṃkāraḥ' ityalaṃkārabhāṣyakārāśayaṃ vyavṛṇuta vimarśinīkāraḥ | tadedamudāharaṇam
līlāsadanakavāṭe lole'pāṅge payodhibālāyāḥ | vallabhamātrasahāyā yathā ramā syāttathā'bhavan sakhyaḥ || 1889 ||
atra sakhībhiḥ śriyo vallabhopabhogautsukyaṃ saṃlakṣitaṃ kelīgṛhakavāṭe kaṭākṣavisāraṇena prakāśitaṃ cetyubhayārthasahitatvam | tadevamādau sukṣmālaṃkāra eva vācyaḥ, sūkṣmasyaivārthasya saṃlakṣyamāṇatvādinā'vasthānāt | yadyapi saṃlakṣitamātramapi lakṣmyā autsukyaṃ sakhībhirviviktatayā bahirnirgamanena ceṣṭitena prakāśitam | tathā'pi sakhīnāṃ tadautsukyasaṃlakṣaṇamātramevātra camatkāri | na tu sakhībahirnirgamanaceṣṭitamityasyālaṃkāratollāsaḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre pihitasara ekonanavatitamaḥ
atha vyājoktyalaṅkārasaraḥ
hetva taroktyā vyājoktiryadākārasya gūhanam ||
nigūḍhasya vastunaḥ kenacitprakaṭatāṃ gatasya hetvantaroktyā yadgopanaṃ tat vyājoktirnāmālaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
prativātavilulitaṃ mama dhammillamimaṃ samīkuruṣva sakhi | iti yamunātaṭakuñjādetya sakhīṃ kā'pi gopikā'lāpīt || 1890 ||
atra nandanandanakṛtasvairopabhogavilulitasya dhammillasya prativātavilulitatvavyājoktyā gopanam | "chekāpahnuterasyāścāyaṃ viśeṣas tasyāṃ vacanasyānyadhānayanenāpahnavaḥ, asyāmākārasya hetvantaravarṇanena gopanamiti | lakṣaṇe lakṣyanāmni coktigrahaṇamākārasya gopanārthaṃ hetvantarapratyāyakavyāpāramātropalakṣaṇam | tataśca
āyāntamālokya hariṃ pratolyāmālyāḥ purastādanurāgamekā |
romāñcakampādibhirucyamānaṃ bhāmā jugūhe praṇamantyathainam ||
ityatrāpi vyājoktireva | atra hyanurāgakṛtasya romāñcādyākārasya bhaktirūpahetvantarapratyāyakena praṇāmena gopanaṃ kṛtam" iti kuvalayānande dīkṣitāḥ | tataśca
yamunātaṭakuñjādvrajamupayāntī ketakīstabakahastā | nvavapuṣi śaurinakhakṣatamagūhata svairakelijaṃ gopī || 1891 ||
ityatrāpi tanmate vyājoktireva | atra hi svairakeliprāptasya bhagavannakhavilekhanarūpākārasya kaṇṭakapracuraketakīnikuñjapraveśarūpahetvantarapratyāyakena tatkusumastabakagrahaṇena gūhanaṃ kṛtam | vastutastu atra yuktyalaṃkāra eveti yuktyalaṃkāranirūpaṇāvasare vakṣyate | sūkṣmapihitālaṃkarayorapi ceṣṭitagrahaṇamuktisādhāraṇavyāpāramātropalakṣaṇam | tataśca
"nalinīdale balākā marakatapātra iva dṛśyate śuktiḥ | iti mama saṃketabhuvi jñātvā bhāvaṃ tadā'bravīdālīm" ||
iti prācīnapadya iva
paśyātra niścalatamā marālamālā'malābjamāleva | ityālīṃ kā'pyavadacchauressaṃketabhāvamadhigamya || 1892 ||
ityādiṣvasmadīyapadyeṣvapi sūkṣmālaṃkāraḥ prasarati | atra padye tāvat āvayoḥ kiṃ saṃketasthānaṃ bhaviṣyatīti praśnāśayaṃ sūcayati bhagavati nandanandane tadabhijñayā vidagdhayā vrajasundaryā sakhīṃ prati sākūtamuktamiti bhavati sūkṣmālaṃkāraḥ | yato'tra haṃsamālāyā niścalatamapuṇḍarīkamālikopamayā tasyāstatra nispandatayā sāśvāsatvaṃ tena tasya pradeśasyātimātraviviktatvaṃ tena tadevāvayossaṃketasthānamiti taṃ prati sūcanaṃ lakṣyate | na cātra dhvanirāśaṅkanīyaḥ vyaṅgyaparaṃparayā vyajyamānasyāpi saṃketasthānapraśnottarasya svoktyaivāviṣkṛtatvāt | evaṃ hi pihitālaṃkāre'pyudāhāryam ||
idaṃ cānyadatrāvadheyam "tata ita iha saṃbhramataḥ | kiṃ cintayase bhadre | kā tvaṃ kā vā kiṃ tava | purato nandakiśore | svakaradhṛtaṃ likhiti nakhaiḥ" ityādiprāgupadarśitagūḍhottarasūkṣmādyalaṃkārodāharaṇeṣu bhāvo na svoktyā''viṣkṛtaḥ | kiṃtu vastusaundaryabalādvaktṛboddhavyaviśeṣaviśeṣitādgamyaḥ | tatraiva vastuto nālaṃkāratvaṃ, dhvanibhāvāspadatvāt | prāktanaissvoktyā''viṣkaraṇe satyalaṃkārāspadatā'stītyudāhṛtāni "yatrāsau vetasī pāntha" ityādīnīti tadanusāribhirasmābhirapi tathāvidhānyudāhṛtāni
padyāni | śakyaṃ hyeṣāmudāharaṇānāmuttaraślokakalpanayā bhāvāviṣkaraṇam ||
yathā
tata ita iha saṃbhramataḥ kuta iva vicinoṣi nandasuta gāṃ tvam | kālindītaṭakuñje kāmaṃ dīvyati hi tatra sulabhaiṣā || 1893 ||
iti kācidvrajalalanā gāṃ nijayūdhacyutāṃ vicinvānam | pṛcchantaṃ nandasutaṃ kāmayamānā jagāda sākūtam || 1894 ||
iti | tasmādasmābhiḥ prāgudāhṛteṣu 'parataruṇīhāridracchuritakapolam' ityādiṣu yeṣu bhāvāviṣkaraṇamasti teṣveva tattadalaṃkāra iti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre vyājoktisaro navatitamaḥ
<41>
atha gūḍhoktisaraḥ
gūḍhoktiritaroddeśyamanyaṃ pratyucyate yadi ||
yaṃ prati kiṃcidgaditavyaṃ tanmājñāsiṣustaṭasthā iti taditaraṃ yaṃkaṃcitprati yat śleṣaṇocyate tat gūḍhoktirnāmālaṃkāro dīkṣitopakramam ||
yathā
tata ita iha saṃbhramataḥ kuta iva vicinoṣi nandasuta gāṃ tvam | kālindītaṭakuñje kāmaṃ dīvyati hi tatra sulabhaiṣā || 1893 ||
iti kācidvrajalalanā gāṃ nijayūdhacyutāṃ vicinvānam | pṛcchantaṃ nandasutaṃ kāmayamānā jagāda sākūtam || 1894 ||
iti | tasmādasmābhiḥ prāgudāhṛteṣu 'parataruṇīhāridracchuritakapolam' ityādiṣu yeṣu bhāvāviṣkaraṇamasti teṣveva tattadalaṃkāra iti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre vyājoktisaro navatitamaḥ
atha gūḍhoktisaraḥ
gūḍhoktiritaroddeśyamanyaṃ pratyucyate yadi ||
yaṃ prati kiṃcidgaditavyaṃ tanmājñāsiṣustaṭasthā iti taditaraṃ yaṃkaṃcitprati yat śleṣaṇocyate tat gūḍhoktirnāmālaṃkāro dīkṣitopakramam ||
yathāvā
tvaṃ vatsajātalālasa āyātastajjighṛkṣurasi bāḍham | avidūre carati priya eṣa tato gopabāla kiṃ tvarase || 1895 ||
idaṃ vakṣojagrahaṇotsukaṃ bhagavantaṃ nandanandanaṃ prati vaktavyaṃ govatsagrahaṇautsukyaśālinaṃ savidhabhuvi sañcarantaṃ vallavamāṇavakaṃ kañcinnirdeśya kayācidgopakāminyā kathyate | he gopabāla vallavakumāra ! pakṣe he kṛṣṇeti saṃbuddhiḥ | tvaṃ vatse tarṇake jātā lālasā autsukyaṃ ‘lālase prārthanautsukye' ityamaraḥ | āyātaḥ | anyatra tvaṃ vatsajātayoḥ vakṣojayoḥ 'vatsaḥ putrādivarṣayoḥ | tarṇake norasi klībam' iti medinī | lālasaḥ utsukassannityarthaḥ ‘lolupo lolubho lolo lampaṭo lālasaśca saḥ' iti trikāṇḍaśeṣaḥ | āyātaḥ tajjighṛkṣuḥ tarṇakagrahecchuḥ vakṣojagrahecchuśca | asi | taṃ jighṛkṣuḥ tau jighṛkṣuriti ca vigrahaḥ 'na loka' iti ṣaṣṭhīniṣedhena karmaṇi dvitīyāyāṃ 'gamyādīnāmupasaṃkhyānam' iti samāsaḥ | bāḍhaṃ sādhvetadityaṅgīkāre | eṣa priyaḥ prītyāspadabhūto varasaḥ | pakṣe eṣa priyaḥ madbhartā avidūre upakaṇṭha eva carati tṛṇaṃ bhakṣayatītyarthaḥ | 'caratirbhakṣaṇe'pi' ityanuśāsanāt | pakṣe sañcarati | kutassaṃtvarase motsuko bhūriti bhāvaḥ | na nu nandanandanaṃ prati vaktavye prastute aprastutānyagopamāṇavakavṛttāntavarṇanādaprastutapraśaṃsaiveyamiti cenna, kāryakāraṇasāmānyaviśeṣasārūpyairanabhivyajyamānatvāt | nāpi prakṛtāprakṛtaśleṣamātraṃ, tatra hyaprastutārthasya prastutārthopamānabhāvena vivakṣā'sti | neha tathā aprastutārthasya vivakṣā | atrāprastutasya kevalamanyāti sa
ndhānāya nirdiṣṭatayā vicchittiviśeṣasadbhāvena pāriśeṣyādgūḍhoktirityalaṅkārāntarameva yuktamāśrayitum | na cātra taṭasthātisaṃdhānarūpavyaṅgyasya svoktyā'viṣkaraṇavirahāddhvanirevāyaṃ nālaṅkāra iti vācyam, śleṣatastasyārthasyāviṣkaraṇena dhvanerasambhavādityāhuḥ ||
yathāvā
karuṇārdracittabhavatāpālyā paridīnamānasā seyam | nānyatkāṅkṣati nātha tvadapāṅgavilāsalābhavaibhavataḥ || 1896 ||
he karuṇārdracitta he nātha jagadīśvara! paridīnamānasā seyaṃ tanvī | tvadapāṅgavilāsalābhavaibhavataḥ tvatkaṭākṣaprasaraṇāvāptivibhavāt anyat na kāṅkṣati | ataḥ bhavatā tvayā pālyā rakṣaṇīyeti bhagavantaṃ prati prārthanā | gūḍhārthastu  he karuṇārdra he nātha vallabha! cittabhavatāpātyeti samastaṃ padam | cittabhavatāpānāṃ manasijatāpānāṃ ālyā paṅktyā kandarpajvaraparaṃparayetyarthaḥ | paridīnamānasā seyaṃ tanvī | tvadapāṅgetyatra tvaditi bhinnaṃ padaṃ pañcamyekavacanāntam | tvat tvattaḥ apāṅgaḥ anaṅgaḥ tasya vilāsaḥ ratirityarthaḥ | tasya lābhaḥ prāptireva vaibhavaṃ tasmāt tattaḥ | anyat na kāṅkṣati | atra nātheti nandanandanaṃ sambodhya tasmādratimeva prārthayamānāyāḥ kasyāścidvrajalalanāyāstaṭasthavañcanāya bhagavantaṃ pratyātmanassaṃsāratāpadīnatvena kaṭākṣamātrāśaṃsanasya pratyāyanam ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre gūḍhoktisara ekanavatitamaḥ ||
<42>
atha vivṛtoktisaraḥ
vivatoktiśśleṣagūḍhaṃ vivṛtaṃ kavinā yadi ||
śleṣeṇa gupto'rtho yathākathaṃcitkavinā svoktyā āviṣkriyate cetsā vivṛtoktirityarthaḥ | iyamapi dīkṣitopajñam ||
yathā
eṣā carati sukhena hi surabhī ravijāpratīrajānugatā | pālyā tvayetyavādīdabhikṛṣṇaṃ kā'pi gopikā gopam || 1897 ||
eṣā surabhiḥ gauḥ ‘surabhirgavi ca striyām | vasantacaitrayoḥ puṃsi triṣu saumyamanojñayoḥ' iti ratnamālā | ravijāyāḥ yamunāyāḥ pratīrajāḥ tarava iti yāvat | teṣu anugatā satī sukhena carati tṛṇamiti śeṣaḥ | kabalayatītyarthaḥ | saṃcaratīti vā | tvayā pālyā yathā anyatra na yāyāttathā pratipālanīyetyarthaḥ | hārdo'rthastu  surabhiḥ sugandhiḥ | anenāsyāḥ padminījātitvaṃ sūcitam | saumyā manojñā vā | kośastūktaḥ| eṣa ca ahamapīti bhāvaḥ | kāmapāravaśyenātmana eva pārokṣyeṇa nirdeśaḥ | na kevalamitarā eva mahilāḥ | kiṃtvamahapīti caśabdābhiprāyaḥ | ravijāpratīre yamunātaṭe jānugatā tvadrūpaniviṣṭā satī | ratisukhena tvayā pālyā prīṇanīyeti yāvat | iti kā'pi gopikā abhikṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇābhimukhaṃ avādīt | idaṃ paravañcanāya guptasyāviṣkaraṇam | pratīyamānārthāntareṇa kaṃcidgopaṃ
pratyanuśāsanarūpeṇa guptasya vivakṣitasya śrīkṛṣṇena sahātmano yatheṣṭavihārasyārthāntarasyābhikṛṣṇamityanenāviṣkaraṇāt ||
yathāvā
gopālasamudyogaṃ tvamehi samayā vilāsayemā gāḥ | patimiti vanameṣyantaṃ gopī kṛṣṇāya tiṣṭhamānā''ha || 1898 ||
iyaṃ śrīkṛṣṇena saha rantumutkaṇṭhitāyāḥ kasyāścidgopakāminyāssvabhartāraṃ prati vañcanoktiḥ | tathāhi  he gopeti svabhartussaṃbuddhiḥ | tvaṃ alasaṃ mandaṃ udyogaṃ prayatnaṃ ehi | dūrajigamiṣāyāṃ hi mahānudyogaḥ kāryaḥ na tu savidhajigamiṣāyāmiti bhāvaḥ | tadetadāviṣkurvāṇā''ha  imāḥ gāḥ dhenūḥ samayā samīpa eva vilāsaya vihāraya iti svabhartāraṃ pratyuktiḥ | gūḍhābhiprāyapakṣe tu gopāla samut yogaṃ tvaṃ ehi saḥ mayā vilāsaye mā gāḥ iti chedaḥ | he gopāla ! saḥ tvaṃ iyantamavadhiṃ mayā pratīkṣitastvamiti bhāvaḥ | samut viviktaviharaṇasya pratyāsannatvātsānandassan mayā saha yogaṃ saṃgatiṃ ehi ratyarthaṃ madupāntaṃ prāpnuhīti bhāvaḥ | sahasā tvadupāntopasarpaṇe tvaṃ māṃ vilāsayervā na vetyāśaṅkāṃ parihartumāha vilāsaye iti | ratisukhavitaraṇenānandaye iti bhāvaḥ | vilāsavantaṃ karomīti vā | mā gāḥ atastvamidānīmanyato mā vrājīḥ | gacchatveṣa patiḥ paśupālanāya | āvāṃ svairaṃ raṃsyāvahe iti bhāvaḥ | idamapi paravañcanāya guptasyāviṣkaraṇam | kṛṣṇāya tiṣṭhamānā svābhiprāyaṃ prakāśayantītyarthaḥ | tiṣṭhateḥ 'prakāśanastheyākhyayośca' iti
taṅ | ‘ślāghahnuṅsthāśapāṃ jñīpsyamānaḥ' iti sampradānasaṃjñāyāṃ kṛṣṇaśabdāccaturthī ||
yathāvā
śithilayati kareṇāgrimabhāge'ndukabandhameṣa punnāgaḥ | hṛṣyecca tadvaśā'sāviti śaurigirā natānanā gopī || 1899 ||
iyaṃ gopataruṇīpayodharapariṣvaṅgecchorbhagavataśśaureḥ kareṇumuddiśyoktiḥ | eṣaḥ punnāgaḥ mataṅgajapuṅgavaḥ agrimabhāge puraḥpradeśe kareṇa śuṇḍādaṇḍena andukabandhanaṃ nigalabandhaṃ svasya kariṇyāśceti bhāvaḥ | ‘anduko nigalo'strī syāt' ityamaraḥ | śithilayati | tat tasmāt bandhavisraṃsanāddhetoḥ asau vaśā kariṇī ca ‘kariṇī dhenukā vaśā' ityamaraḥ | hṛṣyet svairavihāralipsayā pramodeta | gūḍhārthastu  agrimabhāḥ gendukabandhaṃ iti chedaḥ | agrimā śreṣṭhā bhāḥ dīptiḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | eṣaḥ punnāgaḥ puruṣaśreṣṭha iti svasyaiva sāhasikatvapratyāyanāyānyatvena nirdeśaḥ | kareṇa hastena gendukayoḥ kandukatvenādhyavasitayoḥ payodharayoḥ bandhaṃ kañculībandhanaṃ śithilayati ślathayatīti vartamānasāmīpye bhaviṣyati vartamānavannirdeśaḥ | asau tvamiti
vaktavye paravañcanāyādaśśabdena gopikāyā nirdeśaḥ | evamanupadavasyamāṇatacchabdaprathamapuruṣayorapyūhyam | tadvaśā tasya kañculīvisraṃsayiturmameti bhāvaḥ | vaśā āyattā satī hṛṣyet | ratisukhenānandedityarthaḥ | iti śaurigirā bhagavadvacanākarṇaneneti yāvat | gopī natānanā abhūt | idamapi paravañcanāya guptāviṣkaraṇameva ||
mamaiva raṅgarājavilāse yathāvā
mādhavamabhivīkṣyāmuṃ bhujaṅgaśayamadya hṛdi kuruṣveti | naijasakhīṃ pratyuktaṃ kayācana niśamya joṣamāsa yuvā || 1900 ||
idaṃ bhagavato raṅgarājasyotsavasamaye mahājanasammarde svapayodharadattahastamasamayajñaṃ kañcitkāminaṃ prati vaktavyasyārthasya svasakhīṃ prati kasyāścidvidagdhāyāḥ kāminyā vacanam | bhujaṅgaśayaṃ śeṣaśāyinaṃ amuṃ mādhavaṃ śrīvallabhaṃ śrīraṅgarājaṃ abhivīkṣya abhito dṛṣṭvā pratyavayavaṃ vivicya vilokyetyabhiprāyaḥ | hṛdi kuruṣva dhyāyeḥ | gūḍhārthastu mā dhavaṃ iti, bhujaṅga śayaṃ iti ca padadvayam | he bhujaṅga he pallavika ! amuṃ viprakṛṣṭasthitaṃ ‘adasastu viprakṛṣṭe' ityanuśāsanāt | dhavaṃ madbhartāraṃ vīkṣya hṛdi mama vakṣasi śayaṃ pāṇiṃ mā kuruṣva | madbhartā'yamadūrāvasthita eva mā matpayodharau sprākṣīriti bhāvaḥ | idamapi parātisandhānārthaguptāviṣkaraṇam ||
mamaiva nṛsiṃhavilāse yathāvā
ghanaratnaśrīvatsāṅkamanālokyāpi vallabhaṃ kasmāt | māṃ paśyasīti gadite sakhīṃ prati kayā'pi nivavṛte viṭarāṭ || 1901 ||
iyaṃ bhagavato nṛsiṃhasyotsavasaṃmarde pratyāsannamapi bhartāraṃ rāgāndhatayā'nālokyātmānaṃ paśyantaṃ kañcitkāmukaṃ prati nigaditavyasyārthasya svasakhīṃ prati kasyāścitprauḍhāyā uktiḥ | vallabhaṃ
dayitaṃ sarvalokeśvaraṃ vā ‘triṣvadhyakṣe'pi vallabhaḥ' ityamaraḥ | ghanaṃ ratnaṃ kaustubho yasya sa tathoktaḥ | sa cāsau śrīvatsāṅkaḥ bhagavān taṃ anālokyāpi kasmāddhetoḥ māṃ śriyameva paśyasi | bhagavantamapi paśyeti bhāvaḥ | gūḍhārthastu he kamana kāmuka ! vallabhaṃ madbhartāraṃ ālokyāpi ghanā ca sā ratnaśrīśca sā vatse vakṣasi yasyāstāṃ ghanaratnaśrīyuktaṃ vatsaṃ yasyā iti vā | mā kasmātpaśyasi | madbhartussamakṣaṃ mā drākṣīriti bhāvaḥ | idamapi paravañcanārthaguptāviṣkaraṇameva ||
trapāguptāviṣkaraṇaṃ yathā
smaradīnāmenāṃ tvaṃ subhagovindātaptahṛdayāṃ mām | nātha vimṛśanviviktāmiti gopyā śauririṅgitamabodhi || 1902 ||
smara dīnāṃ enāṃ tvaṃ subhagaḥ vinda avataptahṛdayāṃ māṃ iti prāthamikārthakakṣyāyāṃ padacchedaḥ | śobhanaḥ bhagaḥ aiśvaryādiṣāḍguṇyaṃ yasya saḥ subhagaḥ tasya saṃbuddhiḥ he nātha sarveśvara ! tvaṃ enāṃ māmiti bhāvaḥ | dīnāṃ smara | eṣā kṛpaṇeti jānīhīti bhāvaḥ | ata eva avataptahṛdayāṃ tāpatrayakṣubhitamānasāṃ māṃ vinda prāpnuhi | rakṣyatvena svīkuruṣveti yāvat | svaparigrahe hetvantaraṃ cābhiprayantī prāha  viviktāṃ pavitraśīlāṃ śaraṇyāntaracintanāsaṃvalitatayā svacchāmiti bhāvaḥ | ‘viviktau pūtavijanau' ityamaraḥ | vimṛśan samyagvicārayan vindetyanvayaḥ gūḍhābhiprāyapakṣe tu smaradīnāṃ enāṃ tvaṃ subha govinda ava taptahṛdayāṃ māṃ iti chedaḥ | he nātha priya! sarvānapi puruṣārthān tvameva yācanīya iti bhāvaḥ | he govinda! tvaṃ smareṇa dīnāṃ ata eva
taptahṛdayāṃ virahatāpaśithilitamānasāṃ enāṃ māṃ viviktāṃ niśśalākasthānāvasthitāṃ vimṛśan iyamidānīṃ rahasyavasthiteti samyagvijānan ava prīṇaya | avateḥ prīṇanārthakālloṇmadhyamaikavacanam | atra sphuṭapratīyamānena bhagavadabhyarthanarūpeṇa trapayā guptasya vivakṣitaśrīkṛṣṇasaṃbhogābhyarthanarūpasyārthāntarasya iṅgitamabodhītyanena sphuṭīkaraṇam | idaṃ śabdaśaktikroḍīkṛtaguptāviṣkaraṇam ||
arthaśaktimūlaguptāviṣkaraṇaṃ yathā
tvaṃ kila kṛpānidhirmāṃ pratīkṣamāṇāṃ dṛśā'pi nāgṛhṇāḥ | iti sākūtaṃ bruvatī parirabdhā śauriṇā vrajendumukhī || 1903 ||
tvaṃ kila kṛpānidhiriti viparītalakṣaṇā | kuta ityatrāha pratīti | pratīkṣamāṇāṃ cirarātrāya hṛdayaviparivartamānatvadupabhogāśāsanena tvāṃ pratipālayamānāmiti bhāvaḥ | dṛśā'pi nāgṛhṇāḥ | upabhogapratyāśā tāvadāstāṃ dūre kaṭākṣaleśaprasāreṇa vā na māmādriyathā iti bhāvaḥ | kṛpānidhireva na cedevaṃ kutastanuyā ityupālambhaḥ | atra evaṃvidhayā vacanabhaṅgyā cirotkaṇṭhitatvadupabhogaupayikasamayalābhena tvāmahaṃ pratyaikṣiṣi | sa ca na prāpta ityarthaśaktilabhyaṃ vastu sākūtamityanena vivṛtam | sarvamidaṃ kavinibaddhavaktṛguptāviṣkaraṇodāharaṇam ||
kaviguptāviṣkaraṇaṃ yathā
prakṛte pratīpadarśini nijairguṇairmāṃ vimohayasi nityam | kiṃciddayasva vāme tvayā'viyukto labheya nityasukham || 1904 ||
dayasva vāme tvayā aviyuktaḥ iti chedaḥ | prakṛte prastute samaye he pratīpadarśini! he vāme sundari! nijaiḥ guṇaiḥ vaktrimamānādibhiḥ mā vimohayasi | kiṃciddayasva | mā smaivaṃ momuha iti bhāvaḥ | tvayā aviyuktaḥ aviśliṣṭassan | nityaṃ anārataṃ sukhaṃ labheya prāpnuyām | pakṣe dayasva vā me tvayā viyukta iti chedaḥ | he prakṛte ! iti mūlaprakṛtessaṃbodhanam | ‘pradhānaṃ prakṛtiḥ striyām' ityamaraḥ | he pratīpadarśini! 'madīyānādi karmapravāhapravṛttāṃ bhagavatsvarūpatirodhānakarīṃ viparītajñānajananīṃ svaviṣayāyāśca bhogyabuddherjananīṃ' iti gaditaprakāreṇa sarvaṃ pratīpaṃ pratikūlaṃ darśayatīti tathoktā | ṇijantāddṛśerṇiniḥ | tasyāssaṃbuddhiḥ nijaiḥ guṇaiḥ satvādibhiḥ māṃ nityaṃ vimohayasi viparītadarśinaṃ karoṣi | me mama ‘daivīṃ guṇamayīṃ māyāṃ dāsabhūtaśśaraṇāgato'smi tavāsmi dāsa iti vaktāraṃ māṃ tāraya' ityuktarītyā tvāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatasyeti bhāvaḥ | vāśabdo bhinnakramaḥ | kiṃcidvā dayasva | paripūrṇadayākaraṇābhiprāyābhāve kiṃcidapi vā dayāṃ kurvīthā ityarthaḥ | tāvatā ko lābha ityata āha tvayeti | tvayā viyuktaḥ viyogaṃ prāptassan | idameva tava dayākāryaṃ yathā'haṃ mucyeyeti bhāvaḥ | tataśca kiṃ tavetyata āha nityeti | nityasukhaṃ muktānandaṃ labheyeti | atra tāvat īrṣyākaluṣitanāyikāprasādanavyāpāravidhiḥ pratīyate | prakṛtiguṇakaluṣitasya prakṛtiṃ pratyākrośo vivakṣitārthaḥ | sa ca saptamyantasya prakṛte iti padasya pratīpadarśinīti saṃbuddhyantapadāntarasamabhivyāhārapūrvakamuccāraṇena saṃbuddhyantatāmavagamayyāviṣkṛtaḥ | kavinibaddhavaktṛguptaṃ paravañcanārthaṃ, kaviguptaṃ tu svaprauḍhidarśanārthamiti bhidā ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre vivṛtoktisaro dvinavatitamaḥ
<43>
atha yuktisaraḥ
yuktissyānmarmaṇo guptyai kriyayā paravañcanam ||
kenacidvyāpāreṇa svābhiprāyanigūhanāya yatparasya vañcanaṃ sa yuktirnāmālaṃkāraḥ | ayamapi dīkṣitopajñam | yadyapyayamalaṃkāro jayadevena candrāloke alaṃkāraprakaraṇe na lakṣitaḥ, tathā'pi kāvyalakṣaṇanirūpaṇapare tṛtīyamayūkhe kāvyalakṣaṇeṣvekatamatvena yuktirnirūpitā
yuktirviśeṣasiddhiścedvicitrārthāntarānvayāt |
navastvaṃ nīradaḥ ko'pi svarṇaṃ varṣasi yanmuhuḥ ||
iti | dīkṣitaistu yuktiranyadhaiva lakṣitā
"yuktiḥ parātisaṃdhānaṃ kriyayā marmaguptaye" |
iti ||
yathā
goṣṭhe kṛtasaṃketā govindāśleṣakutukinī sāyam | gopī kā'pi svajanāduttasthau dāmanīmupādāya || 1905 ||
uttasthāvityatra utpūrvakatve'pi tiṣṭhaterūrdhvakarmatvena na taṅ | dāmanīṃ paśurajjum | atra dāmanyādānapūrvakotthānakriyayā sāyaṃ govatsasaṃdānārthotthānabhramajananena govindāśleṣautsukyarūpamarmagopanena svajanavañcanaṃ vivakṣitam | idaṃ vartiṣyamāṇagopanam ||
vṛttagopanaṃ yathā
kā'pi kṣataradavasanā gopakumāreṇa gopabimbo
ṣṭhī | svairamupalālayantī kīramupāgātkalindajātīrāt || 1906 ||
atra svairakīropalālanakriyayā adharabimbasya taddaṃśanabhramajananena nandanandanaradanakṣatirūpamarmagopanena paravañcanam | bimboṣṭhīti viśeṣyaṃ bimbīphalabhrānyā śukena daṣṭassyādayamadhara iti bhramotpādanopaṣṭambhakam ||
atra vyājoktāvākāragopanam, yuktau tvāśayagopanamiti bhedaḥ | yadvā vyājoktāvuktyā gopanaṃ, iha tu kriyayā gopanamiti bhedaḥ | evaṃca 'āyāntamālokya hariṃ pratolyām' iti padye'pi yuktireveti kuvalayānande sthitam ||
atredaṃ cintyam uktikriyākṛtagopanabhedādanayorbheda iti na yuktaṃ, vyājoktiprakaraṇe dīkṣitaireva uktiśabdasya gopanasādhanāntarapratyāyakavyāpāramātropalakṣaṇatāyāḥ sphuṭatayā pratipāditatvāt | tathaiva tatraiva "āyāntamālokya" ityudāharaṇe spaṣṭatayā lakṣaṇasyāpyanugamitatvācca | ato vivakṣitasya vastunaḥ kena cidvyāpāreṇa paravañcanārthaṃ yadgopanaṃ tadvyājoktirityeva vyājoktilakṣaṇaṃ prācāṃ yuktyalaṅkāraṃ pṛthagalakṣayatāmabhimataṃ, sa ca vyāpāraḥ kvaciduktirūpaḥ, kvacittadanyarūpaḥ, gopanīyaśca kvacidākāraḥ kvacittadanyadāśayādikamiti vibhāgo yukta iti na vyājoktito'tiricyate yuktiriti | ataḥ ‘āyāntamālokya' ityatra yuktireveti pūrvottaravirodhena lekhanaṃ cintyameva | lāghavena vyājokterevoktarītyā saṃbhavādityāhuḥ ||
asmābhistu vyājoktāvuktigrahaṇasyākāragopanārthahetvantarapratyāyakavyāpāramātropalaṇatāyā asvīkāreṇa uktirūpavyāpāre
ṇākāragopane vyājoktiḥ | tadanyavyāpāreṇāśayagopane yuktirityeva vyavasthāmāśritya pṛthagimāvalaṅkārau lakṣitau | dīkṣitānāmapi vyājoktiprakaraṇe ‘āyāntamālokya' iti padye vyājoktireveti lekhanaṃ matāntarānurodhena | yuktyalaṅkāraprastāve tasminneva padye yuktireveti lekhanaṃ tu tatraiva svanairbharvyaṃ prakāśayitumiti na pūrvottaragranthavirodha iti samādheyam | evaṃca vyājoktyalaṅkāraprakaraṇe udāhṛte 'yamunātaṭīnikuñjādvrajam' iti padye'pi yuktyalaṃkāra eva na tu vyājoktiriti dik ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre yuktisarastrinavatitamaḥ
<44>
atha lokoktisaraḥ
lokoktissyādasau lokapravādānukṛtiryadi ||
lokānāṃ janānāṃ yaḥ prakṛṣṭo vādaḥ uktiḥ prakarṣaśca tattaddeśānusāreṇa tattaddeśīyajanaparaṃparāgatatvam tādṛśavādasyānukṛtiḥ ullekhaḥ yaḥ sa lokoktirityarthaḥ | ayamapyalaṃkāro dīkṣitopajñameva ||
yathā
tava veṣavahanato'bhavadavahasanapadaṃ sa pauṇḍrako bhagavan | kroṣṭeva citrakāyaṃ dṛṣṭvā taptāyasā likhankāyam || 1907 ||
citrakāyaṃ vyāghraṃ ‘puṇḍarīkaḥ pañcanakhaścitrakāyamṛgadviṣau' ityamaraḥ | citrakāyaśabdopādānaṃ vakṣyamāṇajambukakartṛkasvāṅga
ṇākāragopane vyājoktiḥ | tadanyavyāpāreṇāśayagopane yuktirityeva vyavasthāmāśritya pṛthagimāvalaṅkārau lakṣitau | dīkṣitānāmapi vyājoktiprakaraṇe ‘āyāntamālokya' iti padye vyājoktireveti lekhanaṃ matāntarānurodhena | yuktyalaṅkāraprastāve tasminneva padye yuktireveti lekhanaṃ tu tatraiva svanairbharvyaṃ prakāśayitumiti na pūrvottaragranthavirodha iti samādheyam | evaṃca vyājoktyalaṅkāraprakaraṇe udāhṛte 'yamunātaṭīnikuñjādvrajam' iti padye'pi yuktyalaṃkāra eva na tu vyājoktiriti dik ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre yuktisarastrinavatitamaḥ
atha lokoktisaraḥ
lokoktissyādasau lokapravādānukṛtiryadi ||
lokānāṃ janānāṃ yaḥ prakṛṣṭo vādaḥ uktiḥ prakarṣaśca tattaddeśānusāreṇa tattaddeśīyajanaparaṃparāgatatvam tādṛśavādasyānukṛtiḥ ullekhaḥ yaḥ sa lokoktirityarthaḥ | ayamapyalaṃkāro dīkṣitopajñameva ||
yathā
tava veṣavahanato'bhavadavahasanapadaṃ sa pauṇḍrako bhagavan | kroṣṭeva citrakāyaṃ dṛṣṭvā taptāyasā likhankāyam || 1907 ||
citrakāyaṃ vyāghraṃ ‘puṇḍarīkaḥ pañcanakhaścitrakāyamṛgadviṣau' ityamaraḥ | citrakāyaśabdopādānaṃ vakṣyamāṇajambukakartṛkasvāṅga
citrīkaraṇābhilāṣapūrvakataptāyaśśalākākaraṇakasvāṅgalekhanakarmottambhanābhiprāyeṇa | dṛṣṭvā taptāyasā taptāyaśśalākayeti yāvat | kāyaṃ svāṅgaṃ likhan kroṣṭā jambuka iva | spaṣṭamanyat | atrottarārdhe karṇāṭāndhradeśīyabhāṣāprasiddhalokapravādānukṛtiḥ ||
yathāvā
śikṣitamapi kauṭilyaṃ tyakṣyati naivāsuraprakṛti hṛdayam | api veṇudalābaddhaṃ śvāpucchaṃ kimu ṛjūbhavedbhagavan || 1908 ||
śunaḥ pucchaṃ śvāpucchaṃ ‘śuno dantadaṃṣṭrā' ityādinā dīrghaḥ | atrāndhradeśaprasiddhalokapravādānukaraṇam ||
yathāvā
śaure tvadanyadaivaṃ pratyetya bhavābdhitaraṇamāśaṃsan | viśvasya mahābudbudamabhipatati prāvṛṣeṇyasariti janaḥ || 1909 ||
atrāpyāndhradeśīyalokapravādānukṛtiḥ ||
yathāvā
mahatā yogena tvāmiha tāvadupāsya yo mahodāram | ālambetālpaphalaṃ śailaṃ khātvā''khumeṣa gṛhṇāti || 1910 ||
yathāvā
satsvapi sudarśanādiṣu nakharaiḥ praticaskare tvayā daityaḥ | nanu narahare kimarthā nakhāpaneye paraśvathāpekṣā || 1911 ||
yathāvā
samitau satāṃ niviśatāṃ na tāvatā tvatkathāṃ khalo gāyet | lokeśa kiṃnu kākaḥ pañjaranihito'pi pañcamaṃ brūyāt || 1912 ||
yathāvā
tvayi sarve'pyupacārā vandhyāssyurdevatāntarāsaṅge | ekasmin svairatve nātha sahasraṃ vratāni naśyanti || 1913 ||
yathāvā
prāktvadanugrahahānādadhunā'pi tavātyanugrahavidhānāt | abhajāmākiṃcanatāmevaṃca pṛthābhuvāṃ na kila rājyam || 1914 ||
prāk akiṃcanatāṃ daridratāṃ adhunā akiṃcanatāṃ upāyāntaravidhuratāṃ abhajāma | evaṃca aihikaśrīrasmākaṃ davīyasīti bimbamanādṛtya evaṃcetyādipratibimbavarṇanāt lalitojjīviteyam | pūrvodāharaṇeṣūpamānidarśanādyujjīvitatvaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ buddhimadbhiḥ | eṣu sarveṣvapi padyeṣu tattaddeśīyalokapravādānukṛtiḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre lokoktisaraścaturnavatitamaḥ
<45>
atha chekoktisaraḥ
syāccellokoktiransyārthagarbhā chekoktiriṣyate |
yathāvā
samitau satāṃ niviśatāṃ na tāvatā tvatkathāṃ khalo gāyet | lokeśa kiṃnu kākaḥ pañjaranihito'pi pañcamaṃ brūyāt || 1912 ||
yathāvā
tvayi sarve'pyupacārā vandhyāssyurdevatāntarāsaṅge | ekasmin svairatve nātha sahasraṃ vratāni naśyanti || 1913 ||
yathāvā
prāktvadanugrahahānādadhunā'pi tavātyanugrahavidhānāt | abhajāmākiṃcanatāmevaṃca pṛthābhuvāṃ na kila rājyam || 1914 ||
prāk akiṃcanatāṃ daridratāṃ adhunā akiṃcanatāṃ upāyāntaravidhuratāṃ abhajāma | evaṃca aihikaśrīrasmākaṃ davīyasīti bimbamanādṛtya evaṃcetyādipratibimbavarṇanāt lalitojjīviteyam | pūrvodāharaṇeṣūpamānidarśanādyujjīvitatvaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ buddhimadbhiḥ | eṣu sarveṣvapi padyeṣu tattaddeśīyalokapravādānukṛtiḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre lokoktisaraścaturnavatitamaḥ
atha chekoktisaraḥ
syāccellokoktiransyārthagarbhā chekoktiriṣyate |
yathāvā
tvadvimukho'mbujalocana madvaṃśe mā janiṣṭa kaścidapi | vikalassvayaṃ plavaṅgassakalaṃ vanamapi kadarthayennūnam || 1915 ||
atra ‘mā janiṣṭa sa no vaṃśe' ityupakramya ‘ājanmanidhanaṃ yasya vāsudevo na daivatam' iti vacanaikadeśārthasaṃgrāhake prapannavacane vikalassvayaṃ plavaṅga ityāndhradeśaprasiddhalokapravādo'nukṛtaḥ | tatra sarveśvareśvare tvayi vaimukhyamaśnuvānaḥ aticapalatayā kapiprāya eva | sa hi acintyādbhutākliṣṭajñānavairāgyabhaktiparipūrṇatayā mahāvanamivātigabhīratamaṃ svakulamapi svadaurātmyenākulayedityarthāntaramapi garbhitam ||
yathāvā
nanu kasmin valmīke vṛjinagatiḥ kīdṛgastiveti vadan | saṅgātsjanasyāpi trasyati bhagavaṃstvadekaśaraṇo yaḥ || 1916 ||
vṛjinagatiḥ jihmagaḥ | atrāndhrabhāṣāprasiddhalokapravādānukṛtiḥ | prākṛto janastamomayatayā durjantusaṃvalitatayā cātibhayaṅkaro valmīkaprāya eva tasmin vṛjinagatiḥ duritānubandhaḥ kīdṛgvetyarthāntaragarbhitā | atra
apyekapaṅktau nāśnīyādbrāhmaṇaissvajanairapi |
ko vijānāti kiṃ kasya pracchannaṃ pātakaṃ bhavet ||
iti smṛtyarthacchāyā'nusṛtā ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre chekoktisaraḥ pañcanavatitamaḥ
<46>
atha vakroktisaraḥ
anyābhiprāyakānyoktamanyathā'nyena yojyate |
śleṣeṇa yadi kākvā vā sā vakroktiritīryate ||
yatra anyābhiprāyakapuruṣāntaroktasyānyenānyathā yadyojanaṃ tadanukūloktirvakroktirityarthaḥ | sā ca dvidhā, śleṣeṇa kākvā vā anyathā yojanāt ||
tatra śleṣeṇa yathā
na krodhaḥ kāryo'mbudhisute tvayā nāhamambudhiḥ kasmāt | nakramadhaḥ kuryāmiti girā hariṃ jayati mohayantī śrīḥ || 1922 ||
atra krodho na kārya ityabhiprāyeṇoktasya bhagavadvākyasya nakraḥ adhaḥkārya iti bhagavatyā śriyā śleṣeṇārthāntaraṃ kalpitam ||
yathāvā
nayanābjaṃ nyasyermayi na nayāmyabjaṃ sa eṣa tava haste | iti vacasā nijadayitaṃ vimohayantī ramā tanotu śubham || 1923 ||
atra nayanābjaṃ mayi nyasyeḥ kaṭākṣaṃ nikṣiperiti bhagavato'bhyarthanavacanasya naya na abjamiti padacchedana na nayāmyabjaṃ kintu sa eṣaḥ abjaḥ śaṅkhaḥ tava hasta evāsta ityarthāntaraparikalpanam ||
yathāvā
kiṃ cirayasi saṃlapituṃ kiṃcidudañcaya kutūhalaṃ lalane | tailikahālikanikaṭe kutūrhalaṃ cāpi labhyate nātra || 1924 ||
kupyasi kasmādvanite prasīda rūpyākṛte mayi praṇate | kupyetkiṃ rūpyākṛtiriti parihasati sma dayitamabdhisuta || 1925 ||
atrādyaśloke kiñcitkutūhalamāśleṣādāvudañcayeti bhagavatoktasya kutūḥ kṛttisnehapātraṃ halaṃ lāṅgalaṃ cetyanayossamāhāraḥ kutūhalamityarthāntaraṃ parikalpya yathākramaṃ tailikanikaṭe kutūḥ hālikanikaṭe halaṃ ca labhyate nātretyuttaram | dvitīye tu he rūpyākṛte praśastarūpākāre he vanite janitātyanurāge ‘vanitā janitātyarthānurāgāyāṃ ca yoṣiti' ityamaraḥ | praṇate mayi kasmāt kupyasi kopaṃ vidhatse iti bhagavatoditasya āhataṃ rūpamasya rūpyaṃ tasyevākṛtiryasyā ityāhatarūpahemarūpyānyataratulyākṛtitvarūpārthāntaram | kupyasītyasya kupyaṃ kṛtākṛtahemarūpyabhinnaṃ tāmrakāṃsyādi tadivācarasītyarthaṃ ca kalpayitvā rūpyākṛtiḥ kathaṃ kupyedityuttaraṃ bhagavatyā jagajjananyā vitīrṇam | rūpyaśabdaḥ 'rūpādāhatapraśaṃsayoryap' iti rūpaśabdādāhatapraśaṃsayorarthayoryappratyayena niṣpannaḥ | 'syātkośaśca hiraṇyaṃ ca hemarūpye kṛtākṛte |
tābhyāṃ yadanyattatkupyaṃ rūpyaṃ taddvayamāhatam' ityamaraḥ ||
yathāvā
utsṛjya mānakāṭavamudadhibhave madhuramastu tava hṛdayam | na dadhibhavā'haṃ kiṃtūdadhijātā mastu mama kathaṃ hṛdayam || 1926 ||
atra roṣakāṭavamutsṛjya tava hṛdayaṃ priyaṃ bhavatvityarthakasya utsṛjya mānakāṭavaṃ madhuramasviti vākyasya utsṛjyamānaṃ kāṭavaṃ yena tattathāvidhaṃ madhuraṃ mastu dadhimaṇḍamityarthāntaraṃ parikalpya na dadhibhavā'hamityādinā uttaram ||
yathāvā
atra pumāsaṃ paramaṃ viddhi sthitamabjadalaviśālākṣi | māṃsaṃ yā trapu vidyāttāṃ pratyupadeśa eṣa saphalassyāt || 1927 ||
atra tvadagre sthitaṃ māṃ paramapuruṣaṃ viddhītyarthakasya atra pumāṃsamityādibhagavadvākyasya yā mugdhā māṃsaṃ trapu vidyāt tāṃ prati māṃsaṃ atrapu ityupadeśasya sāphalyamityarthāntarakalpanena śriyā uttaram ||
yathāvā
tava nāyakena tanvi pratihāre sthīyatāṃ kiyatkālam | nanu nāyakena hāre hāre sthātavyameva satatamapi || 1928 ||
dvāre tava priyeṇa kiyadavadhi sthātavyamityarthakasya tava nāyakenetyādibhagavadvākyasya nāyakamaṇinā pratimuktāhāramapi
stheyamevetyarthāntaravidhānena lakṣmyā uttaram | hārehāre pratihāre ityavyayībhāvaḥ | 'tṛtīyāsaptamyorbahulam' ityamūbhāvābhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
sahajamarudadhipa eṣa tvayyatitṛṣṇassudhāpayodhisute | sahajaṃ marau dadhi pibannatitṛṣṇa itīdamatitarāṃ citram || 1929 ||
atra sahodaro maghavā yasya sa upendraḥ tvayyatimātraspṛha ityarthakasya sahajamarudadhipa iti bhagavadvākyasya sahajaṃ yathā syāttathā marudeśe dadhi piban ativelapipāsa ityarthāntarakaraṇena śriya uttaram | imāni 'na kodhaḥ kāryo'mbudhi' ityādīni padyāni praṇayakalahe śrīśrīvallabhayoruktipratyuktirūpāṇi ||
yathāvā
vrajasumukhi na tiṣṭhāmi vrajāmi bho nandagopavaṃśamaṇe | vrajasi yadi tvaṃ nandetkathamayamiti śauriragrahīdgopīm || 1930 ||
atra vrajasumukhīti gopalalanāṃ pratyabhimukhīkaraṇāya sambuddhyantatayā bhagavatā prayuktasya samastapadasya samukhitayā vrajasumukhyā he sumukhi vraja gaccheti śleṣeṇārthāntaraṃ parikalpya na tiṣṭhāmi vrajāmi bho nandagopavaṃśamaṇe iti bhagavantaṃ prati vakrottaraṃ vyatāri | tenāpi tayā saṃbuddhyantatayā prayuktasya nandagopavaṃśamaṇe ityasya samastasya padasya he gopavaṃśamaṇe nanda modasva iti chedenārthāntaraṃ prakalpya tvaṃ vrajasi yadi
evaṃ vadantaṃ māmupekṣya gacchasicet ayaṃ tvatpariṣvaṅgamabhilaṣa
māṇaḥ kathaṃ nandediti vakroktyaiva svābhīpsitaṃ bhagavatā prakāśitamiti vakroktyujjīvitā vakroktiriyam ||
yathāvā
manye tvaṃ cyutadhīryanna saṃlapantyā mayā''li saṃlapasi | acyutadhiyaṃ vṛthā māṃ cyutadhiyamātthāli satyamacyutadhīḥ || 1931 ||
idaṃ kayościdvrajasubhruvoruktipratyuktirūpaṃ padyam | atra manye ityādi pūrvārdhaṃ kimapi dhyāyantīṃ kāṃcitsakhīṃ prati kasyāścitsakhyā uktirūpam | cyutadhīḥ galitamatiḥ niścetanetyarthaḥ | uttarārdhe acyutadhiyamityādi ātthetyantaṃ anyasyāstāṃ pratyuktiḥ | ālītyādyavaśiṣṭavākyaṃ punastāṃ prati vadantīṃ pratyādyāyā uktiḥ | atra ṛjubuddhyā kamapi vṛttāntaṃ pratsuvatyā kayācitprativācamadadānāṃ sakhīṃ vīkṣya nūnaṃ tvaṃ niścetanā'sītyuktam | tayā ca nāhaṃ niścetanetyabhiprāyeṇa acyutadhiyaṃ māṃ vṛthaiva cyutadhiyaṃ bravīṣīti pratyuktam | punastayā sakhi satyaṃ tvaṃ acyutadhīḥ kṛṣṇānuṣaktahṛdayaivāsi anyathā saṃlapantīṃ māṃ kathaṃ na saṃlapasītyarthāntaraṃ kalpitam ||
yathāvā
sārasanābhāśleṣaṃ sakhi ghaṭaya mameti mugdhayā gadite | ghaṭayitumevodyogo mameti sasmitamuvāca sairandhrī || 1932 ||
atra mugdhayā kayācidgopabālikayā prasādhayitrīṃ śilpakārikāṃ prati ṛjubuddhyā sārasanasya mekhalāyāḥ ābhāyāḥ
prabhāyāḥ āśleṣaṃ saṃbandhaṃ mama ghaṭayetyabhiprāyeṇa prayuktasya sārasanābhāśleṣamityādivākyasya sārasanābhasya padmanābhasya śrīkṛṣṇasya āśleṣaṃ upagūhanaṃ ghaṭayeti śleṣeṇārthāntaraṃ prakalpya ghaṭayitumevāyaṃ pariṣkārodyoga ityuttaraṃ prauḍhayā sairandhryā dattam ||
yathāvā
gopālike vilokaya kiṃciditaḥ kiṃ vilokayāmyalike | gopetyāmantrayase gopīṃ māṃ mugdha gopa eva tvam || 1933 ||
atrātmānaṃ prati kaṭākṣaprasāraṃ prārthayamānena he gopālike gopi itaḥ kiṃcidvilokayeti bhagavatā śrīnandanandanenoktasya gopālike vilokayeti vākyasya he gopa alike lalāṭe kiṃcidvilokayeti śleṣeṇa prakalpyānyamarthaṃ kiṃ vilokayāmyalike ityādyuttaraṃ vitīrṇaṃ vidagdhayā vrajavarārohayā | he mugdha mūḍha sundaretyākūtaṃ tvameva gopa ityanvayaḥ | nāhaṃ gopaḥ kiṃtu gopī | evaṃ strīpuṃsabhedasyāpyanavagamena striyaṃ puṃvācakaśabdenāmantrayamāṇastvameva paśupāla iti bhāvaḥ | yadvā yathāśrutamevānvayaḥ | evaṃ vadan tvaṃ gopa eva | yadi nāgaraka eva bhaveḥ kathamevaṃ brūyā iti bhāvaḥ | gopālakasya strī gopālikā | puṃyogalakṣaṇaṅīṣaḥ ‘pālakāntānna' iti niṣedhaḥ | ṭāpi ‘pratyayasthāt' itītvam ||
yathāvā
kabalayituṃ khalu śakyo mayā śunāsīra eva kimutānyaḥ | neṣṭe grasituṃ sīraṃ śveti muraṃ prati
vadan harirjayati || 1934 ||
atra indro'pi mayā grasituṃ śakya ityabhiprāyeṇa mureṇoktasya kabalayitumityādeḥ śunā śunakasadṛśena tvayā sīraḥ arkaḥ śunā śunakena halaṃ vā grasituṃ na śakyamiti bhagavatā arthāntarasya kalpanayā pratyuttaraṃ pratipāditam | 'sīro'rkahalayoḥ puṃsi' iti medinī | sarvamidamavikṛtaśleṣavakrokterudāharaṇam ||
vikṛtaśleṣavakroktiryathā
śleṣasyāvikṛtatvaṃ ca samabhivyāhṛtākṣarāparityāgenārthavarṇanam | vikṛtatvaṃ ca kasyacidvarṇasyāvāpodvāpābhyāṃ bhavati ||
asamassuśobhamāno jagadīśo vāmadeva evāyam | asamassuśobhitaścetsa eva jagadīśvaro na saṃdehaḥ || 1935 ||
idaṃ śaivavaiṣṇavayossaṃvādarūpaṃ padyam | tatra asamaḥ asadṛśaḥ suśobhamānaḥ dedīpyamānaḥ ayaṃ asmadupāsyaḥ vāmadevaḥ virūpākṣa eva jagadīśa iti śaivenoktam | vaiṣṇavena tu  vāsudeva eva jagadīśa ityabhiprāyeṇa samaḥ makārasahitaḥ sa na bhavatītyasamaḥ suśobhitaḥ cyāvitamakārasthānakena sukāreṇa śobhitaścet sa eva vāmadevo jagadīśo na saṃdeha iti | vāmadevaśabde makārodvāpaṃ tatraiva sukārāvāpaṃ ca vidhāyārthāntaraṃ śleṣeṇa parikalpitam ||
yathāvā
bāṇāsuravaradāyī kāśīpuravahanapaṭurayaṃ paramaḥ | vedaṃ paṭhātra paramaḥ ka iti tadā vetsi kiṃnu bahujalpaiḥ || 1936 ||
ayamapi śaivavaiṣṇavayorevoktipratyuktirūpaḥ ślokaḥ | kāśīpurasya vahanaṃ uddharaṇaṃ rakṣaṇamiti yāvat | tasmin paṭuḥ vīṇāsuravaradāyī kāśīpuravahanapaṭuḥ ayaṃ śiva eva paramaḥ utkṛṣṭa iti śaivenoktam | vedaṃ paṭha atra paramaḥ ka iti tadā vetsi 'tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ daivatānāṃ paramaṃ ca daivatam | agniravamo devatānāṃ viṣṇuḥ paramaḥ' iti śrutipaṭhane paramaḥ ko vā syāditi tvameva jānāsīti prativadatā vaiṣṇavena atra tvaduktayoḥ bāṇāsuravaradāyī kāśīpuravahanapaṭuriti padayoḥ ve vakārasthāne daṃ dakāraṃ paṭheti vakārodvāpanena tatraiva dakārāvāpanena ca bāṇāsuradaradāyī kāśīpuradahanapaṭurayaṃ śrīvāsudeva eva parama ityarthasya parikalpanādidamapi vikṛtaśleṣavakroktyudāharaṇameva ||
yathāvā
mīnekṣaṇe tyaja ruṣaṃ kamanaṃ puratassthitaṃ nirīkṣasva | mandaṃ jalpa śaṭhātretyavanī śrīśaṃ tiraścakāra ruṣā || 1937 ||
iyaṃ mānakaluṣitāyā bhūdevyāḥ prasādanāya bhagavaduktau tadvakroktiḥ | mīnekṣaṇe ityanena nisargaprasannalocanā tvaṃ naiva jātvapi roṣāvilalocaneti dyotitam | purataḥ sthitaṃ prārthanārthamiti bhāvaḥ | kamanaṃ tvāṃ kāmayitāraṃ vallabhaṃ māṃ na tūdāsīnatayā'vasthitamiti bhāvaḥ | nirīkṣasveti bhagavatkartṛkaprasādanoktiḥ | mandaṃ jalpa śaṭhātreti bhūdevyāḥ pratyuktiḥ | he śaṭha gūḍhavipriyakārin
atra mama saṃnikarṣe mandaṃ jalpa na tūccaiḥ, vidhāya vipriyamatimātraṃ nirbharānurāgamedura iva svairaṃ mā vādīḥ jāne tvāmiti bhāvaḥ |
atra tyajjalpitayoḥ mīnekṣaṇe kamanamiti padayoḥ sthitaṃ maṃ makāraṃ daṃ dakāraṃ jalpa makāraṃ dakāratayoccārayetyarthaḥ | tadā dīnekṣaṇe kadanamiti niṣpadyate | tvayā vañcitāṃ māṃ dīnekṣaṇe iti saṃbodhaya | kadanaṃ manovyatheti yāvat | purata eva sthitaṃ evaṃ vañcayati tvayi spaṣṭamevāgre sthitaṃ na tu mīmāṃsanīyamiti bhāvaḥ | atra makāracyāvanena tatraiva dakāravinyasanena ca vikṛtena śleṣeṇārthāntaraparikalpanam ||
yathāvā
yaśśuklabhāsitaruciryena valakṣaśriyodadhāri glauḥ | sa vṛṣākapistrijagatāṃ nātho'laṃ vada punassa eva bhavet || 1938 ||
ayamapi śaivavaiṣṇavoktipratyuktirūpa eva | yaḥ śuklā śubhrā bhāsītī bhasitasaṃbandhinī ruciḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ dhavalabhasmoddhūlita iti yāvat | valakṣaśriyā svataḥ pāṇḍuramūrtinā yena glauḥ śaśāṅkaḥ udadhāri ucchṛtatvena dhṛtaḥ śirasā dhṛta iti yāvat | saḥ vṛṣākapiḥ haraḥ trijagatāṃ nātha ityabhiprāyavatī śaivoktiḥ | vaiṣṇavena punar alaṃ punarvada paryāptaṃ punarbrūhi paryālocya brūyā ityarthaḥ | sa eva tvadukta eva trijagatāṃ nātho bhavedityanvāruhya vada alaṃ lakārarahitaṃ vadetyarthāntaragarbhīkārāttvaduktavākye lakāralopena yaḥ śukabhāsitaruciḥ yena vakṣaśśriyā udadhāri gauriti niṣpādya yaḥ śuka iva bhāsitā ruciryasya sa tathoktaḥ marakataśyāmala iti bhāvaḥ | vakṣasi śrīḥ yasya vakṣaśśrīḥ tathoktena yena gauḥ bhūḥ udadhāri uddhṛtā ‘uddhṛ
tā'si varāheṇa kṛṣṇena śatabāhunā' iti śruteḥ | saḥ vṛṣākapiḥ bhagavān viṣṇureva ‘haraviṣṇū vṛṣākapī' ityamaraḥ | trijagatāṃ nātha ityabhiprāyaparatayā yojitā | atra lakārasyodvāpamātramiti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
śiva eva hi lokasyādhīśa iti tvaṃ bravīṣi nanu vidvan | vācālocyuta eva sa iti vada tattvaṃ tvameva jānāsi || 1939 ||
vācālaḥ vācāṭaḥ tvaṃ śiva eva hi lokasyādhīśa iti bravīṣi nanu acyuta eva saḥ lokasyādhīśa iti vada | saḥ śivaḥ acyuta eva acyutātmaka eva | acyuta eva sa iti vā yojanā | acyuta eva śivo maṅgalamūrtiḥ ‘śāśvataṃ śivamacyutam' iti śruteḥ | ataeva lokasyādhīśa iti vada | pakṣe vāceti bhinnaṃ padam | saḥ śivaḥ locyuta eva lokāracyavanaṃ prāpta eva lokasyādhīśaḥ kasyādhīśaḥ na kasyāpyadhīśa iti vācā vada | tata eva tattvaṃ jānāsi sarveśvareśvare bhagavati nārāyaṇe jāgrati kasyāyamadhipatissyāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāpi vakroktau varṇodvāpamātram ||
sarvamidaṃ śabdaśleṣamūlāyā vakrokterudāharaṇam | arthaśleṣamūlāyā yathā
gopālaḥ kva paśūnāṃ patiḥ kva vā kvādya tanvi gauścarati | tadveda vṛṣabhavāhana iti narmavaco'vatādumāramayoḥ || 1940 ||
atra gopāla ityādi gaurīparihāsavākyaṃ kṛṣṇābhiprāyagarbhaṃ paśūnāṃpatiḥ kva vetyādinā maheśvaravṛttāntāviṣkaraṇābhiprāyaparatayā śriyā yojitam ||
kākvā yathā
vegādāgāḥ kiṃ vā cikīrṣase kṛṣṇa nanu na jāne'ham | na vijānāsi natabhrūrityāśliṣyan harirjayati gopīm || 1941 ||
atra kasya kāryasya cikīrṣayā tava vegādāgamanamiti na vedmīti ṛjumatitayā'nuyuñjānāṃ vrajalalānāṃ prati na vijānānāsīti kākvā arthāntarakalpanam ||
kākuvikṛtaśleṣābhyāṃ militābhyāmapīyaṃ bhavati | yathā
pracurājipaṭīyāṃsaṃ dāśarathe vipravarṇamukhyaṃ mām | viddhi prasannaśastraṃ bahumanye bhṛguja sādhu vipraṃ tvām || 1942 ||
ayaṃ bhārgavarāghavayossaṃvādarūpaślokaḥ | tatra pracurasamarapaṭutaraṃ brahmavarṇapramukhaṃ nirmalāstraṃ māṃ viddhīti svasya durjayatvamahīyastvādyabhiprāyeṇa bhārgavoktasya caraṇatrayasya he bhṛguja ! sādhu vipraṃ tvāṃ bahumanye iti kākvā aho tava brāhmaṇyamityākūtagarbhayā'nyadhā yojanaṃ bhagavatā rāghaveṇa kṛtam | atraiva he bhṛguja ! tvāṃ sādhu samyak bahu bahulaṃ ca vipraṃ vigatapravarṇaṃ manye iti pravarṇoddhāreṇārthāntarakalpanaṃ ca | tathā sati curājipaṭīyāṃsaṃ vivarṇamukhyaṃ sannaśastramiti ca niṣpadyate | curā cauryaṃ
