tatpracurā ājiḥ yuddhaṃ tatra paṭīyāṃsaṃ ‘taskarācarito mārgo naiṣa vīraniṣevitaḥ' ityuktarītyā cauryasamarapaṭutaraṃ pāmaramukhyaṃ 'vivarṇaḥ pāmara' ityamaraḥ | sannaśastraṃ viśīrṇāstramiti tadarthaḥ||
śleṣakākubhyāṃ vinā yojanābhedenārthāntarakalpane'pīyaṃ saṃbhavati | yathā
na tvayyanurakto'haṃ nīlāmanurañjayeya nanu rādhe | vedmi na mayyanuraktaṃ tvāṃ nīlāṃ rañjayantameva hare || 1943 ||
atra tvayyanurakto'haṃ nīlāṃ nānurañjayeyeti bhagavatā vivakṣitāyā yojanāyāḥ yathāśrutameva yojanena rādhayā'rthāntaraṃ kalpitamiti bhavati vakroktiḥ | yadyapyevaṃvidhā vakroktiḥ prāktanairarvāktanairvā na vivecitā | tathā'pi tādṛśacamatkṛtisadbhāvādvakroktireveyamityasmābhirvyaveci ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre vakroktisaraṣṣaṇṇavatitamaḥ
atha svabhāvoktisaraḥ
jātyādisthasvabhāvoktissvabhāvoktiritīryate ||
jātyādiniṣṭhasvabhāvavarṇanaṃ svabhāvoktirityanvarthasaṃjño'laṅkāraḥ | ādipadena guṇakriyādravyāṇi gṛhyante ||
yathā
vatsasmaraṇaprasnutasupīvarodhrīṃ vilolatarasāsnām | huṃbhāravagambhīrāṃ gavyāṃ sāyaṃ nivartayati śauriḥ || 1944 ||
gavyāṃ gavāṃ samūhaṃ ‘tasya samūhaḥ' ityadhikāre ‘khalagorathāt' iti yat | supīvarāṇi ūdhāṃsi āpīnāni yasyāstāṃ tathoktāṃ ‘ūdhastu klībamāpīnam' ityamaraḥ | ‘ūdhaso'naṅ' ityanaṅi ‘bahuvrīherūdhaso jīṣ'| sphuṭārthakamanyat | atra gojātisvabhāvasya varṇanam ||
khañjitaharinīlamadaṃ mañjulatāpiñchapiñchasacchāyam | añjanayati māmakadṛśamañjanagirikuñjadhāmaharidhāma || 1945 ||
añjanagirikuñjadhāmā yo hariḥ tasya dhāma rociḥ idaṃ kartṛ | māmakadṛśaṃ añjanayati añjanavatīṃ karoti añjanaśabdānmatubantāt ṇāviṣṭhavadbhāvena ‘vinmatoḥ' iti matupo luk | atra guṇasvabhāvavarṇanam ||
itaretarāṃsaghaṭṭanahṛṣitatanuruhaṃ vivāhavedigatam| anyonyaprahitāhṛtadṛgantamābhātu divyamithunaṃ tat || 1946 ||
atra kriyāsvabhāvavarṇanam ||
urasi ramāṃ nidadhānaṃ karayoścakraṃ daraṃ ca vidadhānam | kanakāmbaraṃ dadhānaṃ kanatānmama manasi
phaṇigirinidhānam || 1947 ||
mitasitahasitavikasvarakapolaphalakaṃ kuraṅgamadatilakam | vṛṣaśikharinityabhogyaṃ vṛṇīmahe manasi kimapi saubhāgyam || 1948 ||
anayoḥ padyayordravyasvabhāvavarṇanam ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre svabhāvoktisarassaptanavatitamaḥ
<47>
atha bhāvikasaraḥ
varṇanādadbhutārthasya vastunī bhūtabhāvinī |
pratyakṣe iva dṛśyete yatra tadbhāvikaṃ matam ||
yatrātyadbhutacaritopavarṇanādatītānāgatayorvastunoḥ pratyakṣāyamāṇatvaṃ bhavati sa bhāvikālaṅkāraḥ | na cātītānāgatayoḥ pratyakṣavadavabhāso viruddhaḥ, atyadbhutavastuvarṇanena bhāvikānāṃ hṛdi bhāvanodayāt | tathāca bhāvanāyāḥ prakarṣeṇa ghaṭata eva pratyakṣāyamāṇatvaṃ bhūtabhāvinorapyarthayoḥ | yathā
vṛkṣevṛkṣe ca paśyāmi cīrakṛṣṇājināmbaram |
asīnassaṃviśaṃstiṣṭhan bhuñjānaḥ paryaṭan piban |
cintayāno hṛṣīkeśamapaśyattanmayaṃ jagat ||
ityādau | na ceyaṃ svabhāvoktiḥ, tatra hi vastusvabhāvasya yathāvadvarṇanena pratyakṣāyamāṇatā | iha tu adbhuṃtatvena | nāpi rasavadādyalaṅkāraḥ, yattatra vibhāvānubhāvādyanusaṃdhānenaiva rasāderbhāmitasitahasitavikasvarakapolaphalakaṃ kuraṅgamadatilakam | vṛṣaśikharinityabhogyaṃ vṛṇīmahe manasi kimapi saubhāgyam || 1948 ||
anayoḥ padyayordravyasvabhāvavarṇanam ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre svabhāvoktisarassaptanavatitamaḥ
<48>
atha bhāvikasaraḥ
varṇanādadbhutārthasya vastunī bhūtabhāvinī |
pratyakṣe iva dṛśyete yatra tadbhāvikaṃ matam ||
yatrātyadbhutacaritopavarṇanādatītānāgatayorvastunoḥ pratyakṣāyamāṇatvaṃ bhavati sa bhāvikālaṅkāraḥ | na cātītānāgatayoḥ pratyakṣavadavabhāso viruddhaḥ, atyadbhutavastuvarṇanena bhāvikānāṃ hṛdi bhāvanodayāt | tathāca bhāvanāyāḥ prakarṣeṇa ghaṭata eva pratyakṣāyamāṇatvaṃ bhūtabhāvinorapyarthayoḥ | yathā
vṛkṣevṛkṣe ca paśyāmi cīrakṛṣṇājināmbaram |
asīnassaṃviśaṃstiṣṭhan bhuñjānaḥ paryaṭan piban |
cintayāno hṛṣīkeśamapaśyattanmayaṃ jagat ||
ityādau | na ceyaṃ svabhāvoktiḥ, tatra hi vastusvabhāvasya yathāvadvarṇanena pratyakṣāyamāṇatā | iha tu adbhuṃtatvena | nāpi rasavadādyalaṅkāraḥ, yattatra vibhāvānubhāvādyanusaṃdhānenaiva rasāderbhā
vyatvaṃ na tvadbhutatvena | na cotprekṣā, atītānāgatayoḥ pratyakṣatvena saṃbhāvanābhāvāt | nāpi bhrāntimān, bhāvānāyā abhrāntikṛtatvāt | tasmātsarvottīrṇau vilakṣaṇa evāyamalaṃkāra iti vadanti ||
yathā
vikaṭodbhaṭāṭṭahāsaṃ vidalayato ditisutendrahṛdayataṭīm | adyāpi siṃhaśikhariṇi narasiṃhasyārbhaṭī sphuṭībhavati || 1949 ||
siṃhaśikhariṇi śeṣādrau tadekadeśabhūte ahobile iti yāvat | atra hiraṇyakaśipuvakṣo'ntaravikṣobhaṇadakṣolbaṇasaṃrambhasya śrīnṛsiṃhasya bhagavataḥ atyadbhutātītamahārbhaṭīvarṇanena tathāvidhabhāvanayā tādṛśapratyakṣāyamāṇatā | bhīṣaṇatvodbhāvanamidam ||
yathāvā
janayanti modamakṣṇossanakasanandanasanatkumārādyāḥ | adyāpyadhibhujagācalamādyā munayo hariṃ bhajanto naḥ || 1950 ||
sthānamāhātmyodbhāvanamidam ||
yathāvā
patrīśvaramadhirūḍhe yātrotsavakautukādahigirīndau | grāhagṛhītagajāvanasaṃbhramamadyāpyamuṣya paśyāmaḥ || 1951 ||
yathāvā
sammardakupitabalapativetrahatitruṭitakoṭikoṭīrān | adyāpi padmajādyāṃstvaddvārīkṣāmahe vṛṣādrimaṇe || 1952 ||
yathāvā
bhavatā vālidaśāsyau bhagnau raghuvīra dṛṣṭavantaṃ pūrvam | ahahā ityadhunā'pi ca sahasranayanaṃ sahāsranayanaṃ vīkṣe || 1953 ||
ahaha ā iti chedaḥ| etau dvāvapi nipātau yathākramaṃ vālismaraṇajanitakhede rāvaṇasmaraṇādbhute ca vartete | ā itītyatra 'nipāta ekājanāṅ' iti prakṛtibhāvaḥ | aṅidevāyaṃ nipātaḥ, smaraṇārthakatvāt | anuśiṣyate hi ‘vākyasmaraṇayoraṅit' iti | ahaha ā iti ayaṃ duḥkhāścaryadyotako vāgārambhasaṃbhavo'nubhāvaḥ | ahaha ā iti adyāpi asreṇa śokānandajanyenāsreṇa saha vartante iti sahāsrāṇi ‘vopasarjanasya' ityasya vaikalpikatvānna sahaśabdasya sabhāvaḥ | tādṛśāni nayanāni yasya taṃ tathāvidhaṃ adyāpi vālivadhajanitaśokāśrunayanaṃ rāvaṇavadhajanitapramodāśrunayanaṃ ca vīkṣe iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe sahasranayanaśabdaṃ ahahāḥ avidyamānaḥ haḥ hakāro yena saḥ ahaḥ ahaḥ hāḥ hā iti varṇaḥ yasya tathoktaḥ hakāracyāvanena tatraiva
hākāreṇa ghaṭita ityarthaḥ | iti hetoḥ sahāsranayanaṃ vīkṣe uktarītyā sahasranayanaśabdasya tathābhūtatvāditi bhāvaḥ | idamapi bhūtaviṣayasākṣātkāravarṇanameva | camatkārāntaraṃ tu pūrvebhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
sarvamidaṃ bhūtaviṣayasākṣātkāravarṇanam | bhaviṣyadviṣayasākṣātkāravarṇanaṃ yathā
anudinamanusaṃdhānādarcirmukhasaraṇibhāvisaṃmānāḥ | adyaiva vasanti dṛśoḥ padyāyāṃ śrīnivāsa tava kṛpayā || 1954 ||
atra bandhāvasthāyāmaśakyadarśanatvenādbhutānāmarcirādimārgasaṃmānānāṃ bhāvināṃ sākṣātkāravarṇanam ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre bhāvikasaro'ṣṭanavatitamaḥ
<49>
athodāttasaraḥ
tadudāttaṃ vastu yatra varṇyate susamṛddhimat |
anyopalakṣakaṃ ślāghyacaritraṃ vā nibadhyate ||
yatra saṃpatsamṛddhimadvastu varṇyate, yatra ca mahāpuruṣasaṃbandhiślāghyacaritaṃ ‘yatrāyaṃ sārasassa devadattakedāraḥ' itivadupalakṣakatayā vā nibadhyate tatrodāttālaṅkāraḥ | upalakṣakaṃ jñāpakam | svabhāvoktau bhāvike ca yathāvadvastuvarṇanam | tadvipakṣatvenāropitavastvātmanaḥ prathamodāttasyāvasaraḥ | tatrāsaṃbhāvyamānavibhūtiyuktasya vastuno varṇanaṃ kavipratibhotthāpitamaiśvaryalakṣaṇamudāttaṃ prathamam | dvitīyaṃ tu mahāpuruṣāṇāmudāttacaritānāmaṅgibhūtavastvantarāṅgabhāvenopanibadhyamānaṃ caritam, mahāpuruṣacaritasyodāttatvāt ||
sarvamidaṃ bhūtaviṣayasākṣātkāravarṇanam | bhaviṣyadviṣayasākṣātkāravarṇanaṃ yathā
anudinamanusaṃdhānādarcirmukhasaraṇibhāvisaṃmānāḥ | adyaiva vasanti dṛśoḥ padyāyāṃ śrīnivāsa tava kṛpayā || 1954 ||
atra bandhāvasthāyāmaśakyadarśanatvenādbhutānāmarcirādimārgasaṃmānānāṃ bhāvināṃ sākṣātkāravarṇanam ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre bhāvikasaro'ṣṭanavatitamaḥ
athodāttasaraḥ
tadudāttaṃ vastu yatra varṇyate susamṛddhimat |
anyopalakṣakaṃ ślāghyacaritraṃ vā nibadhyate ||
yatra saṃpatsamṛddhimadvastu varṇyate, yatra ca mahāpuruṣasaṃbandhiślāghyacaritaṃ ‘yatrāyaṃ sārasassa devadattakedāraḥ' itivadupalakṣakatayā vā nibadhyate tatrodāttālaṅkāraḥ | upalakṣakaṃ jñāpakam | svabhāvoktau bhāvike ca yathāvadvastuvarṇanam | tadvipakṣatvenāropitavastvātmanaḥ prathamodāttasyāvasaraḥ | tatrāsaṃbhāvyamānavibhūtiyuktasya vastuno varṇanaṃ kavipratibhotthāpitamaiśvaryalakṣaṇamudāttaṃ prathamam | dvitīyaṃ tu mahāpuruṣāṇāmudāttacaritānāmaṅgibhūtavastvantarāṅgabhāvenopanibadhyamānaṃ caritam, mahāpuruṣacaritasyodāttatvāt ||
yathā
vajraśilāvālāñcitamarakatakalatāruṇāśmapallavite | saha mahilā viharante gṛhanivahe tvatkaṭākṣitāśśaure || 1955 ||
idaṃ samṛddhyudāharaṇam | atrāsaṃbhāvyamānavibhūtiyuktānāṃ bhagavatkaṭākṣitānāṃ varṇanāllakṣaṇasaṃgatiḥ | saṃbhāvyamānavibhūtiyuktasya varṇane tu nāyamalaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā
parikhacitavajrarekhāsphuritamahānīlajālacārutamāḥ | sataṭijjaladabhramadā vṛṣaśikhariṇi bhānti te vihāragṛhāḥ || 1956 ||
atra hi mahāvibhūterbhagavato vihāragṛhāṇāṃ vastuta eva saṃbhavatīdṛśaviśeṣaḥ | ata evāsya kavipratibhotthāpitatvamuktam | evaṃcāsya nāmāpi sārthakamiti vimarśinīkāraḥ ||
ślāghyacaritasyopalakṣakatve yathā
jayatu sa śailo yatra tyaktāstraṃ kenacinnimittena | śrīvāsamabjacakre yatirājo grāhayāmāsa ||
yatirāja bhagavadrāmānujācāryaḥ | kenacinnimittena toṇḍamānnṛpāya ripuvijayakṛte vitīrṇayośśaṅkhacakrayoḥ punastadvaradānāvasare arcārūpe'sminpunaśśaṅkhacakradhāraṇarūpatatprārthanalakṣaṇena
nimittena tyaktāstraṃ tyaktaśaṅkhacakraṃ bhagavantaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ śaṅkhacakre grāhayāmāsa | ‘ajagrahattaṃ janako dhanustat' itivaddvikarmakatā | atra
mā bhaiṣīḥ putra bhadraṃ te śaṅkhacakrau dadāmi te |
tābhyāṃ gaccha purīṃ divyāṃ tau te śatrūn haniṣyataḥ ||
ityuktvā pradadau tasmai tābhyāṃ saha jagāma saḥ |
tau ca śatrūnnihatyāśu kṛtvā rājyamakaṇṭakam ||
ājagmatuḥ kṣitibhṛtā sākaṃ devasya saṃnidhim |
rājā devaṃ vavande'tha stutvā stotrairanekaśaḥ ||
varaṃ varaya bhadreti devo rājānamabravīt |
rājā mamāyudhapradānasya khyātyai devottama prabho ||
arcābimbe śilābimbe cakraśaṅkhe na dhāraya |
iti saṃprārthito devo na dadhāra punaśca tau ||
adṛśyau tiṣṭhataścobhau pārśvataśśārṅgadhanvanaḥ ||
ityādiveṃkaṭādrimāhātmyāntargatabrāhmapurāṇakathābhagavadbhāṣyakārakā ritaśrīnivāsaśaṅkhacakraparigrahakathā cehānusaṃdheyā | atra ślāghyasya bhagavadrāmānujācāryakāritaśrīnivāsaśaṅkhacakradhāraṇarūpamahāpuruṣacaritrasya varṇanīyaṃ veṃkaṭādriṃ pratyupalakṣakatvam ||
yathāvā
sa śiloccayo vijayatāṃ yatrāmṛtado'pyaśeṣavibudhānām | kaṃcana vibudhaṃ tātetyāmantryāyācatāmṛtaṃ jagajjanakaḥ || 1958 ||
anekeṣāṃ vibudhānāṃ devānāṃ viduṣāṃ vā amṛtadaḥ sudhāpradaḥ niśśreyasaprado vā jagajjanakaḥ śrīnivāsaḥ kaṃcana vibudhaṃ śrīśailapūrṇanāmānaṃ mahātmānaṃ vipaścitaṃ tāteti āmantrya saṃbodhya amṛtaṃ sudhāṃ niśśreyasaṃ vā | vārīti tu tattvam | ayācata davīyastarāttumburutīrthādanudinaṃ svārādhanakṛte tīrthānayanakaiṃkarya
māracayatyamuṣmin kṛpayā tādṛśasārvakālikaśramaṃ tasya parijihīrṣuśśabaraveṣadharo bhagavān śrīnivāsastādṛśabhūmikayā śriyā saha madhyemārgamabhyetya taṃ tattīrthaṃ yayāce | sa ca taṃ śabarameva matvā na vyatārīdbhagavadarhaṇāyānīyamānaṃ tīrtham | adadāne ca tasmiṃstattīrthaṃ bhagavāṃstaduttamāṅgadhṛtaṃ tadudakumbhaṃ nirbhidya tatprasrutāṃ vāridhārāmāsyenāgṛhṇātsaha jāyayā | bhagavadarhaṇārthamānīyamānaṃ tīrthaṃ śabareṇa dūṣitamiti tadavaśiṣṭamakhilamapi tīrthaṃ tatraiva parihṛtya punastarāṃ tadānayanāya tumburutīrthaṃ prati prasthite ca tasmin bhagavān kṛpayā svamaiśvaraṃ rūpaṃ pradarśya netaḥparamanudinaṃ tumburutīrthāddavīyaso madarhaṇāyāharaṇīyaṃ pānīyaṃ, alametāvataiva kaiṃkaryeṇa tuṣṭo'smītyabhidhāya
yastāteti mayā''hūtassa tāta iti gīyate |
iti varaṃ vitīryāntaradhāt | ityaitihyam | atra ‘tātetyāmantrya kaṃcidvanabhuvi tṛṣitastoyabinduṃ yayāce' iti ślokapādārtho'nukūlaḥ | atrāpyuktaprakāreṇa mahāpuruṣacaritaṃ śeṣācalāṅgatayā varṇitam ||
aṅgibhūtasya vastuna utkarṣapratipipādayiṣayā aṅgitvenopanibadhyamānaṃ mahāpuruṣacaritameva tadalaṃkārāṅgam | na tūpalakṣaṇamātraparatayopāttamityāhuḥ | tena
nayanāyanagocaratāmayate dvisahasranayanagirirājaḥ | vitrāsinīṃ ripūṇāṃ yatrā'dattādbhutāṃ guhaśśaktim || 1959 ||
ityādau nāyamalaṃkāraḥ | atra hi ṣāṇmāturacaritamupalakṣaṇamātraṃ vivakṣitaṃ na tu tena gireḥ ko'pyutkarṣo vivakṣitaḥ ||
yathāvā
ko'pi sa pumānvijayatāṃ yadghaṇṭā śrutiśirogururbhūtvā | udajīvayaduktyamṛtairdurmatakathakaviṣamūrchitaṃ bhuvanam || 1960 ||
yasya śrīnivāsasya ghaṇṭā kartrī śrutiśirogururbhūtvā
vitrāsinī vibudhavairivarūthinīnāṃ
padmāsanena paricāravidhau prayuktā |
utprekṣyate bhuvi janairupapattibhūmnā
ghaṇṭā haressamajjaniṣṭa yadātmaneti ||
ityuktarītyā nigamāntamahādeśikatvenāvatīryetyarthaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | atrāṅgibhūtasya śrīnivāsasya mahotkarṣapratipipādayiṣayā taddhaṇṭāvatārasya bhagavato nigamāntadeśikasya caritamaṅgatayā nibaddham ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre udāttālaṅkārasara ekonanavatitamaḥ
<50>
athātyuktisaraḥ
adbhutātathyaśauryādivarṇanā'tyuktiriṣyate |
adbhutasyāsatyasya asaṃbhāvitasya śauryādervarṇanamatyuktirnāmālaṅkāraḥ | ādiśabdenaudāryakīrtyādergrahaṇam ||
yathā
bahirantarjvalatā śrīnātha tvattejasā'dbhutatamena | brahmāṇḍamūṣikāyāṃ ravirucirāvartya kanakagiriraraci || 1961 ||
yathāvā
ko'pi sa pumānvijayatāṃ yadghaṇṭā śrutiśirogururbhūtvā | udajīvayaduktyamṛtairdurmatakathakaviṣamūrchitaṃ bhuvanam || 1960 ||
yasya śrīnivāsasya ghaṇṭā kartrī śrutiśirogururbhūtvā
vitrāsinī vibudhavairivarūthinīnāṃ
padmāsanena paricāravidhau prayuktā |
utprekṣyate bhuvi janairupapattibhūmnā
ghaṇṭā haressamajjaniṣṭa yadātmaneti ||
ityuktarītyā nigamāntamahādeśikatvenāvatīryetyarthaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | atrāṅgibhūtasya śrīnivāsasya mahotkarṣapratipipādayiṣayā taddhaṇṭāvatārasya bhagavato nigamāntadeśikasya caritamaṅgatayā nibaddham ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre udāttālaṅkārasara ekonanavatitamaḥ
athātyuktisaraḥ
adbhutātathyaśauryādivarṇanā'tyuktiriṣyate |
adbhutasyāsatyasya asaṃbhāvitasya śauryādervarṇanamatyuktirnāmālaṅkāraḥ | ādiśabdenaudāryakīrtyādergrahaṇam ||
yathā
bahirantarjvalatā śrīnātha tvattejasā'dbhutatamena | brahmāṇḍamūṣikāyāṃ ravirucirāvartya kanakagiriraraci || 1961 ||
he śrīnātha! bahiḥ antaśca jvalatā adbhutatamena mūṣānihitakanakarajaḥkaṇādivilāpanamātrakāriṇaḥ prasiddhādagnerapi vakṣyamaṇaravikiraṇadravīkaraṇaghanībhāvobhayakalpakatayā vilakṣaṇeneti bhāvaḥ | tava tejasā pratāpenaiva agninā | mūṣaiva mūṣikā taijasadravyavilāpanī 'taijasāvartanī mūṣā' ityamaraḥ | tasyāṃ raveḥ ruciḥ jātyabhiprāyakamekavacanaṃ, kiraṇā ityarthaḥ| āvartya vilāpya kanakagiriḥ araci | āvartitāḥ prabhākarakarā eva ghanībhāvanayā sumerutvena niṣpāditā iti bhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
brahmāṇḍāntarasṛṣṭyai parito jvalatā tava pratāpena | jagadaṇḍamūṣikāyāmasyāmāvartyate suvarṇagiriḥ || 1962 ||
brahmāṇḍāntarasya 'tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham' ityuktaprakārasyānyasya brahmāṇḍastha sṛṣṭyai sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ ‘kriyārtha' ityādinā caturthī | paritaḥ samantataḥ jvalatā tava pratāpena asyāṃ vidyamānāyāṃ jagadaṇḍameva mūṣikā tasyāṃ suvarṇagiriḥ hemādriḥ āvartyate vilāpyate | brahmāṇḍamūṣikāyāṃ suvarṇagiridravīkaraṇoktyā pratāpe agnitvaṃ gamyate ||
yathāvā
paratastripādvibhūteḥ paritassphuratā kakutsthakulabhāno | atyarkānaladīptirnityaṃ tvadyodhatejasā'raci sā || 1963 ||
tripādvibhūteḥ ‘tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divi' iti śrutāyāḥ nityavibhūteḥ parataḥ paritaḥ sphuratā jvalatā tvadyodhānāṃ sugrīvahanumadādīnāṃ tejasā pratāpena | sā tripādvibhūtiḥ atyarkānaladīptiḥ atikrāntabhānuvahnijyotiḥ vyaraci | atra
brahmaṇassadanāttasya paraṃ sthānaṃ prakāśate |
devā hi yanna paśyanti divyaṃ tejomayaṃ param ||
atyarkānaladīptaṃ tat sthānaṃ viṣṇormahātmanaḥ |
iti pramāṇamihānusaṃdheyam | iyaṃ śauryātyuktiḥ ||
audāryātyuktiryathā
svakaṭākṣalabdhaniravadhivibhavānāṃ dānamanavadhi narāṇām | vīkṣya svayamarthitayā tadgṛhamabhyetya calati na tataśśrīḥ || 1964 ||
atra śrīrapi svakaṭākṣādhigatavibhavānāṃ janānāṃ gṛhamarthitvenābhyetya tato na calatītyanena tadbhaktānāmaudāryātyuktiḥ | tato na calatītyanena tadbhaktasaṃpado'kṣayatvaṃ vyajyate ||
yathāvā
arthitayā pratihāraṃ tvadudañcitasaṃpadāṃ nṛṇāmamba | gacchaticedbhikṣāko gacchatu dhanado'pi yāti bhikṣāyai || 1965 ||
kubero'pi tvatkaṭākṣasamadhigatasaṃpadāṃ janānāṃ gṛhadvāraṃ
bhikṣārthaṃ gacchatītyaudāryātyuktiḥ | bhikṣāko maheśvara ityarthāntaramapi pratīyate ||
kīrtyatyuktiryathā
tvadupāśritajanasamudayayaśaḥpayorāśivīcivikṣubhitāḥ | brahmāṇḍālābugaṇā nimajjanonmajjanaiḥ praviluṭhanti || 1966 ||
iyaṃ kīrtyatyuktiḥ | nanvatiśayoktito'syāḥ ko bheda iti cet atiśayoktāvasaduktimātraṃ, atyuktau tu atathyasyādbhuteti viśeṣaṇādatyantāsaduktiriti jānīhi | ataeva daṇḍinā
anayoranavadyāṅgi stanayorjṛmbhamāṇayoḥ |
avakāśo na paryāptastava bāhulatāntare ||
iti saṃbhāvyamevaitadviśeṣākhyānasaṃskṛtam |
kāntaṃ bhavati sarvasya lokayātrānuvartinaḥ ||
iti | stanayoratipīvaratvavarṇanaṃ lokasiddhamityetādṛśaviśeṣākhyānena utkarṣakathanena alaṃkṛtatayā kāntaṃ ramaṇīyameva bhavatītyuktvā
lokātīta ivātyarthamadhyāropya vivakṣitaḥ |
yo'rthastenātituṣyanti vidagdhā netare janāḥ ||
ityabhidhāya
alpaṃ nirmitamākāśamanālocyaiva vedhasā |
idamevaṃvidhaṃ bhāvi bhavatyāsstanamaṇḍalam ||
idamayuktirityuktametadgauḍopalālitam |
ityatyukteratiśayoktito vailakṣaṇyaṃ darśitam ||
nanu ca | udāttālaṃkārādatyukteḥ ko bhedaḥ ‘vajraśilāvāla'
ityādiprāgupadarśitatadudāharaṇe kavipratibhāmātragocaratayā asaṃbhāvitāyāssamṛddhervarṇanena vailakṣaṇyābhāvāditicet atrāhuḥ 'saṃpa
dutkarṣātyuktāvudāttaṃ, śauryātyuktāvatyuktiḥ' iti | anye tu "atyadbhutakavipratibhāmātragocarāsaṃbhāvitārthavarṇanamudāttamityudāttā laṃlakāraṃ lakṣayitvā tasyārthasya saṃpacchauryaudāryādinānāprakāratayā tathāvidhasaṃpacchauryādāryādyatyuktīnāṃ bhede pramāṇābhāvānnātyuktiḥ pṛthak lakṣaṇīyā" ityāhuḥ | dīkṣitānāṃ tu tathyatvātathyatvābhyāmanayorbheda ityabhiprāyamavarṇayadvaidyanāthaḥ ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre atyuktisaraśśatatamaḥ
<51>
atha niruktisaraḥ
sā niruktiryogato yannāmno'rthāntarakalpanam ||
yogavaśānnāmno'rthaviśeṣābhidhāyino'rthāntaropavarṇanaṃ niruktirnāmālaṅkāraḥ | yadyapyayaṃ prācīnairnālaṃkāratayā lakṣitaḥ, tathā'pi jayadevīye candrāloke tṛtīyamayūkhe
niruktaṃ syānnirvacanaṃ nāmnassatyaṃ tathā'nṛtam |
īdṛśaiścaritairjāne satyaṃ doṣākaro bhavān ||
iti kāvyalakṣaṇatayā pratipāditaṃ niruktameva kiṃcidvailakṣaṇyena dīkṣitairarthālaṃkāratayā paryagaṇyateti tadanurodhenāsmābhirapyayamalaṃkāro lakṣitaḥ | nāmnaḥ prasiddhyanurodhena kṛtaṃ nirvacanaṃ satyaṃ, anṛtaṃ nirvacanaṃ tvanyathā kalitam ||
yathā
ardayasi janānetānnirdaya iva bhavadurantakāntāre | jagadīśa tadiha satyaṃ janārdano'sīti ko'traṃ sandehaḥ || 1967 ||
dutkarṣātyuktāvudāttaṃ, śauryātyuktāvatyuktiḥ' iti | anye tu "atyadbhutakavipratibhāmātragocarāsaṃbhāvitārthavarṇanamudāttamityudāttā laṃlakāraṃ lakṣayitvā tasyārthasya saṃpacchauryaudāryādinānāprakāratayā tathāvidhasaṃpacchauryādāryādyatyuktīnāṃ bhede pramāṇābhāvānnātyuktiḥ pṛthak lakṣaṇīyā" ityāhuḥ | dīkṣitānāṃ tu tathyatvātathyatvābhyāmanayorbheda ityabhiprāyamavarṇayadvaidyanāthaḥ ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre atyuktisaraśśatatamaḥ
atha niruktisaraḥ
sā niruktiryogato yannāmno'rthāntarakalpanam ||
yogavaśānnāmno'rthaviśeṣābhidhāyino'rthāntaropavarṇanaṃ niruktirnāmālaṅkāraḥ | yadyapyayaṃ prācīnairnālaṃkāratayā lakṣitaḥ, tathā'pi jayadevīye candrāloke tṛtīyamayūkhe
niruktaṃ syānnirvacanaṃ nāmnassatyaṃ tathā'nṛtam |
īdṛśaiścaritairjāne satyaṃ doṣākaro bhavān ||
iti kāvyalakṣaṇatayā pratipāditaṃ niruktameva kiṃcidvailakṣaṇyena dīkṣitairarthālaṃkāratayā paryagaṇyateti tadanurodhenāsmābhirapyayamalaṃkāro lakṣitaḥ | nāmnaḥ prasiddhyanurodhena kṛtaṃ nirvacanaṃ satyaṃ, anṛtaṃ nirvacanaṃ tvanyathā kalitam ||
yathā
ardayasi janānetānnirdaya iva bhavadurantakāntāre | jagadīśa tadiha satyaṃ janārdano'sīti ko'traṃ sandehaḥ || 1967 ||
atra janaissarvān pumarthān ardyate yācyata iti vyutpāditasya janārdananāmnaḥ uktarītyā yogato'rthāntarakalpanam ||
yathāvā
ativiśadamadhuratāvakavacorasaste yatassudhābdhisute | tenaiva guṇena sitā babhūva taccharkarā siteti jage || 1968 ||
he sudhābdhisute! idaṃ tadvāgrase'pi vivakṣitatadguṇānuvartanābhiprāyakam | śarkarā tenaiva uktaviśeṣaṇatāvakavacorasenaiva kartrī guṇena karaṇabhūtayā rajjvā sitā aparādhānuguṇaśikṣaṇāya sandānitā | paramārthatastu  tenaiva guṇena viśadimnā madhurimṇā ca guṇenaiva sitā viśadā baddhā ca ‘ṣiñ bandhane' karmaṇi ktaḥ saṃbaddhetyarthaḥ | uktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaśrīvāgrasacauryādevāsyā vaiśadyamādhuryalābhaśśarkarāyā iti bhāvaḥ | ataḥ evaṃ baddhatvādeva siteti jage agīyata ‘śarkarā sitā' iti naighaṇṭukairiti bhāvaḥ | atra siteti śarkarānāmno yogato'rthāntarakalpanam ||
yathāvā
padavīvaśātpraviśato yadabhūccandrasya viśramasthānam | tvadudavasitaśīrṣagṛhaṃ taducyate nātha candraśāleti || 1969 ||
atra candraśāleti nāmno'rthāntarakalpanaṃ yogena ||
yathāvā
yadviśvanāmarūpavyākaraṇapaṭīyasī tava prekṣā | tattvāṃ śeṣagirīśvaramavaimi sacchabditaṃ hare satyam || 1970 ||
he hare yat yasmāt tava prekṣā prajñā ‘prekṣopalabdhiścitsaṃvit' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe prakṛṣṭā īkṣā 'tadaikṣata bahu syām' ityādyuktasaṃkalpaḥ nāmāni prātipadikāni, upalakṣaṇametadākhyātānām | teṣāṃ rūpāṇi svarūpāṇi viśvāni nāmarūpāṇi yasmiṃstattathoktaṃ yadvyākaraṇaṃ śabdanayaḥ tasmin paṭīyasī | anyatra viśvasya cidacidātmakaprapañcasya nāmarūpavyākaraṇe devādivi citrasṛṣṭitannāmadheyakaraṇe paṭīyasī | tat tasmāt evaṃvidhavyākaraṇanayapaṭutaraprajñatvāt, pakṣe tava evaṃvidhasaṃkalpavatvāt | sacchabditaṃ san vidvān iti śabditaṃ uktaṃ ‘sansudhīḥ' ityamaraḥ | pakṣe ‘sadeva somya' iti sacchabdābhilapanīyaṃ atra sacchabdo mānasaṃbandhayogyatvalakṣaṇaṃ sattvaṃ pravṛttinimittīkṛtya paramātmani vartate | tvāṃ śeṣasya patañjalitayā'vatīrṇasya bhagavato'nantasya giri vāci mahābhāṣye ityarthaḥ | īśvaraṃ samarthaṃ tadadhītibodhapracārapaṭīyāṃsamityarthaḥ | 'svāmīśvarādhipati' ityādinā saptamī | satyaṃ niścitaṃ yathā syāttathā | itaratra śeṣagirīśvaraṃ veṅkaṭādrināthaṃ tvāṃ satyaṃ 'satyaṃ jñānam' ityuktaṃ paraṃ brahma avaimīti yojanā | atra uktarītyā śeṣagirīśvaraśabdasya yogenārthāntarakalpanam ||
yathāvā

svastarurudāramaṇinā tvayā parāstastatassvarurbhūtvā | tvāṃ vijigīṣustvannakhabhinno'nvarthābhidho'bhavannṛhare || 1975 ||
svastaruḥ kalpaśākhī udāramaṇinā tvayā parāstaḥ audāryeṇa nirastaḥ tataḥ vairaśuddhividhānāya svaruḥ dambholiḥ bhūtvā
‘śatakoṭissvaruśśamba' ityamaraḥ | tvāṃ vijigīṣussan tvannakhaiḥ bhinnaḥ vidāritaḥ ataeva anvarthābhidhaḥ suṣṭhu aruḥ yasya saḥ svaruḥ balavadvraṇa iti anvarthasaṃjñakaḥ abhavat | ‘vraṇo'striyāmīrmamaruḥ' ityamaraḥ | nṛhare iti dambholivibhedanakṣamanakhaśikhātaikṣṇyātiśayābhiprāyagarbham | atra svastaruḥ svastaruśabdaḥ parāstaḥ parākṛtaḥ staḥ sta iti varṇo yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ataeva svarurbhūtvā uktarītyā svaruśabdo bhūtvetyartho'pi camatkārātiśayādhāyakaḥ | atra svaruriti nāmno yogenārthāntaraparikalpanaṃ viṣamālaṃkārasaṃkīrṇam ||

yathāvā
āhūto'pi niyoddhuṃ bāhubhyāṃ tava nirastarāhubhyām | nāgādbhujago'cyuta tannāgaṃ taṃ prāhurarthatattvajñāḥ || 1981 ||
atra nāga iti nāmnaḥ nāgacchatīti nāga iti yogādarthāntaraṃ kalpitam ||
yathāvā
tvatpadaruciharaṇāttatprahṛtaśśīrṣe'mba lohitaṃ muñcan | lokāntarhita āsīdyallohitakastadeṣa śoṇamaṇiḥ || 1982 ||
he amba! yat yasmāt tvatpadaruciharaṇāddhetoḥ śīṣaṃ tatprahṛtaḥ tena tvatpadena abhihataḥ ataeva lohitaṃ rudhiraṃ muñcan śīrṣe udvaman san lokāntarhitaḥ loke antardhānaṃ gataḥ āsīt
tvatpadābhighātahetukarudhirasrāvimastakatvaprayuktāvamānena kasyāpi mukhaṃ darśayitumapatrapiṣṇutayā kvāpi nilīna iti bhāvaḥ | tat tasmāt eṣa śoṇamaṇiḥ padmarāgaḥ lohitakaḥ āsīdityanuṣajyate | lohitakaḥ lohitaṃ rudhiraṃ ke śirasi yasya sa tathoktaḥ tannāmā āsīt | paramārthatastu lokayoḥ lovarṇakavarṇayoḥ antaḥ madhye hitau hikāratakārau yasya sa tathoktaḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyam | lohita eva lohitakaḥ tannāmā ‘lohitānmaṇau' iti svārthikaḥ kan | 'śoṇaratnaṃ lohitakaḥ' ityamaraḥ | atra svārthakanā niṣpannasya lohitaka iti nāmno yogāntareṇārthāntarakalpanaṃ camatkārāntaraṃ sphuṭameva ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre niruktisara ekaśatatamaḥ

atha pratiṣedhasaraḥ
pratiṣedhaḥ pratītasya pratiṣedhasya varṇanam |

yathā
na sudhāṃśunā na sudhayā na malayamarutā na cāpi tuhinabhṛtā | nirupamakaruṇāśiśirāssvenaiva kṛtā rameśa tava bhaktāḥ || 1983 ||
atra bhagavadbhaktānāṃ sudhāṃśuprabhṛtinirmitatvābhāvaḥ prasiddha eva kīrtyamānasteṣāṃ sudhāṃśuprabhṛtibhyo'pyatiśayitatayā śaiśirya
tvatpadābhighātahetukarudhirasrāvimastakatvaprayuktāvamānena kasyāpi mukhaṃ darśayitumapatrapiṣṇutayā kvāpi nilīna iti bhāvaḥ | tat tasmāt eṣa śoṇamaṇiḥ padmarāgaḥ lohitakaḥ āsīdityanuṣajyate | lohitakaḥ lohitaṃ rudhiraṃ ke śirasi yasya sa tathoktaḥ tannāmā āsīt | paramārthatastu lokayoḥ lovarṇakavarṇayoḥ antaḥ madhye hitau hikāratakārau yasya sa tathoktaḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyam | lohita eva lohitakaḥ tannāmā ‘lohitānmaṇau' iti svārthikaḥ kan | 'śoṇaratnaṃ lohitakaḥ' ityamaraḥ | atra svārthakanā niṣpannasya lohitaka iti nāmno yogāntareṇārthāntarakalpanaṃ camatkārāntaraṃ sphuṭameva ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre niruktisara ekaśatatamaḥ
atha pratiṣedhasaraḥ
pratiṣedhaḥ pratītasya pratiṣedhasya varṇanam |

yathā
na sudhāṃśunā na sudhayā na malayamarutā na cāpi tuhinabhṛtā | nirupamakaruṇāśiśirāssvenaiva kṛtā rameśa tava bhaktāḥ || 1983 ||
atra bhagavadbhaktānāṃ sudhāṃśuprabhṛtinirmitatvābhāvaḥ prasiddha
eva kīrtyamānasteṣāṃ sudhāṃśuprabhṛtibhyo'pyatiśayitatayā śaiśirya
mityamumarthaṃ vyaktīkaroti | sa ca nirupamakaruṇāśiśirā ityanena parimitaśaiśiryavadbhyassudhāṃśuprabhṛtibhyasteṣāṃ viśeṣamupadarśayatā viśeṣaṇenāviṣkṛtaḥ ||
yathāvā
naitānyarjunarathyā durdāntānīndriyāṇi mama śaure | paśyāma niyacchaitānīśo yadi vipatha eva dhāvanti || 1984 ||
arjunasya rathyāḥ rathavoḍhāro'śvāḥ | īśo yadi samartho yadi vipatha eva dhāvanti etāni indriyāṇi niyaccha niyabhaya paśyāma iti yojanā | atra vakturindriyāṇāmarjunarathyāśvatvābhāvaḥ prasiddha eva kīrtyamānasteṣāṃ durdāntatayā durniyamanatvaṃ vyanakti | tacca durdāntānītyādiviśeṣaṇamahimnā kavinaivāviṣkṛtam ||
yathāvā
na daśānano na vālī sudurādharṣo mamaiṣa doṣagaṇaḥ | tamanāvidhya tvamimaṃ mama nātha bhaveḥ kathaṃ mahādhanvā || 1985 ||
atrāpi vakturdoṣagaṇasya daśānanatvādyabhāvaḥ pratīta eva parikīrtyamānastasya durādharṣatāṃ vyañjayati | sā ca sudhurādharṣa iti viśeṣaṇena svoktyaiva prakāśitaḥ ||
nandasutedaṃ na payo na dadhi na maṇḍaṃ nacāpi
navanītam | hanta hareḥ kiṃnvidamiti manaḥkaṣāyaṃ mamātikaṭu heyam || 1986 ||
idaṃ kiṃnviti kiṃvastviti hareriti yojanā | manasaḥ kaṣāyaṃ rāgādivāsanāṃ prasiddhanimbādikvāthaniṣpāditaṃ kaṣāyaṃ ca 'niryāse'pi kaṣāyo'strī' ityamaraḥ | atra vakturmanaḥkaṣāyasya payastvādyabhāvaḥ prasiddha eva kathyamānastasyā svādutamakṣīrādiharaṇa eva tavādaraḥ na tvasmadīyajugupsitataramanaḥkaṣāyaharaṇe iti parihāsamabhipraiti | sa ca atikaṭu heyamiti viśeṣaṇābhyāṃ kaṣāyamiti viśeṣyeṇa ca prāduṣkṛtaḥ ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre pratiṣedhasaro dvyadhikaśatatamaḥ

atha vidhisaraḥ
sā vidhyalaṃkṛtiryatra siddhameva vidhīyate |
yannirjñātavidhānamanupayuktibādhitaṃ sadarthāntaragarbhikāreṇa sundarataraṃ sa vidhināmā'laṃkāraḥ | yathā''huḥ

abhiprāyāntaraṃ garbhīkurvadvicchittiśāli cet ||
vidhyalaṅkāramāhustaṃ sāhityapariśīlinaḥ | iti ||
yathāvā
tatvaṃ bravīmi bhagavaṃstvaṃ tvaṃ jīvastu jīva eva hare | jagadīśvarataddāsau jagadāte kena matimatā'nanyau || 1987 ||
navanītam | hanta hareḥ kiṃnvidamiti manaḥkaṣāyaṃ mamātikaṭu heyam || 1986 ||
idaṃ kiṃnviti kiṃvastviti hareriti yojanā | manasaḥ kaṣāyaṃ rāgādivāsanāṃ prasiddhanimbādikvāthaniṣpāditaṃ kaṣāyaṃ ca 'niryāse'pi kaṣāyo'strī' ityamaraḥ | atra vakturmanaḥkaṣāyasya payastvādyabhāvaḥ prasiddha eva kathyamānastasyā svādutamakṣīrādiharaṇa eva tavādaraḥ na tvasmadīyajugupsitataramanaḥkaṣāyaharaṇe iti parihāsamabhipraiti | sa ca atikaṭu heyamiti viśeṣaṇābhyāṃ kaṣāyamiti viśeṣyeṇa ca prāduṣkṛtaḥ ||

atha vidhisaraḥ
sā vidhyalaṃkṛtiryatra siddhameva vidhīyate |
yannirjñātavidhānamanupayuktibādhitaṃ sadarthāntaragarbhikāreṇa sundarataraṃ sa vidhināmā'laṃkāraḥ | yathā''huḥ
jñātārthasya vidhānaṃ yadupayogaparicyutam |
abhiprāyāntaraṃ garbhīkurvadvicchittiśāli cet ||
vidhyalaṅkāramāhustaṃ sāhityapariśīlinaḥ | iti ||
yathāvā
tatvaṃ bravīmi bhagavaṃstvaṃ tvaṃ jīvastu jīva eva hare | jagadīśvarataddāsau jagadāte kena matimatā'nanyau || 1987 ||
atra paramātmanaḥ paramātmatvasya pratyagātmanaḥ pratyagātmatvasya ca vidhānamanupayogabādhitaṃ sattayoḥ sarvaśeṣitvataccheṣatvasarvajñatvādibhissvabhāvato'tyantavailakṣaṇyaṃ garbhīkaroti | tajjagadīśvaretyādinā uttarārdhenāviṣkṛtam ||
yathāvā
adyaprabhṛti hi lokā yūyaṃ yūyaṃ vayaṃ tu vayameva | indriyavaśagā yūyaṃ vayaṃ tu bhagavadvaśaṃvadā eva || 1988 ||
atra yūyaṃ vayamityanayoḥ yūyaṃ vayamiti vidhānamanupayuktaṃ sat indriyakiṃkaratvabhagavadekakiṃkaratvādiparasparasāṃgatyānarhatālakṣaṇamarthāntaraṃ garbhīkaroti | taccottarārdhena prakaṭitam | atra
adyaprabhṛti he lokā yūyaṃ yūyaṃ vayaṃ vayam |
nāsti | saṃgatirasmākaṃ yuṣmākaṃ ca parasparam ||
arthakāmaparā yūyaṃ nārāyaṇaparā vayam |
vayaṃ tu kiṃkarā viṣṇoryūyamindriyakiṃkarāḥ ||
ityetadanusaṃdheyam | yathāvā
puṇyāpuṇyābhyāṃ yannahanaṃ jantostvayā kṛtaṃ bhagavan | anukūlaṃ tadbhavatu pratikūlaṃ vā'pi nahanameva khalu || 1989 ||
he bhagavan ! jantoḥ prāṇinaḥ puṇyāpuṇyābhyāṃ sukṛtaduṣkṛtābhyāṃ karmabhyāṃ yat nahanaṃ bandhanaṃ tvayā kṛtaṃ tat nahanaṃ anukūlaṃ puṇyaphalabhūtaihikāmuṣmikasukhakaraṃ pratikūlaṃ pāpaphala
bhūtatāpatrayanarakādiduḥkhakaraṃ vā bhavatu nahanameva khalu yathā tathā vā bandhanameva bhavati khalu na hi suvarṇamayamapi nigalaṃ na bandhanāyāvakalpate ayomayameva bandhakamiti dṛṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ veti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe nahanaṃ nahanamityākārakaṃ padaṃ anukūlaṃ anulomatayā paṭhitaṃ pratikūlaṃ pratilomatayā paṭhitaṃ vā nahanameva ubhayathā'pi nahanamityeva niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atra nahanasya nahanatvavidhānamanupapadyamānaṃ niśśreyasagatipratirodhitāṃ garbhīkaroti | sā ca puṇyāpuṇyakaraṇakatvaviśeṣaṇātprakāśitā | ‘tadā vidvān puṇyapāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti' ityādiśrutibhiḥ puṇyāpuṇyavidhūnanānantarameva hi niśśreyasagatiḥ pratipādyate | yadvā tannahanatvavidhānaṃ bhagavaditaradurmocatvaṃ garbhīkaroti |
paśavaḥ pāśitāḥ pūrvaṃ parameṇa samādhinā |
tenaiva mocanīyāste nānyo mocayituṃ kṣamaḥ ||
iti pramāṇāt 'kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare' ityādikamapyatrānukūlam | eṣu vidhipratiṣedhodāharaṇeṣu arthāntarasaṃkramitavācyalakṣaṇavyaṅgyārthasadbhāve'pi na dhvanitvavyapadeśaḥ, teṣāṃ vyaṅgyānāṃ 'nirupamakaruṇāśiśirā | jagadīśvarataddāsau' iti svotkyaivāviṣkaraṇāt | nanu cākṣepālaṅkārāvāntarabhedatvena vidhipratiṣedhayoḥ pūrvamukterna pṛthagalaṃkāratayā parigaṇanamucitamiti cet bādhitayostayorākṣepabhedatvamabhyadhāyi prāk | iha tu siddhayoralaṃkārateti gṛhyatām ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre vidhisarastryadhikaśatatamaḥ
<54>
bhūtatāpatrayanarakādiduḥkhakaraṃ vā bhavatu nahanameva khalu yathā tathā vā bandhanameva bhavati khalu na hi suvarṇamayamapi nigalaṃ na bandhanāyāvakalpate ayomayameva bandhakamiti dṛṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ veti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe nahanaṃ nahanamityākārakaṃ padaṃ anukūlaṃ anulomatayā paṭhitaṃ pratikūlaṃ pratilomatayā paṭhitaṃ vā nahanameva ubhayathā'pi nahanamityeva niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atra nahanasya nahanatvavidhānamanupapadyamānaṃ niśśreyasagatipratirodhitāṃ garbhīkaroti | sā ca puṇyāpuṇyakaraṇakatvaviśeṣaṇātprakāśitā | ‘tadā vidvān puṇyapāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti' ityādiśrutibhiḥ puṇyāpuṇyavidhūnanānantarameva hi niśśreyasagatiḥ pratipādyate | yadvā tannahanatvavidhānaṃ bhagavaditaradurmocatvaṃ garbhīkaroti |
paśavaḥ pāśitāḥ pūrvaṃ parameṇa samādhinā |
tenaiva mocanīyāste nānyo mocayituṃ kṣamaḥ ||
iti pramāṇāt 'kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare' ityādikamapyatrānukūlam | eṣu vidhipratiṣedhodāharaṇeṣu arthāntarasaṃkramitavācyalakṣaṇavyaṅgyārthasadbhāve'pi na dhvanitvavyapadeśaḥ, teṣāṃ vyaṅgyānāṃ 'nirupamakaruṇāśiśirā | jagadīśvarataddāsau' iti svotkyaivāviṣkaraṇāt | nanu cākṣepālaṅkārāvāntarabhedatvena vidhipratiṣedhayoḥ pūrvamukterna pṛthagalaṃkāratayā parigaṇanamucitamiti cet bādhitayostayorākṣepabhedatvamabhyadhāyi prāk | iha tu siddhayoralaṃkārateti gṛhyatām ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre vidhisarastryadhikaśatatamaḥ
atha hetusaraḥ
sākaṃ hetumatā heturuktaśceddheturīritaḥ |
kāryasya kāraṇena saha varṇanaṃ hetvalaṃkāra ityarthaḥ | ayaṃ jayadevena candrāloke tṛtīyamayūkhe kāryanāmnā kāvyalakṣaṇatayā vyavahṛtaḥ | yathā
kāryaṃ phalopalambhaścedvyāpārādvastuto'thavā | asāvudeti śītāṃśurmānacchedāya subhruvām ||
iti | dīkṣitaistu ayamapi camatkāritayā'laṃkāreṣu parigaṇitaḥ ||
yathā
paripālanāya jagatāmavatārastava na cātra saṃdehaḥ | matpālanāya cirayasi kasmāditi me rameśa saṃdehaḥ || 1990 ||
atra jagatparipālanāvatārayoḥ kāryakāraṇayossahavarṇanam||
yathāvā
marakatakaṃ phaṇiśikhariṇi kimapi tadamarādyatāṃ prapadya tarām | bhuvanamadhaḥkṛtapaṅkaṃ viracayituṃ katakatāmahodhate || 1991 ||
phaṇiśikhariṇi kimapi tat lokottaramiti yāvat | marakatakaṃ gārutmataṃ amarādyatāṃ devottamatvaṃ śrīnivāsatvamityarthaḥ | bhuvanaṃ viṣṭapaṃ salilaṃ ca adhaḥkṛtaḥ adhobhāgaṃ nītaḥ tiraskṛ
taśca paṅkaḥ kardamaḥ kaluṣaṃ ca yasya tattathoktaṃ viracayituṃ katakatāṃ paṅkilāmbuprasādakavṛkṣaviśeṣaphalatāṃ dhatte | aho āścaryametat | yadgārutmataṃ śrīnivāsatāṃ prāpya katakatāṃ dhatta iti | marakatakamiti padaṃ amarādyatāṃ maśca raśca marau ādyau varṇau yasya tat marādyaṃ tanna bhavatītyamarādyaṃ tasya bhāvaḥ tattā tāṃ prapadya katakatāṃ dhatte marakatakaśabdaḥ prāthamikamakārarephayorudvāsane katakamiti niṣpadyata ityarthāntaraṃ ca camatkāri | atra katakatādhāraṇasya hetoḥ bhuvanapaṅkādhaḥkaraṇena hetumatā saha varṇanaṃ śleṣamūlakavirodhottambhitam ||
yathāvā
prāptaśśubhavartitvaṃ suśrīrājyābhiṣekato bhagavan | sumahānākuladīpastamovidhūtyai vibhāti bhavadīyaḥ || 1992 ||
he bhagavan śubhe vartata iti śubhaṃ vartayatīti vā śubhavartī tasya bhāvaḥ śubhavartitvam | pakṣe śubhā vartiḥ daśā yasya tattvaṃ 'vartirdīpadaśāgātralepayormaṇirañjane' iti ratnamālā | prāptaḥ rājyābhiṣekataḥ suśrīḥ | suśrīrājyetyatra ‘ro ri' iti rephalope ‘ḍhralope' iti dīrghaḥ | pakṣe ājyābhiṣekataḥ suśrīḥ sumahāḥ nā kuladīpaḥ iti chedaḥ | śobhanaṃ mahaḥ utsavaḥ
yasya sa tathoktaḥ ‘nityotsavo bhavetteṣām' ityuktarītyā nirantarotsavaśālī mahatejasvīti vā | ‘mahastūtsavatejasoḥ' iti sāntanānārtheṣvamaraḥ | kuladīpaḥ svānvayaprakāśakaḥ bhavadīyaḥ nā pumān | pakṣe bhavadīyaḥ sumahāṃścāsau anākuladīpaśca anaśvaraḥ
pradīpa ityarthaḥ | tamasaḥ tamoguṇasya timirasya ca vidhūtyai tiraskārāya vibhāti | atra prakṛtāprakṛtagocaraśleṣe bhagavadbhaktaprakāśasya tadīyamahādīpaprakāśasya ca hetoḥ tamovidhūteḥ phalasya ca sahavarṇanam ||
yathāvā
khyāto divyāñjanadharavartitayā yena dṛśi vinihitastvam | sa khalu sudṛgabjalocana vettuṃ śaknoti gūḍhamapyartham || 1993 ||
divyaḥ yaḥ añjanadharaḥ añjanādriḥ tasmin vartata iti tathoktaḥ | tasya bhāvaḥ tattā tayā | pakṣe divyaṃ ślāghyaṃ yat añjanaṃ tasya dharā dhārayitrī vartiḥ nayanāñjanalekhā tasyāḥ bhāvaḥ tattā tayā 'vartirbheṣajanirmāṇanayanāñjanalekhayoḥ' iti viśvamedinyau | khyātaḥ tvaṃ yena sudṛśā dṛśi locane vinihitaḥ dṛṣṭa iti yāvat sa sudṛk jñānī | pakṣe parokṣavastusākṣātkārakṣamadṛṣṭirityarthaḥ | gūḍhamapi arthaṃ śāstratatvārthaṃ pakṣe nidhirūpaṃ dhanaṃ vettuṃ jñātuṃ śaknoti khalu | atra śrīnivāsadarśanatattvārthavedanayoḥ añjanavartinayananyāsanidhidarśanayośca śliṣṭayorhetuhetumatossahavarṇanam ||
yathāvā
śubhajanakabhūharo'vadhi yena daśāsyassa eva kosalarāṭ | avadhīcca hiraṇyākṣaṃ tādṛkṣamasaśśubhāya kila jagatām || 1994 ||
śubhā yā janakabhūḥ sītā tasyāḥ haraḥ daśāsyaḥ yena kosalarājā avadhi ahanyata sa eva kosalarāṭ dāśarathiḥ asaḥ sa na bhavatītyasaḥ akosalarājarūpa iti yāvat | ‘etattadoḥ' iti sūtre ‘anañsamāse' iti paryudāsānna tacchabdasya sulopaḥ | pakṣe asaḥ savarṇarahitaḥ kosalarāṭ kolarāṭ | purā varāhaśreṣṭhassannityarthaḥ | tādṛkṣaṃ śubhajanakabhūharaṃ śubhajanikā jagatkṣemapradā yā bhūḥ pṛthvī tasyāḥ haramityarthaḥ | hiraṇyākṣaṃ jagatāṃ śubhāya kalyāṇāya avadhīt | atra rāvaṇahiraṇyākṣavadhalakṣaṇahetunā saha jagacchubhalakṣaṇahetumataḥ pratipādanam ||
yathāvā
yastvayi samrāṭ pratyayamātanute bhāvakarmaṇorekam | so'rhati sāmrājyapadaṃ labdhuṃ vṛddhaṃ samastavarṇaguru || 1995 ||
he samrāṭ rājādhirāja he bhagavan ! ‘mo rāji samaḥ kvau' iti samo makārasya makāra eva | nānusvāraḥ 'samrājo'gnīndraviṣṇvarkā yaśca syānmaṇḍaleśvaraḥ' iti ratnamālā | ‘eṣa brahmalokassamrāḍiti hovāca | rājādhirājassarveṣāṃ viṣṇurbrahmamayo mahān' ityādipramāṇānyatrānusaṃhitāni | yaḥ pumān tvayi viṣaye bhāvakarmaṇoḥ manaḥkarmaṇoḥ | idaṃ vacaso'pyupalakṣaṇam | ekaṃ ekarūpaṃ
'vacasyekaṃ manasyekaṃ karmaṇyekaṃ mahātmanām'
ityuktarītyā avisaṃvādītyarthaḥ | pratyayaṃ viśvāsaṃ dhyānaṃ vā
ātanute | sa pumān vṛddhaṃ abhivṛddhimat samastaiḥ varṇaiḥ brahmakṣatrādibhiḥ guru śreṣṭhaṃ sāmrājyapadaṃ ādhirājyasthānaṃ labdhuṃ arhati |
tvayi samrāji karaṇatrayasārūpyeṇa bhaktiṃ vidadhānaḥ tatkratunayātsāmrājyavaibhavamaśnuta iti bhāvaḥ | anyatra he samrāḍiti samrāṭchabdasyaiva saṃbodhanam | yaḥ pumān tvayi samrāṭchabde ityarthaḥ | bhāvakarmaṇoḥ bhāve karmaṇi ca ekaṃ pratyayaṃ ‘guṇavacanabrāhmaṇādibhyaḥ karmaṇi ca' iti vihitaṃ ṣyañpratyayaṃ ātanute vidhatte sa pumān vṛddhaṃ 'vṛddhiryasyācāmādistadvṛddham' iti vihitavṛddhasaṃjñakaṃ sarve varṇāḥ akṣarāṇi guravo yasya tattathoktaṃ sāmrājyapadaṃ sāmrājyaśabdaṃ labdhumarhati | yassamrāṭchabdāt ṣyañaṃ vidhatte sa pumān vṛddhaṃ sarvavarṇaguru ca sāmrājyamiti padaṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ | sāmrājyamiti padasya uktarītyā niṣpannasya vṛddhatvātsarvavarṇagurutvācca tathoktiḥ | atra pakṣadvaye'pi samrāji pratyayavidhānasya hetossāmrājyapadalābharūpahetumatā saha varṇanam ||
yathāvā
sāmodasaumyasumanāssattaruriva suphaladaśśubhacchāyaḥ | svapadopanataṃ sukhayati bhagavān saṃsṛtigatāgataśrāntam || 1996 ||
sāmodaṃ ca saumyaṃ anugraṃ ca sumanaḥ śobhanaṃ manaḥ yasya saḥ | sāmodāḥ saumyāḥ sumanaso devāḥ nityasūrayo vā yasmātsa iti vā | pakṣe sasaurabhasukumārakusuma ityarthaḥ | suphaladaḥ niśśreyasaparyantasakalaphalapradaḥ | anyatra phalāni svapariṇatāni dadātīti tathoktaḥ | anaghā sarvaduḥkhanivartinī chāyā dīptiḥ anātapaśca yasya saḥ bhagavān sattaruriva mākandādiśubhavṛkṣa iva saṃsṛtigatāgatena saṃsārayātāyātena, anyatra samyak sṛtau mārge gatāgatena śrāntaṃ svapadopanataṃ svacaraṇaśaraṇāgataṃ svamūle viśra
mārthamāgataṃ ca janaṃ sukhayati | atrāpi hetuphalayorbhagavatsukhaprāpaṇayossāhityena varṇanam | pūrvodāharaṇeṣu phalaṃ caturthītumunbhyāṃ sākṣānnirdiṣṭam | atra tu gamyamānaṃ taditi viśeṣaḥ | nanu kāvyaliṅgato'sya ko bheda iti cet ucyate kāvyaliṅge hetuhetumatorupapādakopapādyabhāvaḥ, iha tu prayojakaprayojyabhāvo vicchittyādhāyakaḥ | hotorgamyatāyāmeva cārutātiśayādalaṃkāratvaṃ na tu sākṣāttadvācakapadopādāne iti tūbhayostulyameva | atrodāharaṇe upamāṃsaṃkīrṇatvaṃ ca viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
śrīnidhimapekṣamāṇastvāṃ bhavamevopadhāvati pumānyaḥ | phalamasyedaṃ bhūtiṃ vindan bhrāmyati samaṃ piśācairyat || 1997 ||
śrīnidhiṃ sapannidhānaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ ca tvāṃ upekṣamāṇaḥ pumān bhavaṃ saṃsārameva paśupatimeva upadhāvati sevate tasya idameva phalaṃ yadayaṃ pumān bhūtiṃ saṃpadaṃ vindan 'lakṣaṇahetvoḥ' iti hetvarthe śatā | heturiha phalam | saṃpadaṃ prāptumityarthaḥ | pakṣe bhūtiṃ bhasma vindan prāpnuvan bhasmoddhūlitassannityarthaḥ | piśācaissamaṃ tulyaṃ yathāsyāttathā, anyatra rudraparivāraiḥ piśācairdevayoniviśeṣaiḥ samaṃ sākaṃ bhrāmyatīti yat etadeva tasya phalamityarthaḥ | atrāpi hetuhetumatorbhavāśrayaparibhramaṇayorvarṇanaṃ śleṣasaṃkīrṇam ||
keciddhetumato hetoścaikyaṃ hetuṃ budhā jaguḥ |
kāryakāraṇayorabhedavarṇanaṃ hetvalaṃkāra iti keciprācīnā
bruvanti smetyarthaḥ ||
yathā
tridaśāhitakulaśikṣā tridivavadhūkarṇapatrayugarakṣā | veṃkaṭaśailādhyakṣā vyaktiḥ kā'pyaikṣi jagadavanadakṣā || 1998 ||
atra tridaśāhitakulaśikṣādihetorbhagavatastacchikṣādihetumataścaikyaṃ varṇitam ||
yathāvā
grāhagṛhītamataṅgajajīvitamativelabhāvitaṃ kṛtibhiḥ | kamalānayanānandaṃ kalayemahi kimapi tejasāṃ bṛndam || 1999 ||
atrāpi grāhagṛhītamataṅgajajīvitahetorbhagavataḥ tajjīvitena phalena sahaikyaṃ varṇitam ||
yathāvā
smitagarbhagaṇḍaphalakaṃ sitarucirucirābhamullasattilakam | ānandameva bhagavannadasphuritamānanaṃ tavāvaimi || 2000 ||
sphuritaṃ adaḥ idaṃ tava ānanaṃ ānandameva avaimīti yojanā | pakṣe adasphuritaṃ dakārasphuraṇarahitaṃ ānandaṃ ānanaṃ
avaimi | utsāritadakāramānandaśabdaṃ ānanamiti niṣpannaṃ jānāmītyarthaḥ | atrāpi ānandahetorbhagavadānanasyānandena hetumatā sahaikyam | camatkārāntaraṃ pūrvodāharaṇato viśeṣaḥ ||
manvabhedavyapadeśasyācamatkāritayā kathaṃkāramalaṃkārateti cenna kāryāvaśyaṃbhāvatacchaighryābhyāṃ sahakāryantaranairapekṣyeṇa kāryotpādanapratyāyane tasya camatkāritāyā durnivāratayā'laṃkāratvasya niṣpratyūhatvāt | rūpake sādṛśyādabhedavyapadeśaḥ, iha tu kāryakāraṇabhāvāditi bhidā | rūpakātiśayokterasya ca vailakṣaṇyaṃ tatraivāvādiṣma ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre hetusaraścaturadhikaśatatamaḥ
